### [1] [Budget-Aware Adaptive Adversarial Patches for Black-Box Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18318)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,David Fernandez,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种查询高效、预算自适应的黑盒对抗补丁攻击方法，联合优化补丁位置、纹理和大小，并通过严格的纯图像抑制测试和物理世界实验验证其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对抗补丁攻击存在三个空白：(i) 很少有基于评分的黑盒攻击在紧查询预算下联合优化补丁的位置、纹理和大小；(ii) 攻击成功很少与补丁的视觉占用面积相关联；(iii) 评估常常将期望变换鲁棒性与纯视图抑制混为一谈。

Method: 提出一种查询高效、预算自适应的黑盒攻击方法，结合轻量级上下文汤普森采样定位器与NES风格像素更新，仅在进展停滞时扩大补丁。评估采用严格的纯图像抑制测试，对期望变换单独审计但不作为成功的替代指标，并引入可选的外观/可打印性权重以展现强度与可见性之间的权衡。

Result: 在YOLOv5、Faster R-CNN和YOLOS上，该方法对CNN检测器实现强抑制，对Transformer检测器实现显著抑制，使用紧凑补丁并在查询与视觉占用之间展现出清晰权衡。物理打印-拍摄实验进一步证明了对未见物理对象和视角的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过联合优化补丁位置、纹理和大小，在紧查询预算下实现了高效的黑盒对抗攻击，并建立了更严格的评估标准，揭示了强度与可见性之间的权衡。

Original Abstract: Adversarial patches pose a practical threat to modern object detectors. Prior work shows vulnerability, but three gaps limit actionable insight: (i) few \emph{score-based black-box} attacks \emph{jointly} optimize patch \emph{location, texture, and size} under tight query budgets; (ii) success is rarely tied to the patch's \emph{visual footprint}; and (iii) evaluations often conflate EOT robustness with plain-view suppression. We present \method{}, a query-efficient, budget-adaptive black-box attack that couples a lightweight \emph{Contextual Thompson-Sampling} placer with NES-style pixel updates, growing the patch only when progress stalls. Reporting is anchored by a \emph{strict plain-image} suppression test; EOT is audited but never used as a substitute for success, and optional appearance/printability weights expose strength--visibility trade-offs. Across YOLOv5, Faster R-CNN, and YOLOS, \method{} achieves strong suppression on CNN-based detectors and substantial suppression on the transformer-based detector, using compact patches and exposing clear query--footprint trade-offs relative to fixed-size and heuristic baselines. A print--capture pilot further shows transfer across unseen physical objects and viewpoints.

中文摘要翻译: 对抗补丁对现代目标检测器构成了实际威胁。先前工作展示了其脆弱性，但存在三个空白限制了可操作的见解：(i) 很少有基于评分的黑盒攻击在紧查询预算下联合优化补丁的位置、纹理和大小；(ii) 攻击成功很少与补丁的视觉占用面积相关联；(iii) 评估常常将期望变换鲁棒性与纯视图抑制混为一谈。我们提出一种查询高效、预算自适应的黑盒攻击方法，该方法结合了轻量级上下文汤普森采样定位器与NES风格像素更新，仅在进展停滞时扩大补丁。报告以严格的纯图像抑制测试为锚点；对期望变换进行审计但从不将其作为成功的替代指标，可选的表观/可打印性权重揭示了强度与可见性之间的权衡。在YOLOv5、Faster R-CNN和YOLOS上，该方法对CNN检测器实现了强抑制，对Transformer检测器实现了显著抑制，使用了紧凑补丁，并相对于固定大小和启发式基线展现了清晰的查询-视觉占用权衡。一项打印-拍摄实验进一步证明了对未见物理对象和视角的迁移能力。

</details>


### [2] [CAOA -- Completion-Assisted Object-CAD Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18429)
*Hiranya Garbha Kumar,Minhas Kamal,Balakrishnan Prabhakaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAOA方法，结合点云补全与对称性感知的位姿估计，实现CAD模型与RGB-D扫描对象的精确对齐，在Scan2CAD上提升17%精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RGB-D扫描存在噪声、不完整及分割误差导致的几何畸变，现有CAD对齐方法精度不足，且点云补全方法在真实场景中泛化差。

Method: 1) 语义和上下文感知的点云补全模块；2) 对称性感知的相对位姿估计算法（对称性感知损失）；3) 针对室内场景的合成数据生成策略缩小合成到真实域差距；4) 发布专家标注的S2C-Completion数据集（8500+对）。

Result: 在Scan2CAD基准上，CAOA相比最先进方法实现17%的精度提升；通过定量比较验证了合成数据生成策略有效缩小域差距。

Conclusion: 集成点云补全与对称性感知位姿估计可有效提升CAD与扫描对象的对齐精度，合成数据策略和S2C-Completion数据集为后续研究提供了有价值的资源。

Original Abstract: Accurately aligning CAD models to their corresponding objects in indoor RGB-D scans is a central challenge in 3D semantic reconstruction. The task requires estimating a 9-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) pose-position, rotation, and scale along three axes-but is hindered by noisy and incomplete scans, as well as segmentation errors that cause geometric distortions. We present Completion-Assisted Object-CAD Alignment (CAOA), a method that integrates a semantically and contextually aware point cloud completion module with a symmetry-aware relative pose estimation algorithm, enabling precise alignment of CAD models to scanned objects. Existing completion methods are typically trained and evaluated on synthetic datasets, which often fail to generalize to real-world scans. To bridge this gap, we introduce a synthetic data generation strategy tailored to indoor scenes, significantly reducing the synthetic-to-real domain gap-validated through quantitative comparisons with widely used completion datasets. In addition, we release S2C-Completion, an expert-annotated dataset of over 8,500 object-CAD pairs from Scan2CAD, created for real-world indoor single-object completion and intended as a new benchmark for this task. For object-CAD alignment, we incorporate symmetry information via a symmetry-aware loss, improving robustness to symmetric ambiguities. On the Scan2CAD benchmark, CAOA achieves a 17% accuracy improvement over state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 将CAD模型与其对应的室内RGB-D扫描对象精确对齐是3D语义重建中的一个核心挑战。该任务需要估计9自由度（DoF）位姿——位置、旋转及三轴尺度——但受到噪声和不完整扫描以及导致几何畸变的分割误差的阻碍。我们提出了完成辅助的对象-CAD对齐（CAOA）方法，该方法将语义和上下文感知的点云补全模块与对称性感知的相对位姿估计算法相结合，能够实现CAD模型与扫描对象的精确对齐。现有的点云补全方法通常在合成数据集上训练和评估，往往难以泛化到真实扫描数据。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了一种针对室内场景量身定制的合成数据生成策略，通过定量比较与广泛使用的补全数据集验证，显著缩小了合成到真实的域差距。此外，我们发布了S2C-Completion数据集，这是一个来自Scan2CAD的包含8500多对对象-CAD对的专家标注数据集，用于真实室内单对象补全，旨在作为该任务的新基准。在对象-CAD对齐方面，我们通过对称性感知损失融入对称性信息，增强了对对称歧义性的鲁棒性。在Scan2CAD基准上，CAOA相比最先进方法实现了17%的精度提升。

</details>


### [3] [RegimeVGGT: Layer-Wise Spatially Preserving Redundancy Removal for Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18439)
*Jinhao You,Shuo Lyu,Zhuohang Lyu,Tanxuan Li,Zibo Zhao,Jiaxiang Hu,Kai Tang,Yichen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RegimeVGGT是一种无需训练的加速方法，通过按层进行U型双轴压缩（包括显著性引导的带状合并和选择性保护的K/V降采样），在保持重建质量的同时实现6.7倍加速，解决了VGGT中二次交叉帧注意力限制可扩展性的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VGGT的二次交叉帧注意力限制了其可扩展性，现有训练无关的加速器沿单一轴均匀减少计算量，忽略了层的异质性，缺乏对不同层注意力机制差异的针对性优化。

Method: 通过光谱、探测和因果分析揭示三个注意力层阶段，提出RegimeVGGT，采用按层的U型双轴压缩：1) 显著性引导的带状合并，保护几何和边缘显著令牌；2) 选择性保护的K/V降采样，通过相移空间网格、参考帧锚点以及未压缩的相机/注册令牌，保持跨帧空间覆盖和姿势关键路径。

Result: 在无需训练的条件下，RegimeVGGT在匹配重建质量时相比VGGT*实现了6.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过针对不同层级的注意力机制特性设计差异化压缩策略，可以有效解决VGGT二次注意力带来的可扩展性问题，在保持高质量三维重建的同时实现显著加速。

Original Abstract: Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) recovers dense 3D scene structure from multi-view images in one forward pass, but quadratic cross-frame attention limits its scalability. Existing training-free accelerators reduce computation uniformly along one axis, missing layer heterogeneity. Our spectral, probing, and causal analyses reveal three regimes: shallow layers lack cross-view structure, middle layers drive cross-view alignment, and deep layers are redundant for dense geometry yet their cross-frame attention remains essential for pose. RegimeVGGT applies layer-wise U-shaped compression along two axes: Saliency-Guided Banded Merging protects geometry- and edge-salient tokens, while Selectively Protected K/V Downsampling preserves cross-frame spatial coverage and the pose-critical path through a phase-shifted spatial grid, a reference-frame anchor, and uncompressed camera/register tokens. Training-free, RegimeVGGT achieves a 6.7x speedup over VGGT* at matched reconstruction quality.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉几何基础Transformer（VGGT）能够在一次前向传播中从多视图图像恢复密集的三维场景结构，但其二次交叉帧注意力限制了其可扩展性。现有的无需训练加速器沿单一轴均匀减少计算量，忽略了层的异质性。我们的光谱、探测和因果分析揭示了三个注意力层级阶段：浅层缺乏跨视图结构，中层驱动跨视图对齐，深层对密集几何存在冗余但其交叉帧注意力对位姿估计仍然至关重要。RegimeVGGT沿两个轴应用按层的U型压缩：显著性引导的带状合并保护了几何和边缘显著性令牌，而选择性保护的K/V降采样通过相移空间网格、参考帧锚点以及未压缩的相机/注册令牌，保持了跨帧空间覆盖和位姿关键路径。RegimeVGGT无需训练，在匹配重建质量的情况下相比VGGT*实现了6.7倍加速。

</details>


### [4] [Technical Report for ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge: Leveraging DINOv3 for Robust Outdoor Scene Understanding in Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18582)
*Jaeil Park,Hyobin Choi,Sangjin Lee,Hyungtae Lim,Sung-Hoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对ICRA 2026现场机器人研讨会举办的GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战赛，提出了一种结合自监督DINOv3 ViT-L/16骨干网络、ViT-Adapter和Mask2Former掩码分类解码器的网络设计，以及多尺度、水平翻转测试时增强和集成策略，以76.57%的综合得分获得第一名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决野外场景图像在64类细粒度语义分割任务上的挑战，提升非结构化环境下的语义分割精度。

Method: (a) 网络级设计：结合自监督DINOv3 ViT-L/16骨干网络、ViT-Adapter和Mask2Former掩码分类解码器，并在全局[CLS]标记上添加粗类别辅助损失；(b) 推理时聚合策略：采用多尺度和水平翻转的测试时增强，并集成基于Codabench分数选择的前三个检查点。

Result: 官方综合得分76.57%，包括69.32%的细类mIoU和83.81%的类别级mIoU，在最终阶段排行榜上排名第一。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战赛中取得了最优性能，验证了自监督视觉Transformer与掩码分类范式在野外场景细粒度分割任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: The GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge at the ICRA 2026 Workshop on Field Robotics evaluates dense semantic segmentation of off-road imagery over a fine-grained taxonomy of 64 classes and 11 evaluated non-void coarse categories. We present the first-place solution to this challenge. Our solution comprises two complementary improvements: (a) a network-level design that combines a self-supervised DINOv3 ViT-L/16 backbone, a ViT-Adapter, and a Mask2Former mask-classification decoder, together with a coarse-category auxiliary loss on the global [CLS] token; and (b) an inference-time aggregation strategy based on multi-scale and horizontal-flip test-time augmentation and an ensemble of the top three checkpoints selected using Codabench scores. Our method achieves an official composite score of 76.57%, consisting of 69.32% fine-class mIoU and 83.81% category-level mIoU, and ranks first on the final phase leaderboard: www.codabench.org/competitions/14257/#/results-tab.

中文摘要翻译: 在ICRA 2026现场机器人研讨会上举办的GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战赛，旨在评估在包含64个类别和11个已评估非虚空粗类别的细粒度分类体系下，对野外场景图像进行密集语义分割的性能。我们提出了该挑战赛的第一名解决方案。我们的方案包含两个互补的改进：(a) 网络级设计，结合了自监督DINOv3 ViT-L/16骨干网络、ViT-Adapter和Mask2Former掩码分类解码器，并在全局[CLS]标记上添加了粗类别辅助损失；(b) 推理时聚合策略，基于多尺度和水平翻转的测试时增强，并结合基于Codabench分数选择的前三个检查点的集成。我们的方法取得了76.57%的官方综合得分，包括69.32%的细类mIoU和83.81%的类别级mIoU，在最终阶段排行榜上排名第一：www.codabench.org/competitions/14257/#/results-tab。

</details>


### [5] [Reasoning as Intersection: Consensus-Frame Alignment for Visual Focus in Video-MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18441)
*Chengwen Liu,Zhe Huang,Jisheng Dang,Hong Peng,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CF-GRPO框架，通过构建共识帧先验和模型侧帧使用评分，实现无时间标注的过程级奖励，提升视频多模态大模型的证据感知推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有结果奖励对视频多模态大模型的推理能力提升有限，无法明确指导模型应依据哪些视觉证据来支撑答案，缺乏过程级的时间标注监督信号。

Method: 提出CF-GRPO框架：1) 从视频内在线索（时间覆盖、场景切换线索、查询条件化视觉相关性）构建共识帧先验；2) 从视觉和响应表示计算模型侧帧使用评分；3) 通过共识帧奖励（CFR）优化两者的一致性，采用显著性感知的稀疏聚合和分布锐化，无需人工时间标注。

Result: 在多个复杂视频推理基准上取得竞争性表现，在多个指标上优于代表性Video-MLLM和RL基线方法，且共识先验提供了训练中强调的证据帧的可解释视图。

Conclusion: CF-GRPO通过无标注的过程级奖励有效提升了视频多模态大模型的证据感知推理能力，同时提供了可解释性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but applying outcome-only rewards to video multimodal large language models (Video-MLLMs) provides limited guidance on which visual evidence should support the answer. Inspired by multisensory integration, where consistent cues can enhance the salience and reliability of perceptual estimates, we introduce Consensus Frame GRPO (CF-GRPO), a temporal-annotation-free process-level reward framework for evidence-aware video reasoning. CF-GRPO constructs a consensus frame prior from intrinsic video cues, including temporal coverage, scene-transition cues, and query-conditioned visual relevance. It then computes a model-side frame-use score from visual and response representations and optimizes their agreement through the Consensus Frame Reward (CFR). With salience-aware sparse aggregation and distribution sharpening, CFR provides a high-contrast reward signal without requiring human temporal annotations. Experiments show that VideoCFR achieves competitive performance across complex video reasoning benchmarks and improves several metrics over representative Video-MLLM and RL baselines, while the consensus prior provides an interpretable view of the evidence frames emphasized during training. The implementation is available at https://github.com/1Pansy/VideoCFR.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但仅将结果奖励应用于视频多模态大语言模型（Video-MLLMs）时，对哪些视觉证据应支撑答案所提供的指导有限。受多感觉整合（一致线索可增强感知估计的显著性和可靠性）的启发，我们提出了共识帧GRPO（CF-GRPO），一种无需时间标注的过程级奖励框架，用于证据感知的视频推理。CF-GRPO从视频内在线索（包括时间覆盖、场景切换线索和查询条件化的视觉相关性）构建共识帧先验。然后，它从视觉和响应表示中计算模型侧帧使用评分，并通过共识帧奖励（CFR）优化两者的一致性。通过显著性感知的稀疏聚合和分布锐化，CFR无需人工时间标注即可提供高对比度的奖励信号。实验表明，VideoCFR在多个复杂视频推理基准上取得了竞争性表现，并在多个指标上优于代表性的Video-MLLM和RL基线方法，同时共识先验提供了训练中强调的证据帧的可解释视图。实现代码见 https://github.com/1Pansy/VideoCFR。

</details>


### [6] [Intrinsic 4D Gaussian Segmentation from Scene Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18623)
*Hasan Yazar,Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi,Erchin Serpedin,Mehmet Tuncel,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Intrinsic-GS提出了一种无需训练、无需掩码监督的4D高斯分割方法，通过构建高斯原语间的稀疏亲和图并利用Leiden社区检测实现物体分割，在Neu3D和HyperNeRF基准上达到与SAM监督方法相当的性能，且速度提升12.5倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D动态场景分割方法依赖从SAM等基础模型生成的2D掩码，需跨多帧多视图生成掩码，成本高昂且分割质量高度依赖外部掩码的一致性和质量。作者探索能否从高斯原语自身恢复物体级结构，从而摆脱对外部掩码的依赖。

Method: 提出Intrinsic-GS，一种无需训练、无需掩码的方法。核心步骤：(1) 从高斯原语的外观、朝向、尺度、变形轨迹以及非学习渲染边界线索构建稀疏亲和图；(2) 使用Leiden社区检测算法对图进行分割，无需基础模型和学习的特征场。

Result: 在Neu3D上达到0.746 mIoU，在HyperNeRF上达到0.575 mIoU；Neu3D上纯几何变体达到0.902 mIoU，与SAM监督的TRASE相当。在HyperNeRF上，运行速度比掩码监督流程的掩码生成和特征渲染阶段快12.5倍。

Conclusion: 大量分割信号已编码在高斯原自身中，Intrinsic-GS为3D和4D高斯分割提供了一种快速、无需掩码的方向，在外部掩码不可靠或昂贵的情况下有望实现更泛化、更鲁棒的分割。

Original Abstract: Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs deforming scenes with high fidelity and is increasingly adopted as a representation for dynamic 3D scenes. Putting such a scene to use, for editing, manipulation or motion analysis, first requires segmenting it: grouping the Gaussian primitives into coherent objects. Current pipelines obtain this grouping by importing 2D masks from foundation models such as SAM and lifting or distilling them into the Gaussian representation. In dynamic scenes these masks must be generated across many frames and views, which is costly, and the resulting segmentation can depend strongly on the quality and consistency of those external masks. We ask how much object-level structure can instead be recovered from the Gaussians themselves, and propose Intrinsic-GS, a training-free, mask-free method that builds a sparse affinity graph over Gaussian primitives from appearance, orientation, scale, deformation-trajectory and non-learned rendered-boundary cues. The graph is partitioned with Leiden community detection, requiring no foundation model and no learned feature field. On the standard 4D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, Neu3D and HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS recovers substantial object structure without mask supervision, reaching 0.746 mIoU on Neu3D and 0.575 on HyperNeRF; on Neu3D, a geometry-only variant reaches 0.902 mIoU, matching SAM-supervised TRASE. On HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS runs 12.5x faster than the mask-generation and feature-rendering stages used by mask-supervised pipelines. These results suggest that much of the segmentation signal is already encoded in the Gaussians themselves, offering a fast, mask-free direction for 3D and 4D Gaussian segmentation that may also point toward more generalizable, robust segmentation in settings where external masks are unreliable or expensive.

中文摘要翻译: 动态4D高斯溅射以高保真度重建变形场景，并越来越多地被用作动态3D场景的表示。要将这样的场景用于编辑、操作或运动分析，首先需要对其进行分割：将高斯原语分组为连贯的物体。现有流程通过从SAM等基础模型导入2D掩码，并将其提升或蒸馏到高斯表示中来获得这种分组。在动态场景中，这些掩码必须在多个帧和视角下生成，成本高昂，且最终的分割质量在很大程度上取决于这些外部掩码的质量和一致性。我们探究有多少物体级结构可以从高斯自身恢复出来，并提出了Intrinsic-GS，一种无需训练、无需掩码的方法，该方法基于外观、朝向、尺度、变形轨迹以及非学习的渲染边界线索，在高斯原语间构建一个稀疏亲和图。该图通过Leiden社区检测进行划分，无需基础模型和学习的特征场。在标准4D高斯分割基准Neu3D和HyperNeRF上，Intrinsic-GS在没有掩码监督的情况下恢复了大量的物体结构，在Neu3D上达到0.746 mIoU，在HyperNeRF上达到0.575 mIoU；在Neu3D上，纯几何变体达到了0.902 mIoU，与SAM监督的TRASE相当。在HyperNeRF上，Intrinsic-GS的运行速度比掩码监督流程中使用的掩码生成和特征渲染阶段快12.5倍。这些结果表明，大部分分割信号已经编码在高斯自身中，为3D和4D高斯分割提供了一种快速、无需掩码的方向，也可能指向在外部掩码不可靠或昂贵的情况下更可泛化、更鲁棒的分割。

</details>


### [7] [Domain Generalizable Adaptation of 3D Vision-Language Models via Regularized Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18472)
*Sneha Paul,Zachary Patterson,Nizar Bouguila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFine3D是一个面向3D大型多模态模型的正则化微调框架，通过选择性层调优、多视图一致性、文本多样性、点渲染视觉监督和测试时增强等策略，在域泛化、跨数据集迁移、鲁棒性和少样本学习等任务上显著超越了现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D多模态基础模型在适配下游域时，由于域差距和数据有限，容易出现过拟合和灾难性遗忘问题，亟需一种既能泛化到新域又能保留原有能力的微调方法。

Method: ReFine3D框架包含：(1)选择性层调优，仅微调部分网络层；(2)多视图一致性正则化，通过增强点云保证多视角特征一致性；(3)文本多样性正则化，利用大语言模型生成同义提示词；(4)点渲染视觉监督，引入渲染图像作为辅助监督；(5)测试时增强与置信度聚合机制，提升推理鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个3D域泛化基准上，ReFine3D在基类到新类泛化上提升1.36%，跨数据集迁移提升2.43%，对数据损坏的鲁棒性提升1.80%，少样本准确率最高提升3.11%，且计算开销增量极小。

Conclusion: ReFine3D通过正则化的微调策略有效解决了3D多模态模型域泛化中的过拟合与灾难性遗忘问题，在多个任务上达到了最优性能，验证了其作为域泛化微调框架的有效性和高效性。

Original Abstract: Domain adaptation remains a central challenge in 3D vision, especially for multimodal foundation models that align 3D point clouds with visual and textual data. While these models demonstrate strong general capabilities, adapting them to downstream domains with limited data often leads to overfitting and catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we introduce ReFine3D, a regularized fine-tuning framework designed for domain-generalizable tuning of 3D large multimodal models (LMMs). ReFine3D combines selective layer tuning with two targeted regularization strategies: multi-view consistency across augmented point clouds and text diversity through synonym-based prompts generated by large language models. Additionally, we incorporate point-rendered vision supervision and a test-time augmentation mechanism with confidence-based aggregation to further enhance robustness. Extensive experiments across different 3D domain generalization benchmarks show that ReFine3D improves base-to-novel class generalization by 1.36%, cross-dataset transfer by 2.43%, robustness to corruption by 1.80%, and few-shot accuracy by up to 3.11%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods with minimal added computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 域适应仍然是3D视觉中的一个核心挑战，尤其是对于将3D点云与视觉和文本数据进行对齐的多模态基础模型而言。尽管这类模型展现出强大的通用能力，但将其适配到数据有限的特定下游域时，往往会导致过拟合和灾难性遗忘。为了解决这一问题，我们提出ReFine3D，一个面向3D大型多模态模型（LMMs）域泛化微调的正则化框架。ReFine3D将选择性层调优与两种针对性的正则化策略相结合：跨增强点云的多视图一致性，以及通过大语言模型生成同义提示词实现的文本多样性。此外，我们引入了点渲染视觉监督以及基于置信度聚合的测试时增强机制，以进一步提升鲁棒性。在多个不同3D域泛化基准上的大量实验表明，ReFine3D在基类到新类泛化上提升了1.36%，跨数据集迁移提升了2.43%，对数据损坏的鲁棒性提升了1.80%，少样本准确率最高提升了3.11%，在计算开销增量极小的条件下超越了先前的先进方法。

</details>


### [8] [On-Manifold Variational Learning with Heat-Kernel Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18658)
*Jiarui Xing,Tal Zeevi,Nian Wu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种流形锚定的变分框架，通过几何感知的EM算法将原型选择为图medoid，确保原型位于数据流形上，在心脏疤痕和脑MRI基准上取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度潜变量模型通过欧几里得平均估计高斯混合先验，导致原型偏离弯曲的数据流形，且随着亚群数量增加而退化。

Method: 提出流形锚定变分框架，包含：(1) 几何感知EM算法，其M步选择热核加权潜变量图上的扩散中心性最高的图medoid作为原型；(2) Dirichlet能量正则化器保证潜空间几何平滑性；(3) 每个亚群的置信度分数实现无标签质量评估。

Result: 在心脏疤痕和脑MRI基准上，所提方法在所有比较方法中精度最高，产生迄今为止最清晰的原型，在大亚群数量下仍保持稳定而所有基线方法均退化。

Conclusion: 流形锚定EM是一种通用的几何工具，可扩展标准EM并适用于其他潜变量模型。

Original Abstract: Learning unsupervised representations of medical imaging cohorts can reveal clinically meaningful prototypes without expert labels, which are often noisy and fail to capture true pathological heterogeneity. However, existing deep latent-variable models estimate Gaussian mixture priors via Euclidean averaging, producing prototypes that drift off the curved data manifold and degenerate as the number of sub-populations grows. We propose a manifold-anchored variational framework built on a geometry-aware Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm, whose M-step selects each sub-population prototype as the graph medoid with the highest diffusion centrality on a heat-kernel-weighted latent graph, ensuring that every prototype remains on-manifold. A Dirichlet energy regularizer enforces geometric smoothness of the latent space, and a per-sub-population uncertainty score enables label-free quality assessment. \rev{The manifold-anchored EM is a general-purpose geometric tool that extends standard EM and applies readily to other latent-variable models beyond this setting.} On cardiac scar and brain MRI benchmarks, our framework attains the highest accuracy among all compared methods, produces the sharpest prototypes reported to date, and remains stable at large sub-population counts where all baselines degenerate.

中文摘要翻译: 学习医学影像队列的无监督表示可以在没有专家标签的情况下揭示临床有意义的原型，而专家标签往往带有噪声且无法捕捉真实的病理异质性。然而，现有的深度潜变量模型通过欧几里得平均来估计高斯混合先验，导致原型偏离弯曲的数据流形，并随着亚群数量的增加而退化。我们提出了一种基于几何感知期望最大化（EM）算法的流形锚定变分框架，其M步将每个亚群的原型选择为热核加权潜变量图上扩散中心性最高的图medoid，确保每个原型始终位于流形上。Dirichlet能量正则化器保证了潜空间的几何平滑性，每个亚群的置信度分数则实现了无标签的质量评估。流形锚定EM是一种通用的几何工具，它扩展了标准EM，并且可以很容易地应用于此设置之外的其他潜变量模型。在心脏疤痕和脑MRI基准上，我们的框架在所有比较方法中达到了最高精度，产生了迄今为止报告的最清晰的原型，并且在所有基线方法都退化的大亚群数量下仍保持稳定。

</details>


### [9] [Data-Forcing Distillation: Restoring Diversity and Fidelity in Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18478)
*Siyi Chen,Shaowei Liu,Yixuan Jia,Zian Wang,Huan Ling,Qing Qu,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出数据强制蒸馏（DFD），仅需一行代码修改，通过教师评分差异引导学生恢复真实数据分布，有效解决DMD中的模式崩塌和过饱和问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）方法因反向KL目标函数固有缺陷，导致视频生成多样性显著下降和过饱和伪影（偏离真实视频外观），亟需简单有效的后训练框架来恢复多样性与保真度。

Method: 提出数据强制蒸馏（DFD），核心是引入教师评分差异（teacher score discrepancy），引导学生向真实数据分布靠近：拉向缺失的模式（缓解模式崩塌），远离真实数据中不存在的有问题的模式（避免过饱和）。仅需一行代码修改，无需改变整体架构。

Result: 在文本到视频、图像到视频、自回归视频生成任务上，仅需100-300步微调，DFD在Wan2.1-1.3B和Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B模型上有效恢复了多样性和保真度，解决了过饱和伪影，视频动态和外观显著改善，甚至优于教师模型。

Conclusion: DFD是一个简单而有效的后训练框架，通过教师评分差异机制从原理上修复了分布匹配蒸馏的固有问题，实验证明其在多种视频生成任务和骨干模型上均能显著提升生成质量。

Original Abstract: Recent progress has shown promise in distilling multi-step video diffusion models into efficient few-step students. Among them, Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and its successor DMD2 achieved strong generation quality and fast convergence. However, due to the nature of the reverse Kullback--Leibler (KL) objective, these methods exhibit two persistent failure modes: a substantial drop in sample diversity, and visibly over-saturated outputs that deviate from real-video appearance. In this work, we propose Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD), a simple post-training framework that restores diversity and fidelity in DMD with only a single-line of code change. At its core is the teacher score discrepancy to guide the student toward the real-data distribution, pulling it to missing modes (mitigating mode collapse) and away from problematic modes absent in real data (avoiding over-saturation). We provide an in-depth theoretical analysis of our framework and validate our approach on text-to-video, image-to-video, and autoregressive video generation. With only 100--300 steps of finetuning, DFD effectively restores diversity and fidelity on both Wan2.1-1.3B and Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B model, resolving the over-saturation artifacts with significantly better video dynamics and appearance, and even outperforms the teacher model.

中文摘要翻译: 近期进展表明，将多步视频扩散模型蒸馏为高效的少步学生模型具有巨大潜力。其中，分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）及其后继版本DMD2取得了优异的生成质量和快速收敛。然而，由于反向KL散度目标函数的固有特性，这些方法存在两个持续性的失效模式：样本多样性大幅下降，以及生成结果出现明显的过饱和现象，偏离真实视频的外观。在本工作中，我们提出数据强制蒸馏（DFD），一个简单的后训练框架，仅需一行代码修改即可恢复DMD中的多样性和保真度。其核心是教师评分差异（teacher score discrepancy）机制，引导学生向真实数据分布靠近：将其拉向缺失的模式（缓解模式崩塌），并远离真实数据中不存在的有问题的模式（避免过饱和）。我们对框架进行了深入的理论分析，并在文本到视频、图像到视频以及自回归视频生成任务上验证了方法的有效性。仅需100-300步微调，DFD即可在Wan2.1-1.3B和Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B模型上有效恢复多样性和保真度，消除过饱和伪影，显著提升视频动态和外观质量，甚至超越了教师模型。

</details>


### [10] [Vines-DB: An RGB image dataset for multi-species ornamental vine segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18484)
*Saroj Burlakoti,Utsav Bhandari,Aaron Etienne,Shital Poudyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vines-DB是一个包含1,218幅高分辨率RGB图像的观赏藤本植物数据集，涵盖7个物种，经标注与增强后扩展至2,307幅图像，用于支撑精确园艺和城市生态中的多类实例分割深度学习模型开发与评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为精准园艺和城市生态领域提供高质量的藤本植物实例分割基准数据集，支持自动化冠层覆盖估计、物种识别和可扩展的田间表型分析等应用。

Method: 在田间条件下使用iPhone 16 Pro（48 MP）拍摄7种观赏藤本植物图像，利用黑白聚苯乙烯泡沫板作为背景提高对比度，在Roboflow平台上由训练有素的标注员进行多边形实例分割标注，经预处理和数据增强将数据集扩展到2,307幅图像，并按分层抽样划分为训练集（2,019）、验证集（192）和测试集（96）。

Result: 生成了一个包含7个物种和背景共8个类别的实例分割数据集，原始图像1,218幅，增强后2,307幅，按分层抽样划分为训练、验证和测试集，并通过重复月度成像捕获了冠层发育和植株外观的时间变化。

Conclusion: Vines-DB数据集为多类实例分割深度学习模型的开发与评估提供了有力支撑，可用于自动化冠层覆盖估计、物种识别和可扩展的田间表型分析，其时间序列成像特性增加了在真实田间条件下进行分割基准测试的实用价值。

Original Abstract: The Vines-DB dataset contains 1,218 original high-resolution RGB images of seven ornamental vine species collected under field conditions at the Utah Agricultural Experiment Station's Greenville Research Farm in Logan, Utah, USA. The dataset was generated from 168 individual vine plants that were transplanted in 2022 and photographed repeatedly across multiple months during the 2023 and 2024 growing seasons (July-October). Images were captured with an iPhone 16 Pro equipped with a 48 MP camera between 10:00 AM and 12:00 PM under daylight. Vines were grown on 1.2m x 2.4m trellises and photographed from a distance of 1m against black or white Styrofoam backdrops to improve contrast and reduce background noise. The dataset includes Akebia quinata, Campsis radicans, Hydrangea anomala petiolaris, Lonicera x heckrottii, Campsis x tagliabuana 'Madame Galen', Parthenocissus quinquefolia, and Wisteria floribunda. All original images were manually annotated in Roboflow by trained annotators to produce polygon-based instance segmentation masks for eight classes, including seven species and background. After preprocessing and data augmentation, the working dataset was expanded to 2,307 images for model development and evaluation. The augmented dataset was divided into 2,019 training images, 192 validation images, and 96 test images using stratified sampling to maintain balanced representation. Vines-DB supports the development and evaluation of deep learning models for multi-class instance segmentation in precision horticulture and urban ecology. The dataset enables applications such as automated canopy cover estimation, species identification, and scalable field phenotyping. In addition, repeated monthly imaging of the plants captures temporal variation in canopy development and plant appearance, increasing the dataset's utility for segmentation benchmarking under realistic field conditions.

中文摘要翻译: Vines-DB数据集包含在美国犹他州洛根市犹他农业实验站格林维尔研究农场田间条件下采集的1,218张七种观赏藤本植物物种的高分辨率RGB原始图像。该数据集来源于2022年移植的168株藤本植物，在2023和2024年生长季（7月至10月）的多个月份中重复拍摄。图像使用配备48 MP摄像头的iPhone 16 Pro于上午10:00至中午12:00之间在日光下拍摄。藤本植物生长在1.2m×2.4m的格架上，在1m距离处以黑色或白色聚苯乙烯泡沫板为背景拍摄，以增强对比度并降低背景噪声。数据集包括木通（Akebia quinata）、美洲凌霄（Campsis radicans）、藤绣球（Hydrangea anomala petiolaris）、海氏忍冬（Lonicera x heckrottii）、'Madame Galen'凌霄（Campsis x tagliabuana 'Madame Galen'）、五叶地锦（Parthenocissus quinquefolia）和多花紫藤（Wisteria floribunda）。所有原始图像均由训练有素的标注员在Roboflow平台上进行手动标注，生成包含七个物种和背景共八个类别的多边形实例分割掩码。经过预处理和数据增强，工作数据集扩展至2,307张图像用于模型开发与评估。增强后的数据集通过分层抽样划分为2,019张训练图像、192张验证图像和96张测试图像，以保持平衡的代表性。Vines-DB支持精准园艺和城市生态领域中多类实例分割深度学习模型的开发与评估。该数据集可应用于自动化冠层覆盖估计、物种识别和可扩展的田间表型分析。此外，月度重复成像捕捉了冠层发育和植株外观的时间变化，增强了该数据集在真实田间条件下进行分割基准测试的实用性。

</details>


### [11] [Neural Phase Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18496)
*Cole Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习广义相位相关（Learned Generalized Phase Correlation）方法，通过学习可分解变换的基，将相位相关从全局平移推广到稠密非刚体变形和酉动力学，在医学图像配准和量子物理观测中均取得了优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于学习的图像配准方法通常独立编码两幅图像，再通过相似度函数或深度解码器隐式学习变换，未将变换作为一等对象显式建模；而经典的相位相关方法虽然直接度量图像间关系，但其固定基（傅里叶基）仅能处理全局平移，无法应对非刚体变形等复杂变换。

Method: 提出一种相位相关的学习推广方法：通过学习一组可优化的基函数替换固定的傅里叶基，使得变换可以在该学习到的基上稀疏分解，从而将相位相关从全局平移推广到稠密非刚体变形和酉动力学场景。

Result: 在ACDC心脏MRI基准上，该方法在两个配准方向上匹配或超过已有基线；在CAMUS超声心动图数据集上，无需辅助评分或自适应平滑机制即达到最先进水平；在1D量子谐振子的时间演化波函数对上，该方法仅从观测对中恢复出了厄米函数本征态和未知哈密顿量的量子化能级。

Conclusion: 通过学习可分解变换的基函数，实现了相位相关的灵活推广，在医学图像配准和量子物理观测分析中均展现了强大的泛化能力和物理可解释性。

Original Abstract: Correspondence is fundamentally relational: it seeks the unknown transformation between two observations of a common scene, not the content of either. Yet the dominant learning-based methods do not represent the transformation as a first-class object in the architecture. They encode each image independently and let a learned similarity function or a deep decoder discover the mapping implicitly. Phase correlation is the canonical exception, measuring the inter-image relationship directly in the Fourier domain, but the rigidity of its fixed basis confines it to global translation.
  We introduce a learned generalization of phase correlation that lifts this restriction by learning the basis on which the transformation decomposes. The same algebraic primitive extends to dense non-rigid deformations and to unitary dynamics. On the ACDC cardiac-MRI benchmark the framework matches or exceeds prior published baselines on both registration directions. On CAMUS echocardiography it matches state-of-the-art without auxiliary scoring or adaptive-smoothness mechanisms. Applied to time-evolved wavefunction pairs of the 1-D quantum harmonic oscillator, the same framework recovers the Hermite-function eigenstates and the quantized energy levels of the unknown Hamiltonian from observation pairs alone.

中文摘要翻译: 对应关系本质上是关系性的：它寻求同一场景两次观测之间的未知变换，而非任一观测的内容本身。然而，主流的基于学习方法并未将变换作为架构中的一等对象。它们独立编码每幅图像，让学习到的相似度函数或深度解码器隐式发现映射。相位相关是典型的例外，它直接在傅里叶域度量图像间的关系，但其固定基的刚性使其局限于全局平移。我们引入了一种相位相关的学习推广方法，通过学习变换可分解的基来消除这一限制。相同的代数原语可扩展到稠密非刚体变形和酉动力学。在ACDC心脏MRI基准上，该框架在两个配准方向上均匹配或超过已发表的基线。在CAMUS超声心动图上，它无需辅助评分或自适应平滑机制即达到最先进水平。应用于1D量子谐振子的时间演化波函数对时，该框架仅从观测对中恢复出了厄米函数本征态和未知哈密顿量的量子化能级。

</details>


### [12] [Architectural Bias in Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Comparative Study of Vision Transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18510)
*Ngela Landon Ntung,Floride Tuyisenge,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文实证比较了Vision Transformer与CNN在面部呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，发现预训练的DeiT-S不仅准确率最高（97.27%），而且在不同族群间性能差距更小（ACER差距降至0.13%），在零样本中亚人群上泛化能力显著优于ResNet18（BPCER 2.89% vs 10.44%），表明架构选择对跨人口统计群体的公平性有重要影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有面部PAD系统在不同人口统计群体间存在系统性性能差异，深肤色人群更易受影响，但缺乏对Vision Transformer架构能否减少这种人口统计偏差的系统实证研究。

Method: 在CASIA-SURF跨种族面部反欺骗（CeFA）数据集上，比较三种架构：（1）从头训练的多模态ViT-Tiny，（2）ResNet18 CNN基线，（3）在CeFA上微调的预训练DeiT-S。评估覆盖非洲、东亚及零样本中亚人口群体。

Result: DeiT-S取得最高准确率97.27%和最低EER 0.86%，优于ResNet18的90.15%。DeiT-S将非洲与东亚受试者间的ACER差距降至0.13%，相比LBP方法的0.75%降低了83%。在零样本中亚人群上，DeiT-S的BPCER为2.89%，而ResNet18高达10.44%，泛化优势达3.6倍。

Conclusion: 预训练Vision Transformer在PAD任务中不仅准确率更高，而且在不同人口统计群体间性能差距更小，对未见过的群体具有更公平的泛化能力，表明跨人口统计公平性在一定程度上受架构设计影响。

Original Abstract: Face Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) systems constitute a critical security layer in biometric authentication; however, existing approaches exhibit systematic performance disparities across demographic groups, disproportionately affecting individuals with darker skin tones. This paper presents a comparative empirical investigation of whether Vision Transformer architectures reduce demographic bias in face PAD systems relative to convolutional baselines. Experiments are conducted on the CASIA-SURF Cross-Ethnicity Face Anti-Spoofing (CeFA) dataset. Three architectures are evaluated: a Multimodal ViT-Tiny trained from scratch, a ResNet18 CNN baseline, and a pretrained DeiT-S fine-tuned on CeFA across African, East Asian, and zero-shot Central Asian demographic groups. DeiT-S achieves the highest overall accuracy of 97.27% and the lowest EER of 0.86%, outperforming ResNet18 at 90.15% accuracy. In terms of fairness, DeiT-S reduces the inter-ethnic ACER gap between African and East Asian subjects to 0.13%, compared to 0.75% reported in an LBP-based work [6], representing an 83% reduction. Most notably, while ResNet18 records a BPCER of 10.44% on zero-shot Central Asian subjects, DeiT-S maintains 2.89% on the same unseen group, demonstrating a 3.6x generalization advantage. These results suggest that pretrained Vision Transformers achieve superior PAD accuracy, produce smaller demographic performance gaps, and generalize more equitably across unseen demographic groups, indicating that cross-demographic fairness in PAD may partly be influenced by architectural design.

中文摘要翻译: 面部呈现攻击检测（PAD）系统构成了生物特征认证中的关键安全层；然而，现有方法在不同人口统计群体间表现出系统性的性能差异，对深肤色个体造成不成比例的影响。本文提出了一项比较性实证研究，探究Vision Transformer架构相对于卷积基线是否能减少面部PAD系统中的人口统计偏差。实验在CASIA-SURF跨种族面部反欺骗（CeFA）数据集上进行。评估了三种架构：从头训练的多模态ViT-Tiny、ResNet18 CNN基线，以及在CeFA上微调的预训练DeiT-S，覆盖非洲、东亚和零样本中亚人口群体。DeiT-S取得了最高总体准确率97.27%和最低等错误率（EER）0.86%，优于准确率为90.15%的ResNet18。在公平性方面，DeiT-S将非洲与东亚受试者之间的跨种族ACER差距降至0.13%，而基于LBP的工作[6]报告为0.75%，降低了83%。最值得注意的是，ResNet18在零样本中亚受试者上的BPCER为10.44%，而DeiT-S在相同未见群体上保持2.89%，展现出3.6倍的泛化优势。这些结果表明，预训练Vision Transformer能够实现更优的PAD准确率，产生更小的人口统计性能差距，并在未见过的不同人口统计群体上更公平地泛化，表明PAD中的跨人口统计公平性可能在一定程度上受架构设计影响。

</details>


### [13] [A Prototypical Signature Approach for Writer-Independent Offline Signature Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18528)
*Kecia G. de Moura,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于原型签名（prototypical signatures）的数据驱动策略，用于生成多样且信息丰富的负样本，以提升离线手写签名验证中伪造签名的检测性能，同时提高计算效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 离线手写签名验证中，真实伪造样本稀少，通常从其他用户的真实签名中随机抽取负样本。但这种随机选择缺乏多样性、冗余度高、计算成本大，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的负样本生成策略：利用原型签名（prototypical signatures）——即真实签名特征的紧凑、不可识别的摘要——来生成多样且信息丰富的负样本。

Result: (i) 原型签名能生成更具信息量的负样本，提升熟练伪造签名的检测能力；(ii) 该方法与骨干网络无关，跨架构鲁棒；(iii) 结合原始形式线性SVM可作为RBF模型的替代方案，显著提升可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 基于原型签名的数据驱动负样本生成策略能有效提升离线手写签名验证的性能和效率，且具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Offline handwritten signature verification aims to distinguish genuine from forged signatures using static images. Since real forgeries are rarely available, negative samples are usually randomly drawn from genuine signatures of other users to create training data. However, this random selection often lacks diversity, increases redundancy, and escalates computational cost, leading to inefficient training. We propose a data-driven strategy to generate diverse, informative negative samples using prototypical signatures, which are compact, non-identifiable summaries of genuine signature features. Based on the experiments results, we conclude that (i) prototypical signatures yield more informative negative samples, improving the detection of skilled forgeries; (ii) the proposed approach is backbone-agnostic, showing robustness across architectures; and (iii) when combined with a primal-form linear SVM, it serves as an alternative to RBF-based models while significantly improving scalability and computational efficiency. Implementation of the method is available at https://github.com/kdmoura/proto_hsv.

中文摘要翻译: 离线手写签名验证旨在通过静态图像区分真实签名与伪造签名。由于真实伪造样本难以获取，通常从其他用户的真实签名中随机抽取负样本以构建训练数据。然而，这种随机选择往往缺乏多样性、增加冗余并提升计算成本，导致训练效率低下。我们提出一种数据驱动策略，利用原型签名（prototypical signatures）——即真实签名特征的紧凑、不可识别摘要——生成多样化且信息丰富的负样本。实验结果表明：(i) 原型签名能生成更具信息量的负样本，提升熟练伪造签名的检测能力；(ii) 所提方法与骨干网络无关，跨架构具有鲁棒性；(iii) 当与原始形式线性SVM结合时，可作为RBF模型的替代方案，同时显著提升可扩展性和计算效率。该方法已在https://github.com/kdmoura/proto_hsv开源实现。

</details>


### [14] [Hierarchical Multi-Modal Retrieval for Knowledge-Grounded News Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18553)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Hoang-Bach Ngo,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种检索增强的图像描述框架，通过层级化多模态文章检索和上下文相关性精炼，利用外部知识生成包含对象属性、事件背景和深层意义的丰富描述。在ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025挑战赛中取得第5名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图像描述方法难以生成全面、上下文丰富的描述，尤其对无法直接从视觉线索中观察到的细节（如对象属性、事件背景和深层意义）表现不佳，因此需要利用外部知识来增强描述能力。

Method: 方法包含三阶段：(1) 层级化多模态文章检索——考虑文章结构感知特征（加权文本组件如标题、正文和视觉布局模式）以及多层面相似度计算（内容-视觉、视觉-视觉、话语定位），然后进行上下文相关性精炼；(2) VLM生成简洁图像描述；(3) 基于描述从检索文章中切分相关信息，LLM结合描述与提取知识生成全面描述。

Result: 在ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025挑战赛中，在OpenEvent-V1数据集的私有测试集上以0.2824的总分获得第5名。

Conclusion: 所提出的检索增强框架能够有效利用外部知识生成包含更深层见解的图像描述，实验验证了方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Traditional image captioning methods often struggle to generate comprehensive, context-rich descriptions, especially for details not directly observable from visual cues. To overcome this, we propose a novel retrieval-augmented image captioning framework that generates captions with deeper insights, such as object attributes, event context, and underlying significance, by leveraging external knowledge. Our approach features a hierarchical multi-modal article retrieval mechanism that moves beyond monolithic text entities. This retrieval considers article structure-aware features, including weighted textual components (e.g., headlines, body sections) and visual placement patterns, alongside multi-faceted similarity computations (content--visual, visual--visual, and discourse positioning). A subsequent contextual relevance refinement stage further enhances the retrieved information. The retrieved articles then serve as the knowledge base for caption generation: first, a VLM generates a concise image description; second, we segment relevant information from the retrieved articles based on this description; and finally, an LLM utilizes both the description and extracted knowledge to generate a comprehensive, contextually detailed caption. We participated in the ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025 Challenge and achieved 5th place with an overall score of 0.2824 on the private test set of the OpenEvent-V1 dataset. Source code is publicly released at https://github.com/mf0212/EVENTA-Challange.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的图像描述方法通常难以生成全面、上下文丰富的描述，尤其是对于无法直接从视觉线索中观察到的细节。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种新颖的检索增强图像描述框架，通过利用外部知识生成具有更深层见解的描述，例如对象属性、事件背景和潜在意义。我们的方法采用了一种层级化多模态文章检索机制，超越了单一的文本实体检索。该检索考虑了文章结构感知特征，包括加权文本组件（如标题、正文部分）和视觉布局模式，以及多层面相似度计算（内容-视觉、视觉-视觉和话语定位）。随后的上下文相关性精炼阶段进一步增强了检索信息。检索到的文章随后作为描述生成的知识库：首先，VLM生成简洁的图像描述；其次，基于该描述从检索文章中切分相关信息；最后，LLM利用描述和提取的知识生成全面、上下文详细的描述。我们参加了ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025挑战赛，并在OpenEvent-V1数据集的私有测试集上以0.2824的总分获得第5名。源代码已公开发布在https://github.com/mf0212/EVENTA-Challange。

</details>


### [15] [Forged Calamity: Benchmark for Cross-Domain Synthetic Disaster Detection in the Age of Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18554)
*Duc-Manh Phan,Quoc-Duy Tran,Duy-Khang Do,Anh-Tuan Vo,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trong Le Do,Mai-Khiem Tran,Vinh-Tiep Nguyen,Tam V. Nguyen,Isao Echizen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Forged Calamity基准数据集（30,000张图像，含真实与四种扩散模型生成的灾害场景图像），实验表明当前取证方法在域外和未见生成器上准确率下降高达50%，存在严重的泛化差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型能生成高度逼真的合成图像，可能被用于制造虚假灾害图像（如洪水、火灾、地震），从而传播 misinformation 或干扰应急响应。现有取证方法在应对扩散模型生成内容时存在泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 构建了Forged Calamity基准数据集（6,000张真实+24,000张来自四种扩散模型的合成灾害图像），并在微调与零样本两种设置下对现有取证方法进行全面评估。

Result: 微调检测器在分布内表现良好，但在未见生成器或灾害类型上准确率下降高达50%；零样本通用检测器同样难以保持稳定准确率，仅少数表示鲁棒模型显示出有限韧性。

Conclusion: 当前取证方法存在持续性的泛化差距，迫切需要开发领域无关且模型无关的检测方法来确保扩散时代视觉内容的真实性。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of text-to-image diffusion models has enabled the creation of highly photorealistic synthetic images that closely resemble real photographs, making it increasingly difficult to distinguish authentic content from AI-generated fabrications. This poses challenges for cybersecurity, digital forensics, and disaster response, where fake imagery of floods, fires, or earthquakes can spread misinformation or disrupt emergency operations. To address this, we introduce Forged Calamity, a benchmark dataset for synthetic disaster detection containing 30,000 images, including 6,000 real and 24,000 synthetic samples generated by four diffusion models. Comprehensive experiments across fine-tuned and zero-shot settings reveal consistent weaknesses in current forensic approaches. Fine-tuned detectors perform well in-distribution but lose up to 50\% accuracy on unseen generators or disaster types, showing overfitting to model-specific artifacts. Zero-shot generalized detectors also struggle to maintain stable accuracy, with only limited resilience in a few representation-robust models. These findings highlight persistent generalization gaps and the urgent need for domain- and model-agnostic detection methods to ensure visual authenticity in the diffusion era.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像扩散模型的快速发展使得生成高度逼真的合成图像成为可能，这些图像与真实照片极为相似，导致区分真实内容与AI生成伪造品愈发困难。这对网络安全、数字取证和灾害响应构成了挑战——虚假的洪水、火灾或地震图像可能传播错误信息或干扰应急行动。为此，我们提出了Forged Calamity，一个用于合成灾害检测的基准数据集，包含30,000张图像，其中6,000张为真实图像，24,000张由四种扩散模型生成的合成样本。在微调和零样本两种设置下的全面实验揭示了当前取证方法中普遍存在的弱点。微调检测器在分布内表现良好，但在未见过的生成器或灾害类型上准确率下降高达50%，显示出对模型特定伪影的过拟合。零样本通用检测器同样难以保持稳定的准确率，仅少数表示鲁棒模型展现出有限的韧性。这些发现突显了持续存在的泛化差距，以及迫切需要领域无关和模型无关的检测方法，以确保扩散时代视觉内容的真实性。

</details>


### [16] [Rethinking Text-to-Image as Semantic-Aware Data Augmentation for Indoor Scene Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18555)
*Trong-Vu Hoang,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Hoai-Danh Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Stable Diffusion生成合成室内图像以增强训练数据，并利用DIRE方法检测合成图像，防止其被滥用。实验表明该方法在MIT Indoor Scene数据集上有效，且能100%准确识别SD生成图像。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 室内图像识别面临光照、遮挡和物体布局复杂等挑战，且真实训练数据不足，亟需有效的数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 利用Stable Diffusion生成多样且逼真的合成室内场景图像进行数据增强；2) 基于扩散重建误差（DIRE）设计反制措施，使用轻量级深度模型（如MobileNetV3）检测SD生成的合成图像。

Result: 在MIT Indoor Scene数据集上，该方法在真实数据有限时有效提升了深度模型的训练效果；DIRE方法使用MobileNetV3能以100%的准确率识别SD生成的图像。

Conclusion: Stable Diffusion可用于室内图像数据增强以提升模型性能，同时DIRE能高效准确地检测合成图像，防止其被滥用。

Original Abstract: In the realm of computer vision, indoor image recognition presents challenges due to the intricate interplay of lighting conditions, occlusions, and diverse object arrangements within confined spaces. To address the lacks of training indoor images, we introduce a novel approach leveraging Stable Diffusion (SD) for the generation of synthetic images, which serve as a powerful data augmentation tool. The utilization of SD offers a principled framework for synthesizing diverse and realistic indoor scenes, thereby enriching the training data pool for robust indoor image recognition models. Experimental findings on the MIT Indoor Scene dataset reveal the potential of our proposed approach in enhancing the training of deep models when authentic data is limited. Furthermore, to prevent the misuse of SD synthetic images, we introduce a counter measure based on DIffusion Reconstruction Error (DIRE). The powerful DIRE presentation enables training robust classifiers only using lightweight deep models. Experiments show that our approach can perfectly recognize SD generated images with the accuracy of 100% using MobilenetV3.

中文摘要翻译: 在计算机视觉领域，由于光照条件、遮挡和有限空间内物体布局的复杂交织，室内图像识别面临着诸多挑战。为了解决训练室内图像不足的问题，我们提出了一种利用Stable Diffusion（SD）生成合成图像的新方法，作为一种强大的数据增强工具。SD的运用为合成多样化且逼真的室内场景提供了一个原则性框架，从而为鲁棒的室内图像识别模型丰富了训练数据池。在MIT Indoor Scene数据集上的实验结果表明，当真实数据有限时，我们提出的方法在增强深度模型训练方面具有潜力。此外，为防止SD合成图像的滥用，我们引入了一种基于扩散重建误差（DIRE）的反制措施。强大的DIRE表征使得仅使用轻量级深度模型即可训练鲁棒的分类器。实验表明，我们的方法能够完美识别SD生成的图像，使用MobileNetV3时准确率达到100%。

</details>


### [17] [MolmoMotion: Forecasting Point Trajectories in 3D with Language Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18558)
*Jianing Zhang,Chenhao Zheng,Yajun Yang,Max Argus,Rustin Soraki,Winson Han,Taira Anderson,Chun-Liang Li,Shuo Liu,Jiafei Duan,Zhongzheng Ren,Jieyu Zhang,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以3D世界坐标点作为通用运动表示，构建了大规模数据集MolmoMotion-1M、基准PointMotionBench及预测模型MolmoMotion，用于目标条件3D点运动预测，并在机器人操作和视频生成等下游任务中验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运动预测方法多依赖2D表示或类别特定的先验，缺乏类无关、视角稳定且可直接用于下游任务的通用运动表示。本文旨在探索3D世界坐标点作为通用运动表示的能力，并构建完整的数据、基准和模型体系。

Method: (1) 构建MolmoMotion-1M大规模数据集，从116万无约束视频中标注了动作描述和物体关联的3D点轨迹；(2) 设计PointMotionBench人工验证基准，涵盖111个物体类别和61种运动类型；(3) 提出MolmoMotion模型，支持自回归坐标预测和基于流匹配的轨迹生成两种范式。

Result: MolmoMotion在PointMotionBench上显著优于现有运动预测基线；学到的3D运动先验可迁移至机器人操作任务，提升训练效率和泛化能力；预测轨迹可为视频生成模型提供有效的运动引导，生成更真实的物体运动。

Conclusion: 3D世界坐标点是一种类无关、视角稳定且紧凑的通用运动表示，结合大规模数据和合适的模型架构，可有效预测多样化运动模式，并广泛适用于下游视觉智能任务。

Original Abstract: Motion forecasting is central to visual intelligence: agents must anticipate how objects will move in order to plan actions, reason about physical interactions, and synthesize realistic futures. We argue that 3D points in world coordinates provide a general representation that is class-agnostic, view-stable, compact, and directly useful for downstream tasks. We formalize the task of goal-conditioned 3D point motion forecasting: given a short visual history, a set of 3D query points on an object of interest, and a language description of the intended goal, the model predicts the future 3D trajectory of each point. We introduce a full stack to study this task at scale: (1) MolmoMotion-1M is a large corpus of action-described, object-grounded 3D point trajectories annotated from 1.16M unconstrained videos; (2) PointMotionBench is a human-verified benchmark spanning 111 object categories and 61 motion types; and (3) MolmoMotion is a general motion forecasting model that supports both autoregressive coordinate prediction and flow-matching-based trajectory generation. MolmoMotion accurately predicts diverse motion patterns with different language instructions, and significantly outperforms existing motion prediction baselines on PointMotionBench. Finally, we show that the learned 3D motion prior transfers well to downstream applications: it improves training efficiency and generalization for robot manipulation, and its predicted trajectories provide effective motion guidance for generative models to synthesize videos with more realistic object motion.

中文摘要翻译: 运动预测是视觉智能的核心：智能体必须预测物体将如何运动，才能规划行动、推理物理交互并合成逼真的未来场景。我们认为，世界坐标系下的3D点提供了一种类无关、视角稳定、紧凑且可直接用于下游任务的通用表示。我们形式化了目标条件3D点运动预测任务：给定一段短时视觉历史、目标物体上的一组3D查询点以及一个描述预期目标的语言指令，模型预测每个点的未来3D轨迹。我们构建了一套完整的体系来规模化研究该任务：(1) MolmoMotion-1M是一个大规模语料库，从116万段无约束视频中标注了动作描述和物体关联的3D点轨迹；(2) PointMotionBench是一个人工验证的基准，涵盖111个物体类别和61种运动类型；(3) MolmoMotion是一个通用运动预测模型，同时支持自回归坐标预测和基于流匹配的轨迹生成。MolmoMotion能够准确预测不同语言指令下的多样化运动模式，并在PointMotionBench上显著优于现有运动预测基线。最后，我们展示了学习到的3D运动先验能够很好地迁移到下游应用中：它提升了机器人操作的训练效率和泛化能力，其预测轨迹可为生成模型提供有效的运动引导，从而合成物体运动更逼真的视频。

</details>


### [18] [Experimental Analysis of Neural Network-Based Image Classification on the CIFAR-10 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18565)
*Necati Kagan Erkek,Emre Balci,Berkin Halay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过全连接网络和卷积网络在CIFAR-10基准上进行神经图像分类实验，分析了完整学习流程（图像向量化、归一化、独热编码、损失最小化、学习率选择、小批量训练、卷积特征提取、最大池化和泛化评估）。一个六层卷积加三层池化的架构在10个训练周期、批大小128、Adam优化器（学习率0.001）下达到约74.77%的验证准确率，但验证损失在训练中期开始上升，展示了表示学习与记忆化的实际差异，为后续正则化、数据增强、更深架构和可复现分类教育提供了基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通过全面的实验分析，展示神经图像分类的完整学习流程，并揭示表示学习与记忆化（过拟合）之间的实际差异，为未来研究提供紧凑的实验基线。

Method: 采用全连接网络和卷积网络两种形式，在CIFAR-10基准上实施完整的监督学习流程，包括图像向量化、归一化、独热编码、损失最小化、学习率选择、小批量训练、卷积特征提取、最大池化及基于验证集的泛化评估。具体采用六层卷积层与三层最大池化层的卷积架构，使用Adam优化器（学习率0.001）训练10个周期，批大小为128。

Result: 验证准确率约达74.77%；验证损失在训练中期开始上升，而训练损失持续下降，表明模型出现了过拟合（从表示学习转向记忆化）。

Conclusion: 该实验清晰地展示了表示学习与记忆化之间的实际差异，为未来在正则化、数据增强、更深层架构以及可复现图像分类教育方面的研究提供了紧凑的实验基线。

Original Abstract: An experimental investigation of neural image classification on the CIFAR-10 benchmark is presented through fully connected and convolutional network formulations. The analysis emphasizes the complete learning pipeline: image vectorization, normalization, one-hot class encoding, supervised loss minimization, learning-rate selection, mini-batch training, convolutional feature extraction, max-pooling, and validation-based generalization assessment. A convolutional architecture with six convolutional layers and three max-pooling stages is evaluated for ten training epochs using a batch size of 128 and an Adam optimizer with a learning rate of 0.001. The validation accuracy reaches approximately 74.77%, while the validation loss begins to increase after the middle of training despite continued reduction in training loss. The resulting behavior illustrates the practical difference between representation learning and memorization, and it provides a compact experimental baseline for future studies on regularization, data augmentation, deeper architectures, and reproducible image-classification education.

中文摘要翻译: 本文通过全连接网络和卷积网络两种形式，在CIFAR-10基准上对神经图像分类进行了实验研究。分析强调了完整的学习流程：图像向量化、归一化、独热编码、监督损失最小化、学习率选择、小批量训练、卷积特征提取、最大池化以及基于验证集的泛化评估。本文评估了一个包含六个卷积层和三个最大池化阶段的卷积架构，采用128的批大小和0.001学习率的Adam优化器，训练了十个周期。验证准确率达到约74.77%，而验证损失在训练中期开始上升，尽管训练损失持续下降。该行为展示了表示学习与记忆化之间的实际差异，并为未来关于正则化、数据增强、更深层架构以及可复现图像分类教育的研究提供了一个紧凑的实验基线。

</details>


### [19] [Multi-Modal Hyper-Graph Fusion for Low-Light Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18566)
*Hao-Yuan Ma,Li Zhang,Yushi Qiu,Jie Gao,Yan Zhang,Bangjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光照环境下人群计数的挑战，本文提出了多模态超图融合模块（MHGF）和可变形矩形稀疏注意力（DRSA），构建了低光照计数网络LCNet，并在构建的合成与真实基准上取得了最佳性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要针对光照良好的场景或依赖单模态RGB表示，在极端黑暗和非均匀光照下变得不可靠，而低光照人群计数在实际应用中具有重要意义，但尚未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 构建三个低光照人群计数基准（SHA_Dark、SHB_Dark、LC-Crowd）；(2) 引入深度和Canny边缘线索作为几何与结构先验，增强Retinex物理建模下的内在反射表示；(3) 提出多模态超图融合模块（MHGF），将RGB、深度几何、边缘结构作为超图节点，通过动态超边构建和消息传递捕获高阶互补关系；(4) 提出可变形矩形稀疏注意力（DRSA），通过锚点感知和自适应矩形窗口建模在信息密集区域集中计算。

Result: 在三个基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的LCNet方法在整体性能上优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了结合多模态先验（深度、边缘）与超图融合、稀疏注意力机制能有效提升低光照人群计数的鲁棒性，所提LCNet在合成和真实低光照场景下均达到SOTA性能。

Original Abstract: Crowd counting is a fundamental task in computer vision. However, crowd counting in low-light environments remains largely underexplored, despite its practical importance in the real world. Existing methods mainly focus on well-lit scenes or rely on single-modality Red-Green-Blue (RGB) representations, which often become unreliable under extreme darkness and complex non-uniform illumination. To handle this problem, we construct three new low-light crowd counting benchmarks, which consist of two synthetic datasets, SHA\_Dark and SHB\_Dark, and a real-world benchmark LC-Crowd (Low-light Crowd Dataset). Inspired by Retinex-based physical modeling, we introduce depth and Canny edge cues as complementary geometric and structural priors to enhance the intrinsic reflectance representation under low-light conditions. We propose a Multi-Modal Hyper-Graph Fusion module, which formulates RGB appearance, depth geometry, and edge structure cues as nodes in a unified hyper-graph and explicitly captures their high-order complementary relationships via dynamic hyperedge construction and message passing. Furthermore, to adaptively allocate computation in dense prediction, we propose a Deformable Rectangular Sparse Attention (DRSA) module, which concentrates computation on informative regions through anchor-aware estimation and adaptive rectangular window modeling. Based on these designs, we develop a unified Low-Light Counting Network (LCNet) for robust low-light crowd counting. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves the best overall performance against existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. The code is in the supplementary material. The datasets will be made public upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 人群计数是计算机视觉中的一项基本任务。然而，尽管低光照环境下的人群计数在现实世界中具有重要的实际意义，该问题仍然在很大程度上未被充分探索。现有方法主要聚焦于光照良好的场景或依赖单模态RGB表示，这在极端黑暗和复杂的非均匀光照条件下往往变得不可靠。为解决这一问题，我们构建了三个新的低光照人群计数基准，包括两个合成数据集SHA_Dark和SHB_Dark，以及一个真实世界基准LC-Crowd。受Retinex物理建模启发，我们引入深度和Canny边缘线索作为互补的几何与结构先验，以增强低光照条件下的内在反射表示。我们提出了一种多模态超图融合模块，将RGB外观、深度几何和边缘结构线索作为统一超图中的节点，并通过动态超边构建与消息传递显式捕获它们之间的高阶互补关系。此外，为了在密集预测中自适应分配计算资源，我们提出了一种可变形矩形稀疏注意力模块，该模块通过锚点感知估计和自适应矩形窗口建模，将计算集中在信息丰富的区域。基于这些设计，我们开发了一个统一的低光照计数网络LCNet，用于鲁棒的低光照人群计数。在三个基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在整体性能上优于现有的最先进方法。代码见补充材料。数据集将在接收后公开。

</details>


### [20] [Aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition with patch-level self-supervised learning and expanded reciprocal re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18583)
*Yandi Yang,Xianghong Zou,Jianping Li,Haofeng Xie,Saurav Uprety,Hongzhou Yang,Naser El-Sheimy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于空中-地面LiDAR地点识别的检索与重排序框架，通过多尺度补丁级自监督学习和场景级学习桥接域差距，并利用扩展互惠（ER）重排序算法进一步精化检索结果，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地面LiDAR地点识别存在预访问要求、覆盖不全和视角受限等局限，而利用预先采集的全覆盖机载激光扫描（ALS）数据作为先验地图可以克服这些问题，但空中与地面点云之间的域差距以及初始检索中的误报是主要挑战。

Method: 提出检索与重排序两阶段框架：检索阶段引入多尺度补丁级自监督学习模块，结合场景级学习，增强空中与地面点云全局特征的判别性；重排序阶段提出扩展互惠（ER）算法，利用ALS点云的结构化空间分布，最大化利用邻域信息并基于邻域特征精化各特征，更新相似度矩阵进行最终排序。

Result: 在CS-Urban-Scenes上，平均Recall@1提升9.8%，平均Recall@1%提升3.2%；在CS-Campus3D上亦达到最佳。ER重排序在CS-Campus3D和CS-Urban-Scenes上分别进一步提升平均Recall@1达4.9%和10.2%，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了空中-地面LiDAR地点识别中的域差距和误报问题，显著优于现有方法，且重排序算法无需额外训练即可进一步提升性能。

Original Abstract: LiDAR place recognition determines one's position on a prior point cloud map. The most studied ground-level LiDAR place recognition suffers from pre-visit requirements, incomplete coverage, and limited perspectives. Using pre-acquired, full-coverage Airborne Laser Scanning (ALS) data as an aerial prior map overcomes these drawbacks, making cross-view place recognition necessary and advantageous. However, aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition faces significant challenges, including the domain gap between aerial and ground point clouds, and false positives during initial retrieval. To address these challenges, we present a novel retrieval and re-ranking framework for aerial-ground LiDAR place recognition. Based on the priors that neighboring point cloud patches share similar semantics with anchor patch, our retrieval network introduces patch-level self-supervised learning modules at multiple scales and integrates with scene-level learning to improve global feature discriminativeness between aerial and ground point clouds. Furthermore, leveraging the structured spatial distribution of ALS point clouds, we introduce an Expanded Reciprocal (ER) re-ranking algorithm to exploit neighborhood information maximally and refine each feature based on neighbor features, which are then used to update the similarity matrix for final ranking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our retrieval network outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, achieving a 9.8\% improvement in average Recall@1 and a 3.2\% improvement in average Recall@1\% on the CS-Urban-Scenes, while also showing the best performance on the CS-Campus3D dataset. Additionally, our ER re-ranking algorithm further boosts the average Recall@1 by 4.9\% on CS-Campus3D and 10.2\% on CS-Urban-Scenes without additional training.

中文摘要翻译: LiDAR地点识别在预先构建的点云地图上确定自身位置。现有研究最广泛的地面级LiDAR地点识别存在预访问要求、覆盖不全和视角受限等缺陷。利用预先采集的全覆盖机载激光扫描（ALS）数据作为航空先验地图可克服这些不足，使得跨视角地点识别变得必要且有利。然而，空中-地面LiDAR地点识别面临显著挑战，包括空中与地面点云之间的域差距，以及初始检索中的误报问题。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的空中-地面LiDAR地点识别检索与重排序框架。基于邻近点云补丁与锚点补丁共享相似语义的先验，我们的检索网络在多尺度上引入补丁级自监督学习模块，并与场景级学习相结合，以提升空中与地面点云全局特征的判别性。此外，利用ALS点云的结构化空间分布，我们提出了一种扩展互惠（ER）重排序算法，以最大化利用邻域信息，并基于邻域特征精化各特征，进而用于更新相似度矩阵以进行最终排序。大量实验表明，我们的检索网络在CS-Urban-Scenes上优于现有最先进方法，平均Recall@1提升9.8%，平均Recall@1%提升3.2%，同时在CS-Campus3D数据集上也展现出最佳性能。此外，我们的ER重排序算法无需额外训练，即可在CS-Campus3D和CS-Urban-Scenes上分别将平均Recall@1进一步提升4.9%和10.2%。

</details>


### [21] [APT: Atomic Physical Transitions for Causal Video-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18586)
*Shang Wu,Haoran Lu,Songling Liu,Chenwei Xu,Lie Lu,Pranav Maneriker,Fan Du,Manling Li,Zhaoran Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出原子物理转移（APTs）作为细粒度的物理因果状态变化表示，替代传统的事件级标签，并设计APT-Tune微调方法，使视觉语言模型能够学习物理转移而不遗忘事件级理解能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频理解仅使用事件级标签（如"弹跳"），忽略了构成事件物理有效性的因果状态变化过程（如支撑丢失、接触开始、回弹、沉降）。作者希望使这些隐藏的物理过程变得显式可学习。

Method: 提出原子物理转移（APT）概念，构建包含14种转移类型、27,303个标注实例的混合来源数据集（人工标注+模拟器真值）；提出APT-Tune参数高效微调方法，包含图像填充感知监督、格式条件联合训练和机制条件域到类型解码三个组件。

Result: 现有VLM零样本APT召回率最高仅为14%，错误以漏检为主；直接微调APT链会提升转移检测但导致事件级遗忘；APT-Tune仅用11M LoRA参数（Qwen3-VL-2B）即可显著提升APT召回率同时保持甚至改善事件级视频理解迁移能力。

Conclusion: APT不是一种新的答案格式，而是一种与人类对齐的因果监督信号，能够用于物理视频理解的训练。

Original Abstract: Physical events are not understood by their names alone, but by the causal state changes that compose them. A clip-level label such as "bounce" can be correct while hiding the process that makes the event physically valid, from support loss and contact onset to rebound and settling. To make this hidden process explicit, we introduce Atomic Physical Transitions (APTs): minimal, temporally localized state changes that bind a visible cue to an active physical mechanism and before/after dynamical regimes. An APT chain represents a video as an ordered causal transition sequence rather than a single aggregate event label: event labels tell what happened; APT chains explain why it happened. To make APTs learnable by VLMs, we construct mixed-source APT data from human annotations and simulator ground truth, covering 14 transition types across contact, gravity, friction, and rotation/stability, with 27,303 timed instances over 1,246 trials. Using this data, we find that current VLMs miss transition-level physics, with zero-shot recall at most 14% and errors dominated by missed transitions. Direct fine-tuning on APT chains improves transition detection but causes event-level forgetting, indicating that the model learns a specialized answer format rather than a reusable physical representation. We therefore propose APT-Tune, a parameter-efficient recipe that teaches VLMs to use causal transitions without forgetting how to answer video questions. It combines image-pad-aware supervision, format-conditional co-training, and mechanism-conditioned domain-to-type decoding to make APT learning format-robust and physically grounded. With only 11 M LoRA parameters on Qwen3-VL-2B, APT-Tune substantially improves APT recall while also improving event-level video transfer. These results show that APTs are not a new answer format, but a human-aligned causal supervision signal for physical video understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 物理事件并非仅通过其名称就能被理解，而是通过构成事件的因果状态变化来理解的。像"弹跳"这样的片段级标签可能是正确的，却隐藏了使事件在物理上有效的过程——从支撑丢失和接触开始到回弹和沉降。为了将这些隐藏过程显式化，我们引入了原子物理转移：最小的、时间上局部的状态变化，将可见线索与活跃的物理机制以及前后动力学状态联系起来。APT链将视频表示为有序的因果转移序列，而非单一的事件聚合标签：事件标签说明了发生了什么；APT链解释了为什么发生。为了使VLMs能够学习APTs，我们构建了混合来源的APT数据，来自人工标注和模拟器真值，涵盖14种转移类型（接触、重力、摩擦、旋转/稳定性），包含1,246个试验中的27,303个时间标注实例。利用这些数据，我们发现当前VLMs缺乏转移级别的物理理解，零样本召回率最高仅为14%，且错误以漏检为主。直接对APT链进行微调会改善转移检测，但导致事件级别遗忘，表明模型学习的是专门的答案格式而非可复用的物理表示。因此，我们提出APT-Tune，一种参数高效的训练方案，教会VLMs使用因果转移而不遗忘如何回答视频问题。它结合了图像填充感知监督、格式条件联合训练和机制条件域类型解码，使APT学习具有格式鲁棒性和物理基础性。在Qwen3-VL-2B上仅使用11M LoRA参数，APT-Tune显著提高了APT召回率，同时改善了事件级视频迁移能力。这些结果表明，APT不是一种新的答案格式，而是用于物理视频理解的、与人类对齐的因果监督信号。

</details>


### [22] [Bridging Creative Intent and Visual Quality: Creator-Driven Recurrent Video Generation with Agentic Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18591)
*Denis Savytski,Aiden Lei,Heding Liu,Warren Yang,Sihan Liang,Alexander Liu,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHIEF框架，一种将人类创作者置于循环中心的人工智能协作视频生成方法，通过角色条件化多模态大语言模型提供主观反馈，帮助无经验创作者从1分钟短片到10分钟完整短片的迭代式视频创作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI生成的视频缺乏叙事连贯性和创意方向，尤其在长视频中更为突出。与编程不同，视频生成需要关于情节、场景和叙事的主观反馈，这自然需要融入人类创意指导的方法。

Method: 提出CHIEF框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 人类创作者驱动每次迭代并提供创意方向；(2) 专门的精炼智能体整合创作者修订；(3) 角色条件化多模态大语言模型从观众视角观看生成视频并产生主观批评性反馈，形成迭代式人机协作循环。

Result: 与没有电影制作经验的高中生和大学生合作，成功创作从1分钟短视频到具有复杂剧情的10分钟完整短片。

Conclusion: 将人类创意指导置于循环中心，结合角色条件化多模态LLM提供的主观反馈，能有效提升AI生成视频的叙事连贯性和创意质量，使无经验用户也能创作高质量视频内容。

Original Abstract: Generative AI has made content creation increasingly accessible, but many AI-generated videos lack narrative coherence and creative direction, issues that become more substantial at longer durations. Unlike coding, where AI generation benefits from reliable feedback and techniques such as recurrent self-improvement, video generation requires subjective feedback about plot, scenes, and narrative, which naturally motivates approaches that incorporate human creative direction. We introduce CHIEF, a human-AI co-creation video generation framework that places the creator at the center of human-in-the-loop iterative video refinement, and supports them by providing automatic subjective feedback. The creator incorporates their creative direction by driving each iteration, while their revisions are incorporated by a specialized refiner agent. The feedback loop is generated by persona-conditioned multimodal LLMs that watch generated videos and produce subjective critique from the audience perspectives, providing feedback that self-evaluation alone cannot capture. To test the effectiveness of our proposed framework, we work with high school and college students with no prior filmmaking experience to create videos, from short 1-minute videos to a complete short 10-minute film with a complicated plot.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI使得内容创作日益便捷，但许多AI生成的视频缺乏叙事连贯性和创意指导，在更长视频中这些问题更加突出。与编程不同（AI生成可从可靠反馈和递归自我改进等技术中获益），视频生成需要对情节、场景和叙事的主观反馈，这自然会激发融入人类创意指导的方法。我们提出了CHIEF，一个将创作者置于人机循环迭代视频精炼中心位置的人机协同视频生成框架，并通过提供自动主观反馈来支持创作者。创作者通过驱动每次迭代来融入其创意方向，而他们的修订则由专门的精炼智能体整合。反馈循环由角色条件化的多模态大语言模型生成，这些模型观看生成的视频并从观众视角产生主观批评，提供自我评价无法捕捉的反馈。为测试所提出框架的有效性，我们与没有电影制作经验的高中生和大学生合作创作视频，从1分钟的短视频到具有复杂剧情的完整10分钟短片。

</details>


### [23] [Hallucination Detection and Correction in Medical VLMs via Counter-Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18609)
*Nan Zhou,Ke Zou,Meng Liu,Linchao He,Jiaqi Zhu,Yi Zhang,Hu Chen,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CoEV（反事实证据验证）的无训练、即插即用的框架，通过文本断言与视觉证据之间的双向验证来检测和纠正视觉-语言模型在医学诊断中的幻觉问题，显著提升了检测和校正性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法主要关注生成文本与参考数据之间的事实不一致性，虽有一些研究分析模型关注图像的哪些区域，但很少验证这种关注是否真正反映支持生成文本的视觉证据，因此需要一种基于证据的事实一致性验证方法。

Method: CoEV是一个无训练、即插即用的框架，通过文本断言与视觉证据之间的双向验证来检测和纠正幻觉。它将每个语句分配到一个四象限诊断图中，该图捕捉文本事实性和视觉基础性的组合，并可作为事后精炼工具无需重新训练即可纠正幻觉。

Result: 在四个医学数据集上，幻觉检测方面，CoEV在PR-AUC和ROC-AUC上分别平均提升3.0%和3.9%绝对点，特定VQA场景提升高达18.5%；幻觉校正方面，Micro-F1提升高达12.5%，医学报告生成的幻觉率降低超过11.9%，同时提升了医学VQA准确率。

Conclusion: CoEV能够实现对幻觉的可靠检测和纠正，为临床医生提供可信赖的、基于证据的诊断线索。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) reliability in medical diagnosis is challenged by trust-undermining hallucinations. Existing hallucination detection approaches mainly focus on identifying factual inconsistencies between generated text and reference data. While some studies analyze where models attend in images, they seldom verify whether such attention truly reflects the visual evidence supporting the generated text. To address this gap, we propose Co}unter-Evidence Verification (CoEV), a training-free plug-and-play framework that detects and corrects hallucinations through evidence-based factual consistency verification. CoEV performs bidirectional verification between textual assertions and visual evidence, testing whether each statement is supported by its corresponding evidence region, and assigns each statement into a four-quadrant diagnostic map capturing combinations of text factuality and visual grounding. CoEV detects hallucinated content and serves as a post hoc refinement tool, correcting hallucinations without retraining. Extensive experiments on four medical datasets show that CoEV combats hallucinations in VLMs.For hallucination detection, CoEV consistently outperforms existing methods, improving average PR-AUC and ROC-AUC by 3.0% and 3.9% absolute points respectively, with notable gains of up to 18.5% in specific VQA scenarios. For hallucination correction, it improves Micro-F1 by up to 12.5%, reduces hallucination rates by over 11.9% on medical report generation, and also boosts medical VQA accuracy. These results show that CoEV enables reliable detection and correction of hallucinations, providing clinicians with dependable, evidence-based cues for diagnosis. Code will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型在医学诊断中的可靠性因产生削弱信任的幻觉而受到挑战。现有的幻觉检测方法主要关注识别生成文本与参考数据之间的事实不一致性。虽然一些研究分析了模型关注图像的哪些区域，但很少验证这种关注是否真正反映了支持生成文本的视觉证据。为解决这一差距，我们提出反事实证据验证（CoEV），一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过基于证据的事实一致性验证来检测和纠正幻觉。CoEV执行文本断言与视觉证据之间的双向验证，测试每个陈述是否得到其对应证据区域的支持，并将每个语句分配到一个捕捉文本事实性与视觉基础性组合的四象限诊断图中。CoEV能够检测幻觉内容，并作为一种事后精炼工具，无需重新训练即可纠正幻觉。在四个医学数据集上的大量实验表明，CoEV能有效对抗视觉-语言模型中的幻觉。在幻觉检测方面，CoEV持续优于现有方法，PR-AUC和ROC-AUC分别平均提升3.0%和3.9%绝对点，在特定VQA场景中提升高达18.5%。在幻觉校正方面，它将Micro-F1提升高达12.5%，在医学报告生成中幻觉率降低超过11.9%，同时还提升了医学VQA的准确率。这些结果表明，CoEV能够实现幻觉的可靠检测和纠正，为临床医生提供可信赖的、基于证据的诊断线索。代码将在接收后发布。

</details>


### [24] [Spiking Pyramid Wavelet Transformation for High-efficient and Low-energy Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18644)
*Chen Zhao,Xiantao Hu,Song Wu,Qian Wang,Chen Wu,Rui Xie,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于脉冲金字塔小波变换的模型（SPWM），利用离散小波变换和脉冲神经网络（SNN）进行图像恢复，能在降低计算和能耗的同时保持图像质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于脉冲CNN的图像恢复方法受限于CNN固有的感受野限制，难以建模长程依赖关系，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出脉冲金字塔小波模型（SPWM），核心是开发了脉冲双金字塔小波（SDPW）模块，用于建模长程依赖关系并利用小波域中的退化特性进行图像恢复。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SPWM在保持图像质量的同时，显著降低了计算成本和能耗。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了SNN在图像恢复领域中的潜力，为未来资源受限设备上的应用提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have garnered significant interest in computer vision due to their potential for efficiency and biological inspiration. While spiking CNN-based methods have shown promise for image restoration (IR) tasks, their performance is constrained by the inherent receptive field limitations of CNN operations. In the paper, we explore the benefits of discrete wavelet transformation and propose a spiking pyramid wavelet-based model (SPWM) for high-efficient and low-energy target. Specifically, we develop a spiking dual pyramid wavelet (SDPW) block to model long-range dependency and exploit the properties of the degradation in the wavelet domain. Experimental results on several benchmarks demonstrate that SPWM significantly lowers computational costs and energy consumption while maintaining image quality. Our method showcases the potential of SNNs in the field of IR, offering new insights for future applications of resource-limited devices.

中文摘要翻译: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）因其潜在的高效性和生物启发性，在计算机视觉领域引起了广泛关注。尽管基于脉冲CNN的方法在图像恢复（IR）任务中展现出潜力，但其性能受到CNN操作固有感受野限制的约束。本文探索了离散小波变换的优势，并提出了一种基于脉冲金字塔小波的模型（SPWM），以实现高效和低能耗的目标。具体而言，我们开发了一个脉冲双金字塔小波（SDPW）模块，用于建模长程依赖关系并利用小波域中的退化特性。在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，SPWM在保持图像质量的同时，显著降低了计算成本和能耗。我们的方法展示了SNN在图像恢复领域的潜力，为未来资源受限设备上的应用提供了新的见解。

</details>


### [25] [LandslideAgent with Multimodal LandslideBench: A Domain-Rule-Augmented Agent for Autonomous Landslide Identification and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18661)
*Chengfu Liu,Dongyang Hou,Junwu Xiang,Cheng Yang,Xuezhi Cui,Zeyuan Wang,Liangtian Liu,Zelang Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个指令驱动的智能滑坡灾害解译框架，包含多模态精细数据集LandslideBench、滑坡专用视觉语言模型LandslideVLM以及领域规则增强智能体LandslideAgent，三者协同实现滑坡识别与分析的全程智能化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有滑坡灾害解译方法难以同时提取视觉特征和高层次地质语义，通用视觉语言模型在复杂地质场景中存在感知局限和领域幻觉，亟需专门化解决方案。

Method: 首先通过多VLM交叉验证和交互式标注构建多模态精细数据集LandslideBench；然后基于LoRA微调得到滑坡专用VLM（LandslideVLM）；最后以LandslideVLM为认知主干，设计包含结构化报告元数据约束和交叉验证识别约束的双规则控制器，构建领域规则增强智能体LandslideAgent。

Result: LandslideVLM在滑坡判别、细粒度分类和语义描述质量上分别提升10.96%、32.87%和15.91%；LandslideBench为五个主流模型在细粒度分类和语义分割上提供有效基线；LandslideAgent实现自主多源空间数据推理。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了滑坡识别与分析的全程智能化，显著优于通用模型，验证了领域专用视觉语言模型与规则增强智能体相结合的有效性。

Original Abstract: Intelligent landslide hazard interpretation is critical for disaster prevention, yet current paradigms struggle to simultaneously extract visual features and high-level geoscientific semantics, while general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from perceptual limitations and domain hallucinations in complex geological scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose an instruction-driven agentic framework comprising three components. First, LandslideBench, a multimodal fine-grained dataset with seven subtype labels, high-resolution imagery, pixel-level masks, and high-quality textual descriptions, is constructed via multi-VLM cross-validation and interactive annotation. Then, LandslideVLM, a landslide-oriented VLM, is fine-tuned via LoRA on LandslideBench to enhance geological semantic understanding. Finally, LandslideAgent, a domain rule-enhanced agent taking LandslideVLM as its cognitive backbone, employs a dual-rule controller incorporating structured report metadata constraints and cross-validation identification constraints to regulate automated tool invocation. Experiments demonstrate that LandslideBench provides effective baselines across five mainstream models on fine-grained classification and semantic segmentation. LandslideVLM achieves accuracy improvements of 10.96%, 32.87%, and 15.91% on landslide discrimination, fine-grained classification, and semantic description quality, respectively. LandslideAgent further enables autonomous multi-source spatial data inference, realizing full-process intelligence for landslide identification and analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 智能滑坡灾害解译对防灾减灾至关重要，然而现有范式难以同时提取视觉特征和高层次地球科学语义，同时通用视觉语言模型（VLM）在复杂地质场景中存在感知局限和领域幻觉。针对这些挑战，我们提出一个指令驱动的智能体框架，包含三个组成部分。首先，通过多VLM交叉验证和交互式标注，构建了LandslideBench——一个包含七种子类型标签、高分辨率影像、像素级掩码和高质量文本描述的多模态精细数据集。其次，在LandslideBench上通过LoRA微调得到面向滑坡的VLM（LandslideVLM），以增强地质语义理解。最后，以LandslideVLM为认知主干，构建领域规则增强智能体LandslideAgent，该智能体采用包含结构化报告元数据约束和交叉验证识别约束的双规则控制器，以调控自动化工具调用。实验表明，LandslideBench在五个主流模型的细粒度分类和语义分割任务上提供了有效基线。LandslideVLM在滑坡判别、细粒度分类和语义描述质量上分别实现了10.96%、32.87%和15.91%的准确率提升。LandslideAgent进一步实现了自主多源空间数据推理，达成了滑坡识别与分析的全程智能化。

</details>


### [26] [BrainFusionNet: a deep learning and XAI model to understand local, global, and sequential features of MRI images for improved brain tumour detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18675)
*Md Taimur Ahad,Bo Song,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainFusionNet，一种融合CNN、ViT和GRU的混合深度学习模型，用于脑肿瘤MRI分类，结合SHAP、LIME和GradCAM等可解释AI方法，在两个公开数据集上达到98%的准确率，并发现MRI图像像素强度分布会影响分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MRI噪声使得肿瘤边界模糊、位置和外观复杂，给深度学习分类带来挑战，因此需要一种能有效提取局部和全局特征并具备可解释性的混合模型。

Method: 提出BrainFusionNet，结合CNN（提取空间特征）、Vision Transformer（ViT，提取局部特征并稳定梯度流）和GRU（序列建模）进行脑肿瘤MRI分类，并集成SHAP、LIME和GradCAM实现可解释性分析。

Result: 在两个公开MRI数据集上，K折交叉验证达到98%准确率；对比六种SOTA CNN模型，DenseNet121和VGG16最高为96%；还发现MRI图像像素强度分布影响分类性能。

Conclusion: BrainFusionNet混合模型能有效从MRI图像中提取局部和全局特征，即使在小肿瘤区域和较小肿瘤尺寸下也表现优异，且可解释性分析有助于理解模型决策过程。

Original Abstract: The noise of Magnetic Resonance Imaging MRI poses challenges for Deep Learning DL when tumor boundaries are obscured tumor location and appearance are complex Therefore we develop BrainFusionNet that combines Convolutional Neural Networks CNNs Vision Transformers ViT and Gated Recurrent Units GRUs to extract spatial contextual and sequential features from MRI images for improved brain tumor classification Furthermore explainable AI such as SHAP LIME and GradCAM are integrated to visualise and highlight image regions that contribute to BrainFusionNets decisionmaking process The proposed BrainFusionNet model is evaluated on two publicly available MRI datasets Kfold validation suggests 98 accuracy on both datasets The model was compared with the six stateoftheart SOTA CNNs and transfer learning Among the SOTA CNNs DenseNet121 and VGG16 achieved the highest accuracy of 96 The novelty of BrainFusionNet is that the hybrid model effectively extracts local and global features from MRI images even in smallscale tumor regions and small tumor sizes The model has a balanced sequential CNN architecture to capture lowlevel and deeperlayer features a customized ViT that captures local features stabilizes gradient flow and reduces the risk of vanishing gradients during MRI image training The CNN and ViT outputs are fed into a GRU for final classification Furthermore we analyze pixel intensities to determine whether MRI image quality affects image classification Our findings are very novel in image interpretation as we found that the distribution of pixel intensities in MRI images affects DL performance

中文摘要翻译: 磁共振成像（MRI）的噪声给深度学习（DL）带来了挑战，尤其是当肿瘤边界模糊、肿瘤位置和外观复杂时。为此，我们开发了BrainFusionNet，该模型结合卷积神经网络（CNN）、视觉Transformer（ViT）和门控循环单元（GRU），从MRI图像中提取空间、上下文和序列特征，以改进脑肿瘤分类。此外，集成可解释人工智能方法（如SHAP、LIME和GradCAM），用于可视化和突出显示对BrainFusionNet决策过程有贡献的图像区域。所提出的BrainFusionNet模型在两个公开的MRI数据集上进行了评估，K折交叉验证显示在两个数据集上均达到98%的准确率。该模型与六种最先进的CNN及迁移学习方法进行了比较，其中DenseNet121和VGG16取得了最高的96%准确率。BrainFusionNet的创新之处在于，该混合模型能够有效从MRI图像中提取局部和全局特征，即使在小规模肿瘤区域和小肿瘤尺寸的情况下也是如此。该模型具有平衡的序列化CNN架构以捕获浅层和深层特征，以及定制的ViT以捕获局部特征、稳定梯度流并降低MRI图像训练过程中的梯度消失风险。CNN和ViT的输出被输入GRU进行最终分类。此外，我们分析了像素强度以确定MRI图像质量是否影响图像分类。我们在图像解释方面发现了非常新颖的结果：MRI图像中像素强度的分布会影响深度学习性能。

</details>


### [27] [Moving Beyond Diversity: Visual Token Pruning as Subspace Reconstruction for Efficient VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18681)
*Jaeyeon Lee,Shunjie Wen,Dong-Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARE方法，通过子空间重构将token剪枝建模为列子集选择问题，利用投影残差迭代选择关键token，同时引入反相关性机制提升上下文感知能力，在保留极少视觉token的情况下实现SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖余弦归一化相似度，丢弃了幅度信息，无法忠实近似原始特征表示，尤其在组合型多技能推理任务上表现不佳。

Method: SPARE方法将token剪枝重构为列子集选择问题，通过迭代选择投影残差大的token进行重构驱动剪枝；此外发现并引入反相关性现象——低图文相关度的token更能保留上下文信息，将其作为额外选择标准。

Result: 在多个VLM和基准上达到SOTA，尤其在组合型任务上提升显著；在LLaVA上可去除94%的视觉token同时保留95%的基线性能，且完全无需训练。

Conclusion: SPARE通过重构驱动的剪枝策略和反相关性机制，在极大减少视觉token的同时有效保持模型性能，是一种高效且无需训练的视觉token压缩方法。

Original Abstract: Despite their remarkable performance, Vision Language Models (VLMs) incur substantial computational overhead due to the large number of visual tokens. While diversity maximization has become a dominant strategy for token reduction, existing methods rely on cosine-based normalized similarity that discards magnitude information, failing to faithfully approximate the original feature representation and leading to suboptimal performance, particularly on compositional multi-skill reasoning tasks. In this paper, we introduce SPARE, a subspace reconstruction method that reformulates token pruning as a column subset selection problem and explicitly minimizes reconstruction error. By iteratively selecting tokens with large projection residuals, SPARE performs reconstruction-driven pruning beyond angular diversity. Moreover, we reveal a counterintuitive anti-relevance phenomenon: tokens with lower image-text relevance score can better preserve contextual information. Based on this finding, we incorporate anti-relevance into SPARE as an additional selection criterion to promote context-aware token selection. Extensive experiments across multiple VLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that SPARE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with strong gains on compositional tasks. When applied to LLaVA, SPARE removes up to 94% of visual tokens while retaining 95% of the baseline performance, all in a fully training-free manner.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）表现出色，但由于大量视觉token的存在，其计算开销巨大。虽然多样性最大化已成为token缩减的主流策略，但现有方法依赖基于余弦的归一化相似度，丢弃了幅度信息，无法忠实近似原始特征表示，导致性能次优，尤其在组合型多技能推理任务上。本文提出SPARE，一种子空间重构方法，将token剪枝重新建模为列子集选择问题，并显式最小化重构误差。通过迭代选择投影残差大的token，SPARE实现了超越角度多样性的重构驱动剪枝。此外，我们揭示了一种反直觉的反相关性现象：低图文相关度得分的token反而能更好地保留上下文信息。基于这一发现，我们将反相关性作为额外的选择标准融入SPARE，以促进上下文感知的token选择。在多种VLM和基准上的大量实验表明，SPARE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance，在组合型任务上取得显著提升。当应用于LLaVA时，SPARE能以完全无需训练的方式去除高达94%的视觉token，同时保留95%的基线性能。

</details>


### [28] [Multi-Class Brain Tumor Classification Using Advanced Deep Learning Models: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18682)
*Asad Channa,Asghar Ali Chandio,Akhtar Hussain Jalbani,Mehwish Leghari,Shahzad Memon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种CNN架构（VGG16、VGG19、DenseNet121、EfficientNetB0及自定义基线模型）在MRI脑肿瘤多分类任务上的性能，发现EfficientNetB0以95%的整体准确率和89%的脑膜瘤召回率表现最佳，表明模型架构效率比网络深度更重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管深度学习取得了进展，但从MRI图像中准确分类脑肿瘤仍然存在挑战，尤其是一些肿瘤（如脑膜瘤）在影像上表现非常细微，难以被简单CNN模型有效检测。

Method: 在包含约10,000张MRI图像的临床数据集上，在完全相同的实验框架下训练和测试了五种CNN架构：自定义基线CNN、VGG16、VGG19、DenseNet121和EfficientNetB0，通过整体准确率和各类肿瘤的召回率评估性能。

Result: EfficientNetB0整体分类准确率最高（95%），优于VGG16（94.37%）、VGG19（92.29%）、DenseNet121（90.91%）和自定义CNN（78.00%）。特别重要的是，EfficientNetB0检测脑膜瘤的召回率从简单CNN的约20%提升至89%。此外，更深的VGG19表现反而不如较浅的VGG16。

Conclusion: EfficientNetB0在分类准确率、模型参数量和临床有意义性能之间提供了最优的权衡；在处理医学图像时，CNN模型的架构效率可能比网络深度更为重要。

Original Abstract: Despite recent advancements in deep learning, accurately classifying brain tumors from MRI images continues to pose challenges. In this research, we present a comprehensive evaluation of five different convolutional neural networks (CNN) architectures, including a customized baseline model and four pre-trained models - for use in classifying multi-class brain tumors using a clinically-sourced dataset of approximately 10,000 MRI images. We have utilized five different architectures; VGG16, VGG19, DenseNet121, and EfficientNetB0, which were all tested and trained within an identical experimental framework. Performance was measured by both overall accuracy and tumor-wise recall as a means to measure the clinically-relevant performance of each architecture. We found that EfficientNetB0 had the best overall classification accuracy at 95%, when compared to the other architectures tested; specifically VGG16 (94.37%), VGG19 (92.29%), DenseNet121 (90.91%) and the customized CNN (78.00%). An especially important finding of our research was the considerable improvement in detecting meningiomas; specifically, while simple CNNs could detect meningiomas with a recall rate of approximately 20%, EfficientNetB0 was able to detect meningiomas with a recall rate of 89%. Meningiomas are often difficult to detect because they can appear very subtly on MRI images. Additionally, an interesting finding was that the deeper VGG19 performed worse than the shallower VGG16. This indicates that in many cases the architectural efficiency of a CNN model may be more important than its depth when working with medical images. Overall, EfficientNetB0 appears to provide the optimal trade-off between classification accuracy, number of parameters used in the model and clinically meaningful performance.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管深度学习近年取得了进展，但从MRI图像中准确分类脑肿瘤仍然存在挑战。在本研究中，我们使用一个包含约10,000张MRI图像的临床来源数据集，对五种不同的卷积神经网络（CNN）架构——包括一个自定义基线模型和四个预训练模型——进行了全面评估，用于多类别脑肿瘤分类。我们采用了五种不同架构：VGG16、VGG19、DenseNet121和EfficientNetB0，所有模型均在完全相同的实验框架下进行训练和测试。性能评估同时采用了整体准确率和各类肿瘤的召回率，以衡量每种架构的临床相关性能。我们发现，EfficientNetB0的整体分类准确率最高，达到95%，优于其他测试架构——具体而言，VGG16为94.37%，VGG19为92.29%，DenseNet121为90.91%，自定义CNN为78.00%。本研究的一个重要发现是脑膜瘤检测性能的显著提升：简单CNN检测脑膜瘤的召回率约为20%，而EfficientNetB0能够达到89%的召回率。脑膜瘤通常难以检测，因为它们在MRI图像上可能表现得非常细微。此外，一个有趣的发现是，更深的VGG19表现反而不如较浅的VGG16。这表明在处理医学图像时，CNN模型的架构效率在许多情况下可能比其深度更为重要。总体而言，EfficientNetB0在分类准确率、模型参数量和临床有意义性能之间提供了最优的权衡。

</details>


### [29] [Spatially Stratified Distillation for Heterogeneous Radar Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18687)
*Sagun Singh Shrestha,Samuel Harding,Abdelwahed Khamis,Saimunur Rahman,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间分层蒸馏（SSD）策略，通过非对称空间对齐替代传统均匀蒸馏，解决低成本4D汽车雷达与高密度旋转雷达之间的跨模态地点识别中的稀疏性挑战，在HeRCULES数据集上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低成本4D汽车雷达与高密度旋转雷达之间存在巨大的结构稀疏性差异，现有方法通过统一信号投影到共同表示空间，但在多会话环境中性能显著下降。

Method: 提出空间分层蒸馏（SSD），核心是依据物理雷达回波的几何对应关系进行非对称空间对齐：在两者重叠区域强制强特征对齐，在仅教师雷达有回波而4D学生雷达无回波的稀疏区域内大幅降低蒸馏权重。

Result: 在HeRCULES数据集上的大量评估表明，SSD显著优于先前的地点识别方法，在其具有挑战性的动态序列上取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: SSD通过物理引导的非对称蒸馏策略有效解决了跨模态雷达地点识别中的稀疏性与多会话退化问题，验证了空间对齐方式对异构传感器知识迁移的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Scalable, all-weather place recognition increasingly relies on heterogeneous radar place recognition to bridge diverse hardware platforms. A notable application is matching queries from cost-effective 4D automotive radars against high-fidelity reference maps built by dense spinning radars. This process is fundamentally limited by the extreme sparsity (and narrow field-of-view) of the 4D sensor, which captures only a fraction of the structural density present in the spinning radar database. Prior efforts address this issue by unifying different radar signals. That is, projecting both signals into a common representational space. Yet, they suffer performance degradation in multi-session environments. In this paper, we propose spatially-stratified distillation (SSD); a strategy that replaces standard uniform distillation with an asymmetric spatial alignment derived directly from physical radar returns. In regions where both radars exhibit overlapping returns, SSD enforces strong feature alignment. Crucially, in sparse regions where the 4D student lacks returns but the teacher contains valid structure within the shared field of view, SSD applies heavily discounted distillation weights. Extensive evaluations of the recent HeRCULES dataset demonstrate that SSD significantly outperforms prior place recognition methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on its challenging dynamic sequences.

中文摘要翻译: 可扩展的全天候地点识别越来越依赖异构雷达地点识别来桥接不同的硬件平台。一个显著的应用是将来自低成本4D汽车雷达的查询与由高密度旋转雷达构建的高保真参考地图进行匹配。这一过程从根本上受到4D传感器极端稀疏性（以及窄视场）的限制——它仅捕获旋转雷达数据库中结构密度的一小部分。先前的工作通过统一不同的雷达信号来应对这一问题，即将两种信号投影到共同的表示空间中。然而，这些方法在多会话环境中会出现性能退化。在本文中，我们提出空间分层蒸馏（SSD）；这一策略用直接源自物理雷达回波的非对称空间对齐替代标准的均匀蒸馏。在两个雷达均有重叠回波的区域，SSD强制执行强特征对齐。关键的是，在4D学生雷达无回波但教师雷达在共享视场内含有有效结构的稀疏区域，SSD采用大幅折扣的蒸馏权重。对最新HeRCULES数据集的广泛评估表明，SSD显著优于先前的地点识别方法，在其具有挑战性的动态序列上取得了最先进的结果。

</details>


### [30] [UniTemp: Unlocking Video Generation in Any Temporal Order via Bidirectional Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18702)
*Lin Zhang,Sicheng Mo,Zefan Cai,Jinhong Lin,Zihao Lin,Jiuxiang Gu,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yuheng Li,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniTemp，一种双向蒸馏框架，通过在自回归视频扩散模型中引入块级锚点潜变量，实现任意时序方向的视频生成（前向、后向、中间插值），克服了因果3D VAE在反向生成时导致的块间不连续问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅支持前向时序生成，而实际视频创作需要灵活生成顺序（如基于未来上下文向后扩展、基于过去和未来上下文进行中间插值），现有因果3D VAE结构在反向生成时会导致块间不连续。

Method: 提出UniTemp双向蒸馏框架：1) 引入块级锚点潜变量，在块边界处恢复反向生成时缺失的过去上下文；2) 训练单个自回归学生模型，支持任意方向视频生成；3) 推理时可基于任意过去和/或未来帧进行条件生成。

Result: UniTemp在短视频和长视频生成上与仅前向方法相比保持竞争力，同时支持双向视频扩展、中间插值生成、循环视频生成、场景转换和视觉故事生成等多种工作流。
