Conclusion: 通过块级锚点潜变量解决因果3D VAE在反向生成中的块间不连续问题，UniTemp实现了灵活的多方向视频生成，扩展了自回归视频扩散模型的应用范围。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have emerged as a promising approach for long video generation, achieving strong performance in streaming settings. However, existing methods are restricted to forward temporal generation, whereas practical video creation often requires flexible generation order, e.g., conditioning on future context to extend backward, or on both past and future context for inbetween generation. We bridge this gap by training an autoregressive model that supports generation in arbitrary temporal directions. A key technical challenge arises from the Causal 3D VAE widely used in video diffusion models, which encodes latents strictly conditioned on past context. While suited for forward generation, this causal structure causes inter-block discontinuities when generation proceeds backward. To address this, we introduce blockwise anchor latents, a set of auxiliary latents that restore the missing past context at block boundaries during backward generation. Built on this design, we propose UniTemp, a bidirectional distillation framework that trains a single autoregressive student model for any-direction video generation. At inference time, UniTemp conditions on arbitrary past and/or future frames, improving controllability for both bidirectional and inbetween generation. Experiments show that UniTemp maintains competitive performance on short and long video generation compared to forward-only methods, while enabling diverse workflows such as bidirectional video extension, inbetween generation, looping video generation, scene transition, and visual story generation. Project website: https://lzhangbj.github.io/projects/unitemp/

中文摘要翻译: 自回归视频扩散模型已成为长视频生成的一种有前景的方法，在流式设置中展现了强劲的性能。然而，现有方法仅限于前向时序生成，而实际视频创作通常需要灵活的生成顺序，例如基于未来上下文向后扩展，或基于过去和未来上下文进行中间插值生成。我们通过训练一个支持任意时序方向生成的自回归模型来弥合这一差距。一个关键的技术挑战源于视频扩散模型中广泛使用的因果3D VAE，它以严格依赖过去上下文的方式编码潜变量。虽然这种因果结构适用于前向生成，但在反向生成时会导致块间不连续。为解决这一问题，我们引入了块级锚点潜变量，这是一组辅助潜变量，用于在反向生成过程中恢复块边界处缺失的过去上下文。基于这一设计，我们提出了UniTemp，一种双向蒸馏框架，训练单个自回归学生模型用于任意方向视频生成。在推理时，UniTemp可基于任意过去和/或未来帧进行条件生成，提高了双向生成和中间插值生成的可控性。实验表明，与仅前向方法相比，UniTemp在短视频和长视频生成上保持了有竞争力的性能，同时支持多样化的 workflows，如双向视频扩展、中间插值生成、循环视频生成、场景转换和视觉故事生成。项目网站：https://lzhangbj.github.io/projects/unitemp/

</details>


### [31] [PEFT-MedSAM: Efficient Fine-Tuning of Medical Foundation Models for Explainable Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18707)
*Asad Channa,Abdullah Khan,Asghar Ali Chandio,Aamir Akbar,Shahzad Memon,Aqib Hussain,Ameer Hamza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数高效微调方法PEFT-MedSAM，用于将医学分割大模型（MedSAM）适配到皮肤镜图像病变分割任务，仅训练轻量级掩码解码器而冻结预训练编码器，在ISIC 2018和PH2数据集上取得了优于全量训练U-Net和零样本MedSAM的分割性能，并通过Grad-CAM可解释性分析增强了临床可信度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在皮肤镜图像病变分割任务上性能不佳，而直接使用预训练大模型（如MedSAM）进行零样本推理效果也不理想；同时，全量微调大模型计算成本高昂。因此需要一种参数高效的微调方法，在保持预训练编码器知识的同时高效适配下游任务。

Method: 提出PEFT-MedSAM方法，冻结MedSAM的预训练图像编码器和提示编码器，仅训练其轻量级掩码解码器来实现参数高效微调。同时采用Grad-CAM可解释性分析和基于点游戏（pointing game）的评估方法来评估CNN基线模型的分类定位能力。

Result: 在ISIC 2018数据集上，PEFT-MedSAM的Dice系数为0.9411，IoU为0.8918，优于全量训练U-Net（0.8715 Dice）和零样本MedSAM（0.8997 Dice）。在PH2外部验证集上Dice系数为0.9467（±0.0310标准差）。Wilcoxon符号秩检验p值<0.0001，Bootstrap 95%置信区间为[0.9364, 0.9447]。Grad-CAM可解释性分析在519张验证集上准确率达98.27%。

Conclusion: PEFT-MedSAM以极低的训练成本（仅训练轻量级掩码解码器）显著提升了MedSAM在皮肤病变分割任务上的性能，超越了全量训练的U-Net和零样本MedSAM，具有良好的泛化能力和临床可信度。

Original Abstract: Automated segmentation of skin lesions using deep learning models for dermoscopic images can be very helpful in finding melanomas earlier than they would normally be detected. However, most deep learning methods available do not perform well. The aim of this paper is to present a parameter-efficient fine-tuning method called PEFT-MedSAM for adapting the Medical Segment Anything Model (MedSAM) to automatically segment dermoscopic skin lesions. The PEFT-MedSAM method uses only the lightweight mask decoder for training the model while keeping the pre-trained image encoder and prompt encoder frozen. The experiments performed on the ISIC 2018 benchmark dataset shows that PEFT-MedSAM obtains a dice coefficient of .9411 and an intersection over union value of .8918 when compared to both a fully trained U-Net baseline (.8715 dice coefficient) and zero-shot MedSAM inference (.8997 dice coefficient). The external validation of the model using PH2 dataset shows .9467 dice coefficient with +/- .0310 standard deviation. Supportive evidence for these claims include a p-value less than .0001 for Wilcoxon signed rank tests comparing the two datasets and bootstrap-estimated 95% confidence intervals of [.9364,.9447] that represent the estimated range of possible values for the average dice coefficient obtained by repeating the test. To increase clinical trustworthiness, we used Grad-CAM explainability along with a pointing game based evaluation methodology to evaluate the CNN baseline model on the validation set. The results showed that we had an accuracy rate of 98.27% on the validation set of 519 images and confirmed that the model classified regions containing skin lesions.

中文摘要翻译: 利用深度学习模型对皮肤镜图像进行皮肤病变自动分割，有助于比常规检测更早地发现黑色素瘤。然而，现有的大多数深度学习方法表现不佳。本文旨在提出一种参数高效微调方法——PEFT-MedSAM，用于适配医学分割一切模型（MedSAM）以自动分割皮肤镜下的皮肤病变。PEFT-MedSAM方法仅使用轻量级掩码解码器训练模型，同时冻结预训练的图像编码器和提示编码器。在ISIC 2018基准数据集上的实验表明，与全量训练的U-Net基线（Dice系数0.8715）和零样本MedSAM推理（Dice系数0.8997）相比，PEFT-MedSAM获得了0.9411的Dice系数和0.8918的交并比。使用PH2数据集进行的外部验证显示，Dice系数为0.9467，标准差为±0.0310。支持这些结果的有力证据包括：两个数据集间Wilcoxon符号秩检验的p值小于0.0001，以及Bootstrap估计的95%置信区间[0.9364, 0.9447]，该区间代表了重复测试所得平均Dice系数的估计范围。为提升临床可信度，我们使用Grad-CAM可解释性分析以及基于点游戏（pointing game）的评估方法，在验证集上评估了CNN基线模型。结果表明，在包含519张图像的验证集上，我们取得了98.27%的准确率，并确认模型正确分类了包含皮肤病变的区域。

</details>


### [32] [Rethinking the Pointer Loss in Table Structure Recognition: Geometry-Aware Pointer Loss for Spatial Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18721)
*Hong-Jun Choi,Jongho Lee,Jaeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何感知指针损失（GAP Loss），通过基于空间邻近性重新加权交叉熵损失，减少表格结构识别中相邻单元格间的预测错误，实现了无需额外推理成本的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在指针网络中平等对待所有负候选，但分析发现79.6%的错误发生在空间相邻单元格之间（曼哈顿距离≤2），说明模型在邻近单元格区分上存在瓶颈，需要针对性地改进损失函数。

Method: 提出几何感知指针（GAP）损失，基于预测单元格与真实标签的空间邻近性（曼哈顿距离）对交叉熵目标进行逆距离加权，使邻近单元格获得更强梯度，远离单元格获得较弱梯度，仅需修改损失计算，不改变模型架构。

Result: 在PubTabNet和SynthTabNet上的大量实验表明，GAP损失持续减少相邻单元格错误，取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 在损失层面引入几何归纳偏置为鲁棒的表格结构识别提供了一种简单而有效的方法。

Original Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) using a pointer network achieves impressive results by predicting HTML sequences while aligning tags to detected text (or cell) regions. However, our analysis reveals that when pointer networks fail, 79.6% of errors occur between spatially adjacent cells (Manhattan distance <= 2). Despite this, standard cross-entropy loss weights all negative candidates equally. In this work, we propose Geometry-Aware Pointer (GAP) Loss, which reweights the cross-entropy objective based on spatial proximity to ground truth. By applying inverse distance weighting, GAP focuses gradient flow where the model struggles most: immediate neighbors receive stronger gradients than distant cells. Our approach requires only a straightforward modification to the loss computation, maintaining the same model architecture with zero additional inference cost. Extensive experiments on PubTabNet and SynthTabNet demonstrate that GAP consistently reduces adjacent-cell errors, achieving new state-of-the-art performance. Our findings suggest that incorporating geometric inductive biases at the loss level provides a simple yet effective approach to robust TSR. Our code is available at https://github.com/teamreboott/GAP

中文摘要翻译: 使用指针网络的表格结构识别（TSR）通过预测HTML序列并将标签与检测到的文本（或单元格）区域对齐，取得了令人印象深刻的结果。然而，我们的分析揭示，当指针网络失败时，79.6%的错误发生在空间相邻单元格之间（曼哈顿距离≤2）。尽管如此，标准交叉熵损失对所有负候选赋予同等权重。在这项工作中，我们提出了几何感知指针（GAP）损失，该损失基于与真实标签的空间邻近性重新加权交叉熵目标。通过应用逆距离加权，GAP将梯度流聚焦于模型最困难的部分：与远处单元格相比，邻近单元格获得更强的梯度。我们的方法仅需要对损失计算进行简单的修改，保持相同的模型架构且零额外推理成本。在PubTabNet和SynthTabNet上的大量实验表明，GAP持续减少相邻单元格错误，取得了新的最先进性能。我们的发现表明，在损失层面引入几何归纳偏置为鲁棒的TSR提供了一种简单而有效的方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/teamreboott/GAP获取。

</details>


### [33] [Clinically Aligned Geometry Constraints for Robust IVUS Vessel Boundary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18723)
*Yunshu Chen,Litao Yang,Giuseppe Di Giovanni,Jordan Tan,Deval Mehta,Andrew Lin,Derek Chew,Masasi Fujino,Julie Butters,Stephen Nicholls,Zongyuan Ge,Kyung Hoon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCat 是一种几何一致性网络，通过双编码器、跨域注意力与可微几何损失函数，在 IVUS 图像分割中显著提升了边界精度与几何保真度，实现了更可靠的斑块负荷量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 IVUS 管腔与 EEM 分割方法优先优化重叠分数（如 Dice），但常出现边界漂移和拓扑错误，导致临床测量（如斑块面积、几何尺寸）不准确，亟需一种几何一致的分割方法。

Method: 提出 GeoCat 网络，包含双 Cartesian-Polar 编码器、跨域注意力机制与时间融合模块，处理 5 帧 IVUS 序列；引入可微几何一致性损失函数，直接监督直径、角度、截面积等临床相关几何描述符。

Result: 在 12,242 帧标注数据上，GeoCat 达到 Dice 0.93，95HD 降至 0.14 mm，拓扑违例率仅 1.0%，直径误差 0.13-0.16 mm，角度误差约 8 度，几何保真度显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: GeoCat 通过几何一致性约束有效解决了 IVUS 分割中的边界漂移与拓扑错误问题，为临床斑块负荷量化提供了更可靠的分割基础。

Original Abstract: Intravascular ultrasound (IVUS) lumen and external elastic membrane (EEM) segmentation is important for quantitative coronary plaque burden assessment. Errors in lumen or EEM delineation directly propagate to plaque area, plaque burden and geometric measurements. However, standard methods prioritising overlap scores often suffer from boundary drift and topology errors, leading to inaccurate clinical measurements. We present GeoCat, a geometry-consistent network that processes 5-frame IVUS clips using dual Cartesian-polar encoders with cross-domain attention and temporal fusion. A differentiable geometry consistency loss directly supervises clinically relevant descriptors including diameters, orientations, and cross-sectional areas. The model is trained on 12,242 annotated frames from 146 patients acquired with two commercial IVUS systems. We evaluate performance using both segmentation accuracy and plaque-relevant clinical metrics, including Dice/IoU, boundary measures(95HD (mm), ASSD), topology violation rate, and clinical geometry errors (dmax/dmin, angles, and areas). On our dataset, GeoCat achieves a Dice of 0.93, reduces 95HD to 0.14 mm, and lowers topology violations to 1.0%. Importantly, it significantly improves geometric fidelity, yielding diameter errors of 0.13-0.16 mm and angular errors of ~8 degrees, supporting reliable plaque burden quantification.

中文摘要翻译: 血管内超声（IVUS）管腔与外弹性膜（EEM）分割对于定量评估冠状动脉斑块负荷至关重要。管腔或 EEM 勾画中的误差会直接传播至斑块面积、斑块负荷及几何测量结果。然而，优先优化重叠分数的标准方法常出现边界漂移和拓扑错误，导致临床测量不准确。我们提出 GeoCat，一种几何一致性网络，使用双 Cartesian-Polar 编码器结合跨域注意力与时间融合处理 5 帧 IVUS 序列。可微几何一致性损失直接监督临床相关描述符，包括直径、方向和横截面积。该模型在来自 146 名患者、使用两种商用 IVUS 系统采集的 12,242 帧标注图像上训练。我们使用分割精度和斑块相关临床指标评估性能，包括 Dice/IoU、边界测量（95HD (mm)、ASSD）、拓扑违例率以及临床几何误差（dmax/dmin、角度和面积）。在我们的数据集上，GeoCat 实现了 0.93 的 Dice，将 95HD 降至 0.14 mm，拓扑违例率降至 1.0%。重要的是，它显著提升了几何保真度，直径误差为 0.13-0.16 mm，角度误差约 8 度，为可靠的斑块负荷量化提供了支持。

</details>


### [34] [Toward Training-Free Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection in 3D Medical Images: A Batch-Based Approach Using 2D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18749)
*Tai Le-Gia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CS3F，一种无需训练、基于批次的三维医学图像零样本异常检测框架，利用2D基础模型（如ViT）通过多轴向分解和切片级编码实现异常定位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法主要针对2D图像设计，直接扩展到3D医学体积受到大规模3D基础模型稀缺以及难以利用体积上下文信息的限制。

Method: CS3F将每个3D体积沿多个解剖轴分解，由2D ViT逐切片编码，通过池化相邻切片特征转化为局部体积令牌；基于跨被试互相似性计算异常分数（缺少相似令牌的赋予高异常分）；提出粗到细的令牌化策略以减少深度池化导致的病灶信号衰减。

Result: 在脑部MRI（转移瘤、胶质瘤、中风）和肺部CT上评估，结果表明冻结的2D基础模型可支持3D医学图像的异常定位，且细粒度令牌化的效果强烈依赖于病灶对比度和成像模态。

Conclusion: 冻结的2D基础模型能够有效用于3D医学图像的零样本异常检测，但细粒度令牌化的优势在低对比度病灶或特定模态下受限。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) is attractive for medical imaging because clinical systems must handle heterogeneous acquisition protocols, changing patient populations, and pathologies for which annotated training data may be unavailable. Most existing zero-shot anomaly detection methods are designed for 2D images, and their direct extension to 3D medical volumes is limited by the scarcity of large-scale volumetric foundation models or by the difficulty of utilizing volumetric context. We propose CS3F, a training-free batch-based framework for ZSAD in 3D medical images using 2D foundation models. Each volume is decomposed along multiple anatomical axes and encoded slice-wise by a 2D vision transformer. These are then converted into localized volumetric tokens by pooling neighboring slice features. Anomaly scores are obtained from cross-subject mutual similarity: tokens that lack close analogues in other subjects are assigned higher anomaly scores. To reduce the attenuation of focal lesion signals caused by depth pooling, we introduce a coarse-to-fine tokenization strategy that enables fine-resolution volumetric scoring without exhaustive matching. CS3F is evaluated on brain MRI across metastases, glioma, and stroke, as well as validated on lung CT to test generalizability beyond atlas-aligned brain MRI. The results show that frozen 2D foundation models can support anomaly localization in 3D medical images, and that the benefit of fine tokenization depends strongly on lesion contrast and imaging modality.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本异常检测在医学影像领域极具吸引力，因为临床系统需要处理异构的采集协议、变化的人群分布以及可能缺乏标注训练数据的病理类型。现有的大多数零样本异常检测方法针对2D图像设计，其直接扩展到3D医学体积受到大规模体积基础模型稀缺或难以利用体积上下文的限制。我们提出了CS3F，一个无需训练、基于批次的3D医学图像零样本异常检测框架，利用2D基础模型实现检测。每个体积沿多个解剖轴分解，并由2D视觉Transformer逐切片编码，随后通过池化相邻切片特征将其转换为局部体积令牌。异常分数通过跨被试互相似性获得：在其它被试中缺乏相近对应的令牌被赋予更高的异常分数。为减少深度池化引起的局灶病灶信号衰减，我们引入了一种粗到细的令牌化策略，该策略无需穷举匹配即可实现精细分辨率的体积评分。CS3F在脑部MRI的转移瘤、胶质瘤和中风任务上进行了评估，并在肺部CT上验证了其泛化能力，以测试超出标准空间对齐脑部MRI的表现。结果表明，冻结的2D基础模型能支持3D医学图像中的异常定位，且细粒度令牌化的优势强烈依赖于病灶对比度和成像模态。

</details>


### [35] [SMART: A Flexible, Interpretable, and Scalable Spatio-temporal Brain Atlas from High-Resolution Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18753)
*John Kalkhof,Boris Gutman,Emile d'Angremont,Daniel C. Alexander,Marco Lorenzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SMART提出了一种灵活、可解释且可扩展的时空脑图谱框架，通过学习连续疾病时间图谱解耦全局疾病动态与患者特定解剖表现，在阿尔茨海默病纵向MRI数据上实现了最先进的预测精度和时间一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时空图谱构建方法依赖黑盒生成模型，缺乏灵活性、可解释性差且难以扩展到高维数据。

Method: 学习连续疾病时间图谱，通过区域特异性微分方程建模全局疾病轨迹，并利用多尺度神经细胞自动机（Neural Cellular Automata）参数化的稠密微分同胚位移实现个体化解剖适应。

Result: 在ADNI-1/GO/2、OASIS-3、AIBL五个纵向MRI数据集（>1300名受试者）上，SMART产生了解剖上有意义的疾病进展预测，达到了最先进的预测精度，并相比对抗性生成模型和扩散模型基线具有更好的时间一致性。

Conclusion: 建立了一种灵活、可解释且可扩展的高维医学图像时间序列时空变化建模新范式。

Original Abstract: We introduce SMART, a framework for learning a flexible, interpretable, and scalable spatio-temporal brain atlas from longitudinal high-resolution 3D medical images. Existing approaches to spatio-temporal atlas construction rely on black-box generative models that lack flexibility, limit interpretability, and struggle to scale to high-dimensional data. SMART addresses these challenges by learning a continuous disease-time atlas that decouples global group-wise disease dynamics from their patient-specific anatomical manifestation. Guided by anatomically inspired priors, SMART models interpretable global trajectories of regional progression along a shared disease timeline through region-specific differential equations. Global trajectories are further personalized to individual anatomies via dense diffeomorphic displacements parameterized by a flexible and scalable multi-scale Neural Cellular Automata. Evaluated on five longitudinal MRI datasets in Alzheimer's disease (ADNI-1/GO/2, OASIS-3, AIBL; > 1,300 subjects), SMART produces anatomically meaningful predictions of disease progression and achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy and improved temporal consistency over adversarial and diffusion baselines. Our approach establishes a new paradigm for flexible, interpretable, and scalable modeling of spatio-temporal change in high-dimensional medical image time-series.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了SMART，一个用于从纵向高分辨率3D医学图像中学习灵活、可解释且可扩展的时空脑图谱的框架。现有的时空图谱构建方法依赖黑盒生成模型，缺乏灵活性、可解释性有限且难以扩展到高维数据。SMART通过学习一个连续的疾病时间图谱来应对这些挑战，该图谱将全局群体层面的疾病动态与患者特定的解剖表现解耦。在解剖学启发先验的引导下，SMART通过区域特异性微分方程沿共享疾病时间线对区域进展的可解释全局轨迹进行建模。全局轨迹进一步通过由灵活且可扩展的多尺度神经细胞自动机参数化的稠密微分同胚位移，个性化地适应个体解剖结构。在阿尔茨海默病的五个纵向MRI数据集（ADNI-1/GO/2、OASIS-3、AIBL；超过1300名受试者）上的评估表明，SMART产生了具有解剖学意义的疾病进展预测，实现了最先进的预测精度，并在时间一致性上优于对抗性生成模型和扩散基线模型。我们的方法为高维医学图像时间序列中时空变化的灵活、可解释且可扩展建模建立了新范式。

</details>


### [36] [SpectralDiT: Timestep-Conditioned Spectral Residual Correction for Flow-Matching DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18765)
*Jiayu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpectralDiT通过对流匹配扩散Transformer的MLP残差分支添加时间步条件化的频谱校正模块，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下，显著提升了图像生成质量（CIFAR-10 FID从20.78降至19.71；ImageNet-100相对FID降低8.7%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer（DiT）在残差更新中对低频和高频分量的处理缺乏区分，导致生成图像的频谱分布与真实图像存在差距（径向傅里叶频谱gap），从而限制了生成质量。

Method: 提出SpectralDiT模块，在DiT的MLP残差分支中引入时间步条件化的频谱校正。该模块将每个残差更新在patch-token网格上分解为低频和高频分量，然后学习一个零初始化的加性门控，使模型初始阶段与基线DiT保持一致，随后逐步学习频谱校正。

Result: 在CIFAR-10像素空间生成中，patch size=1时FID从20.78降至19.71，径向傅里叶频谱gap减小；在ImageNet-100潜空间生成中，仅增加0.6%理论FLOPs和1.36%参数，在无分类器引导（CFG 2.0）下实现8.7%的相对FID降低。所有结果均基于5个种子的平均值。

Conclusion: 在扩散Transformer的残差分支中引入轻量级频谱校正，能以极小的额外计算代价有效提升生成质量，且消融实验和门控可视化显示模型学习到了稳定的、逐模块特化的频谱校正模式。

Original Abstract: We propose SpectralDiT, a lightweight modification to flow-matching Diffusion Transformers that adds timestep-conditioned spectral correction to the MLP residual branch. The module decomposes each residual update into low- and high-frequency components on the patch-token grid, then learns a zero-initialized additive gate so the model initially matches the baseline DiT. On CIFAR-10 pixel-space generation, SpectralDiT improves FID from 20.78 to 19.71 at patch size 1 and reduces the radial Fourier spectrum gap. Furthermore, we scale our method to latent diffusion on ImageNet-100. With 0.6% additional theoretical FLOPs and 1.36% additional parameters, SpectralDiT improves latent flow-matching, achieving an 8.7% relative FID reduction under classifier-free guidance (CFG 2.0). All reported results are averaged over five seeds. Ablations and gate visualizations on CIFAR-10 reveal stable block-specific spectral correction patterns.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了SpectralDiT，一种对流匹配扩散Transformer的轻量级改进，它在MLP残差分支中添加了时间步条件化的频谱校正。该模块将每个残差更新在patch-token网格上分解为低频和高频分量，然后学习一个零初始化的加性门控，使模型在初始阶段与基线DiT保持一致。在CIFAR-10像素空间生成任务中，SpectralDiT在patch size为1时将FID从20.78提升至19.71，并缩小了径向傅里叶频谱gap。此外，我们将该方法扩展到ImageNet-100上的潜空间扩散。在仅增加0.6%的理论FLOPs和1.36%参数的情况下，SpectralDiT改进了潜空间流匹配，在无分类器引导（CFG 2.0）下实现了8.7%的相对FID降低。所有报告结果均为五个种子的平均值。CIFAR-10上的消融实验和门控可视化揭示了稳定的、逐模块特化的频谱校正模式。

</details>


### [37] [SAMA: Semantic Anchor-aligned Augmentation for Unified Low-Resource Multimodal Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18780)
*Quanjiang Guo,Chong Mu,Jiazhou Pan,Ming Jia,Ling Tian,Hui Gao,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的多模态数据增强框架SAMA，通过语义锚点对齐、协作多专家多模态大模型、锚点保持扩散和双约束过滤，在MNER、MRE、MEE三个任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MIE任务受限于数据稀缺，而现有数据增强方法存在跨模态对齐粗糙和任务特定设计碎片化的问题，未能利用共享语义知识。

Method: SAMA包含四个模块：(1) 从真实标签构建结构化语义锚点；(2) 协作多专家多模态大模型(CME-MLLM)，含通用适配器和任务特定适配器生成合规文本；(3) 锚点保持扩散机制，使用锚点加权提示和潜在条件保持语义锚点多样化视觉背景；(4) 双约束过滤模块，基于跨模态一致性和锚点保真度选择合成样本。

Result: 在MNER、MRE、MEE的基准数据集上，在全监督和低资源设置下均一致优于最先进的数据增强基线方法。

Conclusion: SAMA作为统一框架有效解决了多模态数据增强中的跨模态对齐和任务碎片化问题，展示了其通用性、鲁棒性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Information Extraction (MIE)-covering tasks such as Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (MNER), Relation Extraction (MRE), and Event Extraction (MEE)-is essential for understanding multimedia content but remains constrained by severe data scarcity. Although data augmentation is a promising remedy, existing approaches are impeded by coarse cross-modal alignment and fragmented, task-specific designs that fail to exploit shared semantic knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Semantic Anchor-aligned Multimodal Augmentation (SAMA), a unified framework for generating high-fidelity, task-aware synthetic data. SAMA constructs structured semantic anchors from ground-truth labels to guide a Collaborative Multi-Experts Multimodal Large Language Model (CME-MLLM), which integrates a Universal Adapter for shared semantics with Task-Specific Adapters to produce diverse yet constraint-compliant textual samples. For image synthesis, SAMA employs an Anchor-Preserving Diffusion mechanism that uses anchor-weighted prompts and latent conditioning to maintain critical semantic anchors while diversifying visual contexts. To eliminate the need for manual verification, SAMA further introduces a Dual-Constraint Filtering module that selects synthetic samples based on both cross-modal consistency and anchor fidelity. Extensive experiments across benchmark datasets for MNER, MRE, and MEE demonstrate that SAMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art augmentation baselines under both fully supervised and low-resource settings, underscoring its versatility, robustness, and effectiveness.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态信息提取（MIE）——涵盖多模态命名实体识别（MNER）、关系提取（MRE）和事件提取（MEE）等任务——对于理解多媒体内容至关重要，但受到严重的数据稀缺问题的制约。尽管数据增强是一种有前景的解决方案，但现有方法受限于粗糙的跨模态对齐以及碎片化的任务特定设计，未能充分利用共享的语义知识。为克服这些限制，我们引入了语义锚点对齐的多模态增强（SAMA），这是一个用于生成高保真、任务感知合成数据的统一框架。SAMA从真实标签中构建结构化语义锚点，以指导协作多专家多模态大语言模型（CME-MLLM），该模型集成了用于共享语义的通用适配器和用于生成多样化且符合约束的文本样本的任务特定适配器。在图像合成方面，SAMA采用锚点保持扩散机制，利用锚点加权提示和潜在条件来维持关键语义锚点，同时多样化视觉上下文。为消除人工验证的需求，SAMA进一步引入了双约束过滤模块，基于跨模态一致性和锚点保真度选择合成样本。在MNER、MRE和MEE基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SAMA在全监督和低资源设置下均一致优于最先进的数据增强基线，证明了其通用性、鲁棒性和有效性。

</details>


### [38] [SCR-Guided Difficulty-Aware Optimization for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18783)
*Yunus Sevim,Behçet Uğur Töreyin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REEM，一种轻量级的SCR引导的困难感知优化框架，通过将信杂比作为物理意义的可见性先验融入训练过程，在不增加参数量和推理开销的情况下，显著提升红外弱小目标检测性能，尤其是在低可见性条件下。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 红外弱小目标检测面临严重背景杂波、低对比度和弱空间响应的挑战，传统的基于几何重叠的损失函数（如IoU）不足以有效表征低可见性目标的检测质量，亟需一种物理上有意义的优化指导机制。

Method: REEM方法在训练阶段计算输入图像的局部信杂比（SCR）作为真值，并将其通过可微调制方式作用于soft-IoU学习信号，实现对低可见性目标的重点优化。该方法无需修改网络架构、不引入额外参数，且推理行为与基线完全一致。

Result: 在基于U-Net的MSHNet上集成REEM后，实验表明在IoU、检测概率（Pd）上取得一致提升，同时显著降低虚警（FA），尤其在低可见性困难场景下改进更为明显。

Conclusion: SCR引导的困难感知优化为红外弱小目标检测提供了一种物理上有依据的有效补充方案，能够超越传统基于重叠的优化目标。

Original Abstract: Infrared small target detection remains challenging due to severe background clutter, low contrast, and weak spatial responses where geometric overlap alone is insufficient to characterize detection quality. In this work, we propose REEM (Reweighted Explicit-visibility Enhanced Modulation), a lightweight SCR-guided difficulty-aware optimization framework that incorporates Signal-to-Clutter Ratio (SCR) as a physically meaningful visibility prior during training. Instead of modifying the network architecture or directly optimizing SCR, REEM computes a ground-truth local SCR from the input image and applies a differentiable modulation to the soft-IoU learning signal, emphasizing low-visibility targets while preserving stable optimization and identical inference behavior. REEM is integrated into a U-Net-based MSHNet without introducing additional parameters, architectural modifications, or inference-time overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over the baseline, achieving higher IoU and detection probability (Pd) together with substantially reduced false alarms (FA), particularly under challenging low-visibility conditions. These results suggest that SCR-guided difficulty-aware optimization provides an effective and physically grounded complement to conventional overlap-based objectives for infrared small target detection. The code is available at https://github. com/yall-in-one/Reemm.

中文摘要翻译: 红外弱小目标检测仍然具有挑战性，原因在于严重的背景杂波、低对比度和弱空间响应，此时仅凭几何重叠不足以表征检测质量。本文提出REEM（重加权显式可见性增强调制），一种轻量级的SCR引导的困难感知优化框架，在训练过程中将信杂比（SCR）作为具有物理意义的可见性先验。REEM不修改网络架构或直接优化SCR，而是从输入图像计算真实的局部SCR，并对soft-IoU学习信号施加可微调制，从而在保持稳定优化和完全一致的推理行为的同时，强调低可见性目标。REEM被集成到基于U-Net的MSHNet中，无需引入额外参数、架构修改或推理开销。大量实验表明，REEM在基线基础上取得了一致改进，实现了更高的IoU和检测概率（Pd），同时大幅降低了虚警（FA），尤其是在具有挑战性的低可见性条件下。这些结果表明，SCR引导的困难感知优化为红外弱小目标检测中传统的基于重叠的优化目标提供了一种物理上有依据的有效补充。代码已开源：https://github.com/yall-in-one/Reemm。

</details>


### [39] [Learned Radius Estimation for UDF-Based Point Cloud Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18787)
*Eito Ogawa,Hiroshi Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种学习型连续支持半径选择器，插入冻结的UDF骨干网络，提升点云表面重建精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统局部面片UDF方法依赖固定或一维曲率启发式选取的支持半径，无法捕捉异质局部几何结构，导致重建精度受限。

Method: 提出一个学习型逐查询连续支持半径选择器，插入到冻结的LoSF-UDF骨干网络中；通过缓存UDF误差曲线的抛物线插值获得离网格目标半径来训练该选择器。

Result: 实验表明该方法在细尺度重建精度上取得改进。

Conclusion: 学习型连续支持半径选择器能够有效提升UDF方法的细尺度点云表面重建精度。

Original Abstract: Surface reconstruction from point clouds is important for consumer-grade 3D capture, including AR/VR and indoor scanning. Local-patch Unsigned Distance Field (UDF) methods are lightweight and generalizable, but their accuracy depends on the support radius, traditionally fixed or selected by a one-dimensional curvature heuristic that cannot capture heterogeneous local geometry. We propose a learned per-query radius selector that predicts a continuous support radius and plugs into a frozen LoSF-UDF backbone. The selector is trained using off-grid target radii obtained by parabolic interpolation of cached UDF error curves. Experiments show improved fine-scale reconstruction accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 从点云中重建表面对消费级3D捕捉（包括AR/VR和室内扫描）至关重要。局部面片无符号距离场（UDF）方法轻量且可泛化，但其精度依赖于支持半径——传统上采用固定的或由一维曲率启发式选取的方式，无法捕捉异质的局部几何结构。我们提出一个学习型逐查询半径选择器，能够预测连续的支持半径，并插入到冻结的LoSF-UDF骨干网络中。该选择器通过缓存UDF误差曲线的抛物线插值得到的离网格目标半径进行训练。实验表明该方法在细尺度重建精度上取得了改进。

</details>


### [40] [HandwritingAgent: Language-Driven Handwriting Synthesis in Scalable Vector Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18788)
*Jaward Sesay,Yue Yu,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandwritingAgent是一种基于大型推理模型的语言驱动智能体，无需风格特定训练即可通过SVG格式生成自然手写笔划序列，在手写模仿、识别、多语言合成及复杂数学表达式生成等任务上达到或超越现有最优方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手写合成方法受限于风格特定的架构设计、对大规模数据集的强依赖、高昂的计算成本，且缺乏通过自然语言对书写风格进行灵活控制的能力。

Method: 利用大型推理模型对目标手写字形进行几何分析，并在离散网格画布环境中以自回归方式生成笔划序列，输出为可缩放矢量图形(SVG)格式。生成过程可根据对话或非对话模式的文本输入以及参考手写风格图像进行条件控制。

Result: 在手写模仿、识别、多语言手写合成以及复杂数学和科学表达式生成等多样化任务中，HandwritingAgent匹配或超越了当前最优的生成式手写模型，同时提供了更高效、可控且泛化性更强的合成方法。

Conclusion: HandwritingAgent提出了一种无需风格特定训练、语言驱动的高效手写合成方法，在多种任务上表现优异，为手写生成领域提供了一种更通用、可控且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Teaching machines to emulate natural handwriting styles remains an open challenge, as it requires synthesizing stroke sequences that dynamically vary in shape, texture, pressure and script - not only across individuals, but also within a single person's handwriting. Attempts at this challenge have largely explored deep learning methods in both online and offline settings. However, these approaches are often constrained by style-specific architectural choices, heavy reliance on large datasets, high compute costs, and a lack of flexible control over writing styles through natural language. To this end, we introduce HandwritingAgent, a language-driven agent that can synthesize natural handwriting sequences directly in Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) format with no need for style-specific training. The agent leverages a large reasoning model to geometrically analyse and autoregressively generate target handwritten glyphs as stroke sequences in a discrete grid canvas environment. Generation is conditioned on texts provided in either conversational or non-conversational mode, along with a reference handwriting-style image. Experiments on diverse handwriting tasks spanning imitation, recognition, multi-lingual handwriting synthesis, and generation of complex handwritten maths and science expressions indicate substantial improvement in performance, with HandwritingAgent matching or surpassing state-of-the-art generative handwriting models, while providing a more efficient, controllable, and generalizable synthesis method.

中文摘要翻译: 教会机器模仿自然手写风格仍然是一个开放的挑战，因为它需要合成在手形、纹理、压力和书写方式上动态变化的笔划序列——不仅在不同个体之间，甚至在同一个人的手写中也存在差异。针对这一挑战的尝试主要探索了在线和离线设置下的深度学习方法。然而，这些方法通常受限于风格特定的架构选择、对大规模数据集的高度依赖、高昂的计算成本，以及缺乏通过自然语言对书写风格进行灵活控制的能力。为此，我们提出了HandwritingAgent，一种语言驱动的智能体，它能够直接在可缩放矢量图形（SVG）格式下合成自然手写笔划序列，无需进行风格特定的训练。该智能体利用大型推理模型对目标手写字形进行几何分析，并在离散网格画布环境中以自回归方式生成笔划序列。生成过程受对话或非对话模式提供的文本以及参考手写风格图像的条件约束。在手写模仿、识别、多语言手写合成以及复杂数学和科学表达式生成等多样化手写任务上的实验表明，HandwritingAgent的性能有显著提升，匹配或超越了当前最优的生成式手写模型，同时提供了一种更高效、可控且泛化性更强的合成方法。

</details>


### [41] [Fuzzy-Geometric Branch-Point Modeling for Structure-Aware Augmentation of Handwritten Chinese Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18793)
*Dongbin Jiao,Yibo Lyu,Qiulu Wei,Fuxiang Lu,Shengcai Liu,Shi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模糊几何驱动的结构感知数据增强框架(FGSA)，用于解决高安全性身份认证场景中手写汉字识别面临的数据稀缺和结构失真问题。通过将分支点建模为模糊集、构建连续隶属度场、自适应优化和无监督骨架解耦，并结合参数化三次贝塞尔重建与多策略扰动生成运动学对齐样本。在CASIA-HWDB1.1、ChiSig和自建LZUSig数据集上，FGSA显著降低了词错误率，在任务增益、结构保真度和判别性特征保持之间实现了稳健权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 手写汉字识别在高安全性身份认证场景中面临数据稀缺和结构失真的双重限制。现有数据增强方法在处理复杂汉字结构时（如笔画交叉、连笔和锐角转折）会导致拓扑和形态损伤，传统分支点检测方法不可靠，亟需一种能在保持结构保真度的同时生成多样化样本的增强方法。

Method: 提出模糊几何驱动的结构感知增强框架(FGSA)。首先将骨架空间中的分支点建模为模糊集，通过融合拓扑邻域证据和方向场散度构建连续的分支点隶属度场。其次利用无监督替代目标自适应优化隶属度场，实现无需人工标注的鲁棒笔画解耦。最后通过参数化三次贝塞尔重建和多策略扰动生成运动学对齐样本，在结构保真度和样本多样性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在CASIA-HWDB1.1、ChiSig和自建大规模高挑战性数据集LZUSig上进行实验，FGSA显著降低了词错误率(ΔWER)，相较于基线方法取得了最优的识别增益。该方法在任务增益、结构保真度和判别性特征保持之间实现了稳健的权衡。

Conclusion: FGSA框架通过模糊几何驱动的结构感知策略有效解决了手写汉字识别中的数据稀缺和结构失真问题，为手写数据增强提供了一种高度可控的解决方案，在保持结构完整性的同时提升了识别性能。

Original Abstract: Data scarcity and structural distortion significantly limit handwriting recognition in high-security authentication. Existing augmentation methods often cause topological and morphological damage, particularly when processing complex Chinese characters where stroke intersections, ligatures, and sharp turns render traditional branch-point detection unreliable. To address this, this paper proposes a fuzzy geometry-driven structure-aware (FGSA) augmentation framework. We model branch points as fuzzy sets within the skeleton space, constructing a continuous branch-point membership field by integrating topological neighborhood evidence with direction field divergence. This membership field is adaptively optimized via an unsupervised surrogate objective, enabling robust stroke decoupling without manual annotation. Finally, kinematically-aligned samples are synthesized through parameterized cubic Bézier reconstruction and multi-strategy perturbations, ensuring a balance between structural fidelity and sample diversity. Moreover, we establish LZUSig, a large-scale, highly challenging dataset specifically dedicated to fine-grained structural degradation in Chinese handwritten signatures. Extensive experiments on CASIA-HWDB1.1, ChiSig, and LZUSig demonstrate that FGSA significantly reduces the word-level error rate ($Δ$WER), achieving optimal recognition gains over the compared baselines. More importantly, it strikes a robust trade-off among task gain, structural fidelity, and discriminative feature preservation, offering a highly controllable solution for handwriting augmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 数据稀缺和结构失真严重限制了高安全性身份认证中的手写识别。现有增强方法通常会导致拓扑和形态损伤，特别是在处理复杂汉字时，笔画交叉、连笔和锐角转折使得传统的分支点检测不可靠。为解决这一问题，本文提出了一种模糊几何驱动的结构感知增强框架(FGSA)。我们将骨架空间中的分支点建模为模糊集，通过融合拓扑邻域证据与方向场散度，构建连续的分支点隶属度场。该隶属度场通过无监督替代目标进行自适应优化，实现了无需人工标注的鲁棒笔画解耦。最后，通过参数化三次贝塞尔重建和多策略扰动合成运动学对齐样本，确保结构保真度与样本多样性之间的平衡。此外，我们建立了LZUSig——一个专门针对中文手写签名细粒度结构退化的大规模高挑战性数据集。在CASIA-HWDB1.1、ChiSig和LZUSig上的大量实验表明，FGSA显著降低了词错误率(ΔWER)，相较于对比基线取得了最优的识别增益。更重要的是，它在任务增益、结构保真度和判别性特征保持之间实现了稳健的权衡，为手写增强提供了一种高度可控的解决方案。

</details>


### [42] [Where Will They Go? Modelling Multimodal Pedestrian Manoeuvres from Ego-centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18824)
*Yuxuan Xie,Nicolas Pugeault,Chongfeng Wei,Hubert P. H. Shum,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPM，一种模式感知的轨迹预测框架，通过分别建模行人过街与不过街两种行为模式的未来轨迹分布，解决第一人称视角下行人轨迹预测中的多模态问题，在PIE和JAAD数据集上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有随机预测器通常从单一单峰分布中采样多个未来轨迹，可能产生位于不同运动模式之间的次优"混合模式"轨迹，在真实场景中不合理。因此需要一种能够显式建模不同语义模式的轨迹预测方法。

Method: 提出MMPM框架，包含两个模块：(1) 行为感知的行人交互模块(PIM)，通过引入注视、头部和手势信息联合捕获行人-车辆和行人-环境交互；(2) 基于CVAE的模式感知轨迹预测器(MTP)，分别对过街和不过街两种模式的未来轨迹分布进行建模。使用查询解码器在解码过程中强制执行模式一致性。MTP为模型无关模块，可集成到BiTrap-NP和SGNet-ED等现有框架中。

Result: 在PIE和JAAD数据集上的实验表明，该方法超越了当前最先进的基线方法。此外，引入了一种数据驱动的验证协议，将预测结果与时空一致的真实轨迹匹配，在帧级位移误差上较前人工作有改进。

Conclusion: MMPM通过显式建模行人的过街/不过街两种行为模式，有效解决了第一人称视角下行人轨迹预测的多模态问题，且MTP模块具有模型无关性，可灵活集成到现有预测框架中提升性能。

Original Abstract: Pedestrian trajectory prediction from an ego-centric camera is challenging since it depends on complex interactions with vehicles and scene context, as well as the intention of the pedestrian. By modelling correlation and intent from the historical and future trajectories of the pedestrian, it will usually result in a multimodal (i.e. multiple modes) distribution. Existing stochastic predictors often sample multiple futures from a single unimodal distribution, which can yield sub-optimal 'mixed-mode' trajectories that lie between distinct motion patterns and become implausible in real scenes. In this paper, we propose MMPM, a mode-aware framework that separately models future trajectory distributions into semantically meaningful modes based on the pedestrian's crossing behavior. MMPM consists of two modules: behavior-aware Pedestrian Interaction Module (PIM) that jointly captures pedestrian-vehicle and pedestrian-environment interactions by introducing gaze, head and hand gesture, and a CVAE-based Mode-aware Trajectory Predictor (MTP) module to model the future trajectory distributions on two modes, crossing and non-crossing the road, separately. A query-based decoder further enforces mode consistency during decoding. Experiments on PIE and JAAD datasets show that our method surpasses state-of-the-art baselines. Our proposed MTP is model-agnostic, which can be integrated into existing frameworks such as BiTrap-NP and SGNet-ED to further improve future trajectory prediction performance. We additionally introduce a data-driven validation protocol that matches predictions to spatio-temporally consistent ground-truth trajectories, demonstrating improved frame-wise displacement errors over previous work.

中文摘要翻译: 从第一人称视角摄像头进行行人轨迹预测具有挑战性，因为它依赖于与车辆和场景上下文的复杂交互，以及行人的意图。通过从行人的历史和未来轨迹中建模相关性和意图，通常会导致多模态（即多个模式）分布。现有的随机预测器通常从单一单峰分布中采样多个未来轨迹，这可能产生位于不同运动模式之间的次优"混合模式"轨迹，在真实场景中不合理。在本文中，我们提出MMPM，一种模式感知框架，基于行人的过街行为将未来轨迹分布分别建模为语义上有意义的模式。MMPM由两个模块组成：行为感知的行人交互模块（PIM），通过引入注视、头部和手势联合捕获行人-车辆和行人-环境交互；以及基于CVAE的模式感知轨迹预测器（MTP）模块，分别对过街和不过街两种模式的未来轨迹分布进行建模。基于查询的解码器进一步在解码过程中强制执行模式一致性。在PIE和JAAD数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法超越了当前最先进的基线方法。我们提出的MTP是模型无关的，可以集成到BiTrap-NP和SGNet-ED等现有框架中，以进一步提高未来轨迹预测性能。此外，我们引入了一种数据驱动的验证协议，将预测结果与时空一致的真实轨迹进行匹配，证明了相较于前人工作在帧级位移误差上的改进。

</details>


### [43] [DreamReg: Belief-Driven World Model for 2D-3D Ultrasound Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18825)
*Luoyao Kang,Yuelin Zhang,Jiwei Shan,Haifan Gong,Qingpeng Ding,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DreamReg，一种基于信念驱动的世界模型框架，用于术中2D超声切片与术前3D体积的实时配准，通过潜在信念状态和内部想象力机制实现持续且鲁棒的配准优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有2D-3D超声配准方法多为单步或短视的，难以随时间积累证据，也无法模拟医生根据屏幕反馈调整探头运动的临床行为，导致在部分可观测、散斑噪声及动作依赖的采集条件下配准效果不佳。

Method: DreamReg构建了一个信念驱动的世界模型框架，维护一个编码历史观测与位姿信息的潜在信念状态，并通过学习到的动力学模型在新切片到达时持续更新变换。训练时暴露于模拟临床扫描的探头运动轨迹；推理时通过内部想象力——展开世界模型模拟候选探头运动及其预测观测——来迭代收敛到精确的刚体变换。

Result: 在CAMUS和u-RegPro数据集上，DreamReg在实时引导场景下相比现有最先进方法展现出更优的鲁棒性和具有竞争力的配准精度。

Conclusion: DreamReg通过信念状态积累与内部想象力机制，有效解决了实时2D-3D超声配准中部分可观测和运动依赖的挑战，为手术导航提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Ultrasound (US) is widely used for surgical navigation, yet real-time registration between intraoperative 2D slices and preoperative 3D volumes remains challenging due to partial observability, speckle noise, and the action-dependent US acquisition. Existing methods are one-shot or short-horizon, making it hard for them to gather evidence over time or capture how surgeons adjust probe motion based on on-screen feedback. We propose DreamReg, a belief-driven world-model framework that formulates 2D-3D registration as belief updating over rigid transformations. DreamReg maintains a latent belief state that summarizes past observations and poses information, and continuously refines the transformation through learned dynamics as new slices arrive. During training, DreamReg is exposed to probe-motion trajectories that mimic clinical scanning behavior and learns to update its belief by conditioning pose refinement on the current US observation. During inference, DreamReg refines registration via internal imagination: it rolls out the learned world model to simulate candidate probe motions and their predicted observations, and integrates these imagined outcomes to converge to an accurate rigid transformation. Experiments on CAMUS and u-RegPro datasets demonstrate improved robustness and competitive registration accuracy for real-time guidance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 超声（US）被广泛用于手术导航，然而由于部分可观测性、散斑噪声以及依赖于动作的超声采集，术中2D切片与术前3D体积之间的实时配准仍然充满挑战。现有方法多为单步或短视的，难以随时间积累证据，也无法捕捉外科医生如何根据屏幕反馈调整探头运动。我们提出DreamReg，一种信念驱动的世界模型框架，将2D-3D配准形式化为关于刚体变换的信念更新过程。DreamReg维护一个潜在信念状态，该状态汇总了历史观测和位姿信息，并在新切片到达时通过学习到的动力学模型持续细化变换。训练期间，DreamReg暴露于模拟临床扫描行为的探头运动轨迹，并通过将位姿细化条件于当前US观测来学习更新其信念。推理期间，DreamReg通过内部想象力来优化配准：它展开学习到的世界模型以模拟候选探头运动及其预测观测，并将这些想象结果进行整合，从而收敛到精确的刚体变换。在CAMUS和u-RegPro数据集上的实验表明，与最先进方法相比，DreamReg在实时引导场景下具有更优的鲁棒性和具有竞争力的配准精度。

</details>


### [44] [Rethinking Air-Ground Collaboration: A Progressive Cross-Task Benchmark and Socialized Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18841)
*Zhoupeng Guo,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Xinjie Yao,Ruipu Zhao,Boan Tao,Yiming Sun,Zhen Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空地渐进协作（AGPC）基准和社交化协同感知（SCP）框架，通过双层级路由器（DLR）实现跨视角、跨任务的渐进式协作，解决空地感知中异构性导致的负迁移问题，在745K+视频帧上验证了任务条件协作优于统一融合。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空地协同感知研究将协作简化为单任务跨视角融合，忽略了定位、目标关联和细粒度解析之间的功能依赖关系；此外，空地视角的异构性（几何、尺度、遮挡差异）导致统一特征共享易产生负迁移。

Method: 构建AGPC基准（745K+时空对齐视频帧），提出SCP框架：从空中全局定位到地面目标关联再到身份感知解析的粗到细渐进协作；核心模块为双层级路由器（DLR），在输入端解耦多尺度专家选择，在输出端进行任务条件调制，实现选择性跨视角/跨任务交互并抑制有害干扰。

Result: SCP在AGPC基准上取得3.73%的协同进化增益，下游平均性能提升7.86%，验证了任务条件协作优于统一融合。

Conclusion: 任务条件协作比统一融合更适用于异构的空地感知场景；渐进式跨任务协作能有效缓解负迁移问题。

Original Abstract: Air-ground collaborative perception is crucial for robust visual understanding in real-world dynamic environments. However, existing studies typically formulate collaboration as single-task cross-view fusion, overlooking the functional dependencies among localization, target association, and fine-grained parsing. In addition, the heterogeneous nature of aerial and ground views introduces substantial geometric, scale, and occlusion discrepancies, making uniform feature sharing vulnerable to negative transfer. To tackle these issues, we model air-ground perception as a progressive cross-task collaboration task and construct the Air-Ground Progressive Collaboration (AGPC) benchmark, a spatio-temporally aligned benchmark comprising more than 745K raw video frames. Built upon this benchmark, we propose Socialized Co-Perception (SCP), a coarse-to-fine framework that organizes collaboration progressively from aerial global localization to ground target association and identity-aware parsing. Its core module, the Dual-Layer Router (DLR), decouples input-side multi-scale expert selection from output-side task-conditioned modulation, enabling selective cross-view and cross-task interaction while suppressing harmful interference. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of SCP. It achieves a 3.73\% coevolutionary gain and a 7.86\% improvement in average downstream performance. These results show that task-conditioned collaboration is more effective than uniform fusion for heterogeneous air-ground perception. The code is available at https://github.com/g1136639260-spec/AGSCP.

中文摘要翻译: 空地协同感知对于现实动态环境中的鲁棒视觉理解至关重要。然而，现有研究通常将协作建模为单任务跨视角融合，忽略了定位、目标关联和细粒度解析之间的功能依赖关系。此外，空中与地面视角的异构性带来了显著的几何、尺度和遮挡差异，使得统一特征共享容易受到负迁移的影响。为解决这些问题，我们将空地感知建模为渐进式跨任务协作任务，并构建了空地渐进协作（AGPC）基准——一个包含超过745K原始视频帧的时空对齐基准。在此基准基础上，我们提出了社交化协同感知（SCP），一个从粗到细的框架，将协作从空中全局定位逐步组织到地面目标关联和身份感知解析。其核心模块——双层级路由器（DLR），将输入端的多尺度专家选择与输出端的任务条件调制解耦，实现了选择性的跨视角和跨任务交互，同时抑制有害干扰。大量实验证明了SCP的有效性。它取得了3.73%的协同进化增益和7.86%的下游平均性能提升。这些结果表明，对于异构的空地感知，任务条件协作比统一融合更为有效。代码已开源：https://github.com/g1136639260-spec/AGSCP。

</details>


### [45] [From Bounding Boxes to Visual Reasoning: An On-Policy Data Annotation Tool for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18846)
*Like Zhang,Runliang Niu,Shiqi Wang,Xiyu Hu,Qianli Xing,Pan Wang,Qingzu He,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScreenAnnotator 是一个开源标注工具，通过统一原子模式、在线策略标注循环和模板驱动的多任务数据合成，解决了视觉-语言模型在结构化视觉推理中的数据标注瓶颈，显著提升标注效率与模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据标注工具无法满足视觉-语言模型对空间坐标、开放词汇描述、结构化属性和拓扑关系统一表示的需求，存在表达力有限、标注-训练严重解耦、数据可重用性差三大系统性瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出统一标注原子模式，将空间、语义和结构基元绑定为单一单元；2) 实现嵌入贝叶斯标注验证器(BAV)的在线策略标注循环；3) 设计模板驱动的多任务数据合成过程，将静态原子动态转化为多样化多维推理任务，消除冗余重标注。

Result: 在线策略循环使流程图标注接受率接近100%，GUI截图标注接受率达77%，同时随标注数据积累逐步降低单图标注时间。在流程图场景下，微调VLM获得76.1%的平均准确率，绝对值提升35.1个百分点。

Conclusion: ScreenAnnotator通过统一标注原子、在线策略验证和模板驱动合成，有效弥合了VLM结构化推理中的数据基础设施缺口，显著提升了标注效率和模型性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are rapidly advancing toward sophisticated grounded structured visual reasoning. Training models for such advanced capabilities demands a new genre of data that seamlessly unifies spatial coordinates, open-vocabulary descriptions, structured attributes, and topological relationships into a singular representation. However, existing data annotation tools fundamentally fail to meet these intricate demands, suffering from three systematic bottlenecks: limited expressiveness, severe annotation-training decoupling, and poor data reusability. To bridge this infrastructure gap, we introduce an open-source annotation tool, ScreenAnnotator. First, we define a unified annotation atom schema that binds spatial, semantic, and structural primitives into a single unit. Second, we implement an on-policy annotation loop embedded with a Bayesian Annotation Verifier (BAV). Finally, we design a template-driven multi-task data synthesis process dynamically transforms static atoms into diverse multi-dimensional reasoning tasks, eliminating redundant re-annotation. The on-policy loop drives the annotation accept rate to nearly 100% on flowcharts and 77% on GUI screenshots, while steadily reducing per-image annotation time as labeled data accumulate. In the flowchart scenario, fine-tuning a VLM yields 76.1% average accuracy, which is a 35.1% point absolute gain. Our code is available at: https://github.com/WnQinm/Annotator.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型正快速向复杂的结构化视觉推理演进。训练具备此类高级能力的模型需要一种新型数据，能够将空间坐标、开放词汇描述、结构化属性和拓扑关系无缝统一到单一表示中。然而，现有数据标注工具从根本上无法满足这些复杂需求，存在三大系统性瓶颈：表达力有限、标注-训练严重解耦以及数据可重用性差。为弥合这一基础设施缺口，我们推出了开源标注工具ScreenAnnotator。首先，我们定义了一个统一标注原子模式，将空间、语义和结构基元绑定为单一单元。其次，我们实现了一个嵌入贝叶斯标注验证器的在线策略标注循环。最后，我们设计了一个模板驱动的多任务数据合成过程，动态地将静态原子转化为多样化的多维推理任务，消除了冗余的重标注。在线策略循环使流程图上的标注接受率接近100%，GUI截图上的标注接受率达77%，同时随着标注数据的积累，单图标注时间稳步降低。在流程图场景中，微调视觉-语言模型达到了76.1%的平均准确率，绝对值提升了35.1个百分点。我们的代码开源在：https://github.com/WnQinm/Annotator。

</details>


### [46] [Quantification of Uncertainty with Adversarial Models in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18860)
*Hana Jebril,Thomas Pinetz,Günter Klambauer,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QUAM-SM，一种基于目标对抗搜索的后验框架，用于医学图像的不确定性量化，通过识别“对抗脆弱”像素来区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，在可靠性和边界敏感性上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准深度学习模型在校准上存在不足，在病理边界等微妙区域产生过度自信的预测，掩盖了潜在的不稳定性，无法满足临床治疗规划和手术干预对可靠像素级不确定性量化的需求。

Method: 提出QUAM-SM后验框架，利用目标对抗搜索识别“对抗脆弱”像素，通过主动寻找能暴露预测不稳定的扰动，突出决策最易被翻转的区域，并解耦认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: 在两个具有多专家标注的公开数据集上，QUAM-SM在可靠性和边界敏感性方面均优于标准不确定性估计方法和近年来的新方法。

Conclusion: QUAM-SM通过对抗性搜索有效实现了像素级不确定性量化，提升了医学图像分析中关键边界区域的预测可靠性，有助于区分真实病理变化与伪影。

Original Abstract: Reliable pixel-level uncertainty quantification holds the potential to transform clinical workflows by enabling high-fidelity longitudinal monitoring and distinguishing true pathological changes from artifacts. Ideally, these models provide the stability required for critical treatment planning and surgical intervention. However, standard deep learning models often suffer from miscalibration, yielding overconfident predictions that mask underlying vulnerabilities at subtle pathological boundaries. To address this, we propose QUAM-SM, a post-hoc framework using targeted adversarial search to identify "adversarially fragile" pixels. By actively seeking perturbations that expose predictive instability, our method highlights regions where decisions are most vulnerable to being flipped. Importantly, the framework disentangles epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty. Experiments on two public datasets with multiple expert annotations demonstrate that QUAM-SM outperforms both standard and recent uncertainty estimation approaches in terms of reliability and boundary sensitivity. Code is available at https://github.com/HanaJebril/quam_sm

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的像素级不确定性量化有潜力通过实现高保真纵向监测、区分真实病理变化与伪影来变革临床工作流程。理想情况下，这些模型能够提供关键治疗规划和手术干预所需的稳定性。然而，标准深度学习模型通常存在校准不良的问题，在微妙的病理边界处产生过度自信的预测，掩盖了潜在的脆弱性。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了QUAM-SM，一种利用目标对抗搜索来识别"对抗脆弱"像素的后验框架。通过主动寻找暴露预测不稳定性的扰动，我们的方法突出了决策最易被翻转的区域。重要的是，该框架将认知不确定性与偶然不确定性进行了解耦。在两个具有多专家标注的公开数据集上的实验表明，QUAM-SM在可靠性和边界敏感性方面均优于标准及近年来的不确定性估计方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/HanaJebril/quam_sm

</details>


### [47] [URDF Synthesis from RGB-D Sequences via Differentiable Joint Inference and Energy-Consistent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18861)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KinemaForge是一种约束驱动的管线，能从RGB-D序列联合推断铰接物体的部件几何、关节拓扑和关节参数，并通过能量一致性验证生成可模拟的数字孪生URDF模型，在关节轴精度和仿真稳定性上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有铰接物体数字孪生重建存在两个关键问题：(i) 部件级几何重建与运动学参数估计相互解耦，导致整体性能受限；(ii) 恢复的模型常违反能量守恒等基本动力学不变量，在物理仿真器中回放URDF时出现漂移。

Method: 提出三组件管线：(1) 运动学约束图，将关节-部件关联编码为软边；(2) 可微分螺旋轴求解器，通过Featherstone铰接体算法从观测渲染反向传播到关节参数；(3) 能量残差损失，惩罚重建模型非物理的自由响应。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility五个类别和内部RGB-D基准上，KinemaForge将平均关节轴误差从4.52°降至2.83°（比PARIS改进37.4%，比Ditto改进46.6%），长时程仿真漂移降低64%，闭环操作成功率提升14.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将几何-运动学联合推理与能量一致性验证相结合，KinemaForge有效弥合了感知与仿真之间的鸿沟，生成的URDF模型具有更高的物理保真度和可仿真性。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready digital twins of articulated objects from sensor observations remains constrained by two persistent gaps: (i) part-level geometric reconstruction is decoupled from kinematic-parameter estimation, and (ii) the recovered models often violate basic dynamic invariants such as energy conservation, leading to drift when the URDF is replayed in physics simulators. We present KinemaForge, a constraint-driven pipeline that jointly infers part-level shape, joint topology, and joint parameters from short RGB-D sequences and validates the result against an energy-consistent verifier built on differentiable rigid-body dynamics. The pipeline introduces three components: a kinematic constraint graph that encodes joint-part incidences as soft edges; a differentiable screw-axis solver that backpropagates from rendered observations through Featherstone's articulated-body algorithm to joint parameters; and an energy residual loss that penalises non-physical free responses of the reconstructed model. Across five PartNet-Mobility categories and an internal RGB-D benchmark, KinemaForge reduces the average joint-axis error from 4.52 degrees to 2.83 degrees (-37.4%) over the strongest geometric baseline (PARIS) and from 5.30 degrees to 2.83 degrees (-46.6%) over the interaction-based Ditto baseline, lowers long-horizon simulation drift by 64% (vs. PARIS) over 50 s rollouts, and yields URDFs whose closed-loop manipulation success rate improves by 14.6 percentage points over Ditto in our preliminary evaluation. Code and reconstruction data will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 从传感器观测中重建可仿真的铰接物体数字孪生仍然受制于两个长期存在的鸿沟：(i) 部件级几何重建与运动学参数估计相互解耦，(ii) 恢复的模型经常违反能量守恒等基本动力学不变量，导致URDF在物理仿真器中回放时出现漂移。我们提出KinemaForge，一种约束驱动管线，能够从短RGB-D序列中联合推断部件级形状、关节拓扑和关节参数，并通过基于可微分刚体动力学构建的能量一致性验证器对结果进行验证。该管线引入三个组件：一个运动学约束图，将关节-部件关联编码为软边；一个可微分螺旋轴求解器，通过Featherstone铰接体算法从渲染观测反向传播到关节参数；以及一个能量残差损失，惩罚重建模型非物理的自由响应。在五个PartNet-Mobility类别和一个内部RGB-D基准上，KinemaForge将平均关节轴误差从4.52度降至2.83度（比最强几何基线PARIS改进37.4%），从5.30度降至2.83度（比基于交互的Ditto基线改进46.6%）；在50秒推演中将长时程仿真漂移降低64%（相较于PARIS）；在我们初步评估中，其生成的URDF的闭环操作成功率比Ditto提升14.6个百分点。代码和重建数据将在论文接收后发布。

</details>


### [48] [Learning to Distort: Weakly-Supervised Image Quality Transfer for Prostate DWI Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18869)
*YuCheng Tang,Wen Yan,Alexander Ng,Natasha Thorley,Pawel Rajwa,Yipei Wang,Aqua Asif,Clare Allen,Louise Dickinson,Francesco Giganti,David Atkinson,Shonit Punwani,Daniel Alexander,Shaheer Ullah Saeed,Veeru Kasivisvanathan,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督的图像质量迁移框架，利用图像级质量标签（扭曲vs未扭曲）指导生成过程，通过原型流匹配算法模拟真实的前列腺扩散加权成像中的磁敏感伪影，进而训练前向校正模型，实现无需成对数据的畸变校正。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单次激发的平面回波前列腺扩散加权成像常受几何畸变影响，而获取成对的畸变与无畸变临床扫描数据极为困难，现有无配对校正方法性能有限，因此亟需一种无需成对数据且能有效模拟真实伪影的校正方法。

Method: 提出弱监督图像质量迁移框架：首先利用图像级质量标签（畸变vs无畸变）在预训练特征空间中建立潜在质量原型；然后通过弱监督原型流匹配算法，将生成轨迹正则化地导向畸变原型，模拟出逼真的磁敏感伪影；最后利用合成的伪影-无伪影配对数据训练前向校正模型。

Result: 生成的图像成功模拟了真实临床伪影的诊断干扰，优于CycleGAN、UNIT-DDPM、OT-FM等现有无配对方法；在PI-RADS和Gleason评分分类的下游临床任务中，在分布内和外部数据集上均取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: 弱监督原型流匹配方法能够有效模拟真实畸变伪影，利用合成配对数据训练的校正模型在临床下游任务中显著优于现有无配对方法，为医学图像校正提供了一种实用的新范式。

Original Abstract: Single-shot echo-planar prostate diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) is frequently complicated by geometric distortions, which impact the ability to derive reliable diagnoses from such images. Developing automated correction methods is challenged by the absence of paired distorted and undistorted clinical scans. In this paper, we first propose a novel weakly-supervised image quality transfer (IQT) framework from undistorted to distorted images that utilizes image quality assessment (IQA) signals to supervise the transfer process. Unlike traditional methods that require expensive, voxel-wise paired data or resort to developing unpaired algorithms, our approach utilizes image-level quality labels (here, distorted vs. undistorted) to establish latent quality prototypes within a pre-trained feature space. Recognizing that simulating realistic distortions is more reliable than direct unpaired correction, we describe a weakly-supervised prototype flow matching algorithm to explicitly regularize generative trajectories towards distorted prototypes, producing realistic susceptibility artifacts that mimic clinical degradations. By synthesizing these realistic pairs, we enable a second IQT model to be trained in the forward direction for distortion correction. Experimental results demonstrate that our generated images successfully mimic the diagnostic interference of real-world artifacts, which leads to more capable distortion correction IQT models. In addition to qualitative comparisons, we also conduct exhaustive quantitative evaluations that compare our approach with existing unpaired approaches (e.g., CycleGAN, UNIT-DDPM, and OT-FM) - as either forward or reverse alternatives - by assessing clinical downstream task performance in PI-RADS and Gleason score classification, using both in-distribution and external data sets.

中文摘要翻译: 单次激发平面回波前列腺扩散加权成像常因几何畸变而复杂化，这影响了从此类图像中获取可靠诊断的能力。开发自动校正方法面临缺乏成对的畸变与无畸变临床扫描数据的挑战。本文首先提出了一种新颖的弱监督图像质量迁移框架，从无畸变图像到畸变图像，利用图像质量评估信号来监督迁移过程。与传统方法需要昂贵的逐体素配对数据或求助于无配对算法不同，我们的方法利用图像级质量标签（此处为畸变vs无畸变）在预训练特征空间中建立潜在质量原型。认识到模拟真实畸变比直接进行无配对校正更为可靠，我们描述了一种弱监督原型流匹配算法，显式地将生成轨迹正则化导向畸变原型，从而产生模拟临床退化的逼真磁敏感伪影。通过合成这些逼真的配对数据，我们使得第二个图像质量迁移模型能够在正向方向上训练以进行畸变校正。实验结果表明，我们生成的图像成功模拟了真实世界伪影的诊断干扰，从而产生了更强大的畸变校正图像质量迁移模型。除了定性比较外，我们还进行了详尽的定量评估，将我们的方法与现有的无配对方法（如CycleGAN、UNIT-DDPM和OT-FM）作为正向或反向替代方案进行比较，通过评估PI-RADS和Gleason评分分类中的临床下游任务性能，并使用分布内和外部数据集。

</details>


### [49] [Bridging Single Distortion Artifacts and Mmultifactorial Clinical Quality: Few-shot Biparametric MRI Quality Assessment via Distortion-trained Prototypical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18872)
*Yuheng Tang,Alexander Ng,Wen Yan,Natasha Thorley,Pawel Rajwa,Yipei Wang,Aqua Asif,Clare Allen,Louise Dickinson,Francesco Giganti,Shonit Punwani,Daniel Alexander,Veeru Kasivisvanathan,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对临床前列腺多参数MRI中DWI图像质量评估存在的主观性强、耗时长、低质量样本稀缺且类别不平衡问题，提出一种基于少样本双参数原型网络的自动IQA方法，利用元学习从易获取的失真标签迁移至复杂PI-QUAL评分预测，仅需5个代表性样本即可有效适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床前列腺多参数MRI中DWI常因直肠气体导致几何失真，PI-QUAL评分虽为新兴临床标准但主观耗时，且低质量样本稀缺（仅6%）类别不平衡，DWI问题中87%为失真但其他质量问题更稀少，亟需一种数据高效的自动化质量评估方法。

Method: 提出少样本双参数原型网络，采用双分支3D ResNet融合T2加权和DWI特征以提供解剖上下文区分真实形态与失真；引入特征级线性调制（FiLM）和梯度反转层（GRL）对齐不同b值下的特征分布并抑制采集相关偏差；通过元学习在客观易获取的失真标签上训练，再仅用5个代表性样本适配至复杂PI-QUAL评分预测。

Result: 在两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于少样本学习基线，为临床工作流中标准化前列腺MRI质量控制提供了数据高效的可行方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的少样本双参数原型网络能够有效利用易获取的失真标签通过元学习适配至复杂临床质量评分预测，仅需极少量标注样本即可实现可靠评估，为解决医学影像质量评估中标注稀缺问题提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Clinical prostate multi-parametric MRI relies heavily on high-quality diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI), yet reading DWI is frequently compromised by geometric distortion, often caused by rectal air. Assessing quality via the PI-QUAL scoring system is an emerging clinical standard, but it is subjective, time-consuming and suffers from a class imbalance where low-quality cases are diverse and relatively scarce. Using the PRIME clinical trial as an example, there are $6\%$ images with PI-QUAL scores lower than 4, $87\%$ of DWI issues are due to distortion. Many of the other clinical quality issues are under-represented. To address this common dual-scarcity of annotated clinical data, we propose a few-shot biparametric prototypical network for automated image quality assessment (IQA). Our framework utilizes a dual-branch 3D ResNet to fuse T2-weighted and DWI features, providing anatomical context to distinguish true morphology from distortion. To handle real-world heterogeneity, we introduce feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM) and a gradient reversal layer (GRL) to align feature distributions conditioned on varying b-values while suppressing acquisition-related biases. We demonstrate that a model meta-trained solely on comparatively objective, readily obtainable distortion labels can effectively adapt to predicting complex, multi-factorial clinical quality scores such as PI-QUAL using only five representative samples. Experimental results on two datasets show that our method significantly outperforms few-shot learning baselines for this challenging IQA task, offering a practically feasible and data-efficient solution for standardizing prostate MRI quality control in clinical workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 临床前列腺多参数MRI高度依赖于高质量的弥散加权成像（DWI），但DWI的判读常因几何失真（通常由直肠气体引起）而受损。通过PI-QUAL评分系统评估质量是一种新兴的临床标准，但该方法具有主观性、耗时且存在类别不平衡问题——低质量病例既多样又相对稀缺。以PRIME临床试验为例，仅有6%的图像PI-QUAL评分低于4，87%的DWI问题源于失真，而许多其他临床质量问题则代表性不足。为解决这种标注临床数据的双重稀缺性问题，我们提出了一种用于自动化图像质量评估（IQA）的少样本双参数原型网络。我们的框架利用双分支3D ResNet融合T2加权与DWI特征，提供解剖上下文以区分真实形态与失真。为处理真实世界的异质性，我们引入了特征级线性调制（FiLM）和梯度反转层（GRL），以对齐不同b值条件下的特征分布，同时抑制与采集相关的偏差。我们证明，仅在相对客观、易于获取的失真标签上进行元训练的模型，能够仅使用五个代表性样本有效适应预测复杂的多因素临床质量评分（如PI-QUAL）。两个数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在具有挑战性的IQA任务上显著优于少样本学习基线，为临床工作流中标准化前列腺MRI质量控制提供了一种实际可行且数据高效的解决方案。

</details>


### [50] [Test-Time Adaptation in Optical Coherence Tomography Using Trajectory-Aligned Time-Independent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18876)
*Veit Hucke,Thomas Pinetz,Gregor Reiter,Ursula Schmidt-Erfurth,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流匹配的测试时自适应方法，通过对噪声OCT图像生成高质量替代图像并校正分布偏移，在AMD生物标志物分割中取得了最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: OCT在眼科中至关重要，但低成本设备图像质量不稳定，阻碍了自动化分析；测试数据与训练数据之间的域差距导致去噪过程中像素分布不匹配。

Method: 通过将测试图像的直方图匹配到合成参考轨迹，对齐输入与预期分布；同时移除网络的时间条件以适应真实噪声分布的微小偏差，从而实现流匹配的测试时自适应。

Result: 在两个阶段的年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）关键生物标志物分割任务上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的测试时自适应方法有效解决了OCT图像质量不一致和域偏移问题，为低成本设备下的自动化分析提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) is essential in ophthalmology, but inconsistent image quality especially in low-cost devices hinders automated analysis. To address this, we introduce a flow-matching-based test-time adaptation method that generates high-quality surrogate images from noisy inputs. Typically, domain gaps between test and training data cause pixel distribution mismatches during the denoising process. We overcome this by matching the test image's histogram to synthetic reference trajectories, successfully aligning the input with expected distributions. Additionally, we remove the network's time conditioning to account for slight deviations in real-world noise distributions. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in segmenting critical biomarkers for two stages of Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD). Code is available: https://github.com/Veit21/tta-flow.

中文摘要翻译: 光学相干断层扫描（OCT）在眼科中至关重要，但图像质量不一致（尤其是在低成本设备中）阻碍了自动化分析。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于流匹配的测试时自适应方法，该方法能从含噪输入中生成高质量的替代图像。通常，测试数据与训练数据之间的域差距会在去噪过程中导致像素分布不匹配。我们通过将测试图像的直方图匹配到合成参考轨迹来克服这一问题，成功地将输入与预期分布对齐。此外，我们移除了网络的时间条件以适应真实噪声分布中的微小偏差。我们的方法在两个阶段的年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）关键生物标志物分割任务中达到了最先进的性能。代码已开源：https://github.com/Veit21/tta-flow。

</details>


### [51] [Performance Gap Analysis between Latin and Arabic Scripts HTR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18884)
*Sana Al-azzawi,Elisa Barney,Marcus Liwicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对阿拉伯语和拉丁语手写文本识别（HTR）进行了系统对比，发现阿拉伯语HTR性能始终落后5-7个CER点，原因包括标注质量差、视觉变异性高、字符分布更重尾，以及视觉相似字符混淆率更高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明阿拉伯语手写文本识别（HTR）性能显著低于拉丁语，但缺乏控制变量下的系统对比分析，其根本原因尚不明确。

Method: 采用统一的CRNN模型，在9个数据集（涵盖阿拉伯语、乌尔都语、波斯语、英语、德语等）上进行行级HTR对比实验，控制训练集大小（K ∈ {100, 500, 1000, 2000, ..., Kfull}），并从标注质量、视觉变异性、字符分布、错误类型等多个维度进行分析。

Result: 1) 性能差距始终存在：低资源时差距大，全量数据时仍有5-7 CER的差距；2) 标注错误是重要因素，清洗后差距缩小但未消除；3) 相同训练样本数下，阿拉伯语因视觉变异性高导致覆盖效率更低；4) 阿拉伯语字符频率分布比拉丁语更重尾；5) 阿拉伯语数据中约30%的替换错误源于视觉相似字符混淆，拉丁语仅约15%。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语HTR性能落后拉丁语的结构性原因包括标注质量、视觉变异性、字符分布偏态和视觉混淆，这些因素共同导致差距难以完全消除，需要针对性的数据策略和模型设计来缩小差距。

Original Abstract: Recent studies have shown that handwritten text recognition (HTR) systems perform worse on Arabic-script datasets than on Latin-script data. However, the reasons for this gap are still not well understood due to the lack of controlled comparisons. In this work, we present a comprehensive study of Arabic and Latin scripts HTR using a unified CRNN model for line-level HTR across nine datasets (including KHATT (Arabic), Muharaf (Arabic), NUST-UHWR (Urdu), PHTD (Persian), IAM (English), READ-2016 (German), and others) and di ferent training sizes (K in {100, 500, 1000, 2000, ..., Kfull}). Our results show the performance gap remains: it is large in low-resource settings, decreases with more data, but remains even at full scale, with a consistent difference of 5-7 CER points. We show that annotation quality matters, as many datasets contain labeling errors. Cleaning reduces error rates and narrows the gap, but does not eliminate it. In addition, we find that a fixed number of training samples provides less effective coverage in Arabic due to higher visual variability, requiring more data to learn similar representations. We compare recognition across datasets in terms of the number of text lines and the number of characters, showing an equivalence trade-off. We compare character frequency distributions across scripts and show that Arabic is significantly more heavy-tailed than Latin. Our error analysis reveals that around 30 percent of substitution errors in Arabic datasets (e.g., KHATT) are caused by confusion between visually similar characters, compared to about 15 percent in Latin-script datasets such as IAM.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的研究表明，手写文本识别（HTR）系统在阿拉伯语数据集上的表现不如在拉丁语数据集上。然而，由于缺乏控制变量下的对比研究，这一差距的原因尚不明确。本文采用统一的CRNN模型，在九个数据集（包括KHATT（阿拉伯语）、Muharaf（阿拉伯语）、NUST-UHWR（乌尔都语）、PHTD（波斯语）、IAM（英语）、READ-2016（德语）等）上进行行级HTR的系统对比研究，并设置不同的训练集规模（K ∈ {100, 500, 1000, 2000, ..., Kfull}）。结果表明，性能差距始终存在：低资源场景下差距较大，随数据量增加而缩小，但在全量数据下仍保持5-7个CER点的差距。我们发现标注质量至关重要，许多数据集存在标注错误。清洗数据可降低错误率并缩小差距，但无法完全消除。此外，我们发现由于阿拉伯语视觉变异性更高，固定数量的训练样本覆盖效率更低，需要更多数据才能学习到相似的表示。我们在文本行数和字符数维度上跨数据集比较识别性能，揭示了二者之间的等价权衡关系。我们比较了不同语系的字符频率分布，表明阿拉伯语比拉丁语具有显著更重的尾部分布。错误分析揭示，阿拉伯语数据集（如KHATT）中约30%的替换错误是由视觉相似字符混淆引起的，而在拉丁语数据集（如IAM）中这一比例约为15%。

</details>


### [52] [LARE: Low-Attention Region Encoding for Text-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18885)
*Abdulmalik Alquwayfili,Faisal Almeshal,Jumanah Almajnouni,Leena Alotaibi,Faisal Alhajari,Mohammed Alkhrashi,Alreem Almuhrij,Abdullah Aldwyish,Raied Aljadaany,Huda Alamri,Muhammad Kamran J. Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LARE框架，通过双编码策略显式建模低注意力区域，以解决拥挤场景中的图像检索问题，并构建了Dense-Set数据集用于更严格的评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统视觉编码器存在显著性偏差，倾向于关注主导对象而忽略低注意力区域，这些区域对于细粒度检索至关重要，因此需要显式建模这些被忽略的区域。

Method: 提出LARE框架，采用双编码策略：并行编码图像的完整特征与低注意力区域特征，生成更丰富多样的图像嵌入。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提框架通过在共享潜在空间中保留微妙、非主导的视觉线索，有效提升了检索性能。

Conclusion: LARE通过显式建模低注意力区域克服了显著性偏差，在拥挤场景下实现了更优的图像检索性能。

Original Abstract: Image retrieval in crowded scenes is particularly challenging due to the salience bias of conventional visual encoders, which tend to focus on dominant objects while neglecting low-attention regions that are often crucial for fine-grained retrieval. We propose LARE (Low-Attention Region Encoding), a framework that explicitly models these overlooked regions. LARE adopts a dual-encoding strategy that encodes low-attention regions of an image and the full image in parallel, leading to more diverse and informative image embeddings. To evaluate image retrieval performance in challenging crowded scenes, we introduce Dense-Set, a challenging subset derived from COCO and Flickr30K. In this subset, images are re-captioned to provide richer descriptions of low-attention or previously overlooked regions. This dataset highlights the limitations of existing retrieval models and enables a more rigorous evaluation under densely crowded scene conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves retrieval performance by preserving subtle, non-dominant visual cues within the shared latent space.

中文摘要翻译: 拥挤场景中的图像检索尤为具有挑战性，因为传统视觉编码器存在显著性偏差，倾向于关注主导对象而忽略对细粒度检索至关重要的低注意力区域。本文提出LARE（低注意力区域编码）框架，显式建模这些被忽视的区域。LARE采用双编码策略，并行编码图像的低注意力区域与完整图像，从而生成更丰富多样的图像嵌入。为评估拥挤场景中的图像检索性能，我们引入了Dense-Set——一个基于COCO和Flickr30K构建的具有挑战性的子集。该子集中的图像经过重新标注，提供了对低注意力或先前被忽略区域的更丰富描述。该数据集揭示了现有检索模型的局限性，使得在密集拥挤场景下能够进行更严格的评估。实验结果表明，所提框架通过在共享潜在空间中保留微妙、非主导的视觉线索，有效提升了检索性能。

</details>


### [53] [DINO-Med3D: Bridging Dimension and Domain Gaps in Volumetric Segmentation via Progressive Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18886)
*Haoyu Hu,Xiyao Ma,Shiqi Liu,Linsen Zhang,Xiaoliang Xie,Xiaohu Zhou,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DINO-Med3D，一个两阶段渐进框架，通过多切片嵌入模块、3D适配器和并行细节恢复流，将预训练DINOv3编码器适配到三维医学图像分割任务，在五个公开数据集上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DINOv3在自然图像上具有强大的语义判别能力，但直接应用于三维医学图像分割时面临维度差异（2D→3D）和领域差异（自然→医学）两大鸿沟。

Method: 两阶段渐进框架：第一阶段，通过多切片嵌入模块引入伪3D上下文弥合维度差距，同时使用分割代理任务将自然场景表示适应到医学领域；第二阶段，在冻结主干中添加轻量级3D适配器增强全局跨切片连续性；此外设计并行细节恢复流显式保留高频边界线索以补偿嵌入过程中的空间信息损失。

Result: 在五个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法成功将DINOv3适应到医学领域，并显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的DINO-Med3D框架有效解决了预训练视觉基础模型从自然图像迁移到三维医学图像分割的维度与领域差距问题。

Original Abstract: Although DINOv3 has demonstrated remarkable semantic discrimination in natural imagery, its direct application to volumetric medical segmentation is hindered by inherent dimension and domain disparities. To resolve these issues, we propose DINO-Med3D, a two-stage progressive framework that repurpose the pre-trained DINOv3 encoder for 3D medical tasks. In the first stage, we mitigate the dimension gap by introducing a multi-slice embedding module that incorporates pseudo-3D context, while simultaneously employing a segmentation proxy task to adapt representations learned from natural scenes to the medical domain. Subsequently, we further enhance volumetric understanding by adding lightweight 3D adapters into the frozen backbone to enforce global inter-slice continuity. Finally, to compensate for the spatial information loss inherent in the embedding process, we design a parallel detail recovery stream to explicitly preserve high-frequency boundary cues. Extensive experiments on five public datasets demonstrate that our approach successfully adapts DINOv3 to the medical domain and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管DINOv3在自然图像上展现出了卓越的语义判别能力，但其直接应用于三维医学图像分割时受到固有的维度差异和领域差异的阻碍。为解决这些问题，我们提出了DINO-Med3D，一个两阶段渐进框架，将预训练的DINOv3编码器重新用于三维医学任务。在第一阶段，我们通过引入一个融合伪3D上下文的多切片嵌入模块来弥合维度差距，同时采用分割代理任务使从自然场景学习到的表示适应医学领域。随后，我们通过在冻结的主干网络中添加轻量级3D适配器来进一步增强体理解能力，以强制实现全局跨切片连续性。最后，为补偿嵌入过程中固有的空间信息损失，我们设计了一个并行的细节恢复流，以显式保留高频边界线索。在五个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法成功地将DINOv3适应到医学领域，并显著优于最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [54] [Automatic ply-specific analyses of CFRP micrographs using shortest-path-based ply distinction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18894)
*Jonas Naumann,Jonas P. Appels,Julius Biermann,Christopher Gorsky,Timo de Wolff,Christoph Brauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化方法，将语义分割掩码视为图结构，利用最短路径算法实现碳纤维增强聚合物显微图像中铺层实例的区分，从而支持定量微观结构分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有语义分割方法只能将像素分类为纤维、基体等类别，但无法区分不同铺层（ply）实例，而铺层级的分析对于揭示制造不均匀性、关联力学性能与微观结构缺陷至关重要。

Method: 将语义分割掩码建模为以像素为顶点的图，使用最短路径算法计算铺层分隔路径，从而将语义分割结果转化为铺层实例分割，实现全局信息驱动下的铺层分配。

Result: 成功应用于多种特征的高分辨率显微图像，包括单层或多层人工间隙、不同铺层顺序以及贯穿裂纹；基于计算路径为每个纤维像素分配所属铺层，实现了局部纤维体积分数、铺层及夹层厚度等微观属性的定量分析。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了语义分割与铺层实例分割之间的鸿沟，能够揭示制造诱导的不均匀性，为制造参数评估及力学性能与微观缺陷关联分析提供了有力工具。

Original Abstract: We present an automated approach to distinguish between ply instances in semantic segmentation masks of high-resolution carbon-fiber reinforced polymer micrographs. Interpreting the segmentation mask as a graph with pixels as vertices, enables us to use a shortest-path algorithm yielding the ply-separating paths. Thereby, we bridge the gap between semantic segmentation and ply instance segmentation using global information. We successfully apply our approach on high-resolution micrographs featuring a broad range of characteristics like artificially added gaps in single or multiple plies, different stacking sequences and ply traversing cracks. Assigning each fiber pixel to a ply based on the calculated paths, allows for a comprehensive, quantitative ply analysis with respect to its microstructural properties like the local fiber volume fraction as well as locally resolved ply and interleaf layer thickness. These insights help to reveal manufacturing-induced inhomogeneities, draw conclusions on manufacturing parameters and link mechanical properties to underlying microstructural imperfections.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种自动化方法，用于在高分辨率碳纤维增强聚合物显微图像的语义分割掩码中区分铺层实例。将分割掩码视为以像素为顶点的图，使得我们能够使用最短路径算法得出铺层分隔路径。由此，我们利用全局信息弥合了语义分割与铺层实例分割之间的差距。我们成功地将该方法应用于具有广泛特征的高分辨率显微图像，包括单层或多层中人工添加的间隙、不同的铺层顺序以及贯穿铺层的裂纹。基于计算出的路径为每个纤维像素分配所属铺层，能够对铺层进行全面的定量分析，分析其微观结构属性，如局部纤维体积分数以及局部分辨的铺层和夹层厚度。这些见解有助于揭示制造-induced的不均匀性，对制造参数得出结论，并将力学性能与潜在的微观结构缺陷联系起来。

</details>


### [55] [BindEdit: Taming Attention Leakage for Precise Multi-Object Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18906)
*Chaewon Park,Soyoon Lee,Naeun Lee,Minjung Shin,Seogkyu Jeon,Kibeom Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BindEdit，通过注意力约束机制解决真实图像编辑中多目标场景下的注意力泄漏问题，包括编辑令牌泄漏和源主导泄漏，在单次扩散轨迹中实现鲁棒的多目标编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有真实图像编辑方法在复杂多目标场景中常出现语义混合、目标重复或不完整编辑等问题，原因是去噪过程中空间区域和文本令牌之间的信号纠缠导致的注意力泄漏，包括编辑令牌泄漏（令牌-区域对齐模糊）和源主导泄漏（未修改源目标令牌压制目标实体注意力）。

Method: 提出BindEdit框架，在单次扩散轨迹中施加三类注意力级约束：（1）联合正则化交叉注意力和自注意力，将每个目标令牌组绑定到对应空间区域并保持实例级分离，抑制编辑令牌泄漏；（2）交叉注意力重平衡机制，放大目标令牌影响并衰减可编辑区域内残留的源语义，抑制源主导泄漏；（3）区域保真度项，确保每个目标概念在整个编辑掩码内连贯表达。

Result: 提出了包含多样目标数量和类别的大规模多目标基准；大量实验表明BindEdit在单次扩散轨迹中一致优于现有方法，在单目标和多目标编辑场景下均保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: BindEdit通过注意力级约束有效解决了多目标编辑中的注意力泄漏问题，在单次扩散轨迹内实现了优于现有方法的编辑效果，无需额外推理步骤。

Original Abstract: Real image editing enables precise manipulation of visual content, yet existing methods often fail in complex multi-object scenarios, causing semantic blending, object duplication, or incomplete edits. We attribute these failures to attention leakage, where signals across spatial regions and text tokens become entangled during the denoising process. Specifically, we identify two distinct forms of leakage: Edit-Token Leakage, where ambiguous token-region alignment leads to object blending, and Source Dominance Leakage, where tokens of unchanged source objects overwhelm the attention intended for target entities. To resolve these leakages, we propose \textbf{BindEdit}, which enforces attention-level constraints within a single diffusion trajectory. To suppress Edit-Token Leakage, BindEdit jointly regularizes cross- and self-attention so that each target token group is bound to its corresponding spatial region while maintaining instance-level separation. To suppress Source Dominance Leakage, a cross-attention re-balancing mechanism amplifies target token influence and attenuates residual source semantics within editable regions. Moreover, a region fidelity term ensures that each target concept is expressed coherently across the entire editing mask. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive multi-object benchmark encompassing diverse object counts and categories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BindEdit consistently outperforms existing methods within a single diffusion trajectory, maintaining robust performance across both single- and multi-object editing scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 真实图像编辑能够实现视觉内容的精确操控，然而现有方法在复杂多目标场景中常常失败，导致语义混合、目标重复或编辑不完整。我们将这些失败归因于注意力泄漏，即在去噪过程中空间区域与文本令牌之间的信号发生纠缠。具体而言，我们识别出两种不同形式的泄漏：编辑令牌泄漏，即模糊的令牌-区域对齐导致目标混合；以及源主导泄漏，即未修改的源目标令牌压制了目标实体应有的注意力。为解决这些泄漏问题，我们提出BindEdit，它在单次扩散轨迹内施加注意力级约束。为抑制编辑令牌泄漏，BindEdit联合正则化交叉注意力和自注意力，使每个目标令牌组绑定到其对应的空间区域，同时保持实例级分离。为抑制源主导泄漏，交叉注意力重平衡机制放大目标令牌的影响，并衰减可编辑区域内残留的源语义。此外，区域保真度项确保每个目标概念在整个编辑掩码内连贯表达。我们还提出了一个涵盖多样目标数量和类别的综合多目标基准。大量实验表明，BindEdit在单次扩散轨迹内一致优于现有方法，在单目标和多目标编辑场景下均保持鲁棒性能。

</details>


### [56] [Physics-IQ Verified](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18943)
*Tim Rädsch,Yuki M Asano,Hilde Kuehne,Stefan Bauer,Priyank Jaini,Robert Geirhos,Carsten T. Lüth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对视频生成模型的物理理解评估基准Physics-IQ进行了系统性审计，发现其存在提示质量、标注准确性和评分公平性等问题，并提出了三项改进措施，最终构建了Physics-IQ Verified基准，显著提升了评估的可靠性和区分度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的Physics-IQ基准虽然被用于量化评估视频生成模型对物理世界的理解能力，但其提示设计、真实标注和评分方式可能存在混杂因素，导致无法准确衡量模型真实的物理理解水平。因此，有必要对该基准进行系统性审计并改进。

Method: (1) 提升提示质量和真实标注质量，减少混杂因素的影响；(2) 引入样本级别的评分系统，对每个样本和每个指标进行等权加权；(3) 在六个图像到视频生成模型上进行比较研究，通过Kendall秩相关系数衡量排名变化。

Result: 改进后的Physics-IQ Verified基准精炼了57.6%的样本，改进了34.8%的提示；在六个图像到视频生成模型的比较中，观察到了中等但有意义的排名变化（Kendall's τ = 0.46）。

Conclusion: Physics-IQ Verified通过消除混杂因素和引入更公平的评分机制，为视频生成模型的物理理解能力提供了更可靠的评估信号，有望推动社区朝着物理更准确的视频生成模型发展。

Original Abstract: Video generative models ( VGMs) have become a new frontier that can be used not just for video generation but for a multitude of downstream tasks, including world modeling. To advance these tasks, a good video model must understand the physical reality of the world. Evaluating this understanding is an emerging field and has led to the Physics-IQ benchmark, which quantifies this explicitly by comparing model-generated videos to real-world videos of physical experiments. In this work, we present a systematic audit of the Physics-IQ benchmark, expose shortcomings and propose three solutions that sharpen how we can measure physical understanding of VGMs. Specifically, we improve prompt and ground-truth quality to reduce the influence of confounding factors and further introduce a sample-level scoring system that weights each sample and metric equally. Our resulting benchmark, Physics-IQ Verified, refines 57.6\% of all samples and improves over 34.8\% of prompts. In a comparison study using six image-to-video generative models, we observe moderate but meaningful ranking changes (Kendall's $τ= 0.46$). We hope Physics-IQ Verified advances the community by providing a more reliable signal toward physically accurate VGMs. The code for the benchmark can be accessed at https://github.com/google-deepmind/physics-iq-benchmark

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型已成为一个新的前沿领域，不仅可用于视频生成，还可用于包括世界建模在内的多种下游任务。为推进这些任务，一个好的视频模型必须理解世界的物理现实。评估这种理解是一个新兴领域，并催生了Physics-IQ基准，该基准通过将模型生成的视频与物理实验的真实视频进行比较来明确量化这一能力。在这项工作中，我们对Physics-IQ基准进行了系统性审计，揭示了其不足之处，并提出了三种解决方案，以增强我们衡量视频生成模型物理理解能力的方式。具体而言，我们改进了提示质量和真实标注质量以减少混杂因素的影响，并进一步引入了样本级别的评分系统，对每个样本和每个指标进行等权加权。由此得到的基准Physics-IQ Verified精炼了57.6%的样本，改进了34.8%的提示。在使用六个图像到视频生成模型的比较研究中，我们观察到了中等但有意义的排名变化（Kendall's τ = 0.46）。我们希望Physics-IQ Verified通过为物理准确的视频生成模型提供更可靠的评估信号来推动社区进步。该基准的代码可访问https://github.com/google-deepmind/physics-iq-benchmark。

</details>


### [57] [SP-TransientBench: A Real-Captured Single Photon Perception Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18952)
*Hongzhou Dong,Zili Zhang,Ziting Wen,Yiheng Qiang,Runrong Deng,Wenle Dong,Ziwen Jiang,Xinyang Li,Rui Lu,Shuoyao Sun,Wenyu Wang,Ziyi Xia,Haitao Zheng,Guodong Shi,Xiaoqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SP-TransientBench (STB)，一个基于固态单光子LiDAR真实采集的多任务基准数据集，覆盖深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解，包含10个场景、10297个视图及全时间飞行直方图，填补了真实场景下单光子感知系统评估的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单光子LiDAR研究大多局限于模拟数据或小规模受控采集，缺乏对真实世界中深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解等任务的系统性评估基准，因此需要构建一个真实采集的多任务基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 使用固态单光子LiDAR（256×192分辨率）真实采集10个多样场景共10297个视图，提供完整时间飞行直方图、多返回行为、标准元数据和校准相机位姿，并对选定场景提供13类3D语义标注，为每个任务设计专属数据划分和评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含10个场景、10297个视图的真实单光子感知基准数据集，提供多任务评估协议和标准化的元数据，将在论文接收后开源数据和代码。

Conclusion: SP-TransientBench填补了真实世界单光子感知系统评估的空白，为深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解等任务提供了可复现的标准化基准，有望推动单光子3D感知的研究进展。

Original Abstract: Single-photon LiDAR (SPL) based on single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) sensing enables time-resolved photon measurements with extreme sensitivity, offering unique potential for active 3D perception in photon-starved scenarios.However, real-world single photon perception remains fundamentally challenging due to unique measurement noise and complex multi-return transient phenomena, which jointly complicate geometric reconstruction and semantic scene understanding. Despite growing interest in SPAD-based sensing, existing studies are largely limited to simulated data or small-scale controlled captures. As a result, systematic evaluation of real-world single photon perception across depth estimation, multi-view reconstruction, and 3D semantic understanding remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce SP-TransientBench (STB), a real-captured multi-task benchmark for single photon perception. SP-TransientBenc comprises 10 diverse scenes and 10,297 views captured using a solid-state single-photon LiDAR at $256\times192$ resolution. Each view provides full time-of-flight histograms with multi-return behavior,standardized metadata, and calibrated camera poses for multi-view evaluation. We further provide 13-class 3D semantic annotations for selected scenes. By providing dedicated data splits and evaluation protocols for each task, STB enables consistent and reproducible benchmarking of real-world single photon perception across multiple 3D vision problems. The dataset and code will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 基于单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）传感的单光子LiDAR（SPL）能够以极高的灵敏度进行时间分辨光子测量，在光子匮乏场景下为主动3D感知提供了独特的潜力。然而，由于独特的测量噪声和复杂的多返回瞬态现象，真实世界的单光子感知仍然面临根本性挑战，这些因素共同使几何重建和语义场景理解变得复杂。尽管对基于SPAD的传感兴趣日益增长，现有研究大多局限于模拟数据或小规模受控采集。因此，在深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解等任务上，真实世界单光子感知的系统性评估仍未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，我们引入了SP-TransientBench（STB），一个真实采集的单光子感知多任务基准。SP-TransientBench包含使用固态单光子LiDAR以256×192分辨率采集的10个多样场景和10297个视图。每个视图提供包含多返回行为的完整时间飞行直方图、标准化元数据以及用于多视图评估的校准相机位姿。我们还为选定场景提供了13类3D语义标注。通过为每个任务提供专属的数据划分和评估协议，STB能够在多个3D视觉问题上实现真实世界单光子感知的一致且可复现的基准评估。数据集和代码将在论文接收后发布。

</details>


### [58] [Motion-Focused Latent Action Enables Cross-Embodiment VLA Training from Human EgoVideos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18955)
*Runze Xu,Yiluo Zhang,Jian Wang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在动作的框架，通过从无标签人类视频中提取通用动作先验，使VLA模型仅需50条轨迹即可在下游任务中达到与大规模标注数据训练模型相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VLA模型训练依赖大规模、多样化且带有高质量动作标注的机器人数据集，而丰富的第一人称人类视频虽然包含大量环境多样性信息，但缺乏动作标签，难以直接用于传统训练范式。

Method: 提出一个潜在动作框架，核心包括：(1) 混合解耦VQ-VAE，通过物理掩码将运动动态与环境背景解耦，构建跨实体动作码本；(2) 在人类视频上预训练VLM骨干网络以学习动作意图的深层表示；(3) 引入意图-感知解耦策略，VLM预测动作意图，独立的冻结视觉编码器提供状态特征给动作专家，减少动作幻觉。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，该方法仅使用无标签人类视频进行预训练，在下游适配仅需50条轨迹，即可与使用大规模标注数据集训练的最先进VLA模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过从无标签人类视频中学习潜在动作先验，可以显著降低VLA模型对标注数据的依赖，实现高效的下游适配。

Original Abstract: Training generalist Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models typically requires massive, diverse robotic datasets with high-fidelity action annotations. While egocentric human manipulation videos are abundant and capture significant environmental diversity, the absence of action labels makes them difficult to use in conventional training paradigms. To address this, we propose a latent-action-based framework designed to extract general action priors from unlabeled human videos. The architecture features a Hybrid Disentangled VQ-VAE that decouples motion dynamics from environmental backgrounds through physical masks, enabling the construction of a cross-embodiment action codebook. By pre-training on human videos with the codebook, the VLM backbone learns deep representations of action intent. For adaptation to specific embodiments, we introduce an intent-perception decoupling strategy where the VLM predicts the action intent while a separate frozen visual encoder provides state-specific features to the action expert, thereby reducing action hallucinations. Results in simulation and real-world environments show that our method, pre-trained exclusively on unlabeled human videos, performs competitively with state-of-the-art VLA models trained on massive annotated datasets, requiring only 50 trajectories for downstream adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 训练通用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常需要大规模、多样化的机器人数据集以及高保真度的动作标注。尽管第一人称的人类操作视频丰富且捕捉了大量的环境多样性，但由于缺乏动作标签，它们在传统训练范式下难以被利用。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一个基于潜在动作的框架，旨在从无标签的人类视频中提取通用的动作先验。该架构的核心是一个混合解耦VQ-VAE，通过物理掩码将运动动态与环境背景解耦，从而构建跨实体的动作码本。通过在人类视频上使用该码本进行预训练，VLM骨干网络能够学习动作意图的深层表示。为了适应具体实体，我们引入了一种意图-感知解耦策略：VLM预测动作意图，而一个独立的冻结视觉编码器为动作专家提供状态特定特征，从而减少动作幻觉。在仿真和真实环境中的实验结果表明，我们的方法仅通过在无标签人类视频上进行预训练，即可与使用大规模标注数据集训练的最先进VLA模型相竞争，且仅需50条轨迹即可完成下游适配。

</details>


### [59] [Mem-World: Memory-Augmented Action-Conditioned World Models for Persistent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18960)
*Zirui Zheng,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,jun shi,Mingyi Li,Chao Zhang,Weiming Li,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Xu Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mem-World，一种记忆增强的多视角动作条件世界模型，通过4D腕部视角中心曲面索引记忆（W-VMem）解决操作任务中的持久世界建模问题，在长时域任务中显著提升策略评估与策略改进效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在操作任务中存在持久建模挑战：末端执行器频繁遮挡和腕部相机快速运动导致当前观测不足以预测未来视图，模型容易遗忘或幻觉化早期帧中看到的场景细节，而现有记忆检索策略难以在动态操作场景中识别信息量丰富的历史帧。

Method: 提出Mem-World模型，核心是W-VMem——一种4D腕部视角中心的曲面索引记忆，将历史观测锚定到随时间演化的表面元素上，通过显式建模场景元素被观测的时间和位置，实现基于未来动作的几何感知历史帧检索；生成时通过基于曲面元素的渲染和评分选择信息丰富且不冗余的历史上下文。

Result: 在复杂操作场景中生成长时域持续展开；相比Ctrl-World，与真实世界性能的Pearson相关性提升14.5%；通过合成数据生成实现策略改进，长时域任务成功率从58%提升至72%。

Conclusion: Mem-World通过记忆增强的世界模型有效解决了操作任务中的持久建模问题，能够生成更可靠的策略评估结果，并支持通过合成数据实现策略改进，在长时域复杂操作任务上具有显著优势。

Original Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot learning, offering a scalable alternative to costly real-world experimentation by generating action-consistent video rollouts. However, persistent world modeling remains challenging in manipulation: frequent end-effector occlusions and rapid wrist-camera motion make the current observation insufficient for predicting future views, causing models to forget or hallucinate scene details seen in earlier frames. Existing memory retrieval strategies often fail to identify informative history in dynamic manipulation scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose Mem-World, a memory-augmented multi-view action-conditioned world model. At its core, we present W-VMem, a 4D wrist-view-centered surfel-indexed memory that anchors historical observations to temporally evolving surface elements. By explicitly modeling when and where scene elements are observed, W-VMem enables geometry-aware retrieval of relevant history frames conditioned on future actions. During generation, relevant history frames are selected via surfel-based rendering and scoring, providing informative and non-redundant context for prediction. Extensive experiments show that Mem-World generates persistent rollouts in complex manipulation scenarios, enables more reliable policy evaluation than Ctrl-World, improving the Pearson correlation with real-world performance by 14.5\%, and supports effective policy improvement through synthetic data generation, increasing success rates from 58\% to 72\% on long-horizon tasks.
