中文摘要翻译: 动作条件世界模型已成为机器人学习中有前景的范式，通过生成动作一致的视频展开提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，避免了昂贵的真实世界实验。然而，在操作任务中持久世界建模仍然具有挑战性：频繁的末端执行器遮挡和快速的腕部相机运动使得当前观测不足以预测未来视图，导致模型遗忘或幻觉化早期帧中看到的场景细节。现有的记忆检索策略通常难以在动态操作场景中识别信息量丰富的历史帧。为解决这一局限性，我们提出Mem-World——一种记忆增强的多视角动作条件世界模型。其核心是W-VMem，一种4D腕部视角中心的曲面索引记忆，将历史观测锚定到随时间演化的表面元素上。通过显式建模场景元素被观测到的时间和位置，W-VMem能够基于未来动作实现几何感知的相关历史帧检索。在生成过程中，通过基于曲面元素的渲染和评分选择相关历史帧，为预测提供信息丰富且非冗余的上下文。大量实验表明，Mem-World能够在复杂操作场景中生成长时域持续展开，比Ctrl-World实现更可靠的策略评估，与真实世界性能的Pearson相关性提升14.5%，并通过合成数据生成支持有效的策略改进，在长时域任务上将成功率从58%提升至72%。

</details>


### [60] [Visual-OPSD: Cross-Modal On-Policy Self-Distillation for Efficient Unified Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18974)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Yuanming Li,Fangzhi Xu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现统一多模态模型（UMMs）中生成“视觉思维”（VT）的推理成本高昂但直接收益有限，并提出Visual-OPSD方法，通过教师-学生蒸馏将生成路径中的语义知识转移到纯文本模型，在显著加速的同时提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有UMMs通过生成视觉思维（VT）来提升空间任务性能，但多步扩散带来的推理成本高达一个数量级。作者发现去除或噪声化VT后准确率变化极小，表明VT的像素级内容并非主要贡献；然而KL诊断显示VT条件作用确实改变了模型分布，说明生成路径本身包含有用的推理信息。这激发了利用生成路径语义知识、同时避免高昂推理成本的需求。

Method: 提出Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (Visual-OPSD)。教师和学生共享相同权重但上下文不同：教师看到特权VT，学生仅看到问题。在学生在策略轨迹上进行token级JSD蒸馏，将教师的推理转移到纯文本学生模型。

Result: 在9个基准上，Visual-OPSD相比生成式教师提升+3.40pp，速度提升14.3倍（每样本10.0s vs 142.8s）；在同规模VLM上VSP任务提升+63.83pp。高斯噪声控制实验（+0.40pp vs 真实VT的+10.28pp）和KL差距闭合58.4%证实收益来自生成路径的语义内容。

Conclusion: 生成路径的语义内容比渲染的视觉像素更重要；Visual-OPSD通过自蒸馏有效提取这一语义知识，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低推理成本。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) interleave generated ''visual thoughts'' (VTs) with text reasoning to improve spatial tasks. This incurs roughly an order-of-magnitude inference cost from multi-step diffusion. We find this cost yields limited direct benefit. On ThinkMorph, removing or noising VTs barely changes accuracy across nine benchmarks. Once rendered, attention concentrates on the VT regardless of content. Yet a KL diagnostic shows that conditioning on a privileged VT trace shifts the model's completion distribution. This suggests the generation pathway encodes useful reasoning beyond the rendered pixels. Motivated by this gap, we propose Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation(Visual-OPSD). Teacher and student share identical weights but differ in context: the teacher sees privileged VTs while the student sees only the question. Token-level JSD distillation on on-policy student trajectories transfers the teacher's reasoning to a text-only student. Across nine benchmarks, Visual-OPSD improves over its generative teacher by $+3.40$pp with $14.3\times$ speedup (10.0s vs. 142.8s per sample) and outperforms same-scale VLMs by $+63.83$pp on VSP. A Gaussian-noise control ($+0.40$pp vs. $+10.28$pp for real VTs) and $58.4\%$ closure of the KL gap confirm that gains come from the semantic content of the generation pathway.

中文摘要翻译: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）将生成的"视觉思维"（VTs）与文本推理交错结合以改进空间任务。这导致了来自多步扩散的约一个数量级的推理成本。我们发现这种代价带来的直接收益有限。在ThinkMorph上，移除或噪声化VT在九个基准上的准确性几乎不变。一旦渲染完成，注意力会集中在VT上，无论其内容如何。然而，KL诊断显示，以特权的VT轨迹为条件会改变模型的补全分布。这表明生成路径编码了超越渲染像素的有用推理。受这一差距启发，我们提出了Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation（Visual-OPSD）。教师和学生共享相同的权重但上下文不同：教师看到特权的VT，而学生仅看到问题。在学生在策略轨迹上进行token级JSD蒸馏，将教师的推理转移到纯文本学生模型。在九个基准上，Visual-OPSD相比其生成式教师提升了+3.40个百分点，同时实现了14.3倍的加速（每样本10.0秒 vs 142.8秒），并且在VSP上比同规模VLM提升了+63.83个百分点。高斯噪声控制实验（+0.40pp vs 真实VT的+10.28pp）以及KL差距闭合了58.4%证实，性能提升来自生成路径的语义内容。

</details>


### [61] [Show, Don't Ask: Generative Visual Disambiguation for Composed Image Retrieval with Turn-Valid Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18992)
*Amsisan Tran,Baogh Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLARA框架，通过向用户展示视觉替代方案面板（而非文本问题）来解决组合图像检索中的模糊性问题，利用保形预测保证多轮交互的覆盖率，并在开放域和时尚基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CIR方法用文本问题解决模糊性存在两个局限：覆盖保证仅在第一轮交互有效，且文本问题难以解决细粒度视觉差异（如外观、属性、视角）。

Method: CLARA框架：1）向用户展示一组视觉原型面板，用户直接选择最接近目标的图像；2）利用用户选择引起的似然比对校准集重新加权，以在多轮交互中维持有效的保形覆盖保证；3）显示的原型受限于当前候选集且来自真实语料库图像，防止生成图像人为提升覆盖率。

Result: 在开放域和时尚基准上，CLARA匹配了单轮最先进的检索性能，在多轮交互中维持了名义覆盖保证，且比强文本问题基线用更少轮次找到目标图像，在处理视角或细粒度属性的模糊性时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 视觉澄清比文本问答在解决细粒度视觉模糊性方面更有效，CLARA框架通过多轮保形预测和视觉选择实现了高效、可靠的组合图像检索。

Original Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) uses a reference image and a text modification to search for a target image. However, such queries often describe several possible images rather than one exact target, making the user's intent ambiguous. Recent methods address this by using conformal prediction to estimate ambiguity and by asking users clarifying text questions. However, these methods have two limitations: their coverage guarantee only holds at the first interaction, and text questions are often insufficient for resolving fine-grained visual differences such as appearance, attributes, or viewpoint. We propose CLARA, a clarification framework that resolves ambiguity by showing users a small panel of visual alternatives. Instead of answering text questions, the user simply selects the prototype image closest to the intended target. This provides a direct visual signal and avoids relying on a model to predict the user's answer. To maintain valid conformal guarantees across multiple interaction rounds, CLARA reweights calibration using the likelihood ratio induced by the user's selection. The displayed prototypes are also constrained to represent the current candidate set and are snapped to real corpus images, ensuring that generated images cannot artificially improve coverage. Experiments on open-domain and fashion benchmarks show that CLARA matches single-turn state-of-the-art retrieval performance, maintains nominal coverage across interaction rounds, and finds the intended target in fewer rounds than strong text-question baselines. Its advantage is especially clear when ambiguity involves viewpoint or fine-grained attributes, where visual clarification is more effective than textual questioning.

中文摘要翻译: 组合图像检索（CIR）使用参考图像和文本修改来搜索目标图像。然而，这类查询通常描述多个可能的图像而非单一确切目标，导致用户意图存在模糊性。近期方法通过使用保形预测来估计模糊性，并向用户提出澄清性文本问题来解决这一问题。但这些方法存在两个局限：其覆盖保证仅在首次交互时成立，且文本问题通常不足以解决细粒度的视觉差异（如外观、属性或视角）。我们提出了CLARA，一个通过向用户展示小型视觉替代方案面板来消除模糊性的澄清框架。用户无需回答文本问题，只需选择最接近预期目标的原型图像。这提供了直接的视觉信号，并避免依赖模型预测用户的答案。为了在多轮交互中维持有效的保形保证，CLARA利用用户选择引起的似然比对校准集进行重新加权。此外，显示的原型受限于当前候选集，且来自真实语料库图像，确保生成的图像无法人为提升覆盖率。在开放域和时尚基准上的实验表明，CLARA匹配了单轮最先进的检索性能，在多轮交互中维持了名义覆盖保证，并且比强文本问题基线用更少轮次找到预期目标。当模糊性涉及视角或细粒度属性时，其优势尤为明显，因为视觉澄清比文本问答更为有效。

</details>


### [62] [FlowObject: Flow Steering for Bridging Generative Priors and Reconstruction Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19019)
*Yuchen Rao,Xuqian Ren,Yinyu Nie,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Biao Zhang,Vincent Lepetit,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowObject 提出了一种无需训练的双空间引导策略，将流匹配生成模型与3D高斯泼溅优化相结合，实现从稀疏视角图像中恢复完整、逼真的3D物体重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型（如流匹配）存在"合成偏差"，生成结果虽完整但缺乏与真实观测的一致性；而优化方法（如3DGS）对可见表面保真度高，但无法推理未观测区域。本文旨在同时实现几何完整性与观测一致性。

Method: 提出FlowObject框架，将稀疏视图3D重建重新定义为无需训练、带引导的逆问题。采用双空间引导策略，在流匹配模型的ODE轨迹上施加约束，利用生成先验补全未观测区域，同时保持与真实观测的一致性；最后通过3DGS精化阶段弥合生成输出与逼真重建之间的差距。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的综合基准测试表明，FlowObject在几何完整性和视角相关外观保真度上显著优于现有的生成模型和基于优化的方法，尤其在严重遮挡场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: FlowObject通过整合生成先验与观测约束，有效解决了稀疏视图3D重建中几何完整性与观测一致性难以兼得的问题，为从少量图像恢复完整3D物体提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Recovering complete 3D representations of objects from few casual image captures remains a significant challenge. Recent 3D generative models, particularly those based on Flow-Matching (FM), can synthesize high-quality textured assets; however, they often suffer from ''synthetic bias'' where learned priors override observational evidence, alongside a lack of alignment with the observed instance. Conversely, optimization-based methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provide high fidelity on visible surfaces but fail to reason about unobserved geometry. In this paper, we present FlowObject, a framework that reformulates sparse-view 3D reconstruction as a training-free, guided inverse problem. Our approach applies a dual-space guidance strategy to steer the Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) trajectory of a flow-matching model, enabling the completion of unseen regions through learned generative priors while enforcing strict consistency with real-world observations. By integrating a 3DGS refinement stage, FlowObject further bridges the gap between ''synthetic-looking'' generative outputs and photorealistic reconstructions. Comprehensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that current state-of-the-art methods often struggle to achieve geometric completeness and observational consistency simultaneously, especially under severe occlusions. In contrast, our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art generative models and optimization-based frameworks in both geometric completeness and view-dependent appearance fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 从少量随意拍摄的图像中恢复物体的完整3D表示仍然是一个重大挑战。最近的3D生成模型，特别是基于流匹配（Flow-Matching, FM）的模型，能够合成高质量的带纹理资产；然而，它们常常遭受"合成偏差"的困扰——即学习到的先验覆盖了观测证据，同时缺乏与观测实例的对齐。相反，基于优化的方法（如3D高斯泼溅，3DGS）在可见表面上提供了高保真度，但无法推理未观测到的几何结构。本文中，我们提出了FlowObject，一个将稀疏视图3D重建重新定义为无需训练的、带引导的逆问题的框架。我们的方法采用双空间引导策略来引导流匹配模型的常微分方程（ODE）轨迹，从而通过学习到的生成先验补全未观测区域，同时强制与真实世界观测保持严格一致性。通过集成3DGS精化阶段，FlowObject进一步弥合了"类合成"的生成输出与逼真重建之间的差距。在合成和真实数据集上的综合基准测试表明，当前最先进方法往往难以同时实现几何完整性和观测一致性，尤其是在严重遮挡情况下。相比之下，我们的方法在几何完整性和视角相关外观保真度上均显著优于最先进的生成模型和基于优化的框架。

</details>


### [63] [Low-Rank Tensor Completion Based on Fractional Regularization with Ky Fan p-k Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19046)
*Shan Fan,Feng Zhang,Jianjun Wang,Xi-Le Zhao,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对低秩张量补全问题，提出了一种新的非凸替代函数——张量核范数与张量Ky Fan p-k范数的比值（TNPK），以更准确地逼近张量管状秩，并开发了高效的ADMM算法，在合成和真实数据集上取得了优越性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有低秩张量补全方法大多使用张量核范数作为凸松弛，但其与真实的张量管状秩之间存在较大偏差，导致恢复精度受限。本文旨在设计一种更能准确逼近张量管状秩的非凸替代函数，同时保持可解性和良好的优化性质。

Method: 提出张量核范数与张量Ky Fan p-k范数的比值（TNPK）作为新的非凸替代函数；在张量零空间性质（NSP）条件下证明低秩张量是所提模型的局部极小值点；推导了Ky Fan p-k逆范数的近端算子，并基于此开发了交替方向乘子法（ADMM）算法，在温和条件下保证子序列收敛性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在性能上优于当前最先进的竞争方法。

Conclusion: TNPK作为一种新的非凸替代函数，能够更准确地逼近张量管状秩，且具有良好的理论性质和优化可行性，为低秩张量补全提供了一种有效的新方法。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses low-rank tensor completion (LRTC) by proposing a novel nonconvex surrogate, namely the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Ky Fan p-k norm (TNPK), to accurately approximate the tensor tubal rank. The TNPK possesses appealing properties, including scale invariance, parameter flexibility, and the existence of closed-form solutions under specific choices of p and k. With specific parameter settings of p and k, it reduces to the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Ky Fan k norm (TNK) or the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Frobenius norm (TNF). We construct a LRTC model and, under the tensor null space property (NSP), prove that low-rank tensors are local minimizers of the proposed model. Moreover, we derive the proximal operator of the Ky Fan p-k inverse-norm and further develop an efficient alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm with guaranteed subsequential convergence under mild conditions. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the superior performance of our method against state-of-the-art competitors.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对低秩张量补全（LRTC）问题，提出一种新颖的非凸替代函数——张量核范数与张量Ky Fan p-k范数的比值（TNPK），以准确逼近张量管状秩。TNPK具有吸引人的性质，包括尺度不变性、参数灵活性以及在特定p和k选择下存在闭式解。在特定的参数设置下，TNPK退化为张量核范数与张量Ky Fan k范数的比值（TNK）或张量核范数与张量Frobenius范数的比值（TNF）。我们构建了一个LRTC模型，并在张量零空间性质（NSP）下证明低秩张量是所提模型的局部极小值点。此外，我们推导了Ky Fan p-k逆范数的近端算子，并进一步开发了一种高效的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）算法，该算法在温和条件下保证子序列收敛性。在合成和真实世界数据集上的大量实验验证了我们的方法相对于最先进竞争方法的优越性能。

</details>


### [64] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Fine-Grained Image Tasks: From Evaluation to Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19053)
*Hong-Tao Yu,Chen-Wei Xie,Yuxin Peng,Serge Belongie,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-BMK，一个包含101万问题和28万图像的大规模细粒度评估基准，用于诊断大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在细粒度图像任务上的不足。实验表明当前LVLM在细粒度识别上表现不足，瓶颈来自视觉表征、语义基础、模态对齐和类别知识等多个方面。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准从整体或任务特定角度评估LVLM，但在细粒度图像任务（计算机视觉基础任务）上的能力尚未得到充分理解，因此需要构建专门评估细粒度能力的基准。

Method: 构建FG-BMK基准（101万问题、28万图像），覆盖从通用物体中心域到专业域的多种场景；通过面向人类和面向机器的范式，联合评估对话级细粒度语义识别和特征级视觉可辨别性；支持诊断分析LVLM失败原因（视觉表征不足、视觉-语义基础薄弱、细粒度知识有限）。

Result: 当前LVLM作为细粒度识别器仍不足，失败源于视觉表征、语义基础、模态对齐和类别知识等多方面交织的瓶颈；分析了训练设计因素对细粒度能力的影响，以及视觉和语言扰动对预测的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果为当前LVLM在细粒度视觉任务上的局限性提供了诊断性见解，并为未来数据构建和模型设计（开发更可靠的LVLM）提供了指导。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal perception and reasoning capabilities. While numerous benchmarks have evaluated LVLMs from holistic or task-specific perspectives, their capabilities on fine-grained image tasks-fundamental to computer vision-remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we introduce FG-BMK, a comprehensive fine-grained evaluation benchmark containing 1.01 million questions and 0.28 million images, covering diverse scenarios from common object-centric domains to specialized domains. FG-BMK jointly evaluates dialogue-level fine-grained semantic recognition and feature-level visual discriminability through human-oriented and machine-oriented paradigms, enabling diagnostic analysis of whether LVLM failures arise from insufficient visual representations, weak visual-to-semantic grounding, or limited fine-grained knowledge. Through extensive experiments on a diverse set of representative LVLMs/VLMs, we find that current LVLMs remain inadequate fine-grained recognizers, with failures arising from intertwined bottlenecks in visual representations, semantic grounding, modality alignment, and category-level knowledge. We further analyze training design factors for improving fine-grained capabilities and examine how visual and linguistic perturbations affect LVLM predictions. These findings provide diagnostic insights into the limitations of current LVLMs and offer guidance for future data construction and model design in developing more reliable LVLMs for fine-grained visual tasks. Our code is open-source and available at https://fg-bmk.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 近期大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的进展展示了显著的多模态感知与推理能力。尽管已有大量基准从整体或任务特定角度评估LVLMs，但它们在细粒度图像任务（计算机视觉的基础任务）上的能力仍未得到充分理解。为填补这一空白，我们提出了FG-BMK，一个综合性细粒度评估基准，包含101万个问题和28万张图像，覆盖从通用物体中心域到专业域的多样化场景。FG-BMK通过面向人类和面向机器的范式，联合评估对话级细粒度语义识别和特征级视觉可辨别性，从而能够诊断分析LVLM的失败究竟是源于视觉表征不足、视觉-语义基础薄弱，还是细粒度知识有限。通过对一系列代表性LVLMs/VLMs的广泛实验，我们发现当前LVLMs作为细粒度识别器仍不充分，其失败源于视觉表征、语义基础、模态对齐和类别知识等多方面交织的瓶颈。我们进一步分析了提升细粒度能力的训练设计因素，并考察了视觉和语言扰动对LVLM预测的影响。这些研究结果为当前LVLMs在细粒度视觉任务上的局限性提供了诊断性见解，并为未来数据构建和模型设计（以开发更可靠的LVLMs）提供了指导。我们的代码已开源，详见https://fg-bmk.github.io/。

</details>


### [65] [DREAM: Extending Vision-Language Models with Dual-Objective Encoding for Cross-Modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19062)
*Kaleem Ullah,Altaf Hussain,Muhammad Munsif,Sung Wook Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM（双路径表示增强与对齐模型），一种通过分层视觉编码器（级联分组注意力）和混合语言建模（掩码+排列语言建模）来增强文本-视频跨模态对齐的框架，在MSRVTT、MSVD和LSMDC基准上取得了新的最先进R1分数（49.4%、49.7%和27.3%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 早期视频检索系统依赖手工特征或浅层跨模态映射，难以捕捉复杂语义和时间动态；现有大规模视觉-语言模型虽改善了跨模态对齐，但在建模细粒度时间依赖关系和细微语言结构方面仍存在挑战。

Method: DREAM包含双路径：1）文本端采用混合语言建模策略，结合掩码语言建模（捕捉局部语义）与排列语言建模（捕捉全局语义）；2）视觉端设计分层视觉编码器，使用级联分组注意力机制，通过多阶段令牌交互和从粗到细的注意力精化，整合空间与时间信息。

Result: 在MSRVTT、MSVD和LSMDC基准上R1分数分别达到49.4%、49.7%和27.3%，均取得新的最先进结果；定性分析表明模型能够在帧间保持连贯注意力，并将复杂查询与动态视频内容对齐。

Conclusion: 分层注意力机制和双目标文本建模能够实现鲁棒的、上下文感知的视频检索，为跨模态表示学习的未来研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: In today's media-driven world, the exponential growth of video content across domains such as surveillance, education, and entertainment has made retrieving semantically relevant videos via natural language queries increasingly critical. Early video retrieval systems relied on handcrafted features or shallow cross-modal mappings, limiting their ability to capture complex semantics and temporal dynamics. While large-scale vision-language models have improved cross-modal alignment, challenges remain in modeling fine-grained temporal dependencies and nuanced linguistic structures. In this paper, we introduce DREAM: Dual-path Representation Enhancement and Alignment Model, a novel multimodal framework that addresses these limitations through enhanced visual and textual encoding. DREAM incorporates a hybrid language modeling strategy that combines masked and permuted language modeling objectives to capture both local and global linguistic semantics. On the visual side, we design a hierarchical vision encoder with cascaded group attention, which integrates spatial and temporal information through multi-stage token interaction and coarse-to-fine attention refinement. We validate DREAM through comprehensive evaluations on the widely-used MSRVTT, MSVD and LSMDC benchmark datasets, where it achieves new state-of-the-art R1 scores of 49.4%, 49.7% and 27.3%, respectively. Qualitative analyses further show the model's ability to maintain coherent attention across frames and align complex queries with dynamic video content. These findings underscore the effectiveness of hierarchical attention and dual-objective textual modeling in enabling robust, context-aware video retrieval, and pave the way for future research in advancing cross-modal representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 在当今媒体驱动的世界中，视频内容在监控、教育和娱乐等领域的指数级增长，使得通过自然语言查询检索语义相关视频变得日益关键。早期的视频检索系统依赖手工特征或浅层跨模态映射，限制了其捕捉复杂语义和时间动态的能力。虽然大规模视觉-语言模型改善了跨模态对齐，但在建模细粒度时间依赖关系和细微语言结构方面仍存在挑战。本文提出DREAM：双路径表示增强与对齐模型（Dual-path Representation Enhancement and Alignment Model），一种通过增强视觉和文本编码来解决上述局限性的新颖多模态框架。DREAM采用了结合掩码语言建模与排列语言建模目标的混合语言建模策略，以捕捉局部和全局语言语义。在视觉方面，我们设计了带有级联分组注意力的分层视觉编码器，通过多阶段令牌交互和从粗到细的注意力精化来整合空间与时间信息。我们在广泛使用的MSRVTT、MSVD和LSMDC基准数据集上进行了全面评估，验证了DREAM的有效性，其R1分数分别达到49.4%、49.7%和27.3%，均取得了新的最先进结果。定性分析进一步表明，该模型能够在帧间保持连贯的注意力，并将复杂查询与动态视频内容对齐。这些发现强调了分层注意力和双目标文本建模在实现鲁棒、上下文感知的视频检索方面的有效性，并为推进跨模态表示学习的未来研究铺平了道路。

</details>


### [66] [Taming I2V models for Image HOI Editing: A Cognitive Benchmark and Agentic Self-Correcting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19073)
*Jiayi Gao,Qingchao Chen,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有图像编辑方法无法处理复杂人-物交互（HOI）的问题，提出了HOI-Edit基准和HOI-Eval自动化评估指标，并发现I2V模型天然适合动态编辑，进而提出SCPE自纠正框架，通过迭代优化提示词约束I2V模型生成，在HOI-Edit上取得与SOTA模型竞争的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法擅长静态属性编辑，但在复杂的人-物交互（HOI）场景下表现不佳；现有基准将HOI与静态属性混为一谈，且依赖全局指标，无法同时评估动态交互的有效性和人-物对的保持。因此需要专门的基准、评估指标和编辑方法来解决HOI编辑问题。

Method: 1）构建HOI-Edit基准，包含三个递进认知层级，并设计HOI-Eval自动化指标，通过让VLM在思考后对包含定位人-物对的图像进行问答来评估实例级交互；2）提出SCPE（自纠正过程编辑）框架，利用I2V模型的时序生成能力，通过迭代优化提示词进行自纠正，约束生成视频更准确地呈现目标HOI，最终提取视频帧作为编辑结果。

Result: SCPE在HOI-Edit基准上，在交互方面的性能与Nano Banana等SOTA编辑模型具有竞争力。此外，I2V模型不仅性能优越，还能提供"失败过程回放"，为错误诊断提供独特可解释性。

Conclusion: I2V模型因其时序生成能力天然适合动态HOI编辑；SCPE自纠正框架通过迭代提示优化能有效提升HOI编辑质量；HOI-Edit和HOI-Eval为该领域提供了可靠的基准和评估工具。

Original Abstract: Current image editing methods excel at static attributes but fail at complex Human-Object Interactions (HOI), a critical challenge unaddressed by existing benchmarks that conflate HOI with static attributes, relying on global metrics incapable of simultaneously assessing dynamic interaction validity and entangled human-object pair preservation. Thus, we first introduce HOI-Edit, a comprehensive benchmark with three progressive cognitive levels, which features an automated metric HOI-Eval that reliably evaluates instance-level interaction by letting VLM Q&A after thinking with images containing grounded Human-Object pairs. Considering the task's essence of remodeling dynamic relationships, we benchmark Image-to-Video (I2V) models, finding them inherently suited for dynamic editing due to their temporal generation capabilities. Crucially, beyond superior performance, this capability provides a "replay of the failure process," offering unique diagnosability into why errors occur. We thus propose SCPE (Self-Correcting Process Editing), a novel, agentic self-correcting framework that constrains the generation of I2V models through iteratively refined prompts, enabling the generated videos to more accurately present the target HOI. Extracted frames from these videos are the final editing results. On HOI-Edit, SCPE achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art (SOTA) editing models like Nano Banana on interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/HOI-Edit.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的图像编辑方法在静态属性上表现出色，但在复杂的人-物交互（HOI）场景中却存在不足。现有基准将HOI与静态属性混为一谈，且依赖全局指标，无法同时评估动态交互的有效性以及人-物对的保持，这是一个亟待解决的关键挑战。为此，我们首先引入了HOI-Edit——一个包含三个递进认知层级的综合性基准，并设计了自动化指标HOI-Eval，通过让视觉语言模型（VLM）在观察包含定位人-物对的图像后进行思考并回答问题，从而可靠地评估实例级交互。考虑到该任务本质上是重塑动态关系，我们对图像到视频（I2V）模型进行了基准测试，发现它们因其时序生成能力而天然适合动态编辑。更重要的是，除了优越的性能外，这种能力还提供了"失败过程回放"，为错误原因的诊断提供了独特的可解释性。基于此，我们提出了SCPE（自纠正过程编辑），一种新颖的、基于智能体的自纠正框架，通过迭代优化提示词来约束I2V模型的生成过程，使生成的视频能够更准确地呈现目标HOI。从这些视频中提取的帧即为最终的编辑结果。在HOI-Edit上，SCPE在交互方面的性能与Nano Banana等最先进的编辑模型具有竞争力。代码已开源：https://github.com/oceanflowlab/HOI-Edit。

</details>


### [67] [PorTEXTO: A European Portuguese Benchmark for Visual Text Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19096)
*João Cardeira,Diogo Glória-Silva,Manuel Letras da Luz,Rafael Ferreira,Diogo Tavares,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PorTEXTO，首个面向当代及文化相关的欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）视觉文本提取基准，发现合成样本到真实世界样本的性能显著下降，且专用多语言数据比模型规模或分辨率更能提升pt-PT性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）在OCR基准测试中严重缺失，现有少量基准集中于历史文献，缺乏面向现代应用的OCR基准。

Method: 采用结合前沿LVLM转录与母语者详尽审核的标注流程构建PorTEXTO基准数据集。

Result: 大多数模型在从合成样本到真实世界样本时性能急剧下降；专用多语言数据对pt-PT性能的驱动作用优于模型规模或分辨率预算。

Conclusion: 当前提升pt-PT OCR性能的关键在于开放专用的多语言数据资源，而非单纯扩大模型或提高分辨率。

Original Abstract: European Portuguese (pt-PT) is largely absent from OCR benchmarks, which skew toward high-resource languages. The few benchmarks that cover pt-PT focus on historical artifacts and literature. This work addresses modern OCR applications, introducing PorTEXTO, the first benchmark for contemporary and culturally relevant pt-PT visual text extraction. To ascertain quality, we employ an annotation pipeline combining transcriptions from a frontier LVLM with exhaustive review by native speakers. We observe a sharp performance drop from synthetic to real world samples in most models, and find that, currently, specialized multilingual data is a better driver for pt-PT performance than model size or resolution budget, motivating the release of open pt-PT OCR resources.

中文摘要翻译: 欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）在OCR基准测试中基本缺席，现有基准偏向高资源语言。少数涵盖pt-PT的基准也集中于历史文物和文学作品。本文针对现代OCR应用，提出了PorTEXTO——首个面向当代及文化相关的欧洲葡萄牙语视觉文本提取基准。为确保质量，我们采用了一种结合前沿LVLM转录与母语者详尽审核的标注流程。我们观察到，大多数模型从合成样本到真实世界样本的性能出现急剧下降，并发现目前专用多语言数据对pt-PT性能的驱动作用优于模型规模或分辨率预算，这促使我们发布开放的pt-PT OCR资源。

</details>


### [68] [DVANet: Degradation-aware Visual-prior Alignment Network for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19097)
*Yanjie Tu,Qingsen Yan,Axi Niu,Tao Hu,Haokui Zhang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DVANet，一种基于半二次分裂优化的深度展开网络，通过退化感知观测一致性与视觉先验引导重建的协同展开，实现复杂退化下的统一图像修复，在多种场景和跨域任务上取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有端到端方法将图像修复视为黑盒映射，缺乏显式优化解释；现有深度展开方法依赖固定退化假设或预定义退化信息，难以适应复杂退化和局部内容损坏下的统一修复需求，限制了退化抑制和结构细节恢复的性能。

Method: 基于半二次分裂优化算法的深度展开网络DVANet，包含两个协同分支：(1) 退化感知观测一致性分支，利用退化表示模块提取全局退化属性和局部退化线索，通过退化条件映射增强对不同退化类型的适应性；(2) 视觉先验引导重建分支，引入DINOv3提供层次化视觉先验（结构+语义），补充受损区域缺失的结构信息以改善细节恢复。

Result: 在多场景退化和跨域图像修复任务上取得优于或可比的性能，展现出良好的退化适应性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DVANet通过将统一图像修复建模为退化感知与视觉先验的协同展开过程，有效解决了复杂退化下的统一修复难题，兼顾了退化适应性与细节恢复质量。

Original Abstract: All-in-One image restoration aims to develop a unified restoration framework for handling diverse degradation types. Existing end-to-end methods usually regard the restoration process as a black-box mapping, lacking an explicit optimization interpretation. Although deep unfolding provides an interpretable iterative modeling paradigm for image restoration, existing methods mostly rely on fixed degradation assumptions or predefined degradation information, making them difficult to adapt to unified restoration requirements under complex degradations and locally damaged content. This limitation restricts their performance in degradation suppression and structural detail recovery. To address these issues, this paper proposes DVANet, a deep unfolding network inspired by the half-quadratic splitting optimization algorithm, which formulates unified image restoration under complex degradations as a collaborative unfolding process between degradation-aware observation consistency and visual-prior-guided reconstruction. Specifically, in the degradation-aware observation consistency branch, a degradation representation module is employed to extract global degradation attributes and local degradation cues, and degradation-conditioned mapping is used to enhance the model's adaptability to different degradation types. In the visual-prior-guided reconstruction branch, DINOv3 is introduced to provide structural and semantic information as hierarchical visual priors, thereby complementing the missing structural information in damaged regions and improving detail recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DVANet achieves superior or competitive performance on multi-scenario degradation and cross-domain image restoration tasks, showing favorable degradation adaptability and generalization ability.

中文摘要翻译: 全合一图像修复旨在开发一个统一的修复框架来处理多种退化类型。现有的端到端方法通常将修复过程视为黑盒映射，缺乏显式的优化解释。尽管深度展开为图像修复提供了一种可解释的迭代建模范式，但现有方法大多依赖于固定的退化假设或预定义的退化信息，难以适应复杂退化和局部内容损坏下的统一修复需求。这限制了它们在退化抑制和结构细节恢复方面的性能。为解决这些问题，本文提出DVANet，一种受半二次分裂优化算法启发的深度展开网络，将复杂退化下的统一图像修复建模为退化感知观测一致性与视觉先验引导重建之间的协同展开过程。具体而言，在退化感知观测一致性分支中，采用退化表示模块提取全局退化属性和局部退化线索，并通过退化条件映射增强模型对不同退化类型的适应性。在视觉先验引导重建分支中，引入DINOv3提供结构和语义信息作为层次化视觉先验，从而补充受损区域缺失的结构信息并改善细节恢复。大量实验表明，DVANet在多场景退化和跨域图像修复任务上取得了优于或可比的性能，展现出良好的退化适应性和泛化能力。

</details>


### [69] [AMALIA-VL: A Native European Portuguese Open-Source Vision and Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19100)
*Diogo Glória-Silva,João Cardeira,Manuel Letras da Luz,Afonso Simplício,Gonçalo Vinagre,Diogo Tavares,Rafael Ferreira,Inês Calvo,Inês Vieira,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMALIA-VL，首个面向欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的原生开源指令微调大视觉语言模型，通过三级训练流程和pt-PT专属多模态数据混合，填补了该语言在多模态模型领域的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的开源多模态大模型严重忽视欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT），要么将其与巴西葡萄牙语混为一谈，要么在训练数据中严重欠代表，导致pt-PT在多模态领域几乎无可用资源。

Method: 提出了一个三级训练流程：(1) 视觉-语言对齐；(2) 通用视觉指令微调；(3) 偏好优化。模型采用高分辨率视觉编码器配合动态图像分块，通过可学习的连接器与完全开源的pt-PT优化语言模型对接。数据方面构建了pt-PT专属多模态数据混合，包含策展数据、翻译公开数据集以及为解决pt-PT多模态资源缺失而新建的数据集。

Result: AMALIA-VL在开放源码pt-PT大视觉语言模型中建立了强基线。评估结果表明其在该语言的多模态任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: AMALIA-VL为欧洲葡萄牙语的多模态AI发展奠定了开源基础，作者将公开模型权重、训练数据、构建流程以及机器翻译的pt-PT评估基准，以推动pt-PT大视觉语言模型的民主化发展。

Original Abstract: Large Vision and Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet European Portuguese (pt-PT) remains systematically underserved by existing open-source multimodal models, which either conflate it with Brazilian Portuguese or severely under-represent it in their training data mixes. We introduce AMALIA-VL, the first open-source instruction-tuned LVLM built natively for pt-PT, pairing a high-resolution vision encoder with dynamic image tiling and a fully open pt-PT-optimized language model via a learned connector. We contribute with a purposefully designed three-stage training process - vision-language alignment, general visual instruction tuning, and preference optimization - together with a pt-PT-centric multimodal data mix combining curated and translated public datasets with novel datasets that address the near-total absence of European Portuguese multimodal resources. Our evaluation shows that AMALIA-VL establishes a strong baseline for open-source pt-PT LVLMs.We will release model weights, training data, and construction pipelines along with machine-translated pt-PT evaluation benchmarks to help democratize pt-PT LVLM development.

中文摘要翻译: 大视觉语言模型（LVLM）发展迅速，但欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）在现有的开源多模态模型中仍系统性地未被充分服务——这些模型要么将其与巴西葡萄牙语混为一谈，要么在其训练数据混合中严重欠代表。我们提出了AMALIA-VL，这是首个原生面向pt-PT构建的开源指令微调大视觉语言模型，通过一个可学习的连接器，将高分辨率视觉编码器与动态图像分块以及一个完全开放的、针对pt-PT优化的语言模型配对。我们贡献了一个精心设计的三阶段训练流程——视觉-语言对齐、通用视觉指令微调和偏好优化——以及一个以pt-PT为中心的多模态数据混合，其中结合了策展和翻译的公开数据集以及为解决欧洲葡萄牙语多模态资源几乎完全缺失而新建的数据集。我们的评估表明，AMALIA-VL为开源pt-PT大视觉语言模型建立了一个强基线。我们将发布模型权重、训练数据和构建流程，以及机器翻译的pt-PT评估基准，以帮助推动pt-PT大视觉语言模型开发的民主化。

</details>


### [70] [ProductConsistency: Improving Product Identity Preservation in Instruction-Based Image Editing via SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19103)
*Mukund Khanna,Raj Singh Yadav,Kunal Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对产品中心图像编辑中对象身份保持困难的问题，构建了ProductConsistency数据集（含87k SFT样本、869个RL样本和评测基准），提出循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward）用于强化学习训练，在Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev上取得了显著的OCR、感知质量和产品一致性提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型在产品中心场景下难以保持产品特征、品牌和文字元素等细粒度对象身份，且缺乏专门针对文本保真度约束的产品图像编辑数据集，导致该问题仅被当作模型的隐式能力来处理。

Method: 构建包含87k监督微调样本和869个强化学习样本的ProductConsistency数据集；提出循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward），通过计算原始产品描述与编辑后图像生成描述之间的语义相似度来强制保持产品身份；基于该数据集对Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev进行微调。

Result: 在OCR和感知指标以及多模态大模型评估中均取得一致性提升；Qwen-Image-Edit-2511模型字符错误率降低5倍。

Conclusion: 提出的数据集和循环一致性奖励方法能有效提升产品中心图像编辑中的产品一致性、文本渲染能力和整体视觉质量。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in instruction-based image editing have enabled models to perform complex visual edits from natural language instructions. However, in product-centric scenarios where preserving product features, branding, and textual elements are critical, current open and closed source models often struggle to maintain this fine-grained object identity. This issue is further compounded by the lack of datasets for instruction-based product image editing with text fidelity constraints, leaving it largely treated as an implicit capability of instruction-based image editing models.
  In this work, we introduce the ProductConsistency dataset which is designed to improve product-centric image editing. Our approach includes a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset of 87k samples for product editing, a reinforcement learning (RL) dataset with 869 unique product images, and a new benchmark dataset, the ProductConsistency Benchmark, to allow rigorous and standardized evaluation of editing models. To guide RL training, we propose a Cyclic Consistency reward that enforces semantic preservation of product identity by using caption similarity between the original product description and captions generated from the edited image. We fine-tune both Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 and Flux.1-Kontext-dev using our dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models in OCR and Perceptual metrics, and MLLM-based evaluations as well, indicating stronger product consistency, text rendering, and overall visual quality; with the Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model achieving a 5x reduction in the character error rate. The code and pipeline is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProductConsistency-6FCC/README.md

中文摘要翻译: 基于指令的图像编辑的最新进展使模型能够根据自然语言指令执行复杂的视觉编辑。然而，在产品中心场景中，保留产品特征、品牌和文字元素至关重要，当前的开源和闭源模型往往难以维持这种细粒度的对象身份。这一问题因缺乏具有文本保真度约束的基于指令的产品图像编辑数据集而进一步加剧，导致该能力在很大程度上被当作指令式图像编辑模型的隐式能力来处理。在本文中，我们引入了ProductConsistency数据集，旨在改进以产品为中心的图像编辑。我们的方法包括一个包含87k样本的产品编辑监督微调（SFT）数据集、一个包含869张独特产品图像的强化学习（RL）数据集，以及一个新的基准数据集ProductConsistency Benchmark，以实现对编辑模型的严格标准化评估。为了指导强化学习训练，我们提出了一种循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward），通过计算原始产品描述与编辑后图像生成的描述之间的字幕相似度来强制保持产品身份的语义。我们使用我们的数据集对Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev进行了微调，并在OCR和感知指标以及多模态大模型评估中均显示出一致的改进，表明产品一致性、文本渲染和整体视觉质量得到了增强；其中Qwen-Image-Edit-2511模型的字符错误率降低了5倍。代码和流程可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProductConsistency-6FCC/README.md获取。

</details>


### [71] [Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset: A Historical Document Dataset for Offline Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition with CRNN-Based Baseline Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19139)
*Ramza Basharat,Muhammad Usman Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对乌尔都语手写文本识别（UHTR）研究滞后的问题，提出了首个专门面向历史时期Katibs书写的离线乌尔都语手写文本行数据集UKHD，并评估了多种CRNN混合模型，发现CNN-BGRU-CTC模型在字符错误率和词错误率上表现最优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 乌尔都语手写文本识别研究相对有限，主要原因在于其草书脚本带来的独特挑战以及基准数据集的稀缺和不可用。为推进该领域研究，亟需构建专门的数据集并探索有效的识别模型。

Method: 构建了名为Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset (UKHD)的专用真实数据集，涵盖Nastalique书法风格中多种平尖笔书写变体；在此基础上评估了多种基于CRNN的混合模型（包括CNN-BGRU-CTC等）以确定最优架构。

Result: 在所分析的模型中，CNN-BGRU-CTC模型表现出更稳健的性能，取得了较低的字符错误率（CER）和词错误率（WER）。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在通过提供专用数据集和最优模型架构，支持和鼓励研究社区开发鲁棒的乌尔都语手写识别系统，以保护乌尔都语手写文学遗产。

Original Abstract: Automatic Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) is inherently a challenging task, and its complexity is further increased when dealing with cursive scripts. Although significant efforts have been made on various cursive scripts, research regarding Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition (UHTR) has been relatively limited. This lag of research is primarily due to the unique challenges posed by its script, and the scarcity and unavailability of benchmark datasets. Therefore, to advance research in UHTR, this study presents a specialized real dataset called the Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset (UKHD). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first offline Urdu handwritten text lines dataset specifically curated from the materials written by Katibs in historical times. It encompasses a diverse range of flat nib writing variations in the Nastalique calligraphic style. Additionally, the effectiveness of different CRNN-based hybrid models has been evaluated to identify the optimal architecture for Urdu Katib Handwriting Recognition (UKHR). Among the analyzed models, the CNN-BGRU-CTC model showed more robust performance, with low Character Error Rate (CER) and Word Error Rate (WER). This research work aims to support and encourage the research community in developing a robust recognition system for preserving Urdu handwritten literature.

中文摘要翻译: 自动手写文本识别（HTR）本质上是一项具有挑战性的任务，当处理草书脚本时，其复杂性进一步增加。尽管在多种草书脚本上已投入了大量工作，但关于乌尔都语手写文本识别（UHTR）的研究仍然相对有限。这种研究滞后主要是由于其脚本带来的独特挑战，以及基准数据集的稀缺和不可用。因此，为了推动UHTR领域的研究，本研究提出了一个名为Urdu Katib手写数据集（UKHD）的专用真实数据集。据我们所知，这是首个专门从历史时期Katibs书写的材料中整理的离线乌尔都语手写文本行数据集。它涵盖了Nastalique书法风格中多种多样的平尖笔书写变体。此外，本研究评估了不同基于CRNN的混合模型的有效性，以确定用于Urdu Katib手写识别（UKHR）的最优架构。在所分析的模型中，CNN-BGRU-CTC模型表现出更稳健的性能，具有较低的字符错误率（CER）和词错误率（WER）。本研究旨在支持和鼓励研究社区开发鲁棒的识别系统，以保护乌尔都语手写文学遗产。

</details>


### [72] [Hand-4DGS: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for 4D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19156)
*Jeongmin Bae,Seoha Kim,Marc Pollefeys,Mahdi Rad,Youngjung Uh,Taein Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hand-4DGS，首个从第一人称视频中直接重建动态4D手部的前馈框架，支持约60 FPS的快速推理和强泛化能力，利用网格引导表示和时间卷积，在H2O和ARCTIC数据集上优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作大多聚焦于多视角3D手部重建或4D人体重建，而第一人称视角下的4D手部重建因头部快速运动、手部动态剧烈、严重遮挡及单视角观测的内在歧义性而极具挑战，亟需一种高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Method: 采用前馈网络框架，结合网格引导表示（mesh-guided representation）提供结构先验，并利用时间卷积建模动态运动，通过高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）实现有效的2D图像监督，无需昂贵的3D手部姿态真实标注。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的第一人称数据集H2O和ARCTIC上评估，相较于基线方法取得了显著提升，实现了约60 FPS的快速推理速度。

Conclusion: Hand-4DGS凭借前馈网络的泛化能力和有效的2D图像监督，在不依赖3D真实标注的情况下实现了高质量的第一人称动态4D手部重建，为AR/VR和AI眼镜等下一代计算平台提供了关键技术支持。

Original Abstract: Dynamic 3D hand reconstruction from egocentric videos is essential for next-generation computing platforms such as AR/VR and AI glasses. Despite its importance, most prior works focus either on multi-view 3D hand reconstruction or on 4D human body reconstruction. Egocentric 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging due to fast head motion, rapid hand dynamics, severe occlusions, and inherent ambiguity from single-view observations. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand-4DGS, the first feed-forward framework for reconstructing dynamic 4D hands directly from egocentric videos, enabling both fast (~60 FPS) inference and strong generalization. Our approach incorporates a mesh-guided representation for structural priors and temporal convolutions to model dynamic motion. We evaluate our framework on two challenging egocentric datasets, H2O and ARCTIC, and demonstrate significant improvements over baselines. Our method benefits from the generalization capability of feed-forward networks and effective 2D image supervision through Gaussian splatting, without requiring expensive 3D hand pose ground-truth annotations.

中文摘要翻译: 从第一人称视频中进行动态3D手部重建对于下一代计算平台（如AR/VR和AI眼镜）至关重要。尽管其重要性不言而喻，但大多数先前的工作要么聚焦于多视角3D手部重建，要么关注4D人体重建。由于头部快速运动、手部动态剧烈、严重遮挡以及单视角观测固有的歧义性，第一人称4D手部重建仍然极具挑战性。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Hand-4DGS，这是首个直接从第一人称视频中重建动态4D手部的前馈框架，实现了快速（约60 FPS）推理和强泛化能力。我们的方法引入了网格引导表示以提供结构先验，并采用时间卷积来建模动态运动。我们在两个具有挑战性的第一人称数据集H2O和ARCTIC上评估了所提出的框架，结果表明相较于基线方法取得了显著改进。我们的方法受益于前馈网络的泛化能力以及通过高斯泼溅实现的有效2D图像监督，且无需昂贵的3D手部姿态真实标注。

</details>


### [73] [When AUC Misleads: Polarization-Aware Evaluation of Deepfake Detectors under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19184)
*Dat Nguyen,Cosmin Radoi,Romain Hermary,Marcella Astrid,Nesryne Mejri,Enjie Ghorbel,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估指标Cross-AUC，用于更真实地衡量深度伪造检测器在域偏移下的泛化性能，该指标结合了每个域的AUC和通过Wasserstein距离量化的预测极化程度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的深度伪造检测评估通常使用各数据集上独立计算的AUC，无法反映真实场景中检测器面对混合数据源和多样化伪影类型的实际情况，缺乏对域偏移鲁棒性的有效衡量。

Method: 提出Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC)指标，通过平均每个域的AUC并结合预测极化度量来考虑域偏移的鲁棒性，其中极化程度由类别分数分布的Wasserstein距离量化。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的实验验证了Cross-AUC的实际相关性，证明其相比传统AUC能更真实地评估检测器在域偏移下的泛化能力，并能更好解释性能下降的原因。

Conclusion: Cross-AUC不仅更真实地评估了深度伪造检测器在域偏移下的泛化能力，而且具有可解释性，能够解释性能下降的原因。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI, such as diffusion models and face-swapping tools, have enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfakes, leading to real-world harms including financial fraud and non-consensual explicit content. In response, deepfake detection has become an active research area, with recent methods increasingly focusing on improving generalization to unseen manipulations. This is typically evaluated using the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) measured separately across multiple datasets. However, such an evaluation fails to reflect real-world scenarios where detectors face a mixture of data sources and varying artifact types. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel metric, Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) that averages per-domain AUCs with a measure of prediction polarization for taking into account the robustness to domain shift. The polarization extent is quantified by the Wasserstein Distance between class score distributions. Cross-AUC not only assesses the generalization capabilities of deepfake detectors under domain shifts more realistically, but it is also interpretable as it better explains the reason behind a drop in performance. Experiments performed on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate its practical relevance.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能（如扩散模型和换脸工具）的最新进展使得高度逼真的深度伪造得以创建，导致了包括金融欺诈和非自愿露骨内容在内的现实危害。为此，深度伪造检测已成为一个活跃的研究领域，近期方法日益关注提高对未见过的篡改操作的泛化能力。这种能力通常通过分别在多个数据集上测量的ROC曲线下面积（AUC）来评估。然而，这种评估方式未能反映检测器面临混合数据源和多样化伪影类型的真实场景。为克服这一局限，我们引入了一种新指标——跨数据集AUC（Cross-AUC），该指标将每个域的AUC与预测极化度量进行平均，以考虑对域偏移的鲁棒性。极化程度通过类别分数分布的Wasserstein距离进行量化。Cross-AUC不仅能够更真实地评估深度伪造检测器在域偏移下的泛化能力，而且具有可解释性，因为它能更好地解释性能下降背后的原因。在七个基准数据集上的实验证明了其实际相关性。

</details>


### [74] [Moebius: 0.2B Lightweight Image Inpainting Framework with 10B-Level Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19195)
*Kangsheng Duan,Ziyang Xu,Wenyu Liu,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Moebius是一个高效轻量级图像修复框架，通过局部-λ混合交互块和自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，以不到2%的参数（0.22B vs 11.9B）和15倍以上的推理加速，达到了与10B级工业模型FLUX.1-Fill-Dev相当甚至更优的修复质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 10B级工业基础模型在图像修复上性能优异，但计算成本过高，难以实际部署。构建轻量级专用模型时，极端结构压缩会导致严重的表示瓶颈，因此需要一种既能大幅压缩参数又能保持高质量修复能力的方法。

Method: 提出Moebius框架：1) 系统重构扩散主干，引入Local-λ混合交互块（LλMI），包含Local-λ和Interactive-λ模块，将空间上下文和全局语义先验总结为固定大小的线性矩阵，在保留复杂潜在交互的同时大幅减少参数；2) 配合自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，在潜在空间内动态平衡多种基于梯度的损失，实现高保真对齐。

Result: 在自然和人像基准测试中，Moebius（0.22B参数）与FLUX.1-Fill-Dev（11.9B参数）相比，生成质量相当甚至更优，参数仅为其不到2%，总推理时间加速超过15倍。

Conclusion: Moebius通过轻量级架构与蒸馏策略的协同优化，证明了极小参数量的专用模型可以在图像修复任务上超越超大规模通用模型，为高效高保真图像修复设立了新的效率标准。

Original Abstract: While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$λ$ Mix Interaction ($LλMI$) block. Comprising Local-$λ$ and Interactive-$λ$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管10B级别的工业基础模型在图像修复领域取得了突破性进展，但其高昂的计算成本严重阻碍了实际部署。构建高度优化的任务专用模型是一个有前景的解决方案，然而极端的结构压缩不可避免地会引发严重的表示瓶颈。为解决这一问题，我们提出Moebius，一个高效的轻量级图像修复框架。我们通过引入局部-λ混合交互块（LλMI）系统性地重构了扩散主干网络。该模块由Local-λ和Interactive-λ子模块组成，巧妙地将空间上下文和全局语义先验总结为固定大小的线性矩阵，在大幅削减参数的同时保留了复杂的潜在交互。此外，为了释放这一高度紧凑架构的完整表示能力，我们将其与自适应多粒度蒸馏策略协同配合。该策略严格在潜在空间内运行以避免昂贵的像素级解码，通过动态平衡多个基于梯度的损失来实现高保真对齐。在自然图像和人像基准上的大量实验表明，这种最优协同使Moebius能够达到甚至超越10B级工业通用模型FLUX.1-Fill-Dev的生成质量。值得注意的是，Moebius仅使用不到2%的参数（0.22B vs 11.9B）即实现了这一性能，同时总推理时间加速超过15倍，为高保真图像修复设立了新的效率标准。项目主页：https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius。

</details>


### [75] [ROSA-TFormer: A Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer for Pinus sylvestris Plantation Classification in Northern Shaanxi Using GEE-Derived Sentinel-1/2 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19204)
*Nengbo Zhang,Chang sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSA-TFormer模型，利用Sentinel-1/2时间序列数据对陕西省北部樟子松人工林进行点级分类，在半月数据上达到99.67%的总体精度和99.56%的宏F1分数，验证了雷达-光学时序融合与传感器感知建模的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 准确识别樟子松人工林对监测陕北地区造林质量和生态恢复具有重要意义，现有方法在充分利用多源遥感时间序列数据方面存在不足，需要一种能够有效融合雷达与光学传感器时序特征的分类模型。

Method: 提出ROSA-TFormer（Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer）模型，包含独立的SAR和光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制以及时序注意力池化模块，利用Google Earth Engine生成的Sentinel-1/2时间序列数据提取多源季节性特征进行分类。

Result: 在月度与半月度点级数据集上实验，HalfMonth-dataBig数据集上总体精度达99.67%，宏F1为99.56%，樟子松F1为98.91%。空间分块验证和消融实验进一步证实了雷达-光学时序融合和传感器感知建模的有效性。

Conclusion: ROSA-TFormer在点级樟子松人工林分类中展现出良好潜力，但仍需更大范围的wall-to-wall验证以进一步确认其泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Accurate identification of Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica plantations is important for monitoring afforestation quality and ecological restoration in northern Shaanxi. This paper proposes ROSA-TFormer, a radar-optical sensor-aware temporal Transformer for P. sylvestris classification using Sentinel-1/2 time-series data generated on Google Earth Engine. The model integrates separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate, and temporal attention pooling to capture multi-source seasonal features. Experiments on monthly and half-month point-level datasets show that ROSA-TFormer achieves strong classification performance, with 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% P. sylvestris F1 on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset. Spatial block validation and ablation results further indicate the effectiveness of radar-optical temporal fusion and sensor-aware modeling. The results demonstrate the potential of ROSA-TFormer for point-level P. sylvestris plantation classification, while broader wall-to-wall validation remains necessary.

中文摘要翻译: 准确识别樟子松人工林对于监测陕北地区的造林质量和生态恢复具有重要意义。本文提出ROSA-TFormer，一种雷达-光学传感器感知时序Transformer模型，利用Google Earth Engine生成的Sentinel-1/2时间序列数据进行樟子松分类。该模型集成了独立的SAR和光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制以及时序注意力池化模块，以捕获多源季节性特征。在月度与半月度点级数据集上的实验表明，ROSA-TFormer取得了强大的分类性能：在半月大数据集（HalfMonth-dataBig）上实现了99.67%的总体精度、99.56%的宏F1分数和98.91%的樟子松F1分数。空间分块验证和消融实验结果进一步证实了雷达-光学时序融合与传感器感知建模的有效性。研究结果展示了ROSA-TFormer在点级樟子松人工林分类中的潜力，但更广泛的wall-to-wall验证仍有必要。

</details>


### [76] [GUMP-Net: An interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19215)
*Liheng Wang,Yinghui Zhang,Licheng Zhang,Hailin Xu,Qiyong Cao,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUMP-Net是一种结合改进测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络的可解释模型-数据混合驱动骨盆分割算法，通过目标检测、边缘检测和迭代演化三个模块实现多类别骨盆精确分割，在小训练数据下表现优异，并推广至踝关节数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 骨盆分割是骨盆骨折精准诊疗和手术规划导航的关键基础问题，现有纯数据驱动深度学习方法可解释性不足且在小样本场景下性能受限，亟需一种兼具可解释性、小样本鲁棒性和高精度的骨盆分割方法。

Method: 提出GUMP-Net，包含三个网络模块：(1) 目标检测模块，用于水平集自动初始化；(2) 边缘检测器模块，学习解剖感知的边缘检测函数；(3) 迭代模块，实现深度水平集演化。该方法将改进的测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络相结合，实现模型驱动与数据驱动的混合范式。

Result: 在骨盆数据集上的大量实验证明了算法的合理性和有效性；相比现有最优方法，GUMP-Net在分割精度、鲁棒性和一致性上表现更优，尤其在小训练数据场景下优势明显；在踝关节数据集上的扩展实验表明该方法可推广到其他解剖结构。

Conclusion: GUMP-Net不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了高效的分割方法，还为理解深度学习分割提供了一个可解释的几何视角，通过水平集表示与深度学习的优势互补实现了更准确鲁棒的多类别骨盆分割。

Original Abstract: Pelvic segmentation is one of the most important and fundamental research problems in precise and intelligent diagnosis and treatment, as well as surgical planning and navigation for pelvic fractures. By combining an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks, we propose GUMP-Net, an interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation, in which three network modules are designed to constitute the overall segmentation framework together: the object detection module for automatic level set initialization, the edge detector module for learning an anatomy-aware edge detector function and the iteration module for deep level set evolution. Leveraging the advantages of level set representation and deep learning, GUMP-Net shows more accurate, robust and consistent segmentation performance, especially in small training data situation, compared to the state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments on pelvic datasets demonstrate the rationality and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. Further experiments extended to ankle dataset indicate broader applications to other anatomies. The proposed algorithm not only provides an efficient segmentation method for complex fracture reduction, but also gives an interpretable geometric perspective for understanding deep learning segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 骨盆分割是骨盆骨折精准智能化诊疗以及手术规划与导航中最重要和基础的研究问题之一。通过将改进的测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络相结合，我们提出了GUMP-Net，一种可解释的模型-数据混合驱动的多类别骨盆分割智能算法。该算法设计了三个网络模块共同构成整体分割框架：用于水平集自动初始化的目标检测模块、用于学习解剖感知边缘检测函数的边缘检测器模块以及用于深度水平集演化的迭代模块。利用水平集表示和深度学习的互补优势，GUMP-Net在分割精度、鲁棒性和一致性方面优于现有最优方法，尤其是在小训练数据场景下。在骨盆数据集上的大量实验证明了所提算法的合理性和有效性。进一步扩展到踝关节数据集的实验表明该方法可推广到其他解剖结构。该算法不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了一种高效的分割方法，还为理解深度学习分割提供了可解释的几何视角。

</details>


### [77] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-I: Spectral Geometry Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19249)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Transformer几何观测站（TGO）框架，系统研究Vision Transformer（ViT）的表征几何与动力学。TGO-I聚焦于谱几何分析，发现ViT训练过程中维度利用率持续增加、各向异性降低、谱熵升高，逐层方差重新分布，尤其是CLS token表征的有效维度最高、各向异性最低，与“训练应将信息集中到少数主方向”的直觉相反。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管Vision Transformers（ViTs）在计算机视觉领域取得了广泛成功，但对其维度几何与表征几何的基础理解仍然相对不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了Transformer几何观测站（TGO）这一系统性实验与分析框架；TGO-I聚焦于ViT表征的谱几何，采用ViT-Small/16模型在ImageNet-100上训练，分析有效秩、稳定秩、参与比、谱熵、谱平坦度、谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱等指标。

Result: 训练过程中维度利用率持续增加，各向异性降低，谱熵升高，参与比增加，特征谱逐渐平坦；方差在表征维度上发生渐进式重新分布；最终CLS token表征在有效维度上最高、各向异性最低。

Conclusion: 与常见的“训练应将信息集中到少数主导方向”的直觉相反，ViT训练过程中表征维度上的方差发生了渐进式重新分布，呈现维度利用率增加、各向异性降低的特征。

Original Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their success across numerous computer vision applications, the fundamental understanding of their dimensional and representational geometry remains relatively underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO), a systematic framework of experiments and analysis pipelines designed to investigate the representational geometry and dynamics of Vision Transformers. TGO-I, the first installment of the framework, focuses on the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, we analyze Effective Rank, Stable Rank, Participation Ratio, Spectral Entropy, Spectral Flatness, Spectral Anisotropy, covariance structure, eigenspectra, and singular value spectra throughout training. Our results reveal a consistent increase in dimensional utilization, accompanied by decreasing anisotropy, increasing spectral entropy, increasing participation ratio, and progressively flatter eigenspectra. Contrary to the common intuition that training should concentrate information into a small number of dominant directions, we observe a progressive redistribution of variance across representational dimensions. This phenomenon is particularly pronounced in the final CLS token representation, which exhibits the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy within the network.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管Vision Transformers（ViTs）已被广泛采用并在众多计算机视觉应用中取得成功，但对其维度几何与表征几何的基础理解仍然相对不足。为填补这一空白，我们引入了Transformer几何观测站（TGO），这是一个由实验与分析流程组成的系统性框架，旨在研究Vision Transformers的表征几何与动力学。TGO-I作为该框架的第一部分，聚焦于ViT表征的谱几何。我们使用在ImageNet-100上训练的ViT-Small/16模型，分析了训练过程中的有效秩、稳定秩、参与比、谱熵、谱平坦度、谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱。我们的结果揭示了维度利用率的持续增加，伴随各向异性降低、谱熵升高、参与比增加以及特征谱逐渐平坦。与“训练应将信息集中到少数主导方向”这一常见直觉相反，我们观察到表征维度上的方差发生了渐进式重新分布。这一现象在最终的CLS token表征中尤为显著，其展现出网络中最高的有效维度和最低的各向异性。

</details>


### [78] [OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19253)
*Bartłomiej Baranowski,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneCanvas将多视图3D场景的补丁特征聚合到单一全景画布上，通过连续经纬度坐标和3D位置编码保留空间信息，无需复杂的几何编码器或大量训练，以极低计算成本实现了SOTA的空间推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM的3D场景理解方法要么依赖复杂的几何编码器，要么需要大量训练预算才能实现空间推理，计算成本高昂且架构复杂。

Method: OneCanvas将各视图的补丁特征通过深度和相机姿态反投影到3D世界坐标，再映射到以画布原点为中心的全景画布的连续经纬度上，添加3D位置编码恢复深度信息，无需光栅化或跨视图特征聚合，预训练VLM像处理普通图像一样消费该表示，并支持从任意姿态中心进行情境化推理。此外，通过程序化地将真实图像中的物体补丁特征放置在空白画布的指定3D位置上，生成空间推理任务的在线监督数据。

Result: 在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上达到SOTA精度，在SPBench上泛化到分布外数据，训练计算量比最强竞品低一个数量级。

Conclusion: OneCanvas通过简单高效的全景画布表示，在不修改骨干网络架构的前提下，以极低计算成本实现了卓越的3D空间推理能力，验证了该表示方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Existing approaches to 3D scene understanding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) either rely on complex, model-specific geometry encoders or large training budgets in pursuit of spatial reasoning. Instead, OneCanvas aggregates patch features from all views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas. Namely, each patch is unprojected to a 3D world coordinate using its depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at the continuous longitude and latitude of that point as seen from the canvas origin, with no rasterization or aggregation across overlapping views. A 3D position embedding of the patch's metric coordinates is added to its feature, restoring the depth lost when collapsing the world position to an angular canvas coordinate. Patches from all frames thus share one spatial coordinate system with no fusion or major architectural modifications of the backbone. The pretrained VLM consumes this representation as if it were an ordinary image. Because the canvas can be centered on any pose of interest, the same representation directly supports situated reasoning from a specific viewpoint, a common requirement in robotics and embodied AI. Thanks to this representation, we can also introduce a spatial pretraining curriculum: by procedurally placing patch features of objects, drawn from real images, at chosen 3D world positions on an otherwise empty canvas, we generate on-the-fly supervision spanning a broad range of spatial reasoning tasks, with answer distributions controlled to reduce spatial reasoning shortcuts. OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, using an order of magnitude less training compute than the strongest competing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的视觉-语言模型（VLM）中3D场景理解方法要么依赖复杂的、特定于模型的几何编码器，要么为了追求空间推理而投入大量训练预算。相反，OneCanvas将所有视图的补丁特征聚合到单个等距柱状全景画布上。具体而言，每个补丁利用其深度和相机姿态被反投影到3D世界坐标，然后放置在画布上该点从画布原点视角所对应的连续经度和纬度位置，无需光栅化或跨重叠视图的聚合。该补丁度量坐标的3D位置编码被添加到其特征中，恢复了将世界位置压缩为角度画布坐标时所丢失的深度信息。来自所有帧的补丁因此共享一个空间坐标系，无需对骨干网络进行融合或重大架构修改。预训练的VLM将这种表示当作普通图像来消费。由于画布可以以任意感兴趣的姿态为中心，同一表示直接支持从特定视角的情境化推理，这是机器人和具身AI中的常见需求。借助这种表示，我们还可以引入空间预训练课程：通过程序化地将从真实图像中提取的物体补丁特征放置在原本空白的画布上选定的3D世界位置，我们在线生成涵盖广泛空间推理任务的监督信号，并通过控制答案分布来减少空间推理捷径。OneCanvas在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上取得了最先进的精度，并在SPBench上泛化到分布外数据，而训练计算量比最强的竞品方法低一个数量级。

</details>


### [79] [CABLE: Cloud-Assisted Bandwidth-efficient LMM-based Encoding for V2X Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19258)
*Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Qianyi Wu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CABLE是一种面向边缘-云端协同感知的带宽高效编码框架，通过自我运动补偿、残差运动线索和走廊包络方法构建鲁棒的兴趣区域（ROI），仅上传ROI掩码图像，形成掩码-ROI-LMM反馈循环，在五个数据集上实现73-87%的ROI像素覆盖减少和5-8倍的LMM预填充加速，同时保持感知性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 云端大模型（LMM）能为车联网（V2X）提供强大的开放词汇感知能力，但直接将全分辨率帧从边缘传输到云端会导致严重的通信开销和云端预填充延迟。

Method: CABLE框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 利用自我运动补偿将前一帧的云分割掩码传播到当前边缘帧；(2) 使用残差运动线索细化传播的掩码；(3) 通过走廊包络方法合并断开的区域，形成鲁棒的ROI。仅上传经ROI掩码后的图像，云端分割输出作为下一帧的先验，形成掩码-ROI-LMM反馈循环。

Result: 在nuScenes、WOD-ZB、Waymo、KITTI和CADC五个数据集上，实现了73-87%的ROI像素覆盖减少，5-8倍的LMM预填充加速，同时检测质量仅有适度下降。

Conclusion: CABLE通过构建高效反馈循环的ROI编码框架，在显著降低通信开销和云端延迟的同时，有效保持了云端LMM的感知性能，为边缘-云端协同感知提供了一种实用的带宽高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Cloud-hosted large multimodal models (LMMs) can provide strong open-vocabulary perception for Vehicle-to-Everything systems, but naively transmitting full-resolution frames from edge to cloud causes severe communication overhead and high cloud-side prefill latency. We present CABLE, a cloud-assisted bandwidth-efficient LMM-based encoding framework for edge-cloud perception. CABLE propagates the previous cloud segmentation mask on the edge using ego-motion compensation, refines it with residual-motion cues, and consolidates disconnected regions via a corridor envelope to form a robust region of interest (ROI). Only ROI-masked images are uploaded, while the cloud segmentation output is fed back as the prior for the next frame, forming a mask-to-ROI-to-LMM feedback loop. Experiments on five datasets (nuScenes, WOD-ZB, Waymo, KITTI, and CADC) show consistent communication savings while largely preserving perception, achieving $73$--$87\%$ ROI pixel-coverage reduction with $5$--$8\times$ estimated LMM prefill speedup at a modest detection-quality trade-off relative to full-frame inference.

中文摘要翻译: 云托管的大规模多模态模型（LMM）可以为车联网（V2X）系统提供强大的开放词汇感知能力，但将全分辨率帧从边缘直接传输到云端会导致严重的通信开销和云端预填充延迟。本文提出CABLE，一种云辅助的、基于LMM的带宽高效编码框架，用于边缘-云端协同感知。CABLE利用自我运动补偿在边缘端传播前一帧的云分割掩码，使用残差运动线索对其进行细化，并通过走廊包络方法合并断开的区域，形成鲁棒的兴趣区域（ROI）。仅上传经ROI掩码后的图像，同时云端分割输出作为下一帧的先验反馈回边缘，形成掩码-ROI-LMM反馈循环。在五个数据集（nuScenes、WOD-ZB、Waymo、KITTI和CADC）上的实验表明，该方法在基本保持感知性能的同时实现了一致的通信节省，相对于全帧推理，ROI像素覆盖减少了73-87%，LMM预填充加速了5-8倍，且检测质量仅有适度折衷。

</details>


### [80] [A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Text-Rich Images from GPT-Image-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19259)
*Yijin Wang,Shuyi Wang,Wenhan Zhang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含8,602张多领域文本丰富图像的基准数据集，用于检测OpenAI GPT Image 2生成的AI图像，评估了五种检测器在零样本设置下的性能，发现检测效果高度依赖领域，且现有方法对JPEG压缩敏感，需开发文本与布局感知的检测方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测基准主要聚焦于以物体为中心的图像，对文本语义和布局组织占主导的场景覆盖有限，缺乏专门针对文本丰富图像的检测基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含8,602张图像、覆盖商业海报、信息图、学术海报、收据、表格和UI截图六个类别的多领域基准数据集；在零样本设置下评估五种代表性AI生成图像检测器，并进行类别维度和后处理鲁棒性分析；同时探索性评估多模态视觉语言模型的检测能力。

Result: 检测器性能高度领域依赖：在某些类别表现良好的方法在其他类别常失效；最强的传统检测器对JPEG压缩表现出严重敏感性；多模态大模型在结构化格式上既有潜力也有局限。

Conclusion: 现有AI生成图像检测方法在文本丰富图像上表现不佳，需开发文本和布局感知的检测方法以应对现代AI生成图像的挑战。

Original Abstract: Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.

中文摘要翻译: 文本丰富的图像通常包含隐私敏感、交易相关或决策关键的信息。随着近年来多模态图像生成模型在合成逼真文本内容和结构化视觉设计方面能力不断增强，检测AI生成的文本丰富图像已成为数字信任和内容真实性领域的重要挑战。然而，现有基准主要聚焦于以物体为中心的图像，对文本语义和布局组织为核心要素的场景覆盖有限。本文提出了一个多领域基准，专门用于检测OpenAI GPT Image 2生成的文本丰富图像。该基准包含8,602张图像，涵盖六个代表性类别：商业海报、信息图、学术海报、收据、表格和UI截图。利用该基准，我们在零样本设置下评估了五种代表性AI生成图像检测器，并分析了它们的整体性能、类别级性能以及后处理鲁棒性。结果表明，检测器性能高度领域依赖：在某些类别表现良好的方法往往在其他类别失效，即使是性能最强的传统检测器也对JPEG压缩表现出严重敏感性。我们还对多模态视觉语言模型进行了探索性评估，揭示了其在结构化格式上的潜力与局限。这些发现凸显了开发文本和布局感知的检测方法以应对现代AI生成图像的必要性。我们的数据集发布于XXX。

</details>


### [81] [A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19277)
*Timothy Agboada,Shikha Chandel,Yadav Raj Ghimire,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RS Adapter的参数高效微调策略，通过在冻结的视觉语言模型骨干网络中注入轻量级瓶颈适配器，以少于5%的可训练参数实现遥感视觉问答任务。实验表明，混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索方面表现最优，为资源受限的遥感VQA场景建立了新基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通用视觉语言基础模型直接应用于遥感VQA面临严重的领域偏移，且全参数微调计算成本过高。现有方法未能有效兼顾参数效率与多模态对齐质量，亟需一种轻量级、可跨架构迁移的微调方案。

Method: 提出RS Adapter，一种统一的结构化手术管线，在冻结骨干网络的注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级瓶颈适配器，仅更新少于5%的参数。该方法分别应用于三种VLM架构：双编码器CLIP、编码器-解码器BLIP和混合架构FLAVA，并在高分辨率RSVQA-x数据集上进行评估。

Result: 所有适配模型均能收敛，其中混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索能力上取得了优于双编码器和编码器-解码器架构的平衡表现，为资源高效的遥感VQA建立了新基线。

Conclusion: RS Adapter以极低的参数量实现了遥感VQA任务的有效迁移，混合VLM架构（FLAVA）相比单模态对应架构更具优势，适用于灾害评估和城市监测等资源受限场景。

Original Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感（RS）领域的视觉问答（VQA）因航拍图像的高分辨率、多尺度目标分布及语义复杂性而面临独特挑战。尽管通用领域的基础模型已取得显著成功，但其直接应用于遥感VQA受限于巨大的领域偏移以及全参数微调的高昂计算成本。本研究对比分析了RS Adapter——一种参数高效微调（PEFT）策略——在三种不同的视觉语言模型（VLM）架构上的表现：双编码器CLIP、编码器-解码器BLIP以及混合架构FLAVA。我们引入了一种统一的架构手术管线，在冻结骨干网络的注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级瓶颈适配器，仅以少于5%的可训练参数实现快速适应。在高分辨率RSVQA-x数据集上的实验结果表明，虽然所有适配模型均实现了收敛，但混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索能力上相比其单模态对应架构提供了更优的平衡。我们的研究结果为灾害评估和城市监测中的资源高效型VQA建立了新基线。

</details>


### [82] [Confidence is Not Reliability: Rethinking MC Dropout in Brain Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19300)
*Xin Ci Wong,Duygu Sarikaya,Kieran Zucker,Marc De Kamps,Nishant Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了MC Dropout在胶质瘤MRI分割中的不确定性估计能力，发现全局不确定性-错误对齐良好（AUROC≈0.97），但增强肿瘤亚区的校准误差（ECE=0.915）和熵值极低（0.054）导致严重误校准，表明仅靠全局指标无法发现对临床关键区域的安全隐患。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分割模型可能在治疗关键亚区静默失败，而Dice等重叠指标无法暴露此类安全隐患。研究者想探究MC Dropout的不确定性估计能否可靠识别临床关键亚区的分割错误，以及校准失败模式是否仅靠标准指标就能检测到。

Method: 基于126例BraTS21患者数据，采用双模型（预训练SegResNet和本地训练的UNet-Res）进行实证案例研究。通过MC Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计，评估熵（H）与分割误差的对齐程度（AUROC），并分析亚区特异性校准误差（ECE）。

Result: MC Dropout保持分割精度（|ΔDice|<0.01），且不确定性-误差对齐良好（AUROC≈0.97）。但UNet-Res在增强肿瘤亚区熵值接近零（0.054），ECE高达0.915，Dice仅0.714，表明严重的误校准在全局指标中不可见。

Conclusion: 强不确定性-误差对齐是临床安全的必要但不充分条件：选择临床部署模型时，除AUROC评估外，必须辅以亚区特异性校准评估。

Original Abstract: Glioma segmentation in multiparametric MRI is a critical component of treatment planning. A segmentation model that fails silently on treatment-critical sub-regions represents a patient safety risk that overlap-based metrics such as Dice scores cannot expose. We ask whether voxel-level uncertainty estimation via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout can reliably identify segmentation errors in clinically critical sub-regions, and whether calibration failure modes are detectable from standard reporting metrics alone. In an empirical two-model case study on 126 BraTS21 patients, we evaluate a high-performance pretrained SegResNet and a locally trained UNet with residual units (UNet-Res). MC dropout preserved segmentation accuracy ($|Δ\text{Dice}|$ $<0.01$) while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC for entropy (H) $\approx$0.97), indicating uncertainty correctly ranks erroneous voxels above correct ones. Entropy-based patient stratification identified a high-uncertainty subgroup with substantially lower segmentation performance (median whole-tumour Dice $0.835$ vs. $0.925$), supporting uncertainty as a practical triage signal. However, global alignment can mask important region-specific differences. Despite similar AUROC, UNet-Res exhibited near-zero enhancing tumour entropy ($0.054$) and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of $0.915$, with a Dice of only $0.714$, indicating severely miscalibrated confidence on the most clinically critical sub-region, a failure mode invisible to standard Dice and AUROC reporting. These findings demonstrate that strong uncertainty-error alignment is necessary but insufficient for clinical safety: sub-region-specific calibration assessment must accompany AUROC evaluation when selecting models for clinical deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 多参数MRI中的胶质瘤分割是治疗计划的关键组成部分。若分割模型在治疗关键亚区静默失败，将构成患者安全风险，而Dice分数等基于重叠的指标无法揭示此类风险。本文探究通过蒙特卡洛（MC）Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计能否可靠识别临床关键亚区的分割错误，以及校准失败模式是否仅从标准报告指标中即可检测。在126例BraTS21患者上开展的双模型实证案例研究中，我们评估了高性能预训练SegResNet和本地训练的残差UNet（UNet-Res）。MC Dropout在保持分割精度的同时（|ΔDice|<0.01），实现了强不确定性-误差对齐（熵H的AUROC≈0.97），表明不确定性能够正确地将错误体素排序在正确体素之上。基于熵的患者分层识别出一个高不确定性亚组，其分割性能显著较低（全肿瘤Dice中位数0.835 vs. 0.925），支持不确定性作为实用的分诊信号。然而，全局对齐可能掩盖重要的区域特异性差异。尽管AUROC相似，UNet-Res的增强肿瘤熵值接近零（0.054），期望校准误差（ECE）高达0.915，Dice仅为0.714，表明在最关键的临床亚区存在严重误校准——这是标准Dice和AUROC报告无法发现的失效模式。这些发现表明，强不确定性-误差对齐是临床安全的必要但不充分条件：在选择临床部署模型时，亚区特异性校准评估必须与AUROC评估相结合。

</details>


### [83] [NeuMesh++: Towards Versatile and Efficient Volumetric Editing with Disentangled Neural Mesh-based Implicit Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19316)
*Chong Bao,Yuan Li,Bangbang Yang,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui,Yinda Zhang,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NeuMesh++，一种基于网格的隐式神经表示方法，通过在网格顶点上编码解耦的几何、纹理和语义特征，实现几何编辑、纹理编辑和语义引导编辑等多种功能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经渲染编辑方法功能有限，仅支持刚性变换和类别特定编辑，无法实现灵活、全面的编辑操作。

Method: 提出网格基表示，在网格顶点上编码解耦的几何、纹理和语义码；开发局部空间参数化提升渲染质量和训练稳定性；引入可学习顶点修改颜色提高纹理编辑保真度；设计空间感知优化策略实现精确纹理编辑；采用语义辅助区域选择简化隐式场编辑标注。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验和编辑示例表明，该方法在表示质量和编辑能力上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: NeuMesh++通过解耦编码和多项技术改进，实现了更全面、高效的神经隐式场编辑功能，在表示质量和编辑灵活性方面具有显著优势。

Original Abstract: Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，神经隐式渲染技术发展迅速，在新视角合成和三维场景重建中展现出显著优势。然而，现有用于编辑目的的神经渲染方法功能有限，例如仅支持刚性变换和类别特定编辑。在本文中，我们提出一种新颖的基于网格的表示方法，通过在网格顶点上编码解耦的几何、纹理和语义码来增强神经辐射场，从而支持一套高效且全面的编辑功能，包括网格引导的几何编辑、通过纹理交换、填充和绘制操作实现的指定纹理编辑，以及语义引导的编辑。为此，我们开发了多项技术，包括一种新颖的局部空间参数化以提升渲染质量和训练稳定性，一种可学习的顶点修改颜色以提高纹理编辑的保真度，一种空间感知优化策略以实现精确的纹理编辑，以及一种语义辅助的区域选择以简化隐式场编辑中繁琐的标注工作。在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验和编辑示例表明，我们的方法在表示质量和编辑能力上均具有优越性。项目页面：https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

</details>


### [84] [Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19338)
*Shengyuan Ding,Xilin Wei,Xinyu Fang,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了RNG-Bench基准测试套件，包含Matching Pairs和3D Maze两个互补游戏，用于隔离评估多模态大模型在闭环策略中基于历史观测进行决策的能力。通过难度控制、对决协议和Memory Gap指标分析，发现模型错误主要源于遗忘而非决策失误，且微调Qwen3.5-9B可提升性能并泛化到现有基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准要么暴露完整状态（混淆了隐状态重建与其他技能），要么仅在回合结束后测试记忆，无法隔离评估基础模型在闭环策略中重建历史观测并据此行动的能力。需要一套专门设计的基准来精确衡量这一能力。

Method: 1) 提出RNG-Bench基准套件，包含两个互补游戏：Matching Pairs（记忆位置-身份匹配）和3D Maze（整合第一人称视角构建空间地图）。2) 设计三个可控难度轴：网格大小、视觉模式、观测模态。3) 引入head-to-head对决协议控制实例级方差。4) 提出Memory Gap指标，分离遗忘与动作选择错误。5) 在最优策略轨迹和过滤后的模型演示上微调Qwen3.5-9B。

Result: 最困难配置每回合约需128K tokens和350张图像输入，前沿多模态大模型远未饱和。Memory Gap分析表明大多数残留错误源于遗忘早期观测而非次优决策。微调Qwen3.5-9B可提升RNG-Bench性能，并泛化到现有基准，且不损害通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: RNG-Bench能有效隔离评估模型的记忆-决策能力，揭示了当前多模态大模型在长程记忆上的根本瓶颈，并为通过微调缓解遗忘提供了可行路径。

Original Abstract: Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.

中文摘要翻译: 将多模态基础模型部署为闭环策略时，越来越多地需要基于已不可见的观测信息来调节动作。然而，现有基准要么暴露完整状态（将隐状态重建与其他智能体技能混为一谈），要么仅在回合结束后测试记忆。我们引入了RNG-Bench（Reconstructive Non-Markov Games，重构性非马尔可夫博弈），一个旨在隔离基础模型在多步交互过程中重建历史观测并据此行动能力的基准套件。RNG-Bench包含两个互补游戏：匹配对（Matching Pairs），其中需要记住特定位置短暂揭示的卡片身份；以及3D迷宫（3D Maze），其中需要将第一人称视角视图整合为空间地图。两个游戏均在统一框架下进行评估，具有三个受控难度轴：网格大小、视觉模式和观测模态。该基准进一步引入了头对头对决协议以控制实例级方差，以及一个记忆鸿沟（Memory Gap）指标，该指标将遗忘与动作选择不当区分开来。最困难的配置每回合需要约128K个token和350张图像输入，前沿多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）远未达到饱和。Memory Gap分析表明，大多数残留错误源于遗忘早期观测，而非次优决策。最后，在最优策略轨迹和过滤后的模型演示上微调Qwen3.5-9B，可提升其在RNG-Bench上的性能，并泛化到现有基准，同时不损害通用多模态能力。

</details>


### [85] [Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19341)
*Zhenghao Xing,Ruiyang Xu,Yuxuan Wang,Jinzheng He,Ziyang Ma,Qize Yang,Yunfei Chu,Jin Xu,Junyang Lin,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniAgent是首个原生全模态代理，将视频理解建模为基于POMDP的迭代观测-思考-行动循环，通过按需行动选择性提取音视频线索到持久文本记忆，将推理复杂度与原始视频时长解耦。结合Agentic SFT和带TAURA的Agentic RL，在10个基准上达到开源SOTA，7B模型在LVBench上超越10倍大的Qwen2.5-VL-72B。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频理解的被动范式需统一处理所有帧，计算成本随视频时长线性增长；而交互式框架依赖全局预扫描，上下文成本仍与视频长度成正比。亟需一种能根据查询难度自适应调度计算资源、将推理复杂度与视频长度解耦的方法。

Method: (1) 将视频理解建模为POMDP下的迭代观测-思考-行动(Observation-Thought-Action)循环，智能体按需执行动作，选择性将音视频线索蒸馏到持久文本记忆中；(2) Agentic SFT：通过最佳N轨迹合成和双阶段质量控制引导原生主动感知；(3) Agentic RL with TAURA：利用回合级熵值重新缩放优势函数，将信用分配引导至关键发现回合。

Result: 在10个基准（如VideoMME、LVBench）上达到开源模型SOTA；在LVBench上，7B参数模型以50.5%超越364B（Qwen2.5-VL-72B）的47.3%。模型表现出正向测试时扩展：推理回合数增加时性能持续提升。

Conclusion: OmniAgent通过主动感知范式有效解耦了推理复杂度与视频时长，验证了"边看边思考"优于"一次性看完"的策略，且首次在长视频理解中实现正向测试时扩展，为可交互的视频理解系统提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).

中文摘要翻译: 长视频理解中的被动模型通常依赖"全量观看"范式，即无论查询难度如何都统一处理所有帧，导致计算成本随视频时长增长。虽然交互式框架已经出现，但它们往往依赖全局预扫描，其上下文成本仍与视频长度成正比。我们提出OmniAgent——首个原生全模态智能体，将视频理解建模为基于POMDP的迭代"观测-思考-行动"循环。OmniAgent按需执行动作，选择性地将音视频线索蒸馏到持久文本记忆中，有效将推理复杂度与原始视频时长解耦。为实现这一目标，我们引入了(1) 智能体监督微调（Agentic SFT），通过最佳N轨迹合成与双阶段质量控制系统引导原生主动感知能力；以及(2) 基于TAURA的智能体强化学习（Agentic RL），利用回合级熵值重新缩放优势函数，将信用分配引导至关键发现回合。关键在于，OmniAgent展现出正向测试时扩展特性，即随着推理回合数增加性能持续提升，验证了主动感知的有效性。在十个基准（如VideoMME、LVBench）上的实验结果表明，OmniAgent在开源模型中达到了最先进的性能。值得注意的是，在LVBench上，我们的7B智能体以50.5%的准确率超越了10倍参数量的Qwen2.5-VL-72B（47.3%）。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>
