Motivation: 标准MDP将信息演化与动作可行性扁平化为阶段相关的状态描述和动作掩码，忽略了信息-动作之间的嵌套不对称性（即信息逐渐丰富的同时可行动作因截止时间、承诺或资源约束而过期），而这种不对称性决定了哪些决策是紧急的、哪些可以推迟。

Method: 提出Maturing Markov Decision Processes (MMDPs)形式化建模信息-动作不对称性；推导了过期动作优先级原则；设计了结构感知的强化学习框架，包括阶段感知的策略设计、过期动作抽象、以及基于搜索增强与蒸馏的学习方法。

Result: 在多供应商补货问题、简化的现金管理环境（复杂度递增）以及生产级模拟器上的实验表明，显式建模信息-动作不对称性能够提高学习效率，并且随着决策问题规模增大，这种收益越来越显著。

Conclusion: MMDPs能够有效捕获序列决策中的信息-动作不对称结构，基于此设计的结构感知强化学习方法在多个任务上优于传统扁平化MDP方法，且在大规模问题中优势更为突出。

Original Abstract: Sequential decision problems often exhibit an asymmetric evolution of information and decision flexibility: as a decision cycle unfolds, the agent receives richer information while feasible actions expire due to operational cutoffs, commitments, or resource constraints. Standard MDP formulations typically flatten this structure into stage-dependent state descriptions and action masks, thereby obscuring the nested information--action asymmetry that determines which decisions are urgent and which can be deferred. We introduce Maturing Markov Decision Processes (MMDPs), a formulation built around this information--action asymmetry. We characterize one of its key consequences through an expiring-action priority principle, which identifies the actions that must be resolved before the next stage. Motivated by this structure, we develop a structure-aware reinforcement learning framework with stage-aware policy design, expiring-action abstraction, and search-augmented learning with distillation. Experiments on a controlled multi-supplier replenishment problem, simplified cash-management environments of increasing complexity, and a production-scale simulator show that explicitly modeling this asymmetry improves learning efficiency and becomes increasingly valuable as decision problems scale.

中文摘要翻译: 序列决策问题通常呈现出信息演化与决策灵活性的非对称性：随着决策周期的展开，智能体获得更丰富的信息，同时由于运营截止时间、承诺或资源约束，可行动作逐渐过期。标准的MDP公式通常将这种结构扁平化为阶段依赖的状态描述和动作掩码，从而掩盖了决定哪些决策紧急、哪些可以推迟的嵌套式信息-动作不对称性。我们提出了成熟马尔可夫决策过程（MMDPs），这是一个围绕这种信息-动作不对称性构建的公式。我们通过一个过期动作优先级原则来刻画其关键后果之一，该原则识别出必须在下一阶段之前解决的动作。受此结构启发，我们开发了一个结构感知的强化学习框架，包含阶段感知的策略设计、过期动作抽象，以及带有蒸馏的搜索增强学习。在受控的多供应商补货问题、复杂度递增的简化现金管理环境以及生产级模拟器上的实验表明，显式建模这种不对称性能够提高学习效率，并且随着决策问题规模的扩大，这种收益变得越来越有价值。

</details>


### [176] [GateMem: Benchmarking Memory Governance in Multi-Principal Shared-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18829)
*Zhe Ren,Yibo Yang,Yimeng Chen,Zijun Zhao,Benshuo Fu,Zhihao Shu,Bingjie Zhang,Yangyang Xu,Dandan Guo,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GateMem是一个针对多主体共享记忆智能体的基准测试，评估其在合法长程请求、跨上下文权限访问控制和主动遗忘三个维度的表现。实验发现现有方法无法同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘，表明当前记忆智能体远未达到可靠的机构部署要求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆基准大多假设单用户场景，忽略了医院、 workplaces、校园和家庭等需要多主体共享记忆的实际部署场景。在这些场景中，多个主体向公共记忆池写入信息并在不同角色、范围和关系下查询，记忆质量不仅需要召回能力还需要治理机制。

Method: GateMem基准测试，覆盖医疗、办公、教育和家庭四个领域，包含长篇幅多方对话、增量记忆注入、隐藏检查点、结构化评判和泄漏目标标注。联合评估三个维度：(1) 带有状态更新的合法长程请求效用；(2) 跨上下文授权边界的访问控制；(3) 显式删除请求后的主动遗忘。

Result: 在多种基线和骨干模型上，没有任何方法能同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘。长上下文提示在治理得分上最优但token成本高；基于检索和外部记忆的方法降低了成本，但仍会泄漏未授权或已删除的信息。

Conclusion: 当前记忆智能体在共享机构部署场景下远未达到可靠水平，多主体共享记忆场景下的治理问题亟待解决。

Original Abstract: Memory benchmarks for LLM agents largely assume single-user settings, leaving shared assistants for hospitals, workplaces, campuses, and households understudied. In these deployments, multiple principals write to a common memory pool and query it under different roles, scopes, and relationships, so memory quality requires governance as well as recall. We introduce GateMem, a benchmark for multi-principal shared-memory agents. GateMem jointly evaluates utility for legitimate long-horizon requests with state updates, access control across contextual authorization boundaries, and agent-facing active forgetting after explicit deletion requests. It spans medical, office, education, and household domains, with long-form multi-party episodes, incremental memory injection, hidden checkpoints, structured judging, and leak-target annotations. Across diverse baselines and backbone models, no method simultaneously achieves strong utility, robust access control, and reliable forgetting. Long-context prompting often yields the best governance score at high token cost, while retrieval-based and external-memory methods reduce cost yet still leak unauthorized or deleted information. These results show current memory agents remain far from reliable shared institutional deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 面向LLM智能体的记忆基准测试大多假设单用户场景，这使得医院、工作场所、校园和家庭中共享助手的场景缺乏研究。在这些部署环境中，多个主体向公共记忆池写入信息，并在不同角色、范围和关系下进行查询，因此记忆质量不仅需要召回能力，还需要治理机制。我们提出了GateMem，一个面向多主体共享记忆智能体的基准测试。GateMem联合评估了三个维度：带有状态更新的合法长程请求的效用、跨上下文授权边界的访问控制，以及显式删除请求后的主动遗忘。该基准覆盖医疗、办公、教育和家庭四个领域，包含长篇幅多方对话、增量记忆注入、隐藏检查点、结构化评判和泄漏目标注释。在多种基线和骨干模型上，没有任何方法能同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘。长上下文提示通常能以高token成本获得最佳治理得分，而基于检索和外部记忆的方法虽然降低了成本，但仍会泄漏未授权或已删除的信息。这些结果表明，当前记忆智能体远未达到可靠的共享机构部署要求。

</details>


### [177] [Target-confidence Recourse Using tSeTlin machines: TRUST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18832)
*K. Darshana Abeyrathna,Sara El Mekkaoui,Nils Enric Canut Taugbøl,Anuja Vats*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRUST框架，通过用户指定的置信度目标生成反事实解释，结合概率Tsetlin机器与贝叶斯优化，在保证鲁棒性的同时实现低成本、可解释的算法补救。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法仅关注最小化输入改动以翻转模型决策，但忽略预测置信度和风险边际，导致生成的补救建议在噪声或模型变化下脆弱且不稳定。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，用户明确指定期望的预测置信度；采用概率Tsetlin机器（PTM）结合贝叶斯优化，直接搜索满足置信度目标的最小输入变化，通过子句激活的稳定性评估决策可靠性。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，TRUST生成的置信度目标反事实比传统边界方法更鲁棒和可解释；在多个基准上实现完美鲁棒性且保持低补救成本，如Haberman数据集上L2距离0.10、置信度0.92。

Conclusion: 通过显式控制置信度并揭示规则级稳定性，TRUST为高风险决策支持提供了可操作的、鲁棒的算法补救方案。

Original Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are widely used to provide algorithmic recourse in high-stakes decision-making systems. Most existing methods seek the smallest change to an input that flips a model's decision. However, decision-makers often rely not only on predicted labels but also on confidence thresholds and risk margins. Counterfactuals that barely cross a decision boundary can be fragile and unstable under noise or model variation.
  In this paper, we propose Target-confidence Recourse Using tSeTlin machines (TRUST), a framework in which users explicitly specify the desired prediction confidence for recourse. Rather than generating counterfactuals and evaluating confidence afterward, TRUST directly searches for minimal changes that satisfy a user-defined confidence target, enabling comparison of recourse options in terms of cost, confidence, and robustness.
  We instantiate TRUST using a Probabilistic Tsetlin Machine (PTM) combined with Bayesian optimization. The probabilistic clause-based structure of PTM links prediction confidence to the stability of decision rules. We show that counterfactuals satisfying the same rules can still differ substantially in reliability depending on how securely they satisfy those rules, revealing whether decisions are supported by robust or fragile clause activations.
  Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that target-confidence counterfactuals produce more robust and interpretable recourse than conventional boundary-based approaches. Across multiple benchmarks, TRUST achieves perfect robustness while maintaining low recourse cost, including an L2 distance of 0.10 on the Haberman dataset at 0.92 confidence. By explicitly controlling confidence and exposing rule-level stability, TRUST provides actionable recourse for high-stakes decision support.

中文摘要翻译: 反事实解释被广泛用于为高风险决策系统提供算法补救。大多数现有方法寻求输入的最小更改以翻转模型的决策。然而，决策者通常不仅依赖预测标签，还依赖置信度阈值和风险边际。那些仅仅跨越决策边界的反事实在噪声或模型变化下可能脆弱且不稳定。在本文中，我们提出了基于Tsetlin机器的目标置信度补救（TRUST）框架，该框架允许用户显式指定所需的预测置信度以进行补救。TRUST并非先生成反事实再评估置信度，而是直接搜索满足用户定义置信度目标的最小更改，从而能够在成本、置信度和鲁棒性方面比较补救选项。我们使用概率Tsetlin机器（PTM）结合贝叶斯优化来实例化TRUST。基于概率子句的PTM结构将预测置信度与决策规则的稳定性联系起来。我们表明，满足相同规则的反事实在可靠性上可能存在显著差异，具体取决于它们满足这些规则的安全程度，从而揭示决策是由稳健还是脆弱的子句激活所支持。在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，目标置信度反事实比传统的基于边界的方法产生更鲁棒且更可解释的补救。在多个基准上，TRUST实现了完美鲁棒性，同时保持低补救成本，包括在Haberman数据集上以0.92置信度达到0.10的L2距离。通过显式控制置信度并揭示规则级稳定性，TRUST为高风险决策支持提供了可操作的补救方案。

</details>


### [178] [Seed-Guided Semi-Supervised Clustering by A-Contrario Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18833)
*Nassir Mohammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于统计对偶性的半监督聚类框架，利用“聚类即异常检测的逆过程”思想，通过感知算法(Perception)和种子引导的“逐次排除”机制，在无需手动调参的情况下实现鲁棒聚类，仅需少量标注种子即可达到优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有聚类方法在噪声环境中面临挑战：划分聚类易将离群点强制归入簇，密度聚类对全局参数敏感且依赖人工调参。亟需一种鲁棒、参数自适应的聚类方法。

Method: 基于反证统计推理(a-contrario)和格式塔邻近原则，将簇定义为相对于均匀随机零假设不含异常的最大子集。核心是Perception算法：利用期望阈值(E<1)无参数地识别离群点。采用“聚类即异常检测对偶”的迭代“逐次排除”机制：种子引导初始化簇中位数，然后逐步吸纳非异常点，自然分离边缘点、孤立噪声和未知新簇。

Result: 在合成数据和真实基准（图像、文本数据集，包括原始、线性降维和邻域保持嵌入表示）上，每簇仅需10-30个种子即可达到有竞争力甚至很强的性能，且对观测数和维度具有线性可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的半监督聚类框架有效结合了统计对偶性与最小人工标注，在低调参条件下实现了鲁棒、可扩展的聚类性能。

Original Abstract: This paper introduces a semi-supervised clustering framework grounded in the statistical duality between grouping principles and anomaly detection. We address the challenge of robust cluster definition in noisy environments -- a task where partitioning algorithms often over-assign outliers and density-based methods remain sensitive to heuristic global parameters. Drawing on \textit{a-contrario} statistical reasoning and Gestalt proximity principles, we define a cluster as a maximal subset of data points containing no anomalies relative to a null hypothesis of uniform randomness. Central to this approach is the Perception algorithm, which utilises a principled expectation-based threshold ($\mathbb{E} < 1$) to identify outliers without manual parameter tuning. By treating clustering as the dual of anomaly detection, we employ an iterative ``clustering-by-exclusion'' mechanism. The algorithm is seed-guided, leveraging minimal user-provided labels to initialise robust cluster medians and form initial groups, which are subsequently expanded by admitting non-anomalous points. This approach naturally isolates fringe points, isolated noise, and emerging unknown clusters. We evaluate the method on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including image and text datasets represented through raw, linear-reduced, and neighbourhood-preserving embeddings. Results demonstrate that with as few as 10--30 seeds per cluster, the proposed method achieves competitive and often very strong performance under a practical low-tuning benchmarking protocol, while maintaining linear scalability with respect to both observations and dimensionality for a fixed number of seeded clusters and iterations.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于分组原则与异常检测之间统计对偶性的半监督聚类框架。我们解决噪声环境下鲁棒簇定义的挑战——在该任务中，划分算法常过度分配离群点，而密度方法仍对启发式全局参数敏感。基于反证统计推理(a-contrario)和格式塔邻近原则，我们将簇定义为相对于均匀随机零假设不含异常的最大数据点子集。该框架的核心是Perception算法，它利用基于期望的原理性阈值(E<1)在无需手动参数调优的情况下识别离群点。通过将聚类视为异常检测的对偶问题，我们采用迭代的"逐次排除"聚类机制。该算法由种子引导，利用最少的用户提供标签来初始化鲁棒的簇中位数并形成初始分组，随后通过接纳非异常点来逐步扩展。该方法自然地分离边缘点、孤立噪声和新兴未知簇。我们在合成数据和真实世界基准上评估了该方法，包括通过原始、线性降维和邻域保持嵌入表示的图像和文本数据集。结果表明，在每簇仅需10-30个种子的条件下，所提方法在实用的低调参基准协议下达到了有竞争力且通常非常强劲的性能，同时对于固定数量的种子簇和迭代次数，在观测数和维度上均保持线性可扩展性。

</details>


### [179] [Identifying Structural Biases from Causal Mechanism Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18834)
*Praharsh Nanavati,Jilles Vreeken,David Kaltenpoth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从因果机制变化中识别隐混淆和选择偏差的方法，通过机制变化的依赖性来区分不同类型的结构偏差，并引入StruBI算法实现有效检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果发现方法通常假设数据独立同分布且无未测量变量，但实际中这些假设常被违反，导致推断不准确。因此，如何在数据分布变化的情况下识别隐混淆和选择偏差成为关键问题。

Method: 本文证明结构偏差会导致依赖性的机制变化，即通过分析不同环境下变量机制的变化情况，可以区分无偏变量、受隐混淆影响的变量和经历选择偏差的变量。将这一准则形式化为基于互信息的可经验检验的标准，并引入StruBI算法来识别哪些节点受到何种偏差。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，StruBI算法能够准确恢复受影响的变量集和偏差类型，性能大幅超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于机制变化依赖性的方法能有效识别因果推断中的隐混淆和选择偏差，StruBI算法在实践中表现优异。

Original Abstract: Causal discovery methods commonly assume that all data is independently and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and that there are no unmeasured variables affecting the system. In practice, these assumptions are often violated, leading to inaccurate inference. In this paper, we study how to identify hidden confounding and selection biases from causal mechanism shifts. In particular, we show that structural biases lead to dependent mechanism shifts. That is, by considering for which variables the mechanisms change given data from different environments, we can tell which variables are unbiased, which are subject to hidden confounding, and which are undergoing selection bias. We formalize this into an empirically testable criterion based on mutual information, and show under which conditions it identifies structural biases. To tell which nodes are subject to what kind of bias, we introduce the StruBI algorithm. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that StruBI works well in practice, accurately recovering affected variable sets and types of biases, outperforming the state-of-the-art by a wide margin.

中文摘要翻译: 因果发现方法通常假设所有数据是独立同分布的（i.i.d.），并且不存在影响系统的未测量变量。然而在实践中，这些假设常常被违反，导致推断结果不准确。本文研究了如何从因果机制变化中识别隐混淆和选择偏差。具体而言，我们证明结构偏差会导致依赖性的机制变化。也就是说，通过考虑在来自不同环境的数据下哪些变量的机制发生了变化，我们可以判断哪些变量是无偏的、哪些变量受到隐混淆的影响、以及哪些变量经历了选择偏差。我们将这一准则形式化为一个基于互信息的可经验检验的标准，并说明在何种条件下它可以识别结构偏差。为了区分哪些节点受到何种偏差，我们引入了StruBI算法。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，StruBI算法在实践中表现良好，能够准确恢复受影响的变量集和偏差类型，其性能大幅超越现有最优方法。

</details>


### [180] [Semantic Robustness Certification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18839)
*Peiyu Yang,Paul Montague,Feng Liu,Andrew C. Cullen,Amardeep Kaur,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对视觉-语言模型(VLM)在语义级分布偏移下的鲁棒性认证框架，利用文本提示作为语义代理，以闭式表征决策边界，量化认证模型预测在语义变换下保持不变的程度区间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实应用中VLM常面临语义层面的分布偏移（如形状、尺寸、风格等），现有鲁棒性认证框架主要处理几何或像素级变换，缺乏针对语义级变换的认证方法。

Method: 利用VLM的开放词汇能力，以文本提示作为语义代理构建参数化语义变换；通过闭式形式刻画VLM决策边界，定量认证预测类别保持不变的程度区间。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该框架能够在多种场景下对多样的语义变化进行鲁棒性认证，且无需为每种变化额外收集数据。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了VLM在语义级变化下的鲁棒性认证，具有实用性和通用性。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are now widely used in downstream tasks. However, real-world applications often expose VLMs to distribution shifts induced by semantic variation (e.g., shape, size, and style). Robustness certification determines if a model's prediction changes when transformations are applied to its input. While most certification frameworks study geometric or pixel-level transformations over inputs, this work proposes a novel framework that enables certifying VLM robustness under semantic-level transformations. Leveraging the open-vocabulary capability of VLMs, we use text prompts as semantic proxies to construct transformations parameterized by an extent that controls the degree of semantic variation. By characterizing the VLM decision boundary in closed form, our framework quantitatively certifies extent intervals for which the predicted class remains unchanged under the semantic transformation. Our framework is the first to certify VLM robustness under semantic-level variations without requiring additional data for each variation, making it practical to apply. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data show that our framework enables certifying robustness under diverse semantic variations across scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）现已被广泛应用于各类下游任务。然而，现实应用常常使VLM面临由语义变化（如形状、尺寸和风格）引发的分布偏移。鲁棒性认证判断模型在输入经过变换后其预测是否发生改变。尽管大多数认证框架研究的是对输入的几何或像素级变换，本文提出了一种新颖的框架，能够在语义级变换下认证VLM的鲁棒性。利用VLM的开放词汇能力，我们使用文本提示作为语义代理，构建由控制语义变化程度的参数化的变换。通过以闭式形式刻画VLM的决策边界，我们的框架定量认证了在语义变换下预测类别保持不变的程度区间。该框架是首个无需为每种变化额外收集数据即可认证VLM在语义级变化下鲁棒性的方法，具有良好的实用性。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，我们的框架能够在多种场景下对多样的语义变化进行鲁棒性认证。

</details>


### [181] [Learning from Your Own Mistakes: Constructing Learnable Micro-Reflective Trajectories for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18844)
*Zhilin Huang,Hang Gao,Ziqiang Dong,Yuan Chen,Yifeng Luo,Chujun Qin,Jingyi Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPO方法，将自蒸馏从隐式分布对齐改进为显式轨迹构建，通过对比正确和错误采样轨迹，生成带有自然语言诊断和修正的微反思训练样本，在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上取得优于GRPO的持续改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法通过最小化KL散度将模型输出对齐到特权目标分布，但由于监督信号来自无控制采样，无法提供关于模型具体错误模式的诊断性信息，也无法针对个体失败模式提供纠正性指导。模型只是模仿特权分布，而非接收细粒度的、定位推理失败原因和位置的纠正。

Method: TAPO（Trajectory-Augmented Policy Optimization）包含三个关键组件：(1) 在RL训练中，对同一查询同时生成正确和错误的采样轨迹，利用对比结构构建微反思纠正（micro-reflective corrections），即在错误推理路径上保留直到失败点的前缀，然后插入基于正确参考的自然语言诊断和修正推理；(2) 引入困难感知候选选择（difficulty-aware candidate selection），在模型能力边界处选取样本；(3) 提出解耦优势估计（decoupled advantage estimation），防止梯度污染。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上的实验表明，在相同训练步数下，TAPO相比GRPO取得了一致的性能提升。进一步分析显示，TAPO同时增强了一轮推理能力（first-pass reasoning）和错误纠正有效性（error-correction effectiveness）。

Conclusion: TAPO通过将自蒸馏从隐式分布对齐推进到显式轨迹构建，为大型语言模型提供了更精细的纠正信号，有效提升了推理性能，且相比KL散度方法更好地保持了模型的在策略分布。

Original Abstract: Self-distillation improves reasoning in large language models by using the model's own rollouts as training signal, typically through implicit logit-level alignment that minimizes KL divergence toward a privileged target distribution. However, because this supervision is generated via uncontrolled sampling, it provides no diagnostic insight into the model's specific errors or corrective guidance for its individual failure patterns. Consequently, the model learns to imitate a privileged distribution rather than receiving fine-grained corrections that pinpoint where and why its reasoning fails. In this paper, we propose Trajectory-Augmented Policy Optimization (TAPO), which advances self-distillation from implicit distributional alignment to explicit trajectory construction. During RL training, the model produces both correct and incorrect rollouts to the same query, and TAPO leverages this contrastive structure to construct micro-reflective corrections, new training trajectories that retain the model's erroneous reasoning up to the point of failure, then insert a natural-language diagnosis and corrected reasoning guided by a correct reference from the same sampling group. Since each trajectory is anchored in the learner's own prefix and solutions, the corrective signal preserves the model's on-policy distribution to a greater extent than the position-wise alignment imposed by KL-based methods. To integrate these trajectories, TAPO introduces difficulty-aware candidate selection at the model's capability boundary and decoupled advantage estimation to prevent gradient contamination. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025 show that TAPO achieves consistent improvements over GRPO under the same number of training steps. Further analysis demonstrates that TAPO strengthens both first-pass reasoning and error-correction effectiveness.

中文摘要翻译: 自蒸馏通过将模型自身的采样输出作为训练信号来改进大型语言模型的推理能力，通常采用隐式的logit级对齐方式，通过最小化KL散度向特权目标分布靠拢。然而，由于这种监督信号来自无控制采样，它无法提供关于模型具体错误模式的诊断性信息，也无法针对个体失败模式提供纠正性指导。因此，模型只是模仿特权分布，而非接收能够精确定位推理失败原因和位置的细粒度纠正。本文提出轨迹增强策略优化（TAPO），将自蒸馏从隐式分布对齐推进为显式轨迹构建。在RL训练过程中，模型对同一查询同时生成正确和错误的采样轨迹，TAPO利用这种对比结构构建微反思纠正——即保留模型错误推理直到失败点的前缀，然后插入基于同一采样组中正确参考引导的自然语言诊断和修正推理，形成新的训练轨迹。由于每条轨迹都锚定在学习者自身的前缀和解法上，纠正信号比基于KL的位置对齐方法更大程度地保持了模型的在策略分布。为整合这些轨迹，TAPO引入了模型能力边界处的困难感知候选选择，以及防止梯度污染的解耦优势估计。在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上的实验表明，在相同训练步数下，TAPO相比GRPO取得了一致的性能提升。进一步分析显示，TAPO同时增强了一轮推理能力和错误纠正有效性。

</details>


### [182] [Investigating Inductive Biases for Machine Learning Emulation of Sudden Stratospheric Warmings in Idealised Isca Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18857)
*Oskar Bohn Lassen,Simon Driscoll,Stephen I. Thomson,Sebastian Schemm,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同机器学习架构（卷积、Transformer、图神经网络）在模拟平流层突然增温（SSW）动力学时的归纳偏置影响，发现显式的三维垂直耦合是模拟平流层动力学的关键归纳偏置，但低预测误差并不能保证物理上准确的波-流相互作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究机器学习模型能否利用平流层变率等预测锚点来提升次季节到季节时间尺度的预测技巧，特别是不同的架构归纳偏置如何影响对平流层突然增温动力学的仿真能力。

Method: 使用配对的理想化Isca模拟数据，仅改变施加的波-2加热扰动，训练卷积、Transformer和图神经网络三种架构进行单步预测，并通过Eliassen-Palm通量诊断评估波-流相互作用的物理忠实性。

Result: 当平流层动力学平静时各模型差异不大，但在SSW类变率活跃时差异显著扩大；显式三维垂直耦合是关键归纳偏置；低预测误差不能保证物理上忠实的波-流相互作用，平流层波驱动力结构中仍存在系统性误差。

Conclusion: 显式的三维垂直耦合是机器学习仿真平流层动力学的重要归纳偏置，但仅凭预测误差指标不足以确保模型捕捉到正确的物理过程，需要结合物理诊断指标进行评估。

Original Abstract: Machine-learning emulators are increasingly used for weather prediction and have the potential to extend skill on subseasonal-to-seasonal timescales by learning dynamically important sources of predictability. A key challenge is whether the models can exploit predictability anchors, such as stratospheric variability, that influence tropospheric circulation beyond short lead times. We test how architectural inductive bias affects emulation of sudden stratospheric warming (SSW) dynamics using paired idealised Isca simulations that differ only in an imposed wave-2 heating perturbation. Across convolutional, transformer, and graph-based architectures trained for one-step prediction, model differences are modest when the stratosphere is dynamically quiet but widen substantially when SSW-like variability is active. Our results identify explicit three-dimensional vertical coupling as a key inductive bias for machine-learning emulation of stratospheric dynamics. However, Eliassen-Palm flux diagnostics show that low forecast error does not guarantee physically faithful wave-mean-flow interaction, with coherent errors remaining in stratospheric wave-driving structure.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习模拟器越来越多地用于天气预报，并且通过学习具有动力学重要性的可预测性来源，有望将技巧扩展到次季节到季节时间尺度。一个关键挑战在于模型能否利用预测锚点（例如影响超过短期预报时效的对流层环流的平流层变率）。我们使用配对的理想化Isca模拟数据（仅改变施加的波-2加热扰动），测试了架构归纳偏置如何影响对平流层突然增温动力学的模拟。在卷积、Transformer和图神经网络架构上进行单步预测训练后，发现当平流层动力学平静时各模型差异不大，但当类似SSW的变率活跃时差异显著扩大。我们的结果表明，显式的三维垂直耦合是机器学习模拟平流层动力学的关键归纳偏置。然而，Eliassen-Palm通量诊断显示，低预测误差并不能保证物理上忠实的波-流相互作用，平流层波驱动力结构中仍存在系统性误差。

</details>


### [183] [Scaling Learning-based AEB with Massive Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18864)
*Xiangyu Wang,Yang Zhan,Mengxiang Hao,Chuanchuan Zhong,Yansong Jia,Junjie Zhang,Yu Han,Xin Jiang,Zhen Cao,Ying Wang,Yulun Song,Zhitao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于元反馈半监督学习（MF-SSL）的稳定化框架，用于在量产约束下利用大规模无标签车队数据扩展学习型自动紧急制动（AEB）系统，通过噪声感知解耦和运动学门控伪标签技术解决了伪标签误差放大问题，在1B窗口数据上训练并部署到数十万车辆中，实现了超过100:1的正误触发比和35%的无事故行驶里程提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在量产自动驾驶系统中，基于学习的AEB模型难以充分利用海量无标签车队数据进行扩展，因为无监督伪标签中存在系统性误差（锚点模糊和标记-无标签数据不匹配），导致虚假触发，影响系统安全性和舒适性。

Method: 提出稳定化MF-SSL框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 噪声感知解耦（Noise-Aware Decoupling），从教师模型的监督更新路径中移除易产生歧义的锚点；(ii) 运动学门控伪标签（kinematics-gated pseudo-labeling）结合教师冲突惩罚，抑制无标签数据上的误检幻觉，同时保持广泛的覆盖范围。

Result: 无标签数据从1M扩展到1B窗口时模型持续性能提升；部署到数十万车辆后，正误触发比超过100:1；相比纯规则基线，无事故行驶里程提升35%；在超过10^9公里驾驶数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定化MF-SSL框架能够有效利用大规模无标签车队数据安全地扩展学习型AEB系统，在保持舒适性的同时显著提升安全性，并且在量产环境中验证了其实际有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper studies how to scale learning-based automatic emergency braking (AEB) with massive unlabeled fleet data under production constraints. Our approach is based on meta-feedback semi-supervised learning (MF-SSL), where a teacher generates pseudo labels for unlabeled driving data and is updated using a small labeled anchor set as safety-critical feedback. In production, anchor ambiguity and labeled-unlabeled mismatch can amplify systematic pseudo-label errors, leading to spurious triggers. We propose a stabilized MF-SSL framework with (i) Noise-Aware Decoupling, which removes ambiguity-prone anchors from the teacher's supervised update path, and (ii) kinematics-gated pseudo-labeling with a teacher conflict penalty to suppress mismatch-induced risk hallucinations on unlabeled data while maintaining broad coverage. Extensive experiments show consistent gains as unlabeled data scale from 1M to 1B windows, improving safety while keeping comfort stable. The 1B-trained student model is deployed to hundreds of thousands of vehicles and validated over \$10^9$ km of driving, achieving a positive-to-false activation ratio exceeding 100:1 and a 35% improvement in accident-free driving mileage over a production rule-only baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究如何在量产约束下利用大规模无标签车队数据扩展基于学习的自动紧急制动（AEB）系统。我们的方法基于元反馈半监督学习（MF-SSL），其中教师模型为无标签驾驶数据生成伪标签，并通过一个小的有标签锚点集作为安全关键反馈进行更新。在量产环境中，锚点模糊性和标记-无标签数据不匹配会放大系统性的伪标签误差，导致虚假触发。我们提出了一种稳定化的MF-SSL框架，包含：(i) 噪声感知解耦，从教师模型的监督更新路径中移除易产生歧义的锚点；(ii) 运动学门控伪标签结合教师冲突惩罚，在保持广泛覆盖的同时抑制无标签数据上由不匹配引起的风险误判。大量实验表明，当无标签数据从1M扩展到1B窗口时，模型性能持续提升，安全性改善同时保持舒适性稳定。基于1B数据训练的学生模型已部署到数十万车辆中，并在超过10^9公里的驾驶数据上得到验证，实现了超过100:1的正误触发比，相比纯规则量产基线，无事故行驶里程提升了35%。

</details>


### [184] [Strategic Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18867)
*Jivat Neet Kaur,Pratik Patil,Divya Shanmugam,Emma Pierson,Michael I. Jordan,Nika Haghtalab,Meena Jagadeesan,Ahmed Alaa,Serena Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源分配决策中，当算法预测器需应对特征的战略性操纵时，如何通过特征选择与岭正则化的联合优化来缓解策略行为的影响，发现仅基于可操纵性排除特征通常是次优的，并提出了一种联合选择特征集与正则化强度的实用算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在医疗等高风险领域，算法预测器常面临输入特征被战略性操纵的问题。现有解决方案往往要求重新设计整个预测器，但实践中决策者通常只能调整现有预测流程中的粗粒度杠杆（如选择排除哪些特征）。本文旨在从理论上研究通过特征选择与岭正则化相结合来应对策略分类问题，为粗粒度政策设计提供理论指导。

Method: 本文首先对通过特征选择进行策略分类及其与岭正则化的交互作用进行了形式化研究。通过精细刻画在最优正则化下特征子集的性能表现，揭示了仅基于可操纵性排除特征的局限性。在此基础上，提出了一种联合选择特征集与岭正则化强度的实用算法。

Result: 主要发现：仅基于特征的可操纵性单独排除特征通常是次优的。提供了在最优正则化下特征子集性能的精细刻画，为政策设计提供了新见解。在医疗支付基准数据集上的真实案例研究展示了所提算法如何指导实际中粗粒度政策杠杆的设计。

Conclusion: 本文为减轻算法决策系统中策略行为的影响提供了一个有原则、实用的框架。研究结果表明，决策者不应仅依据可操纵性简单排除特征，而应联合优化特征选择与正则化强度，从而更有效地应对策略性操纵。

Original Abstract: When algorithmic predictors inform resource allocation in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, these predictors must account for strategic manipulation of input features. The typical solution is to redesign the predictor itself to explicitly account for strategic interactions. In practice, however, decision makers are often constrained to adjusting coarser levers within existing prediction pipelines. For example, healthcare organizations often select which features to exclude based on perceived manipulability, while using standard regularization procedures to shrink the coefficients of retained features. In this work, we initiate a formal study of strategic classification through feature selection and its interaction with ridge regularization. Our main finding is that excluding individual features based on their manipulability alone is generally suboptimal. We provide a fine-grained characterization of the performance of a feature subset under optimal regularization, yielding new insights for policy design. Motivated by this characterization, we develop a practical algorithm for jointly choosing the feature set and the level of ridge regularization. Through a real-world case study on a healthcare payments benchmark, we illustrate how our algorithm can guide the design of coarse policy levers in practice. Our results provide a principled, practical framework for mitigating the effects of strategic behavior in algorithmic decision-making systems.

中文摘要翻译: 当算法预测器为医疗等高风险领域的资源分配提供依据时，这些预测器必须考虑输入特征被战略性操纵的可能性。典型的解决方案是重新设计预测器本身，使其明确考虑策略性交互。然而在实践中，决策者通常只能调整现有预测流程中的粗粒度杠杆。例如，医疗机构通常根据特征的可操纵性来选择排除哪些特征，同时使用标准正则化程序对保留特征的系数进行收缩。本文首次对通过特征选择进行策略分类及其与岭正则化的交互作用展开形式化研究。我们的主要发现是：仅基于特征的可操纵性单独排除特征通常是次优的。我们提供了在最优正则化下特征子集性能的精细刻画，为政策设计带来了新见解。受这一刻画启发，我们开发了一种联合选择特征集与岭正则化强度的实用算法。通过一个医疗支付基准数据集上的真实案例研究，我们展示了该算法如何在实践中指导粗粒度政策杠杆的设计。我们的研究结果为减轻算法决策系统中策略行为的负面影响提供了一个有原则、实用的框架。

</details>


### [185] [Anomaly Detection for Sparse and Irregular Multivariate Time Series with Latent SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18898)
*Martin Uray,Dominik Geng,Florian Graf,Stefan Huber,Roland Kwitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于潜变量随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，用于处理多变量时间序列异常检测中的不规则采样和缺失值问题，在六个基准数据集上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MTSAD方法假设数据均匀采样，但实际数据常存在稀疏、不规则采样或部分观测的问题，亟需能处理这些真实世界不规则性的方法。

Method: 提出基于潜变量随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，将观测时间序列投影到连续时间随机动力系统上，天然支持缺失观测和不规则采样，并能捕获周期性行为。

Result: 在六个异常检测基准数据集上排名第一；在严重数据稀疏下仍保持鲁棒，而基线方法性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 潜变量SDE可作为多变量时间序列异常检测的自然归纳偏置，尤其适用于存在真实世界不规则性的场景。

Original Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) is critical for a wide range of application areas, such as industrial monitoring, cybersecurity, or healthcare. Real-world data is often sparse, irregularly sampled or partially observed, yet existing methods assume uniformly sampled time series. We propose a generative approach based on Latent SDEs that projects the observed time series on a continuous-time stochastic dynamical system, directly being able to handle missing observations and irregular sampling, while also naturally capturing possible cyclic behavior that many real-world use cases inherently possess. Experiments on six anomaly benchmark datasets show that our proposed method ranks first among state-of-the-art baselines. We further demonstrate that our method remains robust under severe data sparsity, while performance significantly degrades for the tested baseline methods. These results highlight latent SDEs as a natural inductive bias for anomaly detection in multivariate time series, especially in presence of real-world irregularities.

中文摘要翻译: 多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）在工业监控、网络安全和医疗等领域至关重要。真实世界数据通常是稀疏的、不规则采样的或部分观测的，而现有方法假设时间序列是均匀采样的。我们提出了一种基于潜变量随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，将观测时间序列投影到一个连续时间随机动力系统上，能够直接处理缺失观测和不规则采样，同时自然地捕获许多真实应用场景中固有的循环行为。在六个异常检测基准数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在所有最先进的基线方法中排名第一。我们进一步证明，在严重数据稀疏的情况下，我们的方法仍保持鲁棒，而测试的基线方法性能显著下降。这些结果凸显了潜变量SDE作为多变量时间序列异常检测的自然归纳偏置，尤其是在存在真实世界不规则性的场景中。

</details>


### [186] [REVES: REvision and VErification--Augmented Training for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18910)
*Yuanxin Liu,Ruida Zhou,Xinyan Zhao,Amr Sharaf,Hongzhou Lin,Arijit Biswas,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Zhaoran Wang,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段迭代框架REVES，通过将中间步骤的“接近正确答案”转化为解耦的修订和验证提示，实现高效的非策略数据生成，在代码、数学和约束满足任务上均取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准后训练方法主要优化单次目标，与多步推理动态存在根本性不匹配；现有多轮RL方法直接优化多步轨迹，未能充分利用中间步骤的高质量错误供模型学习纠正。

Method: 两阶段迭代框架：第一阶段进行在线数据/提示增强，将成功恢复轨迹中的中间步骤（近正确答案）转化为解耦的修订提示和验证提示；第二阶段进行策略优化，集中训练有效的答案转换和错误识别，实现高效的非策略数据生成并降低长程采样的计算开销。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，使用公开测试用例作为反馈，相比RL基线提升+6.5分，相比标准多轮训练提升+4.0分；在圆填充任务上以最小的4B基座模型匹配此前报告的最优结果；数学结果验证了改进的纠错能力；泛化到n皇后和迷你数独等分布外约束满足问题。

Conclusion: 通过解耦中间步骤的修订与验证，REVES框架有效对齐了训练目标与多步推理的动态特性，在多种推理任务上实现显著性能提升，且具有良好的泛化能力和计算效率优势。

Original Abstract: Test-time scaling via sequential revision has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. However, standard post-training methods primarily optimize single-shot objectives, creating a fundamental misalignment with multi-step inference dynamics. While recent work treats this as multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL), conventional approaches optimize over the multi-step trajectories directly, failing to further exploit the high-quality mistakes in intermediate steps that model can learn from correcting them. We propose a two-stage iterative framework that alternates between online data/prompt augmentation and policy optimization. By converting the intermediate steps (``near-miss'' answers) in the successful recovery trajectories into decoupled revision and verification prompts, our approach concentrates training on both effective answer transformation and error identification. This approach enables efficient off-policy data generation and reduces the computational overhead of long-horizon sampling compared to standard multi-turn RL. On LiveCodeBench, using publicly available test cases as feedback, we observe gains of +6.5 points over the RL baseline and +4.0 points over standard multi-turn training. Beyond coding, our approach matches the previously reported SOTA result on circle packing while using the smallest base model (4B) and far fewer rollouts than the much larger evolutionary search systems. Math results under ground-truth verification further confirm improved correction ability. It also generalizes to out-of-distribution constraint-satisfaction puzzles such as n\_queens and mini\_sudoku, where correctness is defined entirely by problem constraints. Code is available at https://github.com/yxliu02/REVES.git.

中文摘要翻译: 通过顺序修订进行测试时扩展已成为增强大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的强大范式。然而，标准的后训练方法主要优化单次目标，这与多步推理动态存在根本性的不匹配。虽然近期工作将其视为多轮强化学习（RL），但传统方法直接优化多步轨迹，未能进一步利用模型可以从纠正中间步骤的高质量错误中学习。我们提出了一个两阶段迭代框架，在在线数据/提示增强与策略优化之间交替进行。通过将成功恢复轨迹中的中间步骤（“接近正确答案”）转化为解耦的修订和验证提示，我们的方法集中训练有效的答案转换和错误识别。与标准多轮RL相比，该方法实现了高效的非策略数据生成，并降低了长程采样的计算开销。在LiveCodeBench上，使用公开可用的测试用例作为反馈，我们观察到相比RL基线提升了+6.5分，相比标准多轮训练提升了+4.0分。除代码任务外，我们在圆填充任务上使用最小的基座模型（4B）且远少于更大规模进化搜索系统的 rollout 次数，即匹配了此前报告的最优结果。在真实验证条件下的数学结果进一步证实了改进的纠错能力。该方法还能泛化到分布外的约束满足谜题（如n皇后和迷你数独），其中正确性完全由问题约束定义。代码已开源：https://github.com/yxliu02/REVES.git。

</details>


### [187] [Some Complexity Results for Robustness Verification for Binarized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18918)
*Harshit Goyal,Sudakshina Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了二值化神经网络（BNN）验证问题的计算复杂度，证明BNN可满足性问题是NP完全的，并发现均匀遮挡下的鲁棒性验证存在多项式时间算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解BNN验证问题的计算复杂度边界，为实际部署中的验证可行性提供理论依据。

Method: 通过从布尔可满足性问题（SAT）归约证明BNN可满足性问题是NP完全的；通过分析均匀遮挡下网络输出的分段常数结构，设计多项式时间的鲁棒性检测算法。

Result: (1) BNN可满足性问题是NP完全的；(2) 均匀图像遮挡下的鲁棒性验证可以在多项式时间内完成。

Conclusion: BNN的可满足性验证本质上是困难的，但均匀遮挡这一特定攻击场景下存在高效的多项式时间验证算法。

Original Abstract: This paper studies the computational complexity of verification problems for Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), where activations (and sometimes weights) are binary. We analyze two problems: satisfiability and robustness under uniform image occlusion. We show that BNN satisfiability is NP-complete via a reduction from Boolean satisfiability problem (SAT), and that uniform occlusion induces a piecewise-constant structure in the network output, enabling a polynomial-time robustness-checking algorithm.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了二值化神经网络验证问题的计算复杂度，其中激活值（有时包括权重）是二值的。我们分析了两个问题：可满足性和均匀图像遮挡下的鲁棒性。我们通过从布尔可满足性问题归约证明了BNN可满足性是NP完全的，并发现均匀遮挡使网络输出具有分段常数结构，从而能够设计多项式时间的鲁棒性检测算法。

</details>


### [188] [GrapNet: A Programmable Dynamic-Architecture Neural Graph Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18923)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GrapNet提出了一种将神经网络架构本身视为可编程图（而非处理图数据）的范式，每个计算节点拥有子节点引用和可训练分配向量，支持关系编辑、子图冻结、结构编辑等操作，在持续学习任务中显著优于传统MLP。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有固定张量神经网络缺乏可编程性接口，编辑关系、冻结子图、审计局部函数或更改执行后端等操作需要通过临时参数手术实现，而非在神经程序层面直接操作。

Method: GrapNet将图视为网络架构和可执行程序本身。每个计算节点拥有指向下一层子节点的引用和一个与这些引用对齐的可训练分配向量；删除关系会物理移除子节点引用及对应分配坐标。结构规则和执行策略位于节点核心之外，使得同一子节点拥有的图可以被生长、冻结、结构编辑、分组为可训练族块、通过注意力路由活跃关系，或在拓扑稳定后降级为密集快照。GrapNet通过向量值父接口与常规模块（如密集层、CNN编码器、ResNet、注意力块、Transformer表示）兼容。

Result: 在十次随机种子的Split Fashion-MNIST实验中，可塑GrapNet+ER头达到63.16%的已见类准确率，而参数更大的密集MLP+ER仅为51.08%，配对差异12.08个点，p=1.3e-5。在Split CIFAR-10上使用冻结的ImageNet ResNet-18编码器，相同架构相比MLP-256在线头提升3.81个点，p=0.0026。

Conclusion: GrapNet是一种可编辑的神经图基板，其核心价值在于结构可编程性及忠实的执行视图。

Original Abstract: Programmability is a missing first-class interface in fixed-tensor neural networks: editing a relation, freezing a subgraph, auditing a local function, or changing the execution backend should be an operation on the neural program rather than ad-hoc parameter surgery. GrapNet studies this graph-as-network setting. The graph is the architecture and executable program, not an input data graph. Each compute node owns its next-layer child references and a trainable allocation vector aligned with those references; deleting a relation physically removes both the child reference and the corresponding allocation coordinate. Structural rules and execution policies live outside the node core, so the same child-owned graph can be grown, frozen, structurally edited, grouped into trainable family blocks, routed by attention over active relations, or lowered to dense snapshots after topology stabilizes. GrapNet composes with conventional modules through a vector-valued parent interface: dense layers, CNN encoders, ResNet feature extractors, attention blocks, and transformer representations can all feed one sensory GrapNode per coordinate. The evaluation is organized as a programmability stress suite rather than as a new replay benchmark. In a matched ten-seed Split Fashion-MNIST study, a plastic GrapNet+ER head reaches 63.16 percent seen-class accuracy versus 51.08 percent for a parameter-larger dense MLP+ER under the same seen-class loss and replay memory, with paired delta 12.08 points and p=1.3e-5. On Split CIFAR-10 with a frozen ImageNet ResNet-18 encoder, the same substrate improves the online head over MLP-256 by 3.81 points, with p=0.0026. These results support GrapNet as an editable neural graph substrate whose core value is structural programmability with faithful execution views.

中文摘要翻译: 可编程性是固定张量神经网络中缺失的一等接口：编辑一个关系、冻结一个子图、审计一个局部函数或更改执行后端，应当是对神经程序的操作，而非临时的参数手术。GrapNet研究这种图即网络的设定。这里的图是架构和可执行程序，而非输入数据图。每个计算节点拥有自己的下一层子节点引用以及一个与该引用对齐的可训练分配向量；删除一个关系会物理移除子节点引用和对应的分配坐标。结构规则和执行策略位于节点核心之外，因此同一份子节点拥有的图可以被生长、冻结、结构编辑、分组为可训练族块、通过注意力机制在活跃关系上进行路由，或在拓扑稳定后降级为密集快照。GrapNet通过向量值父接口与常规模块兼容：密集层、CNN编码器、ResNet特征提取器、注意力块和Transformer表示均可以按坐标输入到一个感知型GrapNode中。评估被组织为可编程性压力测试套件，而非新的回放基准。在十次随机种子的匹配Split Fashion-MNIST研究中，可塑GrapNet+经验回放头达到了63.16%的已见类准确率，而参数更大的密集MLP+经验回放在相同的已见类损失和回放记忆下仅为51.08%，配对差异12.08个点，p=1.3e-5。在Split CIFAR-10上使用冻结的ImageNet ResNet-18编码器时，相同基板相比MLP-256在线头提升了3.81个点，p=0.0026。这些结果支持GrapNet作为一种可编辑的神经图基板，其核心价值在于结构可编程性及忠实的执行视图。

</details>


### [189] [Zero-Shot Active Feature Acquisition via LLM-Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18933)
*Binyamin Perets,Natalie Mendelson,Shiran Vainberg,Yehuda Chowers,Shai Shen-Orr,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本主动特征获取（AFA）框架，通过让大语言模型（LLM）仅提供马尔可夫随机场（MRF）的充分统计量（单变量偏差和成对协变），而非直接进行序列规划，来解决AFA依赖大量标注数据的问题。在IBD患者队列上，该方法在分类和top-k识别任务中均优于LLM基线及现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统主动特征获取（AFA）依赖大量标注数据来拟合概率模型，成本高昂。大语言模型（LLM）虽具备无监督领域知识，但缺乏序列规划能力；若让LLM同时承担知识提供和决策制定，会混淆本应分离的能力。因此需要一种框架，仅让LLM提供其可信赖的统计信息，实现零样本AFA。

Method: 提出零样本AFA框架，通过规约提询（disciplined elicitation）让LLM仅输出马尔可夫随机场（MRF）的充分统计量——单变量偏差和成对协变。针对LLM倾向于返回判别性统计（区分类别而非孤立描述类别）导致的规范歧义（gauge ambiguity），采用最大熵闭式求解进行校正。框架应用于二分类和top-k识别两种场景。

Result: 在炎症性肠病（IBD）患者队列上，该框架在真实标签和LLM自身提取的信念上均优于LLM基线。在最困难的病人上，提出的top-k获取策略显著优于所有现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的知识提供与序列决策分离，利用规约提询和最大熵闭式求解，可以在零样本条件下有效实现主动特征获取，尤其在最困难的病例上表现突出。

Original Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects which features to observe to reach a classification or ranking decision. Its central limitation is reliance on large amount of labeled data to fit probabilistic models guiding acquisition. Large language models (LLMs) supply unsupervised domain knowledge, but are poor sequential planners. Asking one to both know and decide conflates capabilities best kept separate. Here, we develop a framework for zero-shot AFA through disciplined elicitation: asking the LLM only for what it can be trusted to return, the unary deviations and pairwise co-variations that are the sufficient statistics of a Markov random field (MRF). We apply our framework to two settings: binary classification and top-$k$ identification. In practice, the LLM reliably returns only discriminative statistics, what distinguishes the classes rather than each class in isolation, which precludes classical AFA. We apply a maximum-entropy closure that resolves this gauge ambiguity. We evaluate on a cohort of Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD) patients, an active clinical setting where diagnostic ambiguity and patient heterogeneity obstruct stable treatment strategies. Our framework outperforms the LLM both on real labels and on its own extracted beliefs. Where it matters most, on the hardest patients, our top-$k$ acquisition policy markedly outperforms all existing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 主动特征获取（AFA）通过顺序选择待观察的特征来达成分类或排序决策。其核心局限在于依赖大量标注数据来拟合指导特征获取的概率模型。大语言模型（LLM）提供了无监督的领域知识，但缺乏序列规划能力。要求同一模型既负责知识又负责决策，会混淆本应分开处理的能力。本文开发了一个用于零样本AFA的框架，通过规约提询：仅要求LLM返回其可信赖的内容——即马尔可夫随机场（MRF）的充分统计量，包括单变量偏差和成对协变。我们将框架应用于二分类和top-k识别两种场景。实践中，LLM只能可靠地返回判别性统计量（即区分类别而非孤立描述每个类别的统计量），这阻碍了经典的AFA。我们采用最大熵闭式求解来解决这一规范歧义。我们在炎症性肠病（IBD）患者队列上进行评估，这是一个活跃的临床场景，诊断歧义和患者异质性阻碍了稳定的治疗策略制定。我们的框架在真实标签和LLM自身提取的信念上均优于LLM基线。在最关键的困难患者上，我们的top-k获取策略显著优于所有现有方法。

</details>


### [190] [Be Your Own Teacher: Steering Protein Language Models via Unsupervised Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18961)
*Lanqing Li,Shentong Mo,Yang Yu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督奖励优化框架，利用任务无关奖励（模型不确定性+语义一致性）对蛋白质语言模型进行后训练微调，无需湿实验或偏好标注，在组合分布外提示上显著优于DPO、KTO等基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型的后训练微调依赖湿实验验证或人工标注的偏好数据集，成本高昂且难以扩展，亟需一种无需真实标签的无监督优化方法。

Method: 提出无监督奖励优化框架：1) 设计任务无关奖励函数，融合模型内在不确定性与基于蛋白质表征模型的外在语义一致性；2) 提出两种离线算法——软奖励优化(SRO)和二值化奖励优化(BRO)，最大化经典RLHF目标。

Result: 在组合分布外提示上，SRO和BRO显著优于DPO、KTO等基线，接近oracle性能；在不同采样温度、模型规模和蛋白质家族中均表现一致；微调模型在pass@k评估中覆盖率始终高于基础模型。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型自身生成的经验实现自我改进，该框架为标注偏好或实验反馈稀缺场景下的可控蛋白质设计提供了一条可扩展路径。

Original Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for controllable biomolecular design, yet their post-training adaptation typically relies on costly wet-lab validation or curated preference datasets. To overcome this supervision bottleneck, we introduce unsupervised reward optimization of PLMs, a comprehensive framework for steerable protein generation without ground-truth labels. Our key insight is that task-agnostic rewards, which combine intrinsic model uncertainty with extrinsic semantic consistency informed by protein representation models, exhibit strong correlation with controllability measures across base models and temperature regimes. Building upon this discovery, we propose two offline algorithms: Soft Reward Optimization (SRO) and Binarized Reward Optimization (BRO), which effectively maximize the classical RLHF objective induced by these proxy rewards. Extensive experiments on compositional out-of-distribution prompts demonstrate that both methods significantly outperform competitive baselines (DPO, KTO), while approaching oracle performance across multiple sampling temperatures, model scales and protein families. Moreover, PLMs fine-tuned with unsupervised rewards can achieve consistently higher coverage compared to their base model in pass@k evaluations. By enabling self-improvement of PLMs through their own generated experience, our framework provides a scalable pathway toward controllable biomolecular design in settings where labeled preferences or experimental feedback are scarce or unavailable.

中文摘要翻译: 蛋白质语言模型(PLMs)已成为可控生物分子设计的强大工具，但其后训练微调通常依赖成本高昂的湿实验验证或人工整理的偏好数据集。为克服这一监督瓶颈，我们提出了PLMs的无监督奖励优化——一个无需真实标签即可实现可调控蛋白质生成的综合框架。我们的关键发现是，任务无关奖励（将内禀模型不确定性与由蛋白质表征模型引导的外在语义一致性相结合）在不同基础模型和温度条件下与可控性指标呈现强相关。基于这一发现，我们提出了两种离线算法：软奖励优化(SRO)和二值化奖励优化(BRO)，它们能有效最大化由这些代理奖励诱导的经典RLHF目标。在组合分布外提示上的大量实验表明，两种方法均显著优于强基线(DPO, KTO)，并在多种采样温度、模型规模和蛋白质家族中逼近oracle性能。此外，经无监督奖励微调的PLMs在pass@k评估中相比基础模型始终能获得更高的覆盖率。通过使PLMs利用自身生成的经验实现自我改进，我们的框架为在标注偏好或实验反馈稀缺的场景下实现可控生物分子设计提供了一条可扩展路径。

</details>


### [191] [Online Reward-Punishment Learning from Fixed-Channel Perceptual Event Streams without Environment Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18963)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无奖励信号的在线奖惩学习框架OHIRL，通过分离预测、残差建模、内在评估和信念更新四个模块，在仅感知数据流的情况下实现奖惩信号推断，实验证明该方法在多种任务中性能接近传统奖励驱动方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统强化学习依赖标量奖励信号，但生物系统能在没有明确外部奖励信号的情况下进行奖惩学习。本文旨在探索当环境不提供标量奖励或评价标签时，智能体如何仅通过感知数据流（如疼痛、能量、接触等固有维度）推断出内隐的奖惩信号来进行学习。

Method: 提出OHIRL框架，分离四个模块：M_ψ学习下一数据包预测，D_ω建模残差动力学，C_η作为固定内在轨迹评估器（采用恢复正向、持久/增长负向的残差调节方向），B_ξ利用评估证据进行后续策略更新和动作评分。采用无奖励协议暴露观测转移而不提供环境奖励，并通过条件误差分解分离证据估计误差和策略优化误差。

Result: 系数来源审计显示等单元、原始等值和随机单调变体保留了超过92%的发布顶级动作排名，而符号反转保留0%。在2×2-XOR数据包任务中，药物和辣椒在视觉XOR上下文中获得相反价值；B_ξ达到0.952平衡奖励符号准确率。完整在线交错审计中M_ψ达到留出R²=0.907，B_ξ达到0.940符号准确率，策略达到0.979最优动作准确率，而即时数据包分数、预测误差奖励、打乱目标、零奖励和错误减少控制均崩溃。

Conclusion: OHIRL框架能够在无外部奖励信号的条件下，仅通过感知数据流的转移结构成功推断出奖惩信号，证明了内在评估机制可以替代传统外部奖励进行有效策略学习，为无奖励强化学习提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: We study online reward-punishment learning when the environment provides no scalar reward or evaluative label. At each step the agent receives only a fixed-channel perceptual packet, and quantities such as pain, energy, contact, damage, or cognitive error are treated as perceptual dimensions whose valence must be inferred from transition consequences. OHIRL separates four roles: M_psi learns next-packet prediction, D_omega models residual dynamics, C_eta is a fixed internal post-transition trajectory evaluator, and B_xi learns to use the resulting value evidence for later policy updates and action scoring. C_eta uses a recovery-positive and persistence/growth-negative residual-regulation orientation; a coefficient-origin audit shows that equal-unit, raw-equal, and random monotone variants preserve more than 92% of the released top-action rankings, while sign inversion preserves 0%.
  The reward-free protocol exposes observation transitions while withholding environment rewards, delayed external evaluators, success labels, and action-goodness labels. A conditional error decomposition separates B_xi evidence-estimation error from residual policy-optimization error. In a 2x2-XOR packet task, medicine and chili acquire opposite value under visual XOR contexts, and the same pain or spice increase can be positive or negative depending on consequence structure; B_xi reaches 0.952 balanced reward-sign accuracy. In a full online-interleaved audit, M_psi reaches holdout R2=0.907, B_xi reaches 0.940 sign accuracy, and the policy reaches 0.979 optimal-action accuracy, while immediate packet scores, prediction-error rewards, shuffled targets, zero reward, and error-reduction controls collapse. Hidden-reward CartPole and Taxi controls, public-context no-leakage audits, and module-role ablations further test information boundaries and component necessity.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了当环境不提供标量奖励或评价标签时的在线奖惩学习。在每个时间步，智能体仅接收固定通道的感知数据包，其中疼痛、能量、接触、损伤或认知错误等量被视为感知维度，其效价必须从转移后果中推断。OHIRL分离了四个角色：M_ψ学习下一数据包预测，D_ω建模残差动力学，C_η是一个固定的内部转移后轨迹评估器，B_ξ学习利用由此产生的价值证据进行后续策略更新和动作评分。C_η采用恢复正向和持久/增长负向的残差调节方向；系数来源审计显示，等单元、原始等值和随机单调变体保留了超过92%的已发布顶级动作排名，而符号反转保留了0%。无奖励协议暴露观测转移，同时隐藏环境奖励、延迟的外部评估器、成功标签和动作优劣标签。条件误差分解将B_ξ的证据估计误差与残差策略优化误差分离开来。在2×2-XOR数据包任务中，药物和辣椒在视觉XOR情境下获得相反的价值，相同的疼痛或辣度增加可能为正或负，取决于后果结构；B_ξ达到了0.952的平衡奖励符号准确率。在完整的在线交错审计中，M_ψ达到留出R²=0.907，B_ξ达到0.940的符号准确率，策略达到0.979的最优动作准确率，而即时数据包分数、预测误差奖励、打乱目标、零奖励和错误减少控制则全面崩溃。隐藏奖励的CartPole和Taxi控制、公开上下文无泄漏审计、以及模块角色消融进一步测试了信息边界和组件必要性。

</details>


### [192] [EfficientRollout: System-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for RL Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18967)
*Minseo Kim,Minjae Lee,Seunghyuk Oh,Kevin Galim,Donghoon Kim,Coleman Hooper,Harman Singh,Amir Gholami,Hyung Il Koo,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EfficientRollout是一个面向强化学习（RL）训练阶段rollout生成加速的系统感知自推测解码框架，通过从目标模型自蒸馏量化草稿模型、以及系统感知的推测解码切换策略和接受率感知的草稿长度自适应机制，在保持模型质量的同时显著降低RL rollout延迟和端到端训练延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有推测解码（SD）技术主要面向固定LLM的服务推理加速，但直接应用于RL rollout面临两大挑战：(i) 策略模型持续演化，固定草稿模型与目标策略的分布失配加剧；(ii) rollout解码过程中活跃批次大小不断缩小，导致计算从计算密集型转向内存密集型，此时并行验证可以充分利用未被充分利用的计算资源。因此需要一种能适应演化策略、且系统感知的SD方案来加速RL rollout。

Method: 提出了EfficientRollout框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 从目标模型中量化蒸馏出草稿模型（自推测解码），无需额外预训练或在线自适应，保持与演化策略的耦合；(2) 系统感知的推测解码切换策略（SD toggle policy），仅在有益的计算模式下启用推测解码；(3) 接受率感知的草稿长度自适应机制（acceptance-aware draft-length adaptation），根据草稿模型质量动态调整草稿长度。

Result: 相比加速的自回归（AR）rollout基线，EfficientRollout将rollout延迟降低最高19.6%，端到端RL训练延迟降低最高12.7%，同时保持最终模型质量不变。

Conclusion: EfficientRollout通过系统感知的自推测解码设计，有效解决了RL rollout中策略演化与计算模式变化带来的加速瓶颈，在不影响模型质量的前提下显著降低训练延迟。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a representative post-training paradigm for LLMs, enabling strong reasoning and agentic capabilities. However, rollout generation remains a dominant latency bottleneck because autoregressive sampling decodes responses sequentially and a small number of long-tailed generations often determine completion time. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a natural way to address this bottleneck, as it is a well-established technique for serving fixed LLMs that reduces latency by rapidly drafting tokens and accepting them through parallel verification while preserving the target-model distribution. However, its practical speedups do not directly carry over to RL rollouts: (i) the evolving target policy makes any fixed drafter increasingly mismatched with the policy's output distribution; and (ii) active batch sizes shrink throughout rollout decoding, shifting decoding from compute-bound to memory-bound regimes where parallel verification can exploit underutilized compute. Therefore, accelerating RL rollouts requires both a drafter that remains effective under long, high-temperature generations from an evolving policy and system-aware use of SD that avoids compute-bound regimes. We present EfficientRollout, a system-aware self-SD framework designed to address this gap for RL rollouts. EfficientRollout induces a quantized drafter from the target model (i.e. self-speculative decoding), keeping it coupled to the evolving policy without separate drafter pretraining or online adaptation. It further coordinates a system-aware SD toggle policy with acceptance-aware draft-length adaptation, enabling speculation only in beneficial regimes while matching the drafting budget to evolving drafter quality. EfficientRollout reduces rollout and end-to-end latency by up to 19.6% and 12.7%, respectively, over an accelerated AR rollout baseline, while preserving final model quality.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已成为大语言模型（LLM）的一种代表性后训练范式，赋予模型强大的推理和智能体能力。然而，自回归采样逐序列解码响应，且少量长尾生成往往决定完成时间，使得rollout生成仍然是主要的延迟瓶颈。推测解码（SD）为解决这一瓶颈提供了自然途径，因为它是服务固定LLM的一项成熟技术，通过快速生成草稿token并通过并行验证接受它们来减少延迟，同时保持目标模型的分布。然而，其实际加速效果并不能直接迁移到RL rollout中：(i) 演化的目标策略使得任何固定草稿模型与策略的输出分布日益失配；(ii) rollout解码过程中活跃批次大小持续缩小，使解码从计算密集型转向内存密集型，而并行验证可以充分利用未被充分利用的计算资源。因此，加速RL rollout既需要一个在演化策略的长序列、高温采样下仍保持有效的草稿模型，又需要系统感知地使用SD以避免进入计算密集型模式。我们提出了EfficientRollout，一个面向RL rollout的系统感知自推测解码框架。EfficientRollout从目标模型中诱导出量化草稿模型（即自推测解码），无需单独的草稿模型预训练或在线自适应，使其与演化策略保持耦合。它还协调了系统感知的SD切换策略与接受率感知的草稿长度自适应机制，仅在有益的计算模式下启用推测，并将草稿预算与演化中的草稿质量相匹配。相比加速的自回归rollout基线，EfficientRollout将rollout延迟和端到端延迟分别降低最高19.6%和12.7%，同时保持最终模型质量不变。

</details>


### [193] [A Controlled Benchmark of Quantum-Latent GAN Augmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18970)
*Syed Mujtaba Haider,Silvia Figini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个严格的对照基准测试，系统评估量子生成模型在脑部MRI数据增强中的实际效果。实验发现，无论在何种数据量下，量子生成增强均未显著优于纯真实数据训练，且量子与经典生成器表现无统计学差异，表明量子生成增强并未带来实质收益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像分类常受限于标注数据不足，生成式增强是一种常用策略；近期有研究提出量子生成模型用于数据增强并报告了准确率提升，但这些评估通常基于单次训练、未匹配量子与经典生成器的参数预算、也未明确增强有效的具体数据区间。现有评估缺乏严谨性和公平性，亟需一个受控的、统计严谨的基准测试来验证量子生成增强的真实效果。

Method: 将脑部MRI图像编码到KL正则化的潜空间中，在该空间中训练带梯度惩罚的条件Wasserstein GAN（cWGAN-GP），分别使用变分量子生成器和参数数量相近（1648 vs. 1632）的经典生成器。生成的合成样本经解码后用于增强预训练分类器，覆盖从5%到100%的标注数据比例，通过8个随机种子的配对显著性检验（含多重比较校正）以及集内多样性和潜分布分析进行系统评估。

Result: 在所有标注数据比例下，任何增强变体均未显著优于仅使用真实数据的训练；量子生成器与经典生成器在统计上不可区分。在低数据条件下，任何看似有益的提升实际上表现为正则化效应而非忠实的数据扩展——合成样本分布外且严重模式崩溃，量子生成器在多样性上并不优于经典生成器。

Conclusion: 当前量子生成模型在医学图像增强中并未带来实质性收益，其声称的优势源于不严谨的评估方法。研究发布了标准化测试协议，作为未来量子生成增强在医学影像中严格评估的测试平台。

Original Abstract: Medical image classification is often constrained by limited labeled data, motivating generative augmentation; recently, quantum generative models have been proposed for this purpose, frequently reporting accuracy gains. However, such claims are typically based on single training runs, do not match the parameter budgets of the quantum and classical generators, and do not characterize the data regime in which any benefit appears. We present a controlled benchmark that isolates the contribution of a quantum generator to brain-MRI augmentation. Images are encoded into a KL-regularized latent space in which a conditional Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty is trained using either a variational quantum generator or a classical generator of near-identical parameter count (1648 vs. 1632). Synthetic samples are decoded and used to augment a pretrained classifier across labeled data fractions from 5% to 100%, evaluated over eight random seeds with paired significance testing (with multiple-comparison correction) and with intraset diversity and latent-distribution analyses. Across all fractions, no augmentation variant significantly outperforms real-data-only training, and the quantum and classical generators are statistically indistinguishable. Any low-data benefit behaves as regularization rather than faithful data expansion:synthetic samples are off distribution and severely mode collapsed precisely where data is scarce, and the quantum generator is no more diverse thanits classical counterpart. We release the protocol as a testbed for rigorous evaluation of quantum generative augmentation in medical imaging.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像分类常受限于有限的标注数据，这激发了对生成式增强方法的研究；近期，量子生成模型被提出用于此目的，并频繁报告准确率的提升。然而，这类结论通常基于单次训练运行，未匹配量子与经典生成器的参数预算，也未明确任何增益出现的具体数据区间。我们构建了一个受控的基准测试，以隔离量子生成器对脑部MRI数据增强的贡献。图像被编码到KL正则化的潜空间中，在该空间中使用带梯度惩罚的条件Wasserstein GAN进行训练，分别采用变分量子生成器或参数数量几乎相同的经典生成器（1648 vs. 1632）。生成的合成样本被解码并用于增强预训练分类器，覆盖从5%到100%的标注数据比例，通过8个随机种子的配对显著性检验（含多重比较校正）以及集内多样性和潜分布分析进行评估。在所有数据比例下，没有任何增强变体显著优于仅使用真实数据的训练，且量子生成器与经典生成器在统计上不可区分。任何低数据下的收益实际上表现为正则化效应而非忠实的数据扩展：合成样本在数据稀缺时恰恰是分布外的且严重模式崩溃，量子生成器在多样性上并不优于其经典对应模型。我们发布了该协议，作为医学影像中量子生成增强的严格评估测试平台。

</details>


### [194] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang,Xiyao Liu,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViGOS，一种针对多模态大语言模型的视觉引导在线策略自蒸馏框架，通过引入图像专属感知教师和特权推理教师，解决了直接扩展OPSD到MLLM时产生的文本捷径问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 直接将在LLM推理中表现良好的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）扩展到多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）时，会导致捷径问题：特权目标主要依赖文本参考目标而非图像来引导token生成，削弱了模型对视觉信息的利用。

Method: 提出ViGOS框架，学生模型先撰写视觉描述再推理答案。对有效轨迹，使用图像专属感知教师监督描述生成，特权推理教师监督推理和答案；对无效轨迹，使用参考教师恢复输出格式。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间定位和视觉语言先验等多个基准测试中，ViGOS保留了OPSD的主要优势，并在易出现捷径问题的场景中改善了基于图像的行为。

Conclusion: ViGOS有效解决了OPSD扩展到MLLM时的文本捷径问题，在保持OPSD优势的同时增强了模型对视觉信息的依赖，适用于多模态后训练场景。

Original Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

中文摘要翻译: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过在模型自身生成的数据上训练，并使用冻结的副本基于参考目标提供密集的token级目标。该方法在LLM推理中表现良好，但直接扩展到多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）时会产生捷径问题：特权目标可能主要依赖文本参考目标而非图像来引导token生成。本文提出ViGOS，一种面向MLLM后训练的视觉引导在线策略自蒸馏框架。学生模型首先撰写视觉描述，然后逐步推理得出最终答案。对于有效轨迹，图像专属感知教师监督描述生成，而特权推理教师则在相同的学生前缀上监督推理过程和最终答案。仅当轨迹无效时使用参考教师来恢复输出格式。在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间定位和视觉语言先验等基准测试中，ViGOS保留了OPSD的主要优势，并在易出现捷径问题的场景中改善了基于图像的行为。

</details>


### [195] [DIPHINE: Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18997)
*Simon Pedro Galeano Munoz,Mustapha Bounoua,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPHINE，首个利用基于分数的扩散模型联合估计ΦID所需所有互信息项的神经估计器，通过Möbius逆变换恢复十六个信息原子，适用于连续非高斯动力系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ΦID计算方法仅限于高斯或离散系统，无法应用于连续非高斯动力系统，限制了其在真实世界复杂系统信息架构分析中的应用。

Method: 提出DIPHINE，一种基于扩散模型的神经估计器，通过单个摊销网络联合估计ΦID所需的所有互信息项，再通过Möbius逆变换恢复十六个信息原子。

Result: 在合成基准上准确恢复了真实信息原子；相比已有互信息估计器性能更优；在真实数据上无需任何分布假设即可提取出生理上可解释的信息动力学结构；理论分析证明了误差通过逆变换传播时雅可比矩阵为整数值，且协同-协同原子是最难估计的。

Conclusion: DIPHINE成功将ΦID框架扩展到连续非高斯动力系统，为复杂系统信息架构分析提供了无需分布假设的实用工具。

Original Abstract: Uncovering the true informational architecture of real-world complex systems requires disentangling how their components uniquely store, redundantly share, and synergistically integrate information over time. Integrated Information Decomposition ($Φ$ID) is a framework for decomposing the information dynamics of multivariate systems into sixteen non-overlapping atoms that characterize redundant, unique, and synergistic modes of information storage, transfer, and integration. Existing methods to compute $Φ$ID are restricted to Gaussian or discrete systems, preventing its application to continuous non-Gaussian dynamical systems. We address this limitation by proposing DIPHINE (Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator), the first neural estimator that leverages score-based diffusion models to jointly estimate all the mutual information terms required by $Φ$ID from a single amortized network, recovering the sixteen atoms through Möbius inversion. We provide a theoretical analysis of error propagation through the inversion, showing that the Jacobian of the mapping from mutual informations to atoms is integer-valued and that the synergy-to-synergy atom is provably the hardest to estimate. We demonstrate accurate recovery of ground-truth atoms on synthetic benchmarks, superior performance compared to established mutual information estimators, and the ability to extract physiologically interpretable information-dynamic structure on an application involving real data without any distributional assumptions.

中文摘要翻译: 揭示真实世界复杂系统的真实信息架构需要厘清其组件如何随时间以独特方式存储、冗余共享以及协同整合信息。集成信息分解（ΦID）是一个用于分解多变量系统信息动力学的框架，它产生十六个互不重叠的信息原子，分别描述冗余、独特和协同的信息存储、传递和整合模式。现有的ΦID计算方法局限于高斯或离散系统，无法应用于连续非高斯动力系统。针对这一局限，我们提出了DIPHINE（基于扩散的ΦID神经估计器），这是首个利用基于分数的扩散模型从单个摊销网络中联合估计ΦID所需所有互信息项的神经估计器，并通过Möbius逆变换恢复全部十六个信息原子。我们对误差通过逆变换的传播进行了理论分析，结果表明从互信息到信息原子的映射的雅可比矩阵为整数值，且协同-协同原子被证明是最难估计的。我们在合成基准上展示了准确恢复真实信息原子的能力，相比已有互信息估计器性能更优，并在无需任何分布假设的真实数据应用中成功提取出生理上可解释的信息动力学结构。

</details>


### [196] [The Reward Was in Your Data All Along: Correcting Flow Matching with Discriminator-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19162)
*Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Felix Friedrich,Zhang Xiaofeng,Reyhane Askari-Hemmat,Xiaochuang Han,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Michal Drozdzal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分数/流匹配模型存在结构失配问题（匹配损失与样本质量评估目标不一致），提出判别器引导的强化学习（DRL），利用预训练表示空间中的判别器对数几率作为奖励，无需人类偏好即可提升生成质量和语义保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 分数匹配和流匹配模型存在结构失配：匹配损失（如ℓ2回归误差）与推断时评估样本质量的视觉和语义属性不一致。虽然偏好强化学习能绕开这一问题，但依赖昂贵的人类偏好标注，且人类偏好会混淆数据真实性与标注者个人倾向。因此需要一种无需人类偏好、又能有效引导模型生成高质量样本的奖励函数。

Method: 提出判别器引导的强化学习（DRL）。在预训练表示空间中训练一个判别器来区分真实数据与基模型生成的样本，将其对数几率作为KL正则化强化学习的奖励信号。预训练空间将判别器限制在感知有意义的特征方向上，判别器对数几率近似数据与模型之间对数似然比，这是以数据分布为目标的优化下最优奖励函数。

Result: 在SiT、JiT、REPA、RAE等多个骨干网络上，DRL显著降低了无引导FID（如SiT上从9.38降至2.62）和语义空间FD（如SiT在DINOv3上从88.2降至19.3），并提升了人类偏好奖励（无需在此类数据上训练）。在后续偏好后训练中，DRL提供了更好的偏好奖励与图像保真度之间的帕累托前沿，减少了过饱和、过亮等低级伪影。

Conclusion: DRL通过预训练表示空间中的判别器提供高质量奖励信号，在不依赖人类偏好的情况下有效解决了匹配模型的结构失配问题，同时提升了生成质量、语义保真度与偏好对齐，具有广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: Score- and flow-matching models often rely on preference-based reinforcement learning for two purposes: aligning with subjective preferences and, surprisingly, recovering properties such as visual realism and coherent object structure that matching-based training is intended to learn from the data itself. We argue that this reflects a structural mismatch. Matching losses measure $\ell_2$ regression error on the velocity or score field under training-time marginals, a proxy poorly aligned with the visual and semantic properties that determine sample quality at inference. Given a reward aligned with these properties, RL sidesteps the mismatch by evaluating the model on its own samples and following the reward landscape directly. The challenge is to obtain such a reward without relying on human preferences, which are expensive and conflate data realism with annotator inclinations.
  We propose Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL). DRL trains a discriminator to separate data from base-model samples in a pretrained representation space and uses its logit as the reward in KL-regularized RL. The pretrained space restricts the discriminator to perceptually meaningful directions, and the logit estimates the log-likelihood ratio between data and model, which is the optimal reward for targeting the data distribution. Across SiT, JiT, REPA, and RAE, DRL reduces guidance-free FID (e.g., $9.38 \to 2.62$ on SiT) and semantic-space FD (e.g., $88.2 \to 19.3$ on DINOv3 for SiT), with consistent gains across all backbones, and improves human-preference rewards without training on them. It also yields a better Pareto frontier between preference reward and image fidelity under subsequent preference-based post-training, increasing alignment while reducing low-level artifacts such as oversaturation and excessive brightness.

中文摘要翻译: 分数匹配和流匹配模型通常依赖基于偏好的强化学习来实现两个目的：对齐主观偏好，以及——令人惊讶地——恢复视觉真实感和一致的物体结构等属性，而这些属性本应是基于匹配的训练旨在从数据本身中学习的。我们认为这反映了一种结构性失配。匹配损失在训练时的边际分布下衡量速度场或分数场的ℓ2回归误差，这一代理指标与决定推断时样本质量的视觉和语义属性之间对齐不良。给定一个与这些属性对齐的奖励函数，强化学习通过让模型在其自身生成的样本上进行评估并直接跟随奖励曲面来绕过这种失配。挑战在于如何在不依赖人类偏好的情况下获得这样的奖励函数——人类偏好既昂贵，又会将数据真实性与标注者的个人倾向混为一谈。我们提出了判别器引导的强化学习（Discriminator-Guided RL，DRL）。DRL在预训练表示空间中训练一个判别器来区分真实数据与基模型生成的样本，并将其对数几率作为KL正则化强化学习中的奖励信号。预训练空间将判别器限制在感知有意义的特征方向上，而对数几率估计了数据与模型之间的对数似然比，这是以数据分布为目标的最优奖励。在SiT、JiT、REPA和RAE等模型上，DRL降低了无引导FID（例如，SiT上从9.38降至2.62）和语义空间FD（例如，SiT在DINOv3上从88.2降至19.3），在所有骨干网络上均取得一致的提升，且在不经偏好训练的情况下提高了人类偏好奖励。在后续基于偏好的后训练中，DRL在偏好奖励与图像保真度之间产生了更优的帕累托前沿，在提高对齐度的同时减少了过饱和、过亮等低级伪影。

</details>


### [197] [FoMoE: Breaking the Full-Replica Barrier with a Federation of MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19025)
*Lorenzo Sani,Zeyu Cao,Meghdad Kurmanji,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Yan Gao,Wanru Zhao,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoMoE是一种通过跨工作节点划分专家层来打破全模型副本范式、实现分布式MoE训练的系统，相比基线方法显著降低了通信开销并提升了吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布式训练方法（如DiLoCo、Photon）要求每个站点维护完整的模型副本，导致巨大的内存约束和通信开销，无法高效扩展MoE模型到地理分布式、弱连接的数据中心。

Method: FoMoE系统通过跨工作节点划分MoE的专家层，打破全模型副本范式；并提出了一种新颖的skip-token机制，在不影响训练效果的前提下进一步提升吞吐量。

Result: 通信成本相比高效基线降低1.42倍，相比DDP降低45.44倍；通过skip-token机制实现高达1.4倍的吞吐量加速；在训练的代理场景中路由稳定性良好，系统建模表明该方法可扩展至100B参数规模。

Conclusion: FoMoE通过跨节点划分专家层和skip-token机制，在分布式MoE训练中实现了显著更优的通信效率和吞吐量，为跨地理分布式数据中心训练大规模模型提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Pre-training Large Language Models (LLMs) typically demands large-scale infrastructure with tightly coupled hardware accelerators. While increasing model and dataset scale remains the dominant driver of performance, Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) architectures have recently achieved state-of-the-art results by decoupling parameter count from computational cost. This efficiency enables training massive models on constrained compute budgets, yet it typically requires the high-speed interconnects of a single datacenter. To overcome these physical limits, recent approaches such as DiLoCo and Photon use low-communication data-parallel methods to enable scaling across geographically distributed, weakly connected data centers. However, these methods suffer from a fundamental inefficiency: they require full model replicas at every site, which imposes prohibitive memory constraints and communication overheads. In this work, we introduce FoMoE, a system that breaks the full-replica paradigm by partitioning expert layers across workers. We demonstrate that FoMoE: (I) reduces communication costs by up to 1.42x over efficient baselines and 45.44x over DDP via partial expert replication in the studied regimes; (II) achieves empirical throughput speedups of up to 1.4x through a novel skip-token mechanism; and (III) shows stable routing in the trained proxy regimes and projects the communication/memory benefits to 100B-scale configurations through system modelling.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练大型语言模型（LLM）通常需要配备紧耦合硬件加速器的大规模基础设施。虽然增加模型和数据集规模仍然是性能提升的主要驱动力，但混合专家（MoE）架构通过将参数数量与计算成本解耦，近期取得了最先进的结果。这种效率使得在有限计算预算下训练大规模模型成为可能，然而通常需要单个数据中心内的高速互连。为了克服这些物理限制，最近的方法（如DiLoCo和Photon）使用低通信的数据并行方法，使得能够在地理分布、弱连接的数据中心之间进行扩展。然而，这些方法存在一个根本性的低效问题：它们要求每个站点维护完整的模型副本，从而带来了难以承受的内存约束和通信开销。在本工作中，我们引入了FoMoE，一种通过跨工作节点划分专家层来打破全副本范式的系统。我们证明FoMoE：（I）在研究的场景中，通过部分专家复制，相比高效基线降低了最多1.42倍的通信成本，相比DDP降低了45.44倍；（II）通过一种新颖的skip-token机制实现了高达1.4倍的实证吞吐量加速；（III）在训练的代理场景中展示了稳定的路由，并通过系统建模预测了其在100B参数规模配置下的通信/内存优势。

</details>


### [198] [A Hybrid LSTM--Vision Transformer Architecture for Predicting HRRR Forecast Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19026)
*David Aaron Evans,Jay C. Rothenberger,Kara J. Sulia,Nick P. Bassill,Chris D. Thorncroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LSTM-Vision Transformer混合框架，融合地面观测与大气廓线数据，预测HRRR模式降水、风速和温度的预报误差，相较基线LSTM模型预测技能提升约两倍，尤其在对流和边界层活跃期表现突出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高分辨率数值天气预报中的预报误差通常与未解析的边界层过程、对流和地形诱导环流等垂直结构的天气现象有关。纯LSTM模型虽能利用地面观测预测误差，但在复杂大气垂直演化时期性能下降，因此需要引入垂直方向的大气结构信息来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出LSTM-ViT混合框架：LSTM编码地面观测的时间序列特征，Vision Transformer编码大气廓线网络提供的垂直剖面信息，两者融合后共同预测HRRR模式在单个中网观测站点的逐时降水、10米风速和2米温度的预报误差。

Result: 1）引入大气廓线结构在所有三个预报量上均提升了误差预测技能；2）改进在较短预报时效和边界层活跃期最为显著；3）降水预报误差的预测技能提升约两倍，能更好捕捉对流驱动的误差演变，并减少边界层过程带来的性能退化。

Conclusion: 将时间序列学习与垂直注意力机制相结合，为提升业务NWP系统的预报误差预测提供了物理上有意义的途径，可为预报员提供关于模式偏差和预报置信度的增强指导。

Original Abstract: Forecast errors in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) systems are often linked to unresolved planetary boundary layer (PBL) processes, convection, terrain-induced circulations, and other vertically structured atmospheric phenomena. Previous work demonstrated that Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can successfully predict forecast errors in the High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) model using mesonet observations, but we believe performance degradation is linked to periods of complex vertical atmospheric evolution. To address this limitation, we develop a hybrid LSTM-Vision Transformer (LSTM-ViT) framework that combines temporal sequence learning from surface observations with atmospheric profiles from the New York State Mesonet profiler network. The LSTM-ViT framework is trained to predict HRRR hourly precipitation, 10 m wind speed, and 2 m temperature forecast errors at individual mesonet stations. Across all three predictors, incorporation of profiler-derived atmospheric structure improves forecast error prediction skill relative to the baseline LSTM architecture, with the largest gains occurring at shorter forecast lead times and during periods of enhanced PBL activity. Improvements are particularly pronounced for precipitation forecast error, where the LSTM-ViT framework achieves approximately a twofold increase in predictive skill relative to the baseline LSTM while better capturing convectively driven error evolution and reducing degradation associated with PBL processes. These results demonstrate that combining temporal sequence learning with vertically informed attention mechanisms provides a physically meaningful pathway for improving forecast error prediction in operational NWP systems. Our research offers forecasters enhanced guidance regarding model bias and forecast confidence.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率数值天气预报系统中的预报误差往往与未解析的行星边界层过程、对流、地形诱导环流以及其他垂直结构的大气现象有关。先前的研究表明，长短期记忆网络能够利用中网观测数据成功预测高分辨率快速刷新模式的预报误差，但我们认为性能退化与复杂大气垂直演化时期密切相关。为解决这一局限，我们开发了一种混合LSTM-Vision Transformer框架，将来自地面观测的时间序列学习与纽约州中网廓线仪网络提供的大气廓线相结合。LSTM-ViT框架被训练用于预测HRRR模式在单个中网观测站点的逐时降水、10米风速和2米温度预报误差。在所有三个预报量上，引入廓线仪导出的大气结构相比基线LSTM架构均提升了预报误差预测技能，其中最大的提升出现在较短预报时效和边界层活动增强时期。对于降水预报误差的改进尤为显著，LSTM-ViT框架相较基线LSTM实现了约两倍的预测技能提升，同时能够更好地捕捉对流驱动的误差演变并减少与边界层过程相关的性能退化。这些结果表明，将时间序列学习与垂直信息注意力机制相结合，为改进业务NWP系统中的预报误差预测提供了一条具有物理意义的途径。我们的研究为预报员提供了关于模式偏差和预报置信度的增强型指导。

</details>


### [199] [Geometric and Stochastic Analysis of Discontinuities in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19036)
*Tho Tran Huu,Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Thien-Hai Nguyen,Nhat-Tri Ho,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tho Quan,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对稀疏混合专家(SMoE)架构中的不连续性进行了严格的几何和随机分析，将不连续面按阶分类，证明低阶不连续占主导地位，并据此提出一种轻量级平滑机制，在不显著增加计算开销的同时提升模型连续性和实证性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SMoE架构中Top-k专家选择机制导致映射函数本质不连续，在切换面附近微小的输入变化可能激活完全不同的专家集，产生显著不同的输出。这种不连续性影响了模型的稳定性和可靠性，但此前缺乏对这一现象的严格理论分析。

Method: 1) 几何分析：对不连续面按阶分类（阶数由切换事件中并列专家的数量决定），利用测度论切片方法建立增厚不连续面的渐近体积估计。2) 随机分析：将输入空间随机扰动建模为扩散过程，证明路径几乎必然遇到不连续面，且首次命中几乎必然是阶-1不连续，给出有限时间概率上界和占据时间界。3) 提出轻量级平滑机制：在不连续面附近软性融入专家，保证计算开销小。

Result: 理论表明低阶不连续集占主导，高阶不连续集相对体积趋于零；随机路径首次命中几乎必然是阶-1不连续；所提平滑机制在语言和视觉任务上不仅保证了连续性，还提升了实证性能。

Conclusion: 低阶不连续在SMoE中占主导地位，因此针对性的平滑策略可以有效缓解不连续性问题；所提平滑机制计算开销小，且能同时提升模型连续性和任务性能。

Original Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) architectures are now widely deployed in state-of-the-art language and vision models, where conditional routing allows scaling to very large networks. However, this very Top-$k$ expert selection that enables conditional routing also renders the SMoE map inherently discontinuous. In the vicinity of these discontinuity surfaces, even inputs that are arbitrarily close may activate substantially different sets of experts resulting in significantly different outputs. In this work we give a rigorous geometric and stochastic analysis of these discontinuities. We first classify them by order, determined by the number of tied experts at a switching event. Using measure-theoretic slicing arguments, we establish asymptotic volume estimates for the thickened discontinuity surfaces, showing that lower-order discontinuity sets dominate, whereas higher-order ones occupy a vanishingly small relative volume. Next, modeling random perturbations in the input space via a diffusion process, we prove that the path eventually encounter a discontinuity, and moreover that the first hit almost surely occurs on an order-1 discontinuity with explicit finite-time probability bounds. We further derive occupation-time bounds that quantify the duration the random path spend in the neighborhoods of each discontinuity order. These theoretical results imply that inputs are more likely to lie near lower order discontinuities. Motivated by this insight, we propose a simple smoothing mechanism that can be directly applied to existing SMoEs, softly incorporating experts near discontinuities; our analysis guarantees that the added computational overhead remains small while providing localized smoothing near discontinuities, and experiments across language and vision tasks show that smoothing not only enforces continuity of the SMoE map but also enhances empirical performance.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏混合专家(SMoE)架构现已广泛部署在先进的语言和视觉模型中，其中条件路由机制使得网络可以扩展到非常大的规模。然而，正是这种实现条件路由的Top-$k$专家选择机制，也导致SMoE映射本质上是非连续的。在这些不连续面的邻域内，即使任意接近的输入也可能激活截然不同的专家集，从而产生显著不同的输出。本文对这些不连续性进行了严格的几何和随机分析。我们首先按阶对它们进行分类，阶数由切换事件中并列专家的数量决定。利用测度论的切片论证，我们建立了增厚不连续面的渐近体积估计，表明低阶不连续集占主导地位，而高阶不连续集的相对体积趋于零。接着，将输入空间中的随机扰动建模为扩散过程，我们证明了路径最终几乎必然遇到不连续面，并且首次命中几乎必然发生在阶-1不连续上，同时给出了显式的有限时间概率上界。我们还推导了占据时间界，用以量化随机路径在每个阶数不连续邻域内停留的时长。这些理论结果表明输入更可能位于低阶不连续附近。受此启发，我们提出了一种可直接应用于现有SMoE的简单平滑机制，在不连续面附近软性融入专家；我们的分析保证了所增加的计算开销很小，同时在不连续面附近提供局部平滑。在语言和视觉任务上的实验表明，平滑不仅增强了SMoE映射的连续性，还提升了实证性能。

</details>


### [200] [Smoothness-Based Derandomization of PAC-Bayes Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19105)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对平滑损失函数的PAC-Bayes去随机化框架，通过控制Jensen间隙类的Rademacher复杂度，从Gibbs预测器过渡到后验均值处的确定性预测器，推导出含参数Jacobian和Hessian平坦度量的高概率泛化界，并基于这些量设计了实用的正则化器，在CIFAR-10上验证了其效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PAC-Bayes边界通常针对随机预测器（Gibbs预测器），但实际部署中常用的是确定性预测器。本文旨在利用损失函数和预测器类的平滑性，为确定性预测器推导出高概率的泛化界，填补随机预测器与确定性预测器之间的理论空白。

Method: 通过分析从Gibbs预测器到后验均值处确定性预测器的转换代价（即Jensen间隙类），利用Rademacher复杂度控制该间隙类的泛化差距，得到含参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦度量泛化界。针对BatchNorm网络，将BatchNorm变换折叠到相邻仿射权重中计算正则化项。

Result: 理论方面：得到了针对线性预测器和光滑神经网络的确定性预测器泛化界，界中涉及Jacobian和Hessian平坦度量。实验方面：在CIFAR-10上验证了所提正则化器在不同batch size下的行为表现。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了平滑损失下确定性预测器的PAC-Bayes泛化理论，理论导出的Jacobian/Hessian平坦度量可转化为实用的正则化器，在BatchNorm网络上有效。该框架统一适用于有界和无界平滑损失函数。

Original Abstract: We study PAC-Bayes derandomization for smooth loss functions. Our goal is to obtain generalization bounds that hold with high probability for deterministic predictors by exploiting smoothness properties of both the loss and the predictor class. We show that passing from the Gibbs predictor to the deterministic predictor at the posterior mean has a precise cost, given by the generalization gap of the Jensen gap class. We control this class through its Rademacher complexity, leading to bounds for deterministic predictors that involve flatness quantities expressed in terms of parameter Jacobians and Hessians of the score map. The framework applies to both bounded and unbounded smooth loss functions, and we specialize the results to linear predictors and smooth neural networks. Finally, the Jacobian and Hessian quantities appearing in the theory motivate a practical regularizer. For BatchNorm networks, we compute this regularizer with respect to effective BatchNorm weights obtained by folding the BatchNorm transformation into the adjacent affine weights. Experiments on CIFAR-10 illustrate the behavior of this regularizer under different batch sizes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究针对平滑损失函数的PAC-Bayes去随机化。我们的目标是通过利用损失函数和预测器类的平滑性质，为确定性预测器获得高概率成立的泛化界。我们证明了从Gibbs预测器过渡到后验均值处的确定性预测器具有一个精确的代价，该代价由Jensen间隙类的泛化差距给出。我们通过其Rademacher复杂度控制该类，从而得到适用于确定性预测器的界，其中涉及以参数Jacobian和得分映射Hessian表示的平坦度量。该框架同时适用于有界和无界平滑损失函数，并将结果专门应用于线性预测器和光滑神经网络。最后，理论中出现的Jacobian和Hessian量激发了一个实用的正则化器。对于BatchNorm网络，我们通过将BatchNorm变换折叠到相邻的仿射权重中，计算相对于有效BatchNorm权重的正则化器。在CIFAR-10上的实验说明了该正则化器在不同批次大小下的行为。

</details>


### [201] [JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19108)
*Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Xin Liu,Bin Xu,Han Zhao,Xiaowei Liu,Tracy Yu,Hui Gao,Huiji Gao,Liwei He,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了JourneyFormer，一个在Airbnb搜索排序中部署的序列建模解决方案，详细阐述了其设计决策、数据策略和工程优化，并通过离线指标和在线A/B测试证明了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 序列建模在推荐排序中日益流行，但在实际生产部署中面临序列复杂度高、标签稀疏等挑战。Airbnb的客人序列通常较长、探索性强且复杂，而预订标签又极为稀疏，因此需要在效果与可扩展性之间取得平衡，亟需一个有效的生产级序列建模方案。

Method: 提出JourneyFormer序列建模框架，围绕四个关键设计维度展开：客人事件选择、ID嵌入、模型架构和标签归属。同时采用若干定制化策略加速模型训练与推理，最终在Airbnb的两个生产搜索排序面上线部署。

Result: JourneyFormer在离线排序指标上表现提升，并通过在线A/B测试在两个生产面上取得了关键业务指标的显著增长。

Conclusion: JourneyFormer作为Airbnb搜索排序的序列建模方案，成功解决了生产环境中序列复杂和标签稀疏的实际挑战，兼顾了效果与可扩展性，具有显著的工业应用价值。

Original Abstract: Sequence modeling has become increasingly popular in recommendation and ranking algorithms, owing to its capacity to model users' historical behaviors and infer user intentions. Despite its theoretical simplicity, the practical deployment of a sequence model in production is non-trivial due to complexity of the sequence and sparse labels. For example, in Airbnb, guest sequences are often long, exploratory and complex, and we focus on booking labels, which are sparse. As such, we are often required to make various design decisions regarding data and modeling to strike a balance between effectiveness and scalability. This work delved into these production challenges and deployed JourneyFormer, a sequence modeling solution for search ranking at Airbnb. We detail crucial design considerations, covering aspects such as guest event selection, ID embeddings, model architecture, and label attribution. Additionally, we describe several tailored strategies to accelerate model training and inference. JourneyFormer has been successfully deployed within Airbnb's production, where its effectiveness and impact have been evidenced not only by improved offline ranking metrics but also by significant gains in key business metrics through online A/B testing across 2 production surfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 序列建模因其能够建模用户历史行为并推断用户意图的能力，在推荐与排序算法中日益流行。尽管理论简洁，但由于序列的复杂性和稀疏标签，序列模型在生产环境中的实际部署并非易事。以Airbnb为例，客人的序列通常较长、探索性强且复杂，而我们所关注的预订标签又极为稀疏。因此，我们常常需要在数据和建模方面做出各种设计决策，以在效果与可扩展性之间取得平衡。本工作深入探讨了这些生产挑战，并部署了JourneyFormer——一个用于Airbnb搜索排序的序列建模解决方案。我们详细阐述了关键的设计考量，涵盖客人事件选择、ID嵌入、模型架构和标签归属等方面。此外，我们还介绍了几种定制化策略以加速模型训练与推理。JourneyFormer已在Airbnb的生产环境中成功部署，其有效性和影响力不仅通过改进的离线排序指标得到证实，还通过涵盖两个生产面的在线A/B测试在关键业务指标上的显著提升得以体现。

</details>


### [202] [Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19134)
*Arnaud Lequen,Clément Legrand-Lixon,Léo Saulières*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PQLRM是一种结合Pareto Q-Learning和Reward Machines的多目标强化学习算法，能够高效处理非马尔可夫奖励结构下的多策略优化问题，实验表明其收敛速度快于朴素PQL基线，并能合成QRM无法得到的Pareto最优策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法中，Pareto Q-Learning (PQL) 在非马尔可夫奖励信号下样本效率低，而Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM) 无法处理多目标Pareto最优策略的合成问题。因此需要一种既能利用奖励机器结构信息、又能高效逼近Pareto前沿的多策略算法。

Method: PQLRM将Pareto Q-Learning (PQL) 与Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM) 相结合：PQL维护向量值Q估计的集合以近似Pareto前沿，QRM利用奖励信号分解后的自动机结构提升学习效率。两者结合形成一种多策略强化学习算法，专门针对由奖励机器指定的非马尔可夫奖励结构任务。

Result: 实验表明，PQLRM收敛速度快于直接应用于交叉乘积MDP的朴素PQL基线方法，并且能够合成QRM无法得到的Pareto最优策略。

Conclusion: PQLRM有效地弥合了多目标强化学习与结构化奖励建模之间的鸿沟，在保持样本效率的同时实现了多策略Pareto前沿逼近，拓展了强化学习在复杂奖励结构下的应用能力。

Original Abstract: We present Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM), a multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm for tasks whose reward structure is specified by a set of reward machines (RMs). PQLRM combines Pareto Q-Learning (PQL), which maintains sets of vector-valued Q-estimates to approximate the Pareto front, with enhancements from Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM), which exploits the factored automaton structure of the reward signal. This yields a multi-policy algorithm that remains sample-efficient under non-Markovian, RM-encoded rewards. Experimental trials show that PQLRM converges faster than a naive PQL baseline applied to the cross-product MDP and can synthesize Pareto-optimal policies that QRM cannot.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM)，一种用于任务奖励结构由一组奖励机器（RMs）指定的多目标强化学习算法。PQLRM将Pareto Q-Learning（PQL）——该方法维护向量值Q估计的集合以近似Pareto前沿——与Q-Learning with Reward Machines（QRM）的增强策略相结合，后者利用了奖励信号分解后的自动机结构。由此产生了一种多策略算法，在非马尔可夫、由RM编码的奖励下仍能保持样本效率。实验表明，PQLRM比直接应用于交叉乘积MDP的朴素PQL基线收敛更快，并且能够合成QRM无法得到的Pareto最优策略。

</details>


### [203] [INDEQS: Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19138)
*Michael Detzel,Gabriel Nobis,Kristiyan Blagov,Juri Schubert,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出INDEQS，一种将先验有向图结构嵌入神经受控微分方程（NCDE）的图基时间序列预测方法，通过区分“内部混合”与“外部混合”两种架构位置来注入图信息，在合成和真实基准上优于无信息NCDE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准图基NCDE扩展通常完全从数据中学习空间结构，但在许多实际场景中（如水文网络、交通流），有向图结构是预先已知的。现有方法未能有效利用这种先验知识，因此需要一种能将已知图结构有指导性地嵌入预测模型的方法。

Method: INDEQS将图先验知识注入NCDE的两个不同架构位置：内部混合（inner mixing，隐藏状态在图节点间的混合）和外部混合（outer mixing，向量场与控制信号之间的混合）。提供两种变体：轻量级图约束变体（严格遵循已知邻接）和更具表达力的变体（通过自适应图卷积从数据中学习额外连接）。同时比较了离散卷积解码器和连续时间解码器在真实任务上的表现。

Result: 在合成有向图平流模拟、河流水位预测和PeMS08交通流预测三个基准上：外部图信息注入在参数量相近情况下持续优于无信息NCDE，特别是在大图上；内部信息注入在需要严格遵循已知邻接时提供更参数高效的替代方案；连续时间解码器在准确性和时间灵活性上优于离散卷积解码器。

Conclusion: 将先验有向图结构以特定方式（尤其是外部混合）注入NCDE能够显著提升预测性能，且连续时间解码器更适合实际预测任务。

Original Abstract: Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDE) provide a powerful continuous-time framework for forecasting time series, but standard graph-based extensions typically learn spatial structure purely from data, even in settings where a directed graph structure is known a priori. We introduce Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS (INDEQS), a graph-based NCDE forecasting method that incorporates prior knowledge of a directed graph at distinct architectural positions. INDEQS separates inner mixing of hidden states across graph nodes from outer mixing between vector field and control, and offers both a lightweight graph-constrained variant and a more expressive variant, learning additional graph connections from data via adaptive graph convolutions. To systematically study when graph informedness is beneficial in forecasting, we devise a continuous advection simulation on directed graphs, yielding synthetic spatio-temporal datasets with known ground-truth flow structure. We then evaluate INDEQS on two real-world tasks: river discharge forecasting on a hydrological network and traffic flow prediction on PeMS08. Across these synthetic and real-world benchmarks, outer informedness consistently improves mean absolute error over an uninformed NCDE with comparable parameter count, particularly on larger graphs, while inner informedness offers a more parameter-efficient alternative when strict adherence to a known adjacency is desired. A comparison of discrete convolutional and continuous-time decoders further shows that continuous decoders yield better accuracy and greater temporal flexibility on real-world tasks. An implementation of INDEQS and the advection simulation is available at https://github.com/Mitchi1/indeqs.
