中文摘要翻译: 神经受控微分方程（NCDE）为时间序列预测提供了一种强大的连续时间框架，但标准的图基扩展通常完全从数据中学习空间结构，即使在有向图结构先验已知的场景下也是如此。我们提出了信息增强型神经受控微分方程（INDEQS），这是一种基于图的NCDE预测方法，能够在不同的架构位置融入先验有向图知识。INDEQS将隐藏状态在图节点间的内部混合与向量场和控制信号之间的外部混合分离开来，并提供了轻量级图约束变体和更具表达力的变体（后者通过自适应图卷积从数据中学习额外的图连接）。为了系统性地研究图信息在预测中何时有益，我们在有向图上设计了连续平流模拟，生成具有已知真实流结构的合成时空数据集。然后，我们在两个真实任务上评估INDEQS：水文网络上的河流水位预测和PeMS08上的交通流预测。在这些合成和真实基准测试中，外部信息注入在参数量相近的情况下持续优于无信息NCDE（平均绝对误差更低），尤其是在较大图上；而内部信息注入则在需要严格遵循已知邻接时提供了更参数高效的替代方案。对离散卷积解码器和连续时间解码器的进一步比较表明，连续解码器在真实任务上具有更好的准确性和更大的时间灵活性。INDEQS及平流模拟的实现可在 https://github.com/Mitchi1/indeqs 获取。

</details>


### [204] [ChronoSurv: A Clinical Pathway-Guided Graph Framework for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19140)
*Hugo Miccinilli,Theo Di Piazza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChronoSurv，一个基于有向图的异构层次框架，用于头颈癌多模态生存分析，通过建模临床轨迹感知的诊疗过程，在保持校准可靠性的同时实现了最先进的判别性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度生存模型依赖静态融合策略或时间无关建模，无法捕捉结构化临床工作流中多模态数据的异质性和高维性，限制了生存预测的准确性。

Method: 构建异质层次有向图框架ChronoSurv：将患者诊疗过程表示为与关键诊断步骤对齐的进展感知临床轨迹；层次拓扑整合细粒度、粗粒度和全局表示以灵活适应缺失模态；异质消息传递建模跨模态和临床步骤的复杂非对称关系。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，ChronoSurv实现了最先进的判别性能，同时保持统计上可靠的校准性能；消融实验验证了各架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的图建模方法有效提升了多模态生存预测的性能，为个性化治疗规划提供了有力支持。

Original Abstract: Accurate survival prediction is essential for personalized treatment planning in head and neck cancer, yet remains challenging due to the heterogeneous and high-dimensional nature of multimodal clinical data. While deep survival models have improved predictive performance over classical statistical approaches, existing methods typically rely on static fusion strategies or temporally agnostic modeling, limiting their ability to capture structured clinical workflows. In this work, we propose ChronoSurv, a heterogeneous hierarchical directed graph framework for multimodal survival analysis. ChronoSurv represents patient care as a progression-aware clinical trajectory using directed graphs aligned with key diagnostic steps. A hierarchical topology incorporates fine-grained, coarse, and global representations, further supporting flexible adaptation to missing modalities, while heterogeneous message passing models complex and asymmetric relationships across modalities and clinical steps. Experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that ChronoSurv achieves state-of-the-art discriminative performance while maintaining statistically reliable calibration. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the contribution of each architectural component, highlighting the potential of trajectory-aware graph modeling for multimodal survival prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的生存预测对于头颈癌的个性化治疗规划至关重要，但由于多模态临床数据的异质性和高维性，这一任务仍然具有挑战性。尽管深度生存模型相比经典统计方法提升了预测性能，但现有方法通常依赖静态融合策略或时间无关建模，限制了其捕捉结构化临床工作流的能力。本文提出ChronoSurv，一种用于多模态生存分析的异质层次有向图框架。ChronoSurv利用与关键诊断步骤对齐的有向图，将患者诊疗过程表示为进展感知的临床轨迹。层次拓扑整合了细粒度、粗粒度和全局表示，进一步支持对缺失模态的灵活适应，而异质消息传递则建模了跨模态和临床步骤的复杂非对称关系。在两个公开数据集上的实验结果表明，ChronoSurv在保持统计上可靠的校准性能的同时，实现了最先进的判别性能。全面的消融研究进一步证实了各架构组件的贡献，凸显了轨迹感知图建模在多模态生存预测中的潜力。

</details>


### [205] [OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145)
*Till Richter,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出正交正则化（OrthoReg）方法，直接惩罚符号组件与神经网络组件之间的重叠，防止神经部分吸收符号结构，从而在混合建模中实现互补分解，提升符号恢复和分布外泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 混合建模中神经网络可能重新学习机械论部分，导致模型冗余且不可解释，尤其当符号结构从数据中稀疏发现时，基于标准L²正则化的方法因其投影论证失效而无法解决符号-神经重叠问题。

Method: 提出OrthoReg（正交正则化），直接对符号组件与神经网络组件之间的重叠施加惩罚，迫使二者正交，从而实现互补分解：符号部分捕获库可表达的内容，神经部分捕获剩余部分。

Result: 在存在部分库不匹配的基准动力系统上，OrthoReg改善了符号恢复效果和分布外行为性能。

Conclusion: OrthoReg有效地防止了符号结构被神经残差吸收，实现了符号与神经组件的互补分解，在混合建模中优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Dynamical systems are fundamental to modeling the natural world, yet modeling them involves a persistent trade-off: manually prescribed mechanistic models are interpretable by design but often overly simplistic and misspecified; in contrast, flexible data-driven neural methods lack physical insight. Hybrid modeling aims for the best of both worlds by combining a prescribed or symbolic, physics-based component with a flexible neural network. A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 动力系统是建模自然世界的基础，然而建模它们涉及一个持久的权衡：手动指定的机械论模型在设计上是可解释的，但往往过于简化且存在设定错误；相比之下，灵活的、数据驱动的神经方法缺乏物理洞察。混合建模旨在通过将指定的或符号化的、基于物理的组件与灵活的神经网络相结合，实现两全其美。然而，一个关键挑战在于，神经组件可能重新学习机械论部分，产生冗余且不可解释的模型，尤其当符号结构本身是从数据中发现时。基于标准L²正则化的现有方法依赖于一个投影论证，当符号组件通过稀疏发现学习时该论证失效，从而允许神经增强与符号结构重叠。我们引入了\textbf{OrthoReg}（正交正则化），它直接惩罚符号组件与神经网络组件之间的重叠，防止符号结构被神经残差吸收。这产生了一种互补分解：符号部分捕获库所能表达的内容，神经部分捕获剩余部分。在存在部分库不匹配的基准动力系统上，OrthoReg改善了符号恢复和分布外行为。

</details>


### [206] [Complementary Attention Head Pruning for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19150)
*Yaniv Livertovsky,Shahar Somin,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CAHP的无监督后处理框架，将注意力头剪枝问题建模为全局图论问题，通过图聚类和信息论距离度量自动选择互补的注意力头子集，无需预定义稀疏度，在SST-5和MNLI基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于梯度的重要性排序或随机门控的结构化剪枝方法存在不稳定性、结构退化以及需要大量手动超参数调优等问题，亟需一种无需预定义稀疏度、自动且稳定的注意力头剪枝方法。

Method: CAHP将头选择重新定义为全局图论问题，利用基于图的聚类结合信息论距离度量来识别并保留一组拓扑多样化的互补注意力头，通过识别边际性能递减曲线自动确定每层保留的头数，无需预定义稀疏度或剪枝比例。

Result: 在SST-5和MNLI基准上，跨不同Transformer模型尺度，CAHP在高压缩比下持续超越竞争基线；结构分析表明CAHP避免了梯度剪枝方法的"邻近偏差"，能够保留中间层中功能关键的注意力头集合。

Conclusion: CAHP作为一种新颖的后验框架，通过图论方法自动实现注意力头剪枝，在保持性能的同时有效压缩模型，避免了现有方法的手动调参和结构退化问题。

Original Abstract: The remarkable success of Transformer-based models in natural language processing stems from architectural scaling, which leads to a large number of parameters and hinders deployment in resource-constrained environments. While structured pruning offers a pathway to compression, existing state-of-the-art methods often rely on gradient-based importance ranking or stochastic gating, which suffer from instability, structural degeneration, and the need for extensive manual hyperparameter tuning. In this paper, we introduce CAHP (Complementary Attention Head Pruning), a novel post-hoc framework that redefines head selection as a global graph-theoretical problem. Rather than evaluating heads in isolation, CAHP utilizes graph-based clustering combined with information-theoretic distance measures to identify and preserve a topologically diverse subset of complementary attention heads. Without requiring a predefined sparsity level or pruning ratio, the framework automatically determines the number of selected attention heads across layers by identifying a diminishing marginal performance curve, where pruning additional heads leads to a sharp degradation in performance, as determined by the chosen polynomial degree. Extensive evaluations on the SST-5 and MNLI benchmarks, across different Transformer model scales, demonstrate that CAHP consistently outperforms competitive baselines, particularly in high-compression regimes. Furthermore, our structural analysis shows that CAHP avoids the "proximity bias" of gradient-based pruning methods, which tend to preserve heads mainly in layers close to the output, and instead retains a functionally critical set of attention heads in the model's intermediate layers.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Transformer的模型在自然语言处理中取得的显著成功源于架构上的扩展，但这导致参数量庞大，阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署。尽管结构化剪枝提供了一条压缩途径，但现有的最先进方法通常依赖基于梯度的重要性排序或随机门控，这些方法存在不稳定性、结构退化以及需要大量手动超参数调优等问题。本文提出了CAHP（互补注意力头剪枝），一种新颖的后验框架，将头选择重新定义为全局图论问题。CAHP并非孤立地评估注意力头，而是利用基于图的聚类结合信息论距离度量来识别并保留一组拓扑多样化的互补注意力头子集。该框架无需预定义稀疏度或剪枝比例，通过识别边际性能递减曲线自动确定各层所选注意力头的数量——当进一步剪枝导致性能急剧下降时停止，下降程度由所选多项式次数决定。在SST-5和MNLI基准上、跨不同Transformer模型规模的广泛评估表明，CAHP在高压缩比场景下持续优于竞争基线。此外，我们的结构分析表明，CAHP避免了梯度剪枝方法的"邻近偏差"——即倾向于保留靠近输出层的注意力头，而是保留了模型中间层中功能关键的注意力头集合。

</details>


### [207] [Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19164)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Xin Geng,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于本质子空间分解（ESD）的模型合并方法，通过将任务更新分解为本质子空间和残差子空间，并设计静态合并方法ESM和动态合并方法ESM++，有效减少任务间干扰，实现多任务学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 模型合并面临的核心挑战是任务间干扰。作者观察到任务更新引发的输出偏移能量集中在少量主方向上（本质子空间），而其余方向积累的干扰会导致严重干扰，因此需要一种能分离本质与干扰成分的合并方法。

Method: 提出本质子空间分解（ESD），基于激活偏移的主成分将每个任务更新分解为核心的本质成分和干扰性的残差成分。基于ESD，设计两种合并方法：（1）ESM：训练无关的静态合并，正交化并融合本质成分到紧凑的多任务模型中；（2）ESM++：训练无关的动态合并，将任务残差分解为低秩专家，在前向推理时通过原型路由选择最相关专家。

Result: 在多个任务集和模型规模上的大量实验表明，ESM和ESM++有效保留了任务知识同时减少了任务间干扰。

Conclusion: 通过本质子空间分解有效分离任务相关知识与干扰成分，静态合并（ESM）和动态合并（ESM++）均能显著提升多任务模型合并效果。

Original Abstract: Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.

中文摘要翻译: 模型合并旨在通过将多个从同一预训练检查点微调得到的模型的能力整合到单个模型中，从而实现多任务学习。其核心挑战是任务特定参数更新之间的任务间干扰。在本文中，我们分析了任务更新引起的输出偏移，观察到它们的能量集中在少量主方向上。我们将这些方向张成的子空间称为本质子空间。相比之下，大多数剩余方向携带很少的任务相关能量，但它们在多个任务更新中的累积会在合并过程中造成严重干扰。受此观察启发，我们提出了本质子空间分解（ESD），该方法根据激活偏移的主成分分解每个任务更新。基于ESD，我们引入了本质子空间合并（ESM），一种无需训练的动态合并方法，将本质成分正交化并融合成一个紧凑的多任务模型。我们进一步将ESM扩展到ESM++，一种无需训练的动态合并方法，该方法将任务特定残差分解为低秩专家，并在前向推理过程中通过基于原型的路由选择最相关的专家。在多个任务集和模型规模上的大量实验表明，ESM和ESM++有效保留了任务知识同时减少了任务间干扰。

</details>


### [208] [Compute Efficiency and Serial Runtime Tradeoffs for Stochastic Momentum Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19179)
*Depen Morwani,Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Nair,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机动量方法（HB和ASGD）在一致性线性回归中的批次大小权衡，证明了HB仅在更宽的批次窗口内保持SGD级别的计算效率，而ASGD的加速效果依赖于谱衰减速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随机动量方法（如HB、Nesterov动量、ASGD）在现代训练中广泛应用，但其在随机设置下的收益取决于两个不同量：序列运行时间（达到目标精度所需的迭代次数）和计算效率（CE，梯度查询或FLOP成本的倒数）。现有研究对批次大小如何影响这些方法的权衡理解不足，因此需要理论分析来阐明其批次大小权衡边界。

Method: 论文针对具有高斯协变量的一致性线性回归问题，研究了随机HB和ASGD方法，在有限维离散时间框架下推导了其批次大小权衡的下界，并分析了不同谱衰减情况下的性能表现。

Result: (1) HB并未改善SGD的计算效率前沿，而是将SGD级别的CE保持在一个更宽的批次窗口内（该窗口比SGD临界批次大小大$\sqrtκ$倍），允许使用更大批次来减少序列运行时间；(2) ASGD的效果依赖于谱分布：对于快速衰减的幂律谱，ASGD在小批次下提升CE，但随着批次增大需牺牲CE换取序列运行时间改进；(3) 合成线性回归实验验证了上述定性规律。

Conclusion: HB可通过扩大批次窗口来降低序列运行时间但不会提升CE前沿；ASGD的加速效果具有谱依赖性，在快速衰减谱下存在CE与序列运行时间的权衡。

Original Abstract: Stochastic momentum methods such as heavy ball (HB), Nesterov momentum, and variants of Accelerated SGD (ASGD) [Kidambi et al., 2018] are widely used in modern training, but their stochastic benefits depend on two distinct quantities: serial runtime, the number of iterations needed to reach a target accuracy, and compute efficiency (CE), the inverse total gradient-query or FLOP cost. Larger batches reduce serial runtime without hurting CE only when the contraction gap grows linearly with batch size.
  We study stochastic HB and ASGD for consistent linear regression with Gaussian covariates and prove finite-dimensional, discrete-time lower bounds on their batch-size tradeoffs. Our first result shows that HB does not improve the CE frontier over SGD for arbitrary spectra; rather, it preserves SGD-level CE over a larger batch-size window, allowing larger batches to reduce serial runtime until HB reaches its deterministic accelerated scale. This window can be a factor $\sqrtκ$ larger than the SGD critical batch size. For ASGD, the picture is more spectrum-dependent: for rapidly decaying power-law spectra, ASGD improves small-batch CE over HB/SGD, but as batch size grows it trades this CE advantage for improved serial runtime. Synthetic linear-regression experiments verify these qualitative regimes, including near-overlap of ASGD and HB for slowly decaying spectra and the predicted CE--serial tradeoff for rapidly decaying spectra.

中文摘要翻译: 随机动量方法，如重球法（HB）、Nesterov动量以及加速SGD（ASGD）的变体[Kidambi et al., 2018]，在现代训练中被广泛使用，但其随机性收益取决于两个不同的量：序列运行时间（达到目标精度所需的迭代次数）和计算效率（CE，即总梯度查询或FLOP成本的倒数）。更大的批次大小只有在收缩间隙随批次大小线性增长时，才能在不损害CE的前提下减少序列运行时间。本文研究了一致性线性回归（具有高斯协变量）下的随机HB和ASGD，并证明了它们在有限维离散时间框架下关于批次大小权衡的下界。我们的第一个结果表明，对于任意谱分布，HB并未改善SGD的CE前沿；相反，它在一个更大的批次窗口内保持了SGD级别的CE，允许使用更大的批次来减少序列运行时间，直至HB达到其确定性加速尺度。该窗口可以比SGD临界批次大小大$\sqrtκ$倍。对于ASGD，情况更依赖于谱分布：对于快速衰减的幂律谱，ASGD在小批次下相比HB/SGD提升了CE，但随着批次增大，它牺牲了这一CE优势以换取序列运行时间的改进。合成线性回归实验验证了这些定性规律，包括慢衰减谱下ASGD与HB的近乎重叠，以及快速衰减谱下预测的CE-序列运行时间权衡。

</details>


### [209] [AGDN: Learning to Solve Traveling Salesman Problem with Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19185)
*Bolin Shen,Ziwei Huang,Zhiguang Cao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN）用于求解旅行商问题（TSP），通过构造MixScore转移矩阵和各向异性图扩散策略，有效利用图结构信息，在多种规模和分布的数据集上实现了优于现有方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图学习方法在求解TSP时面临两个关键困难：(1) 全连接TSP图缺乏信息丰富的拓扑先验；(2) 常用的图稀疏化技术会导致最优解中的连接节点丢失。因此需要更有效地利用图结构信息的方法。

Method: (1) 构造MixScore转移矩阵，融合节点相似度与成对距离；(2) 提出各向异性图扩散策略，支持多跳信息高效交换，从而构建AGDN框架求解TSP。

Result: 在多种实例规模和节点分布下的全面实验表明，AGDN在保持计算时间竞争力的同时，持续优于现有方法，并且能很好地泛化到训练时未见的问题规模和分布。

Conclusion: AGDN通过各向异性图扩散机制有效解决了TSP中图结构利用不足的问题，在性能、效率和泛化能力上均表现出优势。

Original Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.

中文摘要翻译: 旅行商问题（TSP）是组合优化的基石问题，出现在众多实际场景中。尽管基于图学习的方法已被探索用于求解TSP，但如何更有效地利用图结构这一问题仍然悬而未决。我们提出了各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN），这是一种用于求解TSP的新型图神经网络框架。我们的方法解决了两个核心难点：(1) 全连接TSP图中缺乏信息丰富的拓扑先验；(2) 常用的图稀疏化技术会导致最优解中的连接节点丢失。为克服这些问题，我们构建了一个融合节点相似性与成对距离的MixScore转移矩阵，并开发了一种各向异性图扩散策略，支持跨多跳的高效信息交换。涵盖不同实例规模和节点分布的全面实验表明，AGDN在保持计算时间竞争力的同时，持续优于现有方法。此外，AGDN能够很好地泛化到训练时未见的问题规模和分布。实现代码已公开于：https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN。

</details>


### [210] [Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19199)
*Giuseppe Gabriele,Fabio Pavirani,Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种决策聚焦的强化学习（DF-RL）框架，通过端到端联合训练预测器与充电控制器，解决EV充电中关键特征（如离开时间）缺失导致RL策略效果下降的问题，相比基线方法总奖励提升14%，未供电能量减少55%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在真实EV充电场景中，关键特征（如离开时间）往往不可用，导致强化学习智能体难以学习并执行有效的充电策略。虽然可以用预测模型近似这些未知特征，但传统预测模型仅以精度为目标训练，其预测误差会传播并损害下游控制器的决策质量。

Method: 提出决策聚焦强化学习（DF-RL）框架，将预测器与RL充电策略控制器进行端到端联合训练，使预测器在训练过程中接收来自RL智能体充电策略动作的反馈信号，而非仅以预测精度为目标。

Result: 相比不使用离开时间预测的RL方法，所提出的DF-RL方法总奖励提升14%，未供电能量（因EV已离开而未能完成的充电量）减少55%。

Conclusion: 预测器与控制器的端到端联合训练能够产生更高质量的充电决策，有效缓解关键特征缺失带来的不确定性。

Original Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来电动汽车（EV）普及率的增长给电力系统带来了挑战，包括峰值需求增加和潜在的电网不稳定。基于强化学习（RL）的电动汽车充电智能控制可以通过从历史数据中学习时间与上下文模式来缓解这些问题。然而，在真实场景中，关键特征（如离开时间）往往不可用。这反过来使得RL智能体更难学习和执行有效的充电策略。为减轻这种不确定性，可以使用训练好的预测器从可用数据中近似未知特征。然而，由于这些预测模型通常以精度（而非其对下游智能体决策质量的影响）为目标进行训练，它们的误差可能传播并损害使用这些预测的控制器的整体性能。为避免这一问题，我们提出了一种决策聚焦强化学习（DF-RL）框架，其中预测器以端到端方式训练，即接收来自RL智能体充电策略动作的反馈。这种预测器与控制器的联合训练最终能产生更高质量的动作：与不使用离开时间预测的RL方法相比，我们提出的DF-RL方法能够产生更优的充电决策，总奖励提升高达14%，未供电能量（即因EV已离开而未能完成的充电量）减少55%。

</details>


### [211] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XGBoost-Forget，一种针对XGBoost模型的机器遗忘方法，用于表格型网络入侵检测数据，在不完全重训练的前提下高效移除特定数据点。实验表明该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著提升遗忘效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要聚焦于深度学习和图像数据，缺乏针对网络入侵检测领域（尤其是依赖表格数据的场景）的研究。

Method: 提出XGBoost-Forget方法，针对XGBoost模型设计了一种无需完全重训练即可移除特定数据点的遗忘机制。

Result: 在两个表格型网络入侵数据集（IoT-23和GeNIS）上，XGBoost-Forget在保持与原始模型相近的预测性能的同时，实现了显著更快的遗忘速度。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget在表格型网络入侵检测场景中具有潜力，能够高效实现机器遗忘。

Original Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

中文摘要翻译: 机器遗忘（MU）已成为一种从训练好的模型中移除特定数据点而无需完全重训练的重要技术。然而，现有的大多数MU研究聚焦于深度学习和图像数据，在网络入侵检测领域（该领域高度依赖表格数据）存在空白。本文提出XGBoost-Forget，一种针对XGBoost模型的遗忘方法，以填补这一空白。该方法在两个表格型网络入侵（NI）数据集IoT-23和GeNIS上进行了评估，使用了多种指标来评估模型性能、遗忘效率和遗忘质量。结果表明，XGBoost-Forget在保持与原始模型相近的预测性能的同时，显著加快了遗忘速度，展示了其在表格型NI场景中用于MU的潜力。

</details>


### [212] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST是一种机制对齐的选择性遗忘方法，通过仅更新注意力投影张量的子集来消除RLVR引起的推理能力，相比全参数更新显著降低附带损害。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准全参数梯度上升在消除RLVR诱导的推理能力时，会严重破坏模型保留的数学推理能力（如MATH和GSM8K），因此需要一种更精准、附带损害更低的遗忘方法。

Method: MAST首先分析SFT到RLVR的增量与SFT更新在token级delta-log-probability上的差异，然后通过非主能量、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合幅度三个指标对注意力投影张量进行排序，仅更新排名靠前的子集。

Result: 在主模型上，MAST实现了统计显著的目标遗忘（MATH遗忘从45/150降至37/150；McNemar p=0.0078），同时保持GSM8K（+0.8 pp）和MATH保留集（-0.5 pp）性能。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO目标以及Qwen3模型上均可复现，而全参数遗忘导致GSM8K崩溃。

Conclusion: MAST通过机制引导的参数选择性更新，能够在有效遗忘RLVR诱导推理能力的同时，显著降低对模型其他能力的附带损害，是一种比全参数更新更优的遗忘方法。

Original Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了MAST（机制对齐的选择性目标遗忘），一种机制引导的遗忘方法，用于消除RLVR诱导的推理能力，其附带损害显著低于标准的全参数更新。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B-Base的配对SFT/RLVR检查点上，SFT到RLVR的增量与SFT更新在token级delta-log-probability上存在显著差异，而全参数梯度上升只能通过破坏保留的MATH和GSM8K来达到遗忘效果。MAST通过非主能量、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合幅度三个指标对注意力投影张量进行排序，然后仅更新排名靠前的子集。在主模型上，MAST实现了统计显著的目标遗忘（MATH遗忘从45/150降至37/150；McNemar p=0.0078），同时保持GSM8K（+0.8个百分点）和MATH保留集（-0.5个百分点）的性能。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO目标以及Qwen3模型上均可复现，在Qwen3上MAST保留了GSM8K性能，而全参数遗忘则导致其崩溃。

</details>


### [213] [A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19230)
*Samuel Stricker,Claus Wirnsperger,Alessandro Butté,Laura Helleckes,Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez,Antonio del Rio Chanona,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了帕累托前沿引导采样（PFGS）框架，通过将约束满足概率显式建模为帕累托目标，并引入蒙特卡洛采样策略估计输入鲁棒性，使领域专家能够在人机协同的贝叶斯优化中交互式选择候选点。在八维CHO细胞培养模拟器上验证了该方法能系统识别高性能、可行且鲁棒的操作条件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化方法通常返回单一自动推荐结果，缺乏领域专家交互介入的灵活性；同时难以同时处理约束满足和输入扰动鲁棒性等多重目标之间的权衡。

Method: 在PFGS框架基础上，从两个方向扩展：(1) 约束优化：将满足输出规格限的后验概率（从GP后验分布解析计算）作为显式的帕累托目标；(2) 鲁棒优化：通过蒙特卡洛采样策略，在用户定义的输入扰动分布上估计期望置信下界性能。最终通过交互式仪表板的二维投影展示多维帕累托前沿。

Result: 在八维fed-batch CHO细胞培养模拟器上，该方法成功识别出高性能、满足可行性约束且对扰动具有弹性的操作条件，展示了专家定义需求可作为有原则的停止准则并支持实验资源的合理分配。

Conclusion: 提出的扩展框架使专家能够随着代理模型改进和开发目标演变迭代优化选择标准，为人机协同的贝叶斯优化提供了更灵活、更全面的决策支持工具。

Original Abstract: This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了帕累托前沿引导采样（PFGS）的扩展，这是一种人在回路（HitL）贝叶斯优化（BO）框架。在该框架中，高斯过程（GP）代理模型衍生量被重新表述为多目标优化问题的目标，所得帕累托前沿被呈现给领域专家进行交互式候选选择，而非返回单一的自动推荐结果。该框架在两个方向上进行了扩展：约束优化方面，通过将满足输出规格限的后验概率（从GP后验分布解析计算）作为显式的帕累托目标纳入优化；鲁棒优化方面，采用蒙特卡洛采样策略，在用户定义的输入扰动变化范围内估计期望置信下界性能，以捕获在可能的实现偏差下的性能退化。由此产生的多维帕累托表示通过交互式仪表板上的成对二维投影，使预测性能、模型不确定性、概率约束满足和输入鲁棒性之间的权衡同时可视化，从而能够随着代理模型改进和开发目标演变而迭代优化选择标准。该框架在八维补料分批中国仓鼠卵巢（CHO）细胞培养模拟器上进行了展示，证明了其能够系统性地识别高性能、满足可行性约束且对扰动具有弹性的操作条件，并说明了专家定义的需求如何提供有原则的停止准则并支持实验资源的合理分配。

</details>


### [214] [STARE: Surprisal-Guided Token-Level Advantage Reweighting for Policy Entropy Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19236)
*Haipeng Luo,Qingfeng Sun,Songli Wu,Can Xu,Wenfeng Deng,Han Hu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GRPO等可验证奖励强化学习算法中策略熵崩塌的问题，通过一阶梯度分析揭示了token级信用分配不匹配现象，并提出STARE方法——通过惊奇度引导的token级优势重新加权和目标熵闭环门控，在多个规模模型和任务上稳定熵并提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可验证奖励强化学习算法（如GRPO）在LLM复杂推理后训练中常遭遇策略熵崩塌，导致探索-利用失衡，严重影响训练稳定性和最终性能。本文从理论上分析了熵崩塌的根源，并据此提出解决方案。

Method: STARE方法包含三个关键组件：（1）通过批次内惊奇度分位数识别熵关键token子集；（2）对这些token的选择性重新加权有效优势；（3）引入目标熵闭环门控机制实现稳定的熵调节。

Result: 在1.5B到32B规模的模型、三种任务（短CoT、长CoT、多轮工具使用）上，STARE能在数千步训练中维持稳定策略熵。在AIME24和AIME25上，比DAPO等基线平均准确率提升4%-8%，反射token和响应长度同步增长，表明实现了持续的探索-利用平衡。

Conclusion: STARE通过解决token级信用分配不匹配问题，有效稳定了基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练中的策略熵，在多个规模和任务上取得显著性能提升，并进一步释放了RL训练潜力。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards algorithms like GRPO have emerged as the dominant post-training paradigm for complex reasoning in LLMs, yet commonly suffer from policy entropy collapse during training. We conduct a first-order gradient analysis of token-level entropy dynamics under GRPO and identify a token-level credit assignment mismatch: the per-token entropy variation decomposes into the product of the trajectory-level advantage and an entropy sensitivity function over the next-token distribution, yielding an advantage-surprisal four-quadrant structure and a near-criticality property. Motivated by it, we propose STARE (Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability), which identifies entropy-critical token subsets via batch-internal surprisal quantiles, selectively reweights their effective advantages, and incorporates a target-entropy closed-loop gate for stable entropy regulation. Across model scales from 1.5B to 32B and three task families (Short CoT, Long CoT, and Multi-Turn Tool Use), STARE sustains stable RL training over thousands of steps while maintaining policy entropy within the target band. On AIME24 and AIME25, STARE outperforms DAPO and other competitive baselines by 4%-8% in average accuracy, with reflection tokens and response length growing in tandem, indicating sustained exploration-exploitation balance that further unlocks RL training potential.Code is available at https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE.

中文摘要翻译: 以GRPO为代表的可验证奖励强化学习算法已成为大语言模型复杂推理后训练的主流范式，但训练中常出现策略熵崩塌问题。我们对GRPO下的token级熵动态进行了一阶梯度分析，识别出token级信用分配不匹配：每个token的熵变分解为轨迹级优势与下一个token分布上熵敏感度函数的乘积，形成了优势-惊奇度四象限结构及近临界特性。受此启发，我们提出STARE（惊奇度引导的token级优势重新加权以实现策略熵稳定性），该方法通过批次内惊奇度分位数识别熵关键token子集，选择性重新加权其有效优势，并引入目标熵闭环门控以实现稳定的熵调节。在1.5B至32B的模型规模以及三个任务族（短思维链、长思维链和多轮工具使用）上，STARE在数千步训练中维持稳定的RL训练，同时将策略熵保持在目标带内。在AIME24和AIME25上，STARE相比DAPO及其他有竞争力基线在平均准确率上提升4%-8%，反射token与响应长度同步增长，表明实现了持续的探索-利用平衡，进一步释放了RL训练潜力。代码已开源：https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE。

</details>


### [215] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多尺度聚类的时序异常检测方法SCAN，通过在表示层和异常准则层引入聚类约束，解决重建类方法中过度泛化与欠泛化难以平衡的问题，在多个真实数据集上取得了最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 重建类时序异常检测方法存在过度泛化（将异常也重建得很好）和欠泛化（正常模式也无法很好重建）的矛盾，现有方法难以平衡两者，因此需要引入聚类信息来约束模型聚焦于代表性的正常模式进行重建。

Method: 在表示层，将正常模式的聚类中心表示引入模型，约束重建过程聚焦于代表性正常模式；在异常准则层，基于聚类隶属概率推导异常置信度分数，与重建误差构成双重检测标准；同时提取邻域中心表示进行多视角聚类以提升聚类性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SCAN方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 多尺度聚类能够有效增强重建类时序异常检测方法，通过聚类约束同时缓解过度泛化和欠泛化问题，双重异常准则提升了检测效果。

Original Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列异常检测在广泛的现实应用中扮演着关键角色。基于重建的方法已成为主流范式，但这类方法存在过度泛化和欠泛化问题，两者难以平衡。为了解决这一问题，我们引入多尺度聚类来增强基于重建的方法。在表示层面，我们整合正常模式的聚类中心表示，约束模型以代表性正常模式为目标进行重建，从而防止强大容量和表示能力带来的主导性。在异常准则层面，我们基于聚类隶属概率推导异常置信度分数，并将其与重建误差相结合，为检测提供双重准则。此外，聚类中心表示和异常置信度分数的有效性依赖于聚类性能。为此，我们提取邻域中心表示进行多视角聚类，以提高聚类性能。在多个来自不同应用领域的真实世界数据集上进行的大量实验表明，SCAN达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [216] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了仅使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据（内容无关信号）进行GPU工作负载分类的对抗鲁棒性，通过5轮监控-逃避迭代，在4代架构的9种GPU模型上测试了20种逃避策略，发现分类器在全数据集上达到98.2%的训练工作负载识别精度，面对最困难的反欺骗工作负载仍保持43-87%的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 硬件级GPU工作负载监控是AI计算治理的基础，但如果开发者能够击败监控机制，相关方案将不可行。因此需要评估基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类在面对对抗性逃避时的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用5轮监控-逃避者迭代方法，在4代架构的9种GPU模型上评估20种逃避策略系列，使用仅基于零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测（内容无关信号，不访问模型权重、训练数据或超参数）进行GPU工作负载分类。

Result: 分类器在全数据集上达到98.2%的训练工作负载二元识别精度，面对最有挑战性的意外工作负载（即使经过对抗性伪装）仍保持43-87%的精度。

Conclusion: 基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类方法在面对对抗性逃避时仍保持较高的鲁棒性，即使开发者试图欺骗监控机制，该方法仍能有效识别训练工作负载。

Original Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

中文摘要翻译: 硬件支持的GPU工作负载监控是许多AI计算治理方案的基础，但如果开发者能够击败监控机制，这些方案将不可行。我们评估了仅使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据进行GPU工作负载分类的对抗鲁棒性：这些内容无关信号观察计算过程的物理效应，无需访问模型权重、训练数据或超参数。通过5轮监控-逃避者迭代，我们在跨越4代架构的9种GPU模型上评估了20种逃避策略系列。我们开发了一个分类器，在全数据集上实现98.2%的训练工作负载二元识别精度，面对最有挑战性的意外工作负载（即使经过对抗性伪装）仍保持43-87%的精度。

</details>


### [217] [Structured Inference with Large Language Gibbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19264)
*Sanghyeok Choi,Henry Gouk,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Large Language Gibbs方法，利用LLM的条件分布作为马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）的转移算子，对结构化概率推理进行迭代重采样，避免了单次自回归生成中的顺序偏差，在合成分布、一致性推理和贝叶斯结构学习任务上表现出有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型编码的知识可用于对复杂世界变量进行结构化推理，但以概率一致的方式访问这些知识是一个困难的推理问题。现有单次自回归生成方式存在顺序偏差，无法获取稳定且一致的概率分布。

Method: 提出Large Language Gibbs（LLG）方法，将LLM的条件分布作为MCMC中的转移算子，通过迭代地对单个变量在给定其他变量条件下的重采样，替代单次自回归生成，从而获得反映所有局部条件分布折中的平稳分布。

Result: 在合成分布采样、一致性推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习三个场景下的实验结果表明，该方法是有序生成的一种可行替代方案，能够有效利用LLM噪声条件分布进行结构化概率推理。

Conclusion: 在MCMC中使用LLM条件分布是替代单次自回归生成的一种实用方案，可用于在嘈杂的LLM条件分布所表示的世界先验下进行结构化概率推理。

Original Abstract: The knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) can serve as a substrate for structured reasoning over variables describing a complex world, but accessing this knowledge in a probabilistically coherent manner poses a difficult inference problem. We propose Large Language Gibbs, a scheme for structured probabilistic inference that uses conditional distributions of an LLM as transition operators. Rather than sampling structured objects through single-pass autoregressive generation, we iteratively resample individual variables conditioned on others using an LLM's next-token conditionals. This approach avoids order-dependent biases and produces a stationary distribution that reflects a compromise between all local conditionals. We apply this approach to sampling from synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning. The results suggest that the use of LLM conditionals in MCMC is a practical alternative to one-pass generation for structured probabilistic inference under a world prior accessible through noisy LLM conditionals.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中编码的知识可以作为对描述复杂世界变量进行结构化推理的基体，但以概率一致的方式访问这些知识是一个困难的推理问题。我们提出了Large Language Gibbs，一种利用LLM的条件分布作为转移算子的结构化概率推理方案。该方法并非通过单次自回归生成来采样结构化对象，而是利用LLM的下一个词元条件分布，迭代地以其他变量为条件对单个变量进行重采样。这种方法避免了顺序依赖偏差，并产生了一个反映所有局部条件分布之间折中的平稳分布。我们将该方法应用于合成分布采样、一致性推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习。结果表明，在可通过嘈杂的LLM条件分布访问的世界先验下，在MCMC中使用LLM条件分布是替代单次生成进行结构化概率推理的一种实用方案。

</details>


### [218] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了环境因素（光强和声压级）能否独立预测ICU患者谵妄，使用四种序列神经网络模型在9个ICU的309名患者数据上进行多预测窗口评估。卷积模型表现最佳（AUC=0.80），声音是主要预测因子，结合声音和光能改善短期预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 谵妄是ICU常见且严重的并发症，早期预测和预防仍具挑战。环境因素（如环境声音和光照）可能影响谵妄的发生，但在风险评估中常被忽略。因此，作者探究光强度和声压级能否作为多预测时间窗口下谵妄的独立预测因子。

Method: 在9个ICU的309名患者中采集光强和声压级数据，使用四种高效的序列神经网络模型（包括卷积模型），在10种不同预测窗口大小下进行谵妄预测，并采用Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) 分析特征重要性和影响方向。

Result: 卷积模型在声音数据和组合数据上均达到AUC=0.80的最佳判别性能。声音特征是整体上的主导预测因子。结合声音与光照可改善短期（<1周）预测效果，且组合模型在感知周期结束后立即赋予最高风险。

Conclusion: 被动环境感知，尤其是声音监测，可为谵妄风险评估提供临床上有意义的可解释信号，并为增强多模态ICU预测与预防策略提供实用路径。

Original Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 谵妄是重症监护病房（ICU）中常见且严重的并发症，与发病率增加、住院时间延长和医疗成本升高相关。尽管其普遍存在，早期预测和预防仍然具有挑战性。环境因素如环境声音和光照可能影响谵妄的发生，但在风险评估中常被忽视。在本研究中，我们考察了光强度和声压级能否在多个预测时间窗口下独立预测谵妄。我们在9个ICU的309名患者中采集数据，评估了四种高效的序列神经网络模型，以在10种预测窗口大小下预测谵妄。我们使用Shapley Additive Explanations（SHAP）分析报告了特征重要性和影响方向。卷积模型在声音数据和组合数据上均实现了最强的判别能力，AUC达到0.80。声音特征是整体上的主导预测因子。将声音与光照结合可改善短期（<1周）预测效果，且组合模型在感知周期结束后立即赋予最高风险。这些发现表明，被动环境感知，尤其是声音监测，可以为谵妄风险评估提供临床上有意义的可解释信号，并为增强多模态ICU预测与预防策略提供一条实用路径。

</details>


### [219] [Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19297)
*Nikita Kachaev,Andrey Moskalenko,Matvey Skripkin,Nikita Kurlaev,Daria Pugacheva,Albina Burlova,Mikhail Kolosov,Denis Shepelev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Elena Tutubalina,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Act2Answer，一种通过动作回答问题来评估具身VLA模型知识保留能力的轻量级协议，发现VLA在简单概念上表现良好，但在丰富语义类别上与源VLM存在差距，且答案相关信息在VLA中层达到峰值后衰减。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VLA模型通常由预训练VLM微调得到，但微调后模型保留了多少常识性和事实性知识尚不清楚。知识敏感任务上的失败难以区分是由于知识缺失还是低层控制泛化不足，因此需要一种能减少控制混杂因素的知识评估方法。

Method: 提出Act2Answer协议：将VLM知识基准测试改编为VLA评估，要求智能体通过动作来回答问题。每个问题对应一个简短桌面操作场景，智能体执行单个物体放置动作来从候选答案中选择，从而得到基于动作的成功率，减少控制层面的干扰。同时引入层间意图探针（layerwise intent probing）来定位VLM骨干网和动作头中答案相关信息的位置。

Result: 对7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线进行系统评估发现：VLA在简单概念上表现扎实，但在语义更丰富的类别上与源VLM相比差距更大；VQA协同训练与更好的知识保留相关；答案相关信息在VLA中层达到峰值，但在上层衰减。

Conclusion: Act2Answer提供了一种有效的VLA知识评估方法，揭示了VLA模型在知识保留方面存在的不足，并为未来改进提供了方向（如VQA协同训练、中层特征利用等）。

Original Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.

中文摘要翻译: 具身视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常通过在机器人数据上微调强大的预训练VLM获得，但尚不清楚它们在适配后保留了多少常识性和事实性知识。在知识敏感任务上的失败具有歧义性，混淆了知识缺失与低层控制泛化不足。我们提出Act2Answer，一种轻量级协议，通过要求智能体以动作回答问题的方式将VLM知识基准测试适配到VLA评估中。每个问题变成一个简短的桌面操作场景，智能体执行单个物体放置动作来选择候选答案，从而得到基于动作的成功率，并减少控制混淆因素。我们整理了一套涵盖多种常识和世界知识类别的测试环境，并引入层间意图探针来定位VLM骨干网和动作头中与答案相关的信息。在对7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线的大规模研究中，我们系统地按类别对模型进行排名，发现VLA在简单概念上表现扎实，但在语义更丰富的类别上与源VLM相比展现出更大的差距；VQA协同训练与更好的知识保留相关；答案相关信息在VLA中层达到峰值，但在上层衰减。Act2Answer可在https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/获取。

</details>


### [220] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络（P-K-GCN），用于在不规则几何体上进行时空超分辨率重建，通过结合连续样条GCN、Koopman算子理论和物理损失约束，在3D心脏电动力学重建中实现了优于基线模型的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高保真时空动力学模拟计算成本高昂，现有数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，而简单物理信息学习方法难以处理不规则空间几何和复杂时间动力学。

Method: 首先设计基于连续样条的GCN从粗粒度图中提取空间依赖关系；引入Koopman算子理论将非线性动力学投影到紧凑潜空间实现时间演化线性化；在优化目标中加入基于物理定律的损失函数增强数据驱动重建的物理一致性；提供理论分析证明物理增强和Koopman正则化通过降低Rademacher复杂度、收紧泛化边界来数学保证超分辨率误差的降低。

Result: 在3D心脏几何体上从稀疏低分辨率测量值重建高分辨率心脏电动力学的数值实验中，该方法相比基线模型取得了更优异的精度。

Conclusion: P-K-GCN通过融合图卷积、Koopman理论和物理约束，有效解决了不规则几何体上的时空超分辨率问题，并在理论上和实验上验证了其优越性。

Original Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

中文摘要翻译: 高保真度的时空动力学仿真在计算上是极其昂贵的，因此需要高效的超分辨率技术来从粗粒度输入重建高分辨率数据。传统的数据驱动方法往往缺乏物理约束，而简单的物理信息学习方法难以处理不规则的空间几何结构和复杂的演化时间动态。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络（Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network, P-K-GCN），用于不规则几何体上的时空超分辨率。具体而言，首先设计了一个基于连续样条的GCN，直接从粗粒度图中提取空间依赖关系；同时引入Koopman算子理论，将非线性动力学投影到一个紧凑的潜空间中，在该空间中时间演化过程被线性化。其次，我们在优化目标中增补了一个基于物理定律的损失项，强制数据驱动的重建结果遵循物理规律，从而提高预测的保真度和鲁棒性。最后，我们提供了严格的理论分析，证明物理增强和Koopman正则化通过降低Rademacher复杂度并收紧泛化边界，从数学上保证了超分辨率误差的降低。我们在三维心脏几何体上从稀疏低分辨率测量值重建高分辨率心脏电动力学的任务中评估了该框架。数值实验表明，与基线模型相比，我们的方法实现了更优的精度。

</details>


### [221] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diffusion-Proof，首个将扩散LLM（dLLM）应用于形式化定理证明的框架，包含全证明生成模型dLLM-Prover-7B和基于扩散的局部校正模型dLLM-Corrector-7B，在ProofNet和MiniF2F基准上超越自回归基线，并成功解决一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B未能解出的IMO问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归LLM在形式化定理证明中存在长程连贯性差和错误累积问题，而扩散LLM（dLLM）通过迭代去噪多token块生成文本，具有保持长程连贯性的潜力，但dLLM在形式化数学中的应用尚属空白。

Method: 提出Diffusion-Proof框架，包含两个模型：(1) dLLM-Prover-7B，利用dLLM进行整个证明的生成，保持长程连贯的策略使用；(2) dLLM-Corrector-7B，基于大块扩散的校正模型，利用dLLM的填充能力进行双向信息驱动的局部证明校正。

Result: 在相同数据集下，Diffusion-Proof显著优于自回归基线：ProofNet-Test上绝对提升1.61%，MiniF2F-Test上绝对提升6.14%。此外，成功解决了一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B无法解出的IMO问题。

Conclusion: 扩散LLM在形式化定理证明中具有独特优势，特别是在长程连贯性和错误校正方面，为形式化数学推理提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，提升大型语言模型（LLM）的形式化数学推理能力已成为数学与计算机科学领域的核心关注点。尽管使用最先进的自回归（AR）LLM进行形式化定理证明取得了显著进展，但这些模型存在固有局限性：其逐token预测的生成方式因长程连贯性挑战和长序列上的错误累积，可能导致次优性能。近期扩散LLM（dLLM）的进展——通过多token块的迭代去噪生成文本——提供了一种有前景的替代方案。然而，dLLM在形式化数学中的应用，尤其是在需要保持长程连贯性的场景中，仍鲜有研究。为解决上述挑战，我们提出了**Diffusion-Proof**，据我们所知，这是首个训练并应用dLLM进行形式化定理证明的框架。该框架包含两个模型的训练与推理方法：第一个是*dLLM-Prover-7B*，能够通过长程连贯的策略使用进行整体证明书写；第二个是*dLLM-Corrector-7B*，一种新颖的基于大块扩散的校正模型，它利用dLLM的填充能力，通过双向信息进行局部证明校正。大量实验表明，**Diffusion-Proof**在相同数据集上显著优于自回归LLM基线。与基线相比，**Diffusion-Proof**在ProofNet-Test上取得了**1.61%**的绝对提升，在MiniF2F-Test上取得了**6.14%**的绝对提升。值得注意的是，**Diffusion-Proof**成功解决了一个更先进的推理模型DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B未能解出的IMO问题，展现了dLLM在形式化定理证明中的独特优势。

</details>


### [222] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的流水线，通过生成可执行的Python程序来近似替代Transformer语言模型中的注意力头，实现了对深度神经网络组件的可解释性近似。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长期以来，可解释深度学习的目标是用人类可理解的符号描述来替代不透明的神经计算。论文旨在解决如何将神经网络中的注意力头行为转化为可读、可执行的符号程序这一挑战。

Method: 首先计算给定注意力头在随机训练样本上的注意力矩阵；然后利用预训练语言模型根据矩阵摘要生成一组Python程序，使其能根据输入句子文本复现注意力模式；最后根据程序在保留样本上的预测表现进行重排序，选出最优程序集。

Result: 在GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B上，少于1000个生成程序即可复现注意力模式，平均IoU超过75%；替换25%的注意力头仅导致平均困惑度增加16%，且在下游问答基准上保持性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可扩展的反向工程流水线，用人类可读的可执行代码来理解Transformer模型中的注意力头，推动了神经模型向符号透明性方向的发展。

Original Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

中文摘要翻译: 可解释深度学习的长期目标是用人类可理解的符号描述来替代不透明的神经计算。在本文中，我们提出了一种用可执行程序来近似深度网络组件行为的方法。我们聚焦于Transformer语言模型中的注意力头。对于给定的注意力头，我们首先在随机选取的训练样本上计算其关联的注意力矩阵。接着，我们向一个预训练语言模型提供这些矩阵的摘要，并指示它生成一组Python程序，这些程序能够仅根据输入句子的文本复现相应的注意力模式。最后，我们根据最终程序集在保留输入上的行为预测表现对其进行重排序。我们证明，少于1000个这样的生成程序即可复现GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B中注意力头的注意力模式，在TinyStories数据集上实现了平均交并比相似度超过75%。此外，最佳拟合程序可以在不显著影响模型行为的情况下替代神经注意力头：在这三个模型中替换25%的注意力头为程序化替代品，仅导致平均困惑度增加16%，同时在多种下游问答基准上保持性能。这项工作提供了一个可扩展的流水线，用于使用人类可读、可执行的代码对Transformer模型中的注意力头进行反向工程，推动了神经模型向符号透明性方向的发展。

</details>


### [223] [UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19328)
*Mohamed Nabail,Leo Cheng,Jingmin Wang,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的不平衡偏好规划(UBP2)方法，通过联合推理奖励、动力学和值函数的不确定性来主动引导探索，显著提升偏好强化学习的样本效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有偏好强化学习方法依赖被动数据收集，在早期学习阶段样本效率低下，缺乏主动探索机制。

Method: 提出UBP2方法，使用奖励、动力学和值函数的集成模型，根据统一评分（结合期望奖励、终值和外推不确定性）评估候选轨迹，在规划中显式平衡利用与信息获取。

Result: 在标准正则性假设下，证明有限时域和无限时域设置下的次线性遗憾界；在Meta-World基准上，UBP2的样本效率显著优于无模型偏好方法和非乐观的基于模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过联合不确定性推理和主动探索，基于模型的方法能有效解决偏好RL的样本效率问题，无需启发式探索策略即可实现利用-探索的显式权衡。

Original Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 基于偏好的强化学习提供了一种从行为两两比较中学习奖励模型的方法，无需显式设计奖励。然而，现有方法通常依赖被动数据收集，在学习的早期阶段样本效率低下。我们提出了一种基于模型的方法，通过联合推理奖励、动力学和值函数的不确定性来主动引导探索。我们的方法——不平衡偏好规划(UBP2)——使用奖励、动力学和值函数的集成模型，根据结合期望奖励、终值和外推不确定性的统一评分来评估候选轨迹。在该目标下的规划显式地实现了利用与信息获取之间的权衡，无需ad hoc探索启发式。在标准正则性假设下，我们为有限时域和无限时域设置建立了次线性遗憾保证。在Meta-World基准上的实验表明，UBP2相比无模型偏好方法和非乐观的基于模型基线，实现了显著更高的样本效率。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Can neurons speak? Semantic narration of vision at single-cell resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18667)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Richard Hakim,Sara Matias,Venkatesh N. Murthy,Na Li,Demba Ba*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出NEURRATOR框架，利用CLIP和多模态语言模型将神经尖峰活动解码为自由形式的自然语言描述，实现了单神经元分辨率的视觉场景叙述，并用于量化解码保真度及揭示单个神经元和遗传标记抑制性细胞类型对视觉表征的贡献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高阶视皮层中单个神经元编码的内容难以通过直觉参数化表征，现有的深度网络嵌入方法又存在黑箱问题，因此需要一种可解释的、自然语言驱动的解码方法来揭示单个神经元的视觉编码内容。

Method: 学习一个编码器将任意子集的同步记录尖峰序列映射到冻结CLIP的图像块嵌入空间，再由多模态语言模型和稀疏自编码器生成并验证描述（无需语言侧训练）。应用于小鼠视觉皮层Neuropixel记录的自然电影观看数据。

Result: (i) 量化了解码保真度随群体规模和皮层区域的变化；(ii) 用通俗语言"叙述"了单个神经元及基因标记抑制性细胞类型对视觉表征的贡献，将细胞身份从分类目标转变为视觉系统的功能探针。

Conclusion: NEURRATOR将细胞身份重新定义为视觉系统的功能探针，为神经系统的生物学洞察提供了新单位。

Original Abstract: Identifying what individual neurons encode in higher-order visual cortex is an open problem. Responses resist intuitive parameterization, and the deep-network embeddings used in their place are black boxes. Here, we introduce NEURRATOR, a framework that decodes spiking activity into free-form natural-language narration of the viewed scene at single-neuron resolution. A learned encoder maps spike trains from arbitrary subsets of simultaneously-recorded neurons into the patch-embedding space of a frozen CLIP, from which a multimodal language model and sparse autoencoder generates and validates a description with no language-side training. Applied to Neuropixel recordings of mouse visual cortex during natural-movie viewing, NEURRATOR narrates from thousands of neurons, singular cortical regions, local populations, or from a molecularly-defined cell-types. We use this property to (i) quantify how decoding fidelity scales with population size and cortical region, and (ii) "neurrate", in plain language, what individual neurons and genetically-tagged inhibitory cell-types contribute to visual representation. This recasts cell identity from a classification target into a functional probe of the visual system, providing a new unit of biological insights in neural systems.

中文摘要翻译: 在高阶视皮层中识别单个神经元编码的内容是一个尚未解决的问题。神经元反应难以通过直觉参数化来表征，而用于替代的深度网络嵌入方法又是黑箱。本文引入NEURRATOR，一个将尖峰活动解码为自由形式自然语言描述（以单神经元分辨率呈现所观看场景）的框架。该框架学习一个编码器，将来自任意同步记录神经元子集的尖峰序列映射到冻结CLIP的图像块嵌入空间，然后通过多模态语言模型和稀疏自编码器生成并验证描述，无需在语言侧进行训练。将该方法应用于自然电影观看过程中小鼠视觉皮层的Neuropixel记录，NEURRATOR能够从数千个神经元、单一皮层区域、局部神经元群体或分子定义的细胞类型进行叙述。我们利用这一特性来：(i) 量化解码保真度如何随群体规模和皮层区域变化；(ii) 用通俗语言"叙述"单个神经元及基因标记抑制性细胞类型对视觉表征的贡献。这将细胞身份从分类目标重新定义为视觉系统的功能探针，为神经系统的生物学洞察提供了新的分析单位。

</details>


### [225] [Retrieval-Based Brain Decoding by Alignment, not Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19081)
*Matteo Ciferri,Matteo Ferrante,Nicola Toschi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用对比性目标函数将fMRI活动映射到基础模型的嵌入空间中，发现线性对比解码器在多种模态（图像、文本、音频）下均优于岭回归和非线性方法，表明解码性能的提升更多来自训练目标的选择而非架构复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于认知科学中概念在高维向量空间中组织的理论，以及脑解码的目标是从神经活动中重建或检索刺激表征，作者希望探索对比性目标函数作为逆转大脑损失函数的生物合理候选方法。

Method: 将fMRI活动通过线性对比解码器（对比性目标函数+线性映射）映射到视觉、语言和音频基础模型的嵌入空间中，并在多个数据集上与岭回归和非线性方法进行对比实验。

Result: 在多个数据集上，线性对比解码器在图像、文本和声音模态下一致地优于岭回归和标准非线性替代方法。解码性能的提升主要来自训练目标（对比性目标）的选择，而非模型架构的复杂度。

Conclusion: fMRI测量由于对信号进行时空平均和噪声平滑，有效线性化了可观测的神经表征，因此线性对比模型是脑解码的一种有原理的策略。

Original Abstract: A prominent theory in cognitive science suggests that concepts in the brain are organized as high-dimensional vectors, with semantic meaning captured by directions and relative angles in this space. Brain decoding is the effort of reconstructing or retrieving stimuli (or their representations) from neural activity and involves finding a function that approximates how the brain represents concepts. This motivates the investigation of contrastive objectives as biologically plausible candidates to reverse the brain loss function. In this work, we study how functional MRI (fMRI) activity can generally be mapped with the embedding spaces of foundation models in vision, language, and audio. Although neural computations are highly non-linear at the microscale, fMRI measurements average signals across space and time, further smoothed by noise, effectively linearizing the observable representation. Consistent with these views, our experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that linear contrastive decoders consistently outperform ridge regression and standard non-linear alternatives, and that these results generalize across images, text, and sound. These findings indicate that decoding gains arise more from the choice of training objective than from architectural complexity, pointing to contrastive-linear models as a principled strategy for brain decoding.

中文摘要翻译: 认知科学中的一个重要理论认为，大脑中的概念被组织为高维向量，语义含义由该空间中的方向和相对角度捕捉。脑解码是从神经活动中重建或检索刺激（或其表征）的过程，涉及寻找一个近似大脑如何表征概念的函数。这激发了对对比性目标函数作为逆转大脑损失函数的生物合理候选方法的研究。在本工作中，我们研究了如何将功能性磁共振成像（fMRI）活动一般性地映射到视觉、语言和音频基础模型的嵌入空间中。尽管神经计算在微观尺度上是高度非线性的，但fMRI测量对空间和时间上的信号进行了平均，并进一步被噪声平滑，从而有效线性化了可观测表征。与这些观点一致，我们在多个数据集上的实验表明，线性对比解码器一致地优于岭回归和标准非线性替代方法，并且这些结果在图像、文本和声音模态中具有普适性。这些发现表明，解码性能的提升更多来自训练目标的选择而非架构复杂度，指向对比线性模型作为脑解码的一种有原理的策略。

</details>
